// v4.0.0: PRECOMPUTED HIERARCHICAL OVERLAY — nationwide major-road topology is prebuilt in GitHub Actions and stored at graph/v4/TW/overlay.json. Query-time routing runs overlay A* first, then one detailed A* inside the selected corridor; HERE/TDX traffic and signal ETA logic are preserved. Motorcycle /route is unchanged.
// v3.7.1: BOUNDED URBAN SEARCH — keep traffic logic unchanged. Short/medium routes now use an adaptive geographic envelope and skip extra candidate passes when the baseline search is already expensive, preventing dense Taipei/New Taipei trips from hitting Cloudflare 1102.
// v3.7.0: ISOLATED SEGMENT GRAPHS — keep traffic logic unchanged. Long gateway legs now run in fresh per-segment graph stores so previously loaded Taiwan tiles/nodes do not accumulate across the entire request and trigger Cloudflare 1102.
// v3.6.4: MOUNTAIN GATEWAY FIX — keep traffic logic unchanged. Add extra mountain-access gateway legs for east-coast→Yilan/interior destinations so the final backbone leg does not consume the whole expansion budget at once.
// v3.6.3: GEOENVELOPE FIX — fix `geoEnvelope is not defined` in the long-distance response path; no routing, HERE/TDX traffic, matcher, cache, or ETA behavior changes.
// v3.6.2: ADAPTIVE LONG MODE — long-mode selection no longer relies only on straight-line distance; east-coast and mountain-crossing trips can enter segmented long routing even when air distance is under 60 km.
// v3.6.1: STRICT SEGMENTED BACKEND — force Hualien↔north-Taiwan long trips through shorter coastal gateway legs, tighten per-leg envelopes, and cap per-leg expansions to reduce Cloudflare 1102 risk.
// v3.6.0: REGIONAL GATEWAY BACKEND — cross-island long routes are segmented through Taiwan regional gateways so each A* leg has a bounded search area instead of fan-searching across the Central Mountain Range.
// v3.5.0: LONG-DISTANCE ENDPOINT ANCHORS — split long routes into local-start + major-road backbone + local-end. The backbone stops at nearby major-road anchors instead of expanding deep into dense city streets, preventing Taipei/New Taipei endpoint CPU blowups.
// v3.4.1: LONG-DISTANCE PRUNING — tighten endpoint minor-road access and prune same-side long routes with a generous geographic envelope before loading more R2 tiles. Prevents dense Taipei tail expansion from causing 1102.
// v3.4.0: LONG-DISTANCE BACKBONE — routes >=60 km switch to one lightweight major-road A* pass, defer traffic until a route exists, then fetch HERE/TDX only along the finished route corridor. Prevents Hualien↔Taipei style requests from exhausting Worker CPU.
// v3.3.0: MULTI-CANDIDATE + POST-ROUTE ETA — generate normal, expressway-biased, and live-corridor candidates; score only completed routes with full HERE/TDX matching plus R2 signal/turn delay, then select the lowest final ETA.
// v3.2.4: FAST CORRIDOR — replace O(nodes × route-points) corridor scans with O(1) corridor-cell lookup; disable expensive HERE chain-alias inference during A* search and only use it on the final route; overlap traffic fetch with baseline A*.
// v3.2.3: TWO-PASS TRAFFIC CORRIDOR — first find a cheap baseline route, then run traffic-aware A* only inside a corridor around that route; avoids matching HERE/TDX against hundreds of thousands of explored edges.
// v3.2.2: DYNAMIC TRAFFIC PRELOAD — short routes no longer fetch a 50 km HERE circle; HERE radius scales with trip distance, and HERE/TDX preload runs in parallel.
// v3.2.1: FAST NODE-ONLY A* — signals default OFF; when signals=0, A* state is node-only instead of (node,incoming-edge), removing the state explosion seen on multi-km routes.
// v3.2.0: AUTO ROAD-GRAPH QUEUE — missing ROAD_GRAPH tiles are automatically queued and built shard-by-shard in the background; routing never blocks for graph construction.
// v3.1.0: SIGNAL/TURN-AWARE A* — reuse the existing SIGNALS R2 database and residual-delay profiles inside custom-car A*; search state is (node,incoming-edge), so signals and turns can change route choice.
// v3.0.2: HERE ROAD-CHAIN MATCHING — cautiously recover name-mismatched HERE traffic only when the same HERE road is geometrically stable across a sufficiently long OSM way chain.
// v3.0.1: TRAFFIC MATCH GUARD — reject HERE candidates whose non-empty road names conflict with the OSM edge; keep TDX route-number aliases allowed.
// v3.0.0: LIVE TRAFFIC A* — HERE + TDX traffic snapshot is matched to OSM graph edges during A*; TDX first, HERE fallback, baseline last; closures hard-excluded.
// v2.7.0: edge snapping — snap start/end to nearest drivable directed road segment and inject virtual routing nodes; fixes large node-snap errors.
// v2.6.5: sharded graph provisioning — one small OSM API shard per build request, 16 R2 shards + manifest; avoids Worker 1102 resource exhaustion.
// v2.6.4: graph provisioning fallback — build graph tiles from official OSM API /map JSON on a small grid; routing remains R2-only.
// v2.6.3: graph builder source fix — current Overpass mirrors, direct 4-way first split, no stale kumi endpoint, consistent version reporting.
// v2.6.2: stabilize custom-car routing — no Overpass inside route requests, failed-tile dedupe, role-aware snap, prioritized missing-tile diagnostics.
// v2.6.1: custom-car lazy graph fill — dedupe R2 tile loads and auto-build up to 2 missing graph tiles only when A* actually reaches them.
// v2.6.0: add /route/custom-car — first online R2-backed car A* router (baseline speeds, lazy graph-tile loading, no motorcycle changes).
// v2.5.2: adaptive Overpass split — dense/timeout bbox auto-splits into 4, recursively up to 16 sub-bboxes, then merges/dedupes ways.
// v2.5.1: speed up /graph/build-tile — Overpass way geometry only + parallel first-success endpoint race + 8.5 s bound.
// v2.5.0: add /graph/build-tile — fetch OSM car roads from Overpass, build directed graph tile, persist to ROAD_GRAPH R2.
// v2.4.1: add /graph/test — ROAD_GRAPH R2 binding read/write/delete diagnostic.
// v2.4.0: add /traffic/area — 50 km area traffic preload, 75 km long-trip centers, lazy-area ready.
// v2.3.7: motorcycle-only TDX expressway denylist; HERE retains its original matching rules.
// v2.3.7: guarantee progress through HERE null gaps; long missing runs remain unmatched.
// v2.3.7: exact nearest-segment BVH, deferred bearing, early candidate rejection; finer HERE CPU-stage diagnostics.
// v2.3.3: Fast traffic bypasses OSRM node lookup at every route length; Full retains node matching and signal delays.
// v2.3.2: bounded HERE network wait; motorcycle does not require unused TDX credentials.
// v2.3.1: long-route HERE restored by capping expensive true RoadLink matching to the near route.
/**
 * Cloudflare Worker — Traffic (TDX + HERE) + Google (Geocoding + Routes)
 * v2.2.1 — RoadLink Chain + Sticky HERE Matching + R2 Signal DB
 *   - /traffic/match-route auto-derives real OSRM node-pair road links when frontend does not send navLinkHints
 *   - node-pair RoadLinks are grouped into longer RoadLink Chains; continuity no longer resets at every OSM node
 *   - HERE matching is sticky inside a safe chain, but still breaks at real road/class/direction boundaries
 *   - isolated short HERE jumps are smoothed only when the previous/next HERE segment geometrically fits
 *   - signal nodes are read from R2 tiles, clustered, and assigned one-to-one to route intersections
 *   - navigation-time Overpass lookup is removed; missing R2 coverage becomes UNKNOWN (+0s)
 *   - bounded-concurrency R2 tile reads keep full ETA fast and stable
 *   - signal delay is residual-only (traffic-aware) with a 20% driving-time safety cap
 *   - returns edgeTimeline for segment-by-segment remaining ETA
 *
 * Endpoints
 *   GET  /health
 *   GET  /graph/test
 *        Temporary ROAD_GRAPH R2 diagnostic: put -> get -> verify -> delete.
 *   GET  /graph/build-tile?lat=25.03&lon=121.46&force=0&part=optional
 *        Build ONE of 16 small graph shards per request; repeat until complete=true.
 *   GET/POST /route/custom-car
 *        body: { start:[lat,lon], end:[lat,lon], traffic:true, debug:false }
 *        v4: precomputed hierarchical overlay + one detailed corridor A* + existing HERE/TDX post-route scoring.
 *
 *   (TDX traffic)
 *   GET  /traffic/geometry?scope=freeway|highway|taipei
 *   GET  /traffic/live?scope=freeway|highway|taipei
 *
 *   (HERE traffic)
 *   GET  /traffic/here/flow?lat=25.0330&lon=121.5654&radius=5000
 *        optional: &fc=1,2,3,4,5
 *
 *   GET  /traffic/area?lat=25.0330&lon=121.5654&radius=50000
 *   POST /traffic/area
 *        body can be:
 *          { lat, lon, radiusM?:50000 }
 *          { start:[lat,lon], end:[lat,lon], radiusM?:50000, spacingM?:75000 }
 *          { centers:[{lat,lon,radiusM?}, ...] }
 *        Short trip (<=100 km): one midpoint R50 km.
 *        Long trip (>100 km): centers along the straight line about every 75 km, each R50 km.
 *        Response merges HERE + available TDX segments and is ready for later A* edge weighting.
 *
 *   GET  /navigation/route?start=lat,lon&via=lat,lon|lat,lon&end=lat,lon
 *        start/end/via also accept text places; backend geocodes when needed.
 *        returns OSRM route + TDX/HERE traffic + signal delay + final ETA.
 *
 *   POST /traffic/match-route
 *        body: {
 *          route: [[lat,lon], ...],
 *          osrmDurationSec?: number,
 *          fallbackSpeedKph?: number,
 *          maxEdgeM?: number,
 *          navLinkHints?: [...optional OSRM node-pair hints...]
 *        }
 *        若前端沒有 navLinkHints，後端會用 OSRM Match 將既有路線貼回道路網並取得 node annotations，建立真正 node-pair RoadLink；
 *        若自動建立失敗，再退回 baseline 分段 RoadLink，不再直接退成每個 matcher edge 一個 LOCAL link。
 *        TDX 先匹配；TDX 沒覆蓋的 route 區段以 HERE corridor 補資料。
 *
 *   (Google)
 *   GET  /geocode?q=...&region=tw&lang=zh-TW
 *   POST /route
 *
 * Required env
 *   TDX_CLIENT_ID
 *   TDX_CLIENT_SECRET
 *   GOOGLE_MAPS_KEY   (enable: Geocoding API + Routes API)
 *   HERE_API_KEY        (HERE Real-Time Traffic API v7)
 *   SIGNALS             (R2 bucket binding -> navigation-signals; contains signals/v1/z13/<x>/<y>.json)
 *   ROAD_GRAPH          (R2 bucket binding -> navigation; future custom-car routing graph tiles)
 *
 * Optional env (recommended)
 *   FREEWAY_GEOM_URL  (public JSON URL, e.g. R2 public)
 *   HIGHWAY_GEOM_URL  (public JSON URL, e.g. R2 public)
 *   HIGHWAY_STD_URL   (public JSON URL, e.g. R2 public)
 */

export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    let requestUrl = null;
    try {
      const url = new URL(request.url);
      requestUrl = url;

      if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
        return cors(new Response(null, { status: 204 }));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/" || url.pathname === "/health") {
        return cors(json({
          ok: true,
          version: "4.0.0",
          routingEngine: "hierarchical-overlay-v4",
          roadGraphOverlayKey: "graph/v4/TW/overlay.json",
          matcherVersion: "2.3.7-indexed-nearest",
          signalsR2Bound: !!(env?.SIGNALS && typeof env.SIGNALS.get === "function"),
          roadGraphR2Bound: !!(env?.ROAD_GRAPH && typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.get === "function" && typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.put === "function"),
          roadGraphQueueBound: !!(env?.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE && typeof env.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE.send === "function"),
          ts: Date.now()
        }));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/graph/test") {
        if (request.method !== "GET") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const bound = !!(
          env?.ROAD_GRAPH &&
          typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.get === "function" &&
          typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.put === "function" &&
          typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.delete === "function"
        );

        if (!bound) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            version: "3.7.1",
            roadGraphBound: false,
            writeOk: false,
            readOk: false,
            deleteOk: false,
            error: "ROAD_GRAPH_binding_missing",
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, 500));
        }

        const testKey = "_diagnostics/road-graph-binding-test.json";
        const nonce = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
        const payload = {
          type: "road_graph_r2_binding_test",
          nonce,
          writtenAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };

        let writeOk = false;
        let readOk = false;
        let deleteOk = false;
        let readBack = null;

        try {
          await env.ROAD_GRAPH.put(
            testKey,
            JSON.stringify(payload),
            {
              httpMetadata: {
                contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
              },
            }
          );
          writeOk = true;

          const obj = await env.ROAD_GRAPH.get(testKey);
          if (obj) {
            const raw = await obj.text();
            try {
              readBack = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
            } catch (_) {
              readBack = raw;
            }
            readOk = !!(
              readBack &&
              typeof readBack === "object" &&
              readBack.nonce === nonce
            );
          }

          await env.ROAD_GRAPH.delete(testKey);
          deleteOk = true;

          return cors(json({
            ok: writeOk && readOk && deleteOk,
            version: "3.7.1",
            roadGraphBound: true,
            writeOk,
            readOk,
            deleteOk,
            key: testKey,
            readBack,
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, writeOk && readOk && deleteOk ? 200 : 500));
        } catch (err) {
          try {
            await env.ROAD_GRAPH.delete(testKey);
            deleteOk = true;
          } catch (_) {}

          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            version: "3.7.1",
            roadGraphBound: true,
            writeOk,
            readOk,
            deleteOk,
            key: testKey,
            error: "ROAD_GRAPH_r2_test_failed",
            message: String(err?.message || err),
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, 500));
        }
      }


      if (url.pathname === "/graph/build-tile") {
        if (request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "POST") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const bound = !!(
          env?.ROAD_GRAPH &&
          typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.get === "function" &&
          typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.put === "function"
        );
        if (!bound) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            version: "4.0.0",
            error: "ROAD_GRAPH_binding_missing",
            roadGraphBound: false,
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, 500));
        }

        let input = {};
        if (request.method === "POST") {
          input = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
        }

        const lat = Number(input?.lat ?? url.searchParams.get("lat"));
        const lon = Number(input?.lon ?? input?.lng ?? url.searchParams.get("lon") ?? url.searchParams.get("lng"));
        const forceRaw = input?.force ?? url.searchParams.get("force");
        const force = forceRaw === true || forceRaw === 1 || String(forceRaw || "").toLowerCase() === "true" || String(forceRaw || "") === "1";

        if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_lat" }, 400));
        }
        if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_lon" }, 400));
        }

        try {
          const partRaw = input?.part ?? url.searchParams.get("part");
          const part = partRaw == null || partRaw === "" ? null : Number(partRaw);
          const out = await buildAndStoreOsmCarGraphTile(env, { lat, lon, force, part });
          return cors(json(out, out?.ok === false ? (out?.status || 500) : 200));
        } catch (err) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            version: "3.7.1",
            error: "graph_build_tile_internal_error",
            message: String(err?.message || err),
            stack: String(err?.stack || "").split("\n").slice(0, 8),
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, 500));
        }
      }


      if (url.pathname === "/graph/autobuild") {
        if (request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "POST") {
          return cors(json({ ok:false, error:"method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        let input = {};
        if (request.method === "POST") {
          input = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
        }

        const lat = Number(input?.lat ?? url.searchParams.get("lat"));
        const lon = Number(input?.lon ?? input?.lng ?? url.searchParams.get("lon") ?? url.searchParams.get("lng"));

        if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) {
          return cors(json({ ok:false, error:"invalid_lat" }, 400));
        }
        if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) {
          return cors(json({ ok:false, error:"invalid_lon" }, 400));
        }

        const tile = graphTileInfo(lat, lon);
        const queued = await enqueueRoadGraphBuildKeys(
          env,
          [tile.key],
          "manual-autobuild",
          1
        );

        const progress = await graphShardProgress(env, tile);
        return cors(json({
          ok:true,
          version:"3.7.1",
          tileId:tile.tileId,
          key:tile.key,
          queue:queued,
          progress:{
            completedParts:progress.completedCount,
            totalParts:progress.totalCount,
            remainingParts:progress.missing,
            complete:progress.complete
          },
          ts:Date.now()
        }, queued.bound ? 202 : 503));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/graph/autobuild/status") {
        const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get("lat"));
        const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get("lon"));

        if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) {
          return cors(json({ ok:false, error:"lat_lon_required" }, 400));
        }

        const tile = graphTileInfo(lat, lon);
        const progress = await graphShardProgress(env, tile);
        let queueMarker = null;
        if (env?.TRAFFIC_KV && typeof env.TRAFFIC_KV.get === "function") {
          try {
            const raw = await env.TRAFFIC_KV.get(roadGraphAutobuildMarkerKey(tile.key));
            queueMarker = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
          } catch (_) {}
        }

        return cors(json({
          ok:true,
          version:"3.7.1",
          tileId:tile.tileId,
          key:tile.key,
          complete:progress.complete,
          completedParts:progress.completedCount,
          totalParts:progress.totalCount,
          remainingParts:progress.missing,
          queueMarker,
          queueBound:!!(env?.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE && typeof env.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE.send === "function"),
          ts:Date.now()
        }));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/traffic/signals/status") {
        const bound = !!(env?.SIGNALS && typeof env.SIGNALS.get === "function");
        const manifest = bound ? await loadSignalDbManifest(env, "taiwan") : null;
        return cors(json({
          ok: true,
          version: "3.7.1",
          bound,
          tileZoom: SIGNAL_TILE_Z,
          tilePrefix: SIGNAL_TILE_PREFIX,
          manifest: manifest?.ok ? manifest.data : null,
          manifestSource: manifest?.source || null,
          ts: Date.now(),
        }));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/geocode") {
        const gkey = getGoogleKey(env);
        if (!gkey) return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "GOOGLE_MAPS_KEY_missing" }, 500));

        const q0 = (url.searchParams.get("q") || "").trim();
        if (!q0) return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "q_required" }, 400));

        const q = q0.replace(/[臺台]/g, "台");
        const region = (url.searchParams.get("region") || "tw").trim();
        const lang = (url.searchParams.get("lang") || "zh-TW").trim();

        const out = await googleGeocodeCompatCached({ googleKey: gkey }, { q, region, lang });
        return cors(json({ ...out, ts: Date.now() }));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/navigation/route" || url.pathname === "/nav-route") {
        if (request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "POST") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const hkey = getHereKey(env);
        if (!hkey) return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "HERE_API_KEY_missing" }, 500));
        if (!env.TDX_CLIENT_ID || !env.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "TDX_credentials_missing" }, 500));
        }

        let input;
        if (request.method === "POST") {
          input = await request.json().catch(() => null);
          if (!input) return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_json" }, 400));
        } else {
          const viaRaw = url.searchParams.getAll("via");
          const viaSplit = [];
          for (const v of viaRaw) {
            for (const x of String(v || "").split("|")) {
              if (x.trim()) viaSplit.push(x.trim());
            }
          }
          input = {
            start: url.searchParams.get("start") || url.searchParams.get("origin") || "",
            end: url.searchParams.get("end") || url.searchParams.get("destination") || "",
            vias: viaSplit,
            maxHereQueries: Number(url.searchParams.get("maxHereQueries")) || undefined,
          };
        }

        try {
          const out = await computeNavigationRouteV214(env, { hereKey: hkey, googleKey: getGoogleKey(env) }, input);
          return cors(json(out, out?.ok === false ? (out?.status || 400) : 200));
        } catch (err) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            error: "navigation_route_internal_error",
            message: String(err?.message || err),
          }, 500));
        }
      }


      if (url.pathname === "/route/custom-car") {
        if (request.method !== "POST" && request.method !== "GET") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const bound = !!(
          env?.ROAD_GRAPH &&
          typeof env.ROAD_GRAPH.get === "function"
        );
        if (!bound) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            version: "3.7.1",
            error: "ROAD_GRAPH_binding_missing",
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, 500));
        }

        let input = {};
        if (request.method === "POST") {
          input = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
        } else {
          input = {
            start: url.searchParams.get("start") || "",
            end: url.searchParams.get("end") || "",
            traffic: !["0","false","off","no"].includes(String(url.searchParams.get("traffic") || "1").toLowerCase()),
            signals: ["1","true","on","yes"].includes(String(url.searchParams.get("signals") || "0").toLowerCase()),
            postsignals: !["0","false","off","no"].includes(String(url.searchParams.get("postsignals") || "1").toLowerCase()),
            autograph: !["0","false","off","no"].includes(String(url.searchParams.get("autograph") || "1").toLowerCase()),
            debug: ["1","true","on","yes"].includes(String(url.searchParams.get("debug") || "0").toLowerCase()),
          };
        }

        try {
          const out = await routeCustomCarAStar(env, input, {
            hereKey: getHereKey(env),
          });

          const autograph = customCarBool(input?.autograph, true);
          let graphAutobuild = null;

          if (
            autograph &&
            out?.ok === false &&
            Array.isArray(out?.missingGraphTiles) &&
            out.missingGraphTiles.length
          ) {
            graphAutobuild = await enqueueRoadGraphBuildKeys(
              env,
              out.missingGraphTiles,
              `route:${out.error || "missing-graph"}`,
              ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_TILES_PER_ROUTE
            );

            if (graphAutobuild?.queuedCount > 0) {
              out.routeError = out.error || null;
              out.error = "road_graph_preparing";
              out.status = 202;
              out.retryAfterSec = ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_RETRY_AFTER_SEC;
              out.message = "Missing ROAD_GRAPH data is being built automatically in the background. Retry this route shortly.";
            }
          }

          if (graphAutobuild) out.graphAutobuild = graphAutobuild;

          return cors(json(out, out?.ok === false ? (out?.status || 400) : 200));
        } catch (err) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            version: "3.7.1",
            error: "custom_car_route_internal_error",
            message: String(err?.message || err),
            stack: String(err?.stack || "").split("\n").slice(0, 8),
            ts: Date.now(),
          }, 500));
        }
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/route") {
        if (request.method !== "POST") return cors(json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));

        const gkey = getGoogleKey(env);
        if (!gkey) return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "GOOGLE_MAPS_KEY_missing" }, 500));

        const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
        if (!body) return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_json" }, 400));

        const out = await googleComputeRoutesRobust({ googleKey: gkey }, body);
        return cors(json(out));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/traffic/area") {
        if (request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "POST") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        let body = {};
        if (request.method === "POST") {
          body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
          if (!body || typeof body !== "object") {
            return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_json" }, 400));
          }
        } else {
          body = {
            lat: url.searchParams.get("lat"),
            lon: url.searchParams.get("lon") ?? url.searchParams.get("lng"),
            radiusM: url.searchParams.get("radius") ?? url.searchParams.get("radiusM"),
            spacingM: url.searchParams.get("spacing") ?? url.searchParams.get("spacingM"),
            start: url.searchParams.get("start"),
            end: url.searchParams.get("end"),
            fc: url.searchParams.get("fc"),
            includeHere: url.searchParams.get("includeHere"),
            includeTdx: url.searchParams.get("includeTdx"),
          };
        }

        const parsed = normalizeTrafficAreaRequest(body);
        if (!parsed.ok) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            error: parsed.error || "invalid_traffic_area_request",
            message: parsed.message || null,
          }, 400));
        }

        const includeHere = parsed.includeHere;
        const hkey = includeHere ? getHereKey(env) : null;
        if (includeHere && !hkey) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "HERE_API_KEY_missing" }, 500));
        }

        try {
          const out = await getTrafficArea(
            env,
            { hereKey: hkey },
            parsed
          );
          return cors(json(out, out?.ok === false ? (out?.status || 400) : 200));
        } catch (err) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            error: "traffic_area_internal_error",
            message: String(err?.message || err),
            stack: String(err?.stack || "").split("\n").slice(0, 8),
          }, 500));
        }
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/traffic/here/flow") {
        if (request.method !== "GET") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const hkey = getHereKey(env);
        if (!hkey) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "HERE_API_KEY_missing" }, 500));
        }

        const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get("lat"));
        const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get("lon") ?? url.searchParams.get("lng"));
        const radiusRaw = Number(url.searchParams.get("radius") || 5000);
        const radius = Math.round(radiusRaw);

        if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_lat" }, 400));
        }
        if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_lon" }, 400));
        }
        if (!Number.isFinite(radius) || radius < 100 || radius > 50000) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_radius", allowedMeters: "100..50000" }, 400));
        }

        const fc = normalizeHereFunctionalClasses(url.searchParams.get("fc"));

        const out = await getHereFlowCached(
          { hereKey: hkey },
          { lat, lon, radius, functionalClasses: fc }
        );
        return cors(json(out));
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/traffic/debug-here-route") {
        if (request.method !== "POST") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const hkey = getHereKey(env);
        if (!hkey) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "HERE_API_KEY_missing" }, 500));
        }

        const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
        if (!body) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_json" }, 400));
        }

        try {
          const out = await debugHereRouteOnly({ hereKey: hkey }, body);
          return cors(json(out, out?.ok === false ? (out?.status || 400) : 200));
        } catch (err) {
          return cors(json({
            ok: false,
            error: "here_debug_internal_error",
            message: String(err?.message || err),
            stack: String(err?.stack || "").split("\n").slice(0, 8),
          }, 500));
        }
      }

      if (url.pathname === "/traffic/match-route") {
        if (request.method !== "POST") {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405));
        }

        const hkey = getHereKey(env);
        if (!hkey) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "HERE_API_KEY_missing" }, 500));
        }

        const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
        if (!body) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "invalid_json" }, 400));
        }

        if (body.etaPolicy !== "HERE_GOOGLE_MOTO" && (!env.TDX_CLIENT_ID || !env.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET)) {
          return cors(json({ ok: false, error: "TDX_credentials_missing" }, 500));
        }
        const out = await matchTrafficToRoute(env, { hereKey: hkey }, body);
        return cors(json(out, out?.ok === false ? (out?.status || 400) : 200));
      }

      const scope = (url.searchParams.get("scope") || "").trim().toLowerCase();

      if (url.pathname === "/traffic/geometry") {
        if (!isValidTrafficScope(scope)) {
          return cors(json({ error: "invalid_scope", allowed: TRAFFIC_SCOPES }, 400));
        }

        if (scope === "freeway") {
          const geometries = await getFreewayGeometryCached(env);
          return cors(json({ scope: "freeway", ts: Date.now(), updateIntervalSec: 604800, geometries }));
        }

        if (scope === "highway") {
          const geometries = await getHighwayGeometryCached(env);
          return cors(json({ scope: "highway", ts: Date.now(), updateIntervalSec: 604800, geometries }));
        }

        const geometries = await getTaipeiGeometryCached(env);
        return cors(json({ scope: "taipei", ts: Date.now(), updateIntervalSec: 604800, geometries }));
      }

      if (url.pathname !== "/traffic/live") {
        return cors(json({ error: "not_found" }, 404));
      }

      if (!isValidTrafficScope(scope)) {
        return cors(json({ error: "invalid_scope", allowed: TRAFFIC_SCOPES }, 400));
      }

      if (!env.TDX_CLIENT_ID || !env.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET) {
        return cors(json({ error: "TDX_credentials_missing" }, 500));
      }

      if (scope === "freeway") {
        // Live is the only hard dependency. Section metadata is optional decoration only.
        const live = await getFreewayLiveCached(env);
        const sectionMeta = await safeOptional(() => getFreewaySectionMetaCached(env), {}, "freeway section metadata");

        const segments = [];
        for (const s of live?.LiveTraffics || []) {
          const meta = sectionMeta?.[s.SectionID] || {};
          const start = meta.start || "";
          const end = meta.end || "";

          const speed = toNumberOrNull(s.TravelSpeed);
          const level = normalizeLevelNumber(s.CongestionLevel);
          const status = resolveTdxTrafficStatus("freeway", speed, level);
          segments.push({
            provider: "TDX", scope: "freeway", sectionId: s.SectionID,
            road: meta.road || "", direction: meta.directionLabel || "", start, end,
            label: start || end ? `${start} → ${end}` : "",
            travelTimeSec: toNumberOrNull(s.TravelTime), speedKph: speed, level,
            trafficLevel: status.trafficLevel, validTraffic: status.validTraffic,
            closed: status.closed, insufficient: status.insufficient, fallbackToHere: status.fallbackToHere,
            dataTime: s.DataCollectTime || null,
          });
        }

        return cors(json({
          scope: "freeway", ts: Date.now(), updateIntervalSec: 30, segments,
          stale: live?.__stale === true,
          staleReason: live?.__staleReason || null,
        }));
      }

      if (scope === "taipei") {
        const live = await getTaipeiLiveCached(env);
        const sectionMeta = await safeOptional(() => getTaipeiSectionMetaCached(env), {}, "taipei section metadata");

        const segments = [];
        for (const s of live?.LiveTraffics || []) {
          const meta = sectionMeta?.[s.SectionID] || {};

          const speed = toNumberOrNull(s.TravelSpeed);
          const tsec = toNumberOrNull(s.TravelTime);
          const level = normalizeTaipeiLevel(s.CongestionLevel);

          const status = resolveTdxTrafficStatus("taipei", speed, level);

          segments.push({
            provider: "TDX",
            scope: "taipei",
            sectionId: s.SectionID,
            road: meta.road || "",
            direction: meta.directionLabel || "",
            start: meta.start || "",
            end: meta.end || "",
            label: (meta.start || meta.end) ? `${meta.start || ""} → ${meta.end || ""}` : "",
            travelTimeSec: tsec,
            speedKph: speed,
            level,
            trafficLevel: status.trafficLevel,
            validTraffic: status.validTraffic,
            closed: status.closed,
            insufficient: status.insufficient,
            fallbackToHere: status.fallbackToHere,
            color: status.trafficLevel > 0 ? getTaipeiCongestionColor(level) : null,
            dataTime: s.DataCollectTime || null,
          });
        }

        return cors(json({
          scope: "taipei", ts: Date.now(), updateIntervalSec: 30, segments,
          stale: live?.__stale === true,
          staleReason: live?.__staleReason || null,
        }));
      }

      const live = await getHighwayLiveCached(env);
      const sectionMeta = await safeOptional(() => getHighwaySectionMetaCached(env), {}, "highway section metadata");
      const stdMap = await safeOptional(() => getHighwayStandardCached(env), {}, "highway congestion standard");

      const segments = [];
      for (const s of live?.LiveTraffics || []) {
        const meta = sectionMeta?.[s.SectionID] || {};

        const speed = toNumberOrNull(s.TravelSpeed);
        const tsec = toNumberOrNull(s.TravelTime);

        const level = resolveHighwayLevel(
          { congestionLevel: s.CongestionLevel, congestionLevelID: s.CongestionLevelID, speedKph: speed },
          stdMap
        );

        const status = resolveTdxTrafficStatus("highway", speed, level);

        segments.push({
          provider: "TDX",
          scope: "highway",
          sectionId: s.SectionID,
          road: meta.road || "",
          direction: meta.directionLabel || "",
          start: meta.start || "",
          end: meta.end || "",
          label: (meta.start || meta.end) ? `${meta.start || ""} → ${meta.end || ""}` : "",
          travelTimeSec: tsec,
          speedKph: speed,
          level,
          trafficLevel: status.trafficLevel,
          validTraffic: status.validTraffic,
          closed: status.closed,
          insufficient: status.insufficient,
          fallbackToHere: status.fallbackToHere,
          dataTime: s.DataCollectTime || null,
        });
      }

      return cors(json({
        scope: "highway", ts: Date.now(), updateIntervalSec: 30, segments,
        stale: live?.__stale === true,
        staleReason: live?.__staleReason || null,
      }));
    } catch (err) {
      // TDX token / upstream transient errors must not make the front-end traffic loop spam HTTP 500.
      // For /traffic/live, degrade to an empty 200 response; the next 30 s refresh can recover automatically.
      if (requestUrl?.pathname === "/traffic/live" && isTdxTransientError(err)) {
        const scope = (requestUrl.searchParams.get("scope") || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        return cors(json({
          ok: false,
          provider: "TDX",
          scope,
          unavailable: true,
          transient: true,
          ts: Date.now(),
          updateIntervalSec: 30,
          segments: [],
          error: "TDX_temporarily_unavailable",
          message: err?.message || String(err),
        }, 200));
      }

      return cors(json({
        ok: false,
        error: "worker_exception",
        message: err?.message || String(err),
        stack: err?.stack || null,
      }, 500));
    }
  },

  async queue(batch, env, ctx) {
    for (const message of (batch?.messages || [])) {
      try {
        const body = message?.body || {};
        const lat = Number(body.lat);
        const lon = Number(body.lon);
        const key = String(body.key || "");
        const step = Math.max(0, Number(body.step) || 0);
        const failures = Math.max(0, Number(body.failures) || 0);

        if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon) || !key) {
          message.ack();
          continue;
        }

        const out = await buildAndStoreOsmCarGraphTile(env, {
          lat,
          lon,
          force:false,
          part:null,
        });

        if (out?.ok && out?.complete) {
          await clearRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, key);
          message.ack();
          continue;
        }

        if (out?.ok && step < ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_STEPS) {
          await env.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE.send({
            ...body,
            step:step + 1,
            failures:0,
            lastCompletedParts:Number(out?.completedParts) || 0,
            lastTotalParts:Number(out?.totalParts) || (ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID * ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID),
            updatedAt:Date.now(),
          }, { delaySeconds: ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_NEXT_SHARD_DELAY_SEC });

          await writeRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, key, {
            key,
            lat,
            lon,
            state:"building",
            step:step + 1,
            completedParts:Number(out?.completedParts) || 0,
            totalParts:Number(out?.totalParts) || (ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID * ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID),
            updatedAt:Date.now(),
          });

          message.ack();
          continue;
        }

        if (!out?.ok && failures < ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_FAILURES) {
          await env.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE.send({
            ...body,
            failures:failures + 1,
            lastError:out?.error || "graph_build_failed",
            updatedAt:Date.now(),
          }, { delaySeconds: ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_FAILURE_DELAY_SEC });

          await writeRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, key, {
            key,
            lat,
            lon,
            state:"retrying",
            step,
            failures:failures + 1,
            lastError:out?.error || "graph_build_failed",
            updatedAt:Date.now(),
          });

          message.ack();
          continue;
        }

        await writeRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, key, {
          key,
          lat,
          lon,
          state:"stopped",
          step,
          failures,
          lastError:out?.error || (step >= ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_STEPS ? "step_limit" : "unknown"),
          updatedAt:Date.now(),
        });
        message.ack();
      } catch (err) {
        // Let Cloudflare retry this message if the consumer itself fails.
        try {
          message.retry({ delaySeconds: ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_FAILURE_DELAY_SEC });
        } catch (_) {}
      }
    }
  },
};


/* =========================
 * OSM car graph tile builder — v2.6.5
 * ========================= */


const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_TILES_PER_ROUTE = 2;
const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_STEPS = 28;
const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MAX_FAILURES = 6;
const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_NEXT_SHARD_DELAY_SEC = 2;
const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_FAILURE_DELAY_SEC = 20;
const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_RETRY_AFTER_SEC = 8;
const ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MARKER_TTL_SEC = 1800;

function roadGraphAutobuildMarkerKey(graphKey) {
  return `roadgraph-autobuild:${String(graphKey || "")}`;
}

async function writeRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, graphKey, data) {
  if (!env?.TRAFFIC_KV || typeof env.TRAFFIC_KV.put !== "function") return;
  try {
    await env.TRAFFIC_KV.put(
      roadGraphAutobuildMarkerKey(graphKey),
      JSON.stringify(data || {}),
      { expirationTtl: ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_MARKER_TTL_SEC }
    );
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function clearRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, graphKey) {
  if (!env?.TRAFFIC_KV || typeof env.TRAFFIC_KV.delete !== "function") return;
  try {
    await env.TRAFFIC_KV.delete(roadGraphAutobuildMarkerKey(graphKey));
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function enqueueRoadGraphBuildKeys(env, keys, reason = "route", maxTiles = 1) {
  const queueBound = !!(
    env?.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE &&
    typeof env.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE.send === "function"
  );

  if (!queueBound) {
    return {
      bound:false,
      queuedCount:0,
      skippedCount:0,
      reason:"GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE_binding_missing",
      requestedKeys:[...new Set(keys || [])].slice(0, Math.max(1, maxTiles)),
    };
  }

  const unique = [...new Set(keys || [])];
  const queued = [];
  const skipped = [];

  for (const key of unique) {
    if (queued.length >= Math.max(1, Number(maxTiles) || 1)) break;

    const center = graphTileCenterFromKey(key);
    if (!center) {
      skipped.push({ key, reason:"invalid_graph_key" });
      continue;
    }

    if (await roadGraphObjectExists(env.ROAD_GRAPH, key)) {
      skipped.push({ key, reason:"already_complete" });
      continue;
    }

    let existingMarker = null;
    if (env?.TRAFFIC_KV && typeof env.TRAFFIC_KV.get === "function") {
      try {
        existingMarker = await env.TRAFFIC_KV.get(roadGraphAutobuildMarkerKey(key));
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    if (existingMarker) {
      skipped.push({ key, reason:"already_queued" });
      continue;
    }

    const body = {
      type:"road_graph_tile_build",
      version:1,
      key,
      lat:center[0],
      lon:center[1],
      reason,
      step:0,
      failures:0,
      createdAt:Date.now(),
    };

    try {
      await writeRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, key, {
        ...body,
        state:"queued",
        updatedAt:Date.now(),
      });

      await env.GRAPH_BUILD_QUEUE.send(body);
      queued.push({ key, lat:center[0], lon:center[1] });
    } catch (err) {
      await clearRoadGraphAutobuildMarker(env, key);
      skipped.push({ key, reason:"queue_send_failed", error:String(err?.message || err) });
    }
  }

  return {
    bound:true,
    queuedCount:queued.length,
    skippedCount:skipped.length,
    queued,
    skipped,
    retryAfterSec:ROAD_GRAPH_AUTOQUEUE_RETRY_AFTER_SEC,
  };
}

const ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG = 0.05;
const ROAD_GRAPH_PADDING_M = 900;
const ROAD_GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
const ROAD_GRAPH_R2_PREFIX = "graph/v1/TW";
const ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_TIMEOUT_MS = 7000;
const ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_MAX_SPLIT_DEPTH = 2;
const ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_SPLIT_CONCURRENCY = 4;
const ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API = "https://api.openstreetmap.org/api/0.6/map";
const ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_GRID = 4;              // 4 x 4 small bboxes per graph tile
const ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_CONCURRENCY = 2;       // be conservative with the main OSM API
const ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_TIMEOUT_MS = 12000;
const ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_MAX_SPLIT_DEPTH = 1;   // a failed 4x4 cell may split once more
const ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID = 4;                 // one logical 0.05° tile = 16 R2 shards
const ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_QUERY_PAD_M = 180;        // small overlap so cross-shard edge geometry is available
const ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_PREFIX = "graph/v1/TW";

const ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_ENDPOINTS = [
  // Current global public mirrors. Server-to-server Worker fetch is not affected by browser CORS.
  "https://maps.mail.ru/osm/tools/overpass/api/interpreter",
  "https://overpass.private.coffee/api/interpreter",
];

const ROAD_GRAPH_CAR_HIGHWAYS = new Set([
  "motorway", "motorway_link",
  "trunk", "trunk_link",
  "primary", "primary_link",
  "secondary", "secondary_link",
  "tertiary", "tertiary_link",
  "unclassified", "residential",
  "living_street", "service", "road",
]);

const ROAD_GRAPH_DEFAULT_SPEED_KPH = Object.freeze({
  motorway: 90,
  motorway_link: 50,
  trunk: 70,
  trunk_link: 45,
  primary: 55,
  primary_link: 40,
  secondary: 45,
  secondary_link: 35,
  tertiary: 40,
  tertiary_link: 30,
  unclassified: 35,
  residential: 30,
  living_street: 15,
  service: 20,
  road: 30,
});

function floorGraphCoord(v) {
  const n = Math.floor((Number(v) + 1e-10) / ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG);
  return n * ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG;
}

function graphTileInfo(lat, lon) {
  const south = floorGraphCoord(lat);
  const west = floorGraphCoord(lon);
  const north = south + ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG;
  const east = west + ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG;

  const latPad = ROAD_GRAPH_PADDING_M / 111320;
  const midLat = (south + north) / 2;
  const lonPad = ROAD_GRAPH_PADDING_M / metersPerDegLon(midLat);

  const tileId = `${south.toFixed(2)}_${west.toFixed(2)}`;
  const key = `${ROAD_GRAPH_R2_PREFIX}/${tileId}.json`;

  return {
    tileId,
    key,
    bounds: { south, west, north, east },
    paddedBounds: {
      south: south - latPad,
      west: west - lonPad,
      north: north + latPad,
      east: east + lonPad,
    },
  };
}

function pointInsideGraphTile(lat, lon, bounds) {
  return (
    Number(lat) >= bounds.south &&
    Number(lat) < bounds.north &&
    Number(lon) >= bounds.west &&
    Number(lon) < bounds.east
  );
}

function parseOsmMaxspeedKph(raw) {
  const s = String(raw || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return null;

  // Common OSM forms: "50", "50 km/h", "30 mph".
  const m = s.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
  if (!m) return null;

  let v = Number(m[1]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) return null;
  if (/\bmph\b/.test(s)) v *= 1.609344;

  // Discard implausible/encoded values instead of poisoning routing costs.
  if (v < 5 || v > 160) return null;
  return Math.round(v * 10) / 10;
}

function osmTagDeniedForCar(tags) {
  const t = tags || {};
  const vals = [
    t.access,
    t.vehicle,
    t.motor_vehicle,
    t.motorcar,
  ].map(v => String(v || "").toLowerCase());

  const hardDenied = new Set(["no", "private", "agricultural", "forestry"]);
  if (vals.some(v => hardDenied.has(v))) return true;

  if (String(t.highway || "").toLowerCase() === "service") {
    const service = String(t.service || "").toLowerCase();
    // Parking aisles remain usable; emergency-only/service-only driveways are not useful through-routes.
    if (service === "emergency_access") return true;
  }

  return false;
}

function isOsmWayDrivableCar(tags) {
  const highway = String(tags?.highway || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!ROAD_GRAPH_CAR_HIGHWAYS.has(highway)) return false;
  if (osmTagDeniedForCar(tags)) return false;

  const status = String(tags?.status || tags?.construction || "").toLowerCase();
  if (highway === "construction" || status === "construction") return false;

  return true;
}

function osmWayDirection(tags) {
  const oneway = String(tags?.oneway || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const junction = String(tags?.junction || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const highway = String(tags?.highway || "").trim().toLowerCase();

  if (oneway === "-1" || oneway === "reverse") return -1;
  if (["yes", "1", "true"].includes(oneway)) return 1;

  // OSM defaults: roundabouts and motorways are normally one-way unless explicitly overridden.
  if (junction === "roundabout" || highway === "motorway" || highway === "motorway_link") {
    if (["no", "0", "false"].includes(oneway)) return 0;
    return 1;
  }

  return 0;
}

function graphDefaultSpeedKph(tags) {
  const explicit = parseOsmMaxspeedKph(tags?.maxspeed);
  if (explicit) return explicit;

  const highway = String(tags?.highway || "").toLowerCase();
  return Number(ROAD_GRAPH_DEFAULT_SPEED_KPH[highway]) || 30;
}

function graphEdgeLengthM(a, b) {
  return haversineM([a.lat, a.lon], [b.lat, b.lon]);
}

function compactOsmRoadName(tags) {
  return String(tags?.name || tags?.ref || "").trim().slice(0, 120);
}


function buildOverpassCarWayQuery(bounds) {
  const { south, west, north, east } = bounds;
  const highwayRegex = [
    "motorway", "motorway_link",
    "trunk", "trunk_link",
    "primary", "primary_link",
    "secondary", "secondary_link",
    "tertiary", "tertiary_link",
    "unclassified", "residential",
    "living_street", "service", "road",
  ].join("|");

  return `[out:json][timeout:10];
way["highway"~"^(${highwayRegex})$"](${south},${west},${north},${east});
out body geom qt;`;
}

async function fetchOverpassBBoxFirstSuccess(bounds) {
  const query = buildOverpassCarWayQuery(bounds);
  const controllers = ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_ENDPOINTS.map(() => new AbortController());

  async function attempt(endpoint, index) {
    const controller = controllers[index];
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_TIMEOUT_MS);
    const t0 = Date.now();

    try {
      const r = await fetch(endpoint, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8",
          "Accept": "application/json",
          "User-Agent": "SelfMadeNavigation-GraphBuilder/3.7.1",
        },
        body: "data=" + encodeURIComponent(query),
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      const responseText = await r.text();
      let raw = null;
      try { raw = responseText ? JSON.parse(responseText) : null; } catch (_) {}

      if (!r.ok || !raw || !Array.isArray(raw.elements)) {
        const err = new Error(
          String(raw?.remark || raw?.error || responseText.slice(0, 600) || `HTTP ${r.status}`)
        );
        err.graphFetch = {
          endpoint,
          status: r.status,
          error: "overpass_http_or_json_error",
          message: err.message,
          elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
          responseBytes: responseText.length,
        };
        throw err;
      }

      return {
        ok: true,
        endpoint,
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
        responseBytes: responseText.length,
        raw,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err?.graphFetch) throw err;

      const wrapped = new Error(String(err?.message || err));
      wrapped.graphFetch = {
        endpoint,
        status: 0,
        error: controller.signal.aborted ? "overpass_timeout" : "overpass_fetch_error",
        message: String(err?.message || err),
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
        responseBytes: 0,
      };
      throw wrapped;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  try {
    const winner = await Promise.any(
      ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_ENDPOINTS.map((endpoint, i) => attempt(endpoint, i))
    );

    for (const c of controllers) {
      if (!c.signal.aborted) {
        try { c.abort("overpass_winner_selected"); } catch (_) {}
      }
    }

    return {
      ...winner,
      attempts: [{
        endpoint: winner.endpoint,
        status: 200,
        error: null,
        message: null,
        elapsedMs: winner.elapsedMs,
        responseBytes: winner.responseBytes,
      }],
    };
  } catch (aggregate) {
    const errors = Array.isArray(aggregate?.errors) ? aggregate.errors : [];
    const details = errors.map(e => e?.graphFetch).filter(Boolean);
    const last = details[details.length - 1] || {
      endpoint: null,
      status: 0,
      error: "overpass_all_failed",
      message: String(aggregate?.message || aggregate || "All Overpass endpoints failed"),
      elapsedMs: ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_TIMEOUT_MS,
      responseBytes: 0,
    };
    return { ok: false, ...last, attempts: details };
  }
}

function splitBBox4(bounds) {
  const midLat = (Number(bounds.south) + Number(bounds.north)) / 2;
  const midLon = (Number(bounds.west) + Number(bounds.east)) / 2;
  return [
    { south: bounds.south, west: bounds.west, north: midLat, east: midLon },
    { south: bounds.south, west: midLon, north: midLat, east: bounds.east },
    { south: midLat, west: bounds.west, north: bounds.north, east: midLon },
    { south: midLat, west: midLon, north: bounds.north, east: bounds.east },
  ];
}

function mergeOverpassWayResults(results) {
  const ways = new Map();
  let responseBytes = 0;

  for (const item of results || []) {
    responseBytes += Number(item?.responseBytes) || 0;
    const elements = Array.isArray(item?.raw?.elements) ? item.raw.elements : [];
    for (const el of elements) {
      if (el?.type !== "way" || el?.id == null) continue;
      const key = String(el.id);
      const prev = ways.get(key);
      if (!prev) {
        ways.set(key, el);
        continue;
      }

      // If the same way is returned by adjacent sub-bboxes, keep the more
      // complete geometry representation.
      const prevGeom = Array.isArray(prev.geometry) ? prev.geometry.length : 0;
      const curGeom = Array.isArray(el.geometry) ? el.geometry.length : 0;
      if (curGeom > prevGeom) ways.set(key, el);
    }
  }

  return {
    raw: { elements: [...ways.values()] },
    responseBytes,
    uniqueWays: ways.size,
  };
}

async function fetchOsmBBoxAdaptive(bounds, depth = 0, stats = null) {
  const s = stats || {
    requestCount: 0,
    splitCount: 0,
    maxDepthUsed: 0,
    endpointHits: {},
    failures: [],
  };

  s.requestCount++;
  s.maxDepthUsed = Math.max(s.maxDepthUsed, depth);

  const direct = await fetchOverpassBBoxFirstSuccess(bounds);
  if (direct?.ok) {
    s.endpointHits[direct.endpoint] = (s.endpointHits[direct.endpoint] || 0) + 1;
    return {
      ok: true,
      pieces: [direct],
      stats: s,
    };
  }

  s.failures.push({
    depth,
    bounds,
    error: direct?.error || "overpass_failed",
    status: direct?.status || 0,
    endpoint: direct?.endpoint || null,
    elapsedMs: direct?.elapsedMs || null,
    attempts: direct?.attempts || [],
  });

  if (depth >= ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_MAX_SPLIT_DEPTH) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      status: direct?.status || 502,
      error: direct?.error || "overpass_failed_after_split",
      message: direct?.message || "Overpass failed after adaptive split.",
      endpoint: direct?.endpoint || null,
      elapsedMs: direct?.elapsedMs || null,
      attempts: direct?.attempts || [],
      stats: s,
    };
  }

  s.splitCount++;
  const children = splitBBox4(bounds);

  // Fetch all four children concurrently. Any child that still fails will split
  // itself again, up to depth=2 (maximum 16 final sub-bboxes).
  const childResults = await mapLimit(
    children,
    ROAD_GRAPH_OVERPASS_SPLIT_CONCURRENCY,
    async child => fetchOsmBBoxAdaptive(child, depth + 1, s)
  );

  const pieces = [];
  for (const r of childResults) {
    if (r?.__error) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 502,
        error: "adaptive_split_worker_error",
        message: String(r.__error?.message || r.__error),
        stats: s,
      };
    }
    if (!r?.ok) return r;
    pieces.push(...(r.pieces || []));
  }

  return { ok: true, pieces, stats: s };
}


function splitBBoxGrid(bounds, n) {
  const out = [];
  const latStep = (Number(bounds.north) - Number(bounds.south)) / n;
  const lonStep = (Number(bounds.east) - Number(bounds.west)) / n;

  for (let y = 0; y < n; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < n; x++) {
      out.push({
        south: Number(bounds.south) + y * latStep,
        west: Number(bounds.west) + x * lonStep,
        north: y === n - 1 ? Number(bounds.north) : Number(bounds.south) + (y + 1) * latStep,
        east: x === n - 1 ? Number(bounds.east) : Number(bounds.west) + (x + 1) * lonStep,
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

async function fetchOsmMainApiBBox(bounds) {
  const bbox = [
    Number(bounds.west),
    Number(bounds.south),
    Number(bounds.east),
    Number(bounds.north),
  ].join(",");

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_TIMEOUT_MS);
  const t0 = Date.now();

  try {
    const url = `${ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API}.json?bbox=${encodeURIComponent(bbox)}`;
    const r = await fetch(url, {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "User-Agent": "SelfMadeNavigation-GraphBuilder/3.7.1",
      },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    const responseText = await r.text();
    let raw = null;
    try { raw = responseText ? JSON.parse(responseText) : null; } catch (_) {}

    if (!r.ok || !raw || !Array.isArray(raw.elements)) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: r.status,
        error: "osm_main_api_http_or_json_error",
        message: String(
          raw?.message ||
          raw?.error ||
          responseText.slice(0, 800) ||
          `HTTP ${r.status}`
        ),
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
        responseBytes: responseText.length,
        bounds,
      };
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      status: r.status,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
      responseBytes: responseText.length,
      raw,
      bounds,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      status: 0,
      error: controller.signal.aborted ? "osm_main_api_timeout" : "osm_main_api_fetch_error",
      message: String(err?.message || err),
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
      responseBytes: 0,
      bounds,
    };
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

async function fetchOsmMainApiBBoxAdaptive(bounds, depth = 0, stats = null) {
  const s = stats || {
    requestCount: 0,
    splitCount: 0,
    maxDepthUsed: 0,
    failures: [],
    successfulPieces: 0,
  };

  s.requestCount++;
  s.maxDepthUsed = Math.max(s.maxDepthUsed, depth);

  const r = await fetchOsmMainApiBBox(bounds);
  if (r?.ok) {
    s.successfulPieces++;
    return { ok: true, pieces: [r], stats: s };
  }

  s.failures.push({
    depth,
    status: r?.status || 0,
    error: r?.error || "osm_main_api_failed",
    message: r?.message || null,
    elapsedMs: r?.elapsedMs || null,
    bounds,
  });

  if (depth >= ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_MAX_SPLIT_DEPTH) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      status: r?.status || 502,
      error: r?.error || "osm_main_api_failed_after_split",
      message: r?.message || "Official OSM API map request failed.",
      stats: s,
    };
  }

  s.splitCount++;
  const children = splitBBox4(bounds);
  const childResults = await mapLimit(
    children,
    ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_CONCURRENCY,
    child => fetchOsmMainApiBBoxAdaptive(child, depth + 1, s)
  );

  const pieces = [];
  for (const child of childResults) {
    if (child?.__error) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 502,
        error: "osm_main_api_split_worker_error",
        message: String(child.__error?.message || child.__error),
        stats: s,
      };
    }
    if (!child?.ok) return child;
    pieces.push(...(child.pieces || []));
  }

  return { ok: true, pieces, stats: s };
}

function mergeOsmMainApiResults(pieces) {
  const nodes = new Map();
  const ways = new Map();
  let responseBytes = 0;

  for (const piece of pieces || []) {
    responseBytes += Number(piece?.responseBytes) || 0;
    const elements = Array.isArray(piece?.raw?.elements) ? piece.raw.elements : [];

    for (const el of elements) {
      if (el?.type === "node" && el?.id != null) {
        const id = String(el.id);
        if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, el);
      } else if (el?.type === "way" && el?.id != null) {
        const id = String(el.id);
        const prev = ways.get(id);
        if (!prev || (Array.isArray(el.nodes) ? el.nodes.length : 0) > (Array.isArray(prev.nodes) ? prev.nodes.length : 0)) {
          ways.set(id, el);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Convert normal OSM API ways to the same shape the existing graph builder
  // expects from Overpass `out ... geom`: same node IDs + parallel geometry.
  const nodeCoord = new Map();
  for (const [id, n] of nodes) {
    const lat = Number(n?.lat), lon = Number(n?.lon);
    if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon)) {
      nodeCoord.set(id, { lat, lon });
    }
  }

  const normalizedWays = [];
  for (const way of ways.values()) {
    const ids = Array.isArray(way.nodes) ? way.nodes.map(String) : [];
    const geometry = ids.map(id => {
      const p = nodeCoord.get(id);
      return p ? { lat: p.lat, lon: p.lon } : null;
    });

    normalizedWays.push({
      ...way,
      nodes: ids,
      geometry,
    });
  }

  return {
    raw: { elements: normalizedWays },
    responseBytes,
    uniqueNodes: nodes.size,
    uniqueWays: ways.size,
  };
}

async function fetchOsmForGraphTile(paddedBounds) {
  const t0 = Date.now();

  // Public Overpass mirrors were repeatedly timing out from the Worker.
  // For the prototype graph-provisioning endpoint, use the official OSM map
  // API in small cached cells. This is intentionally NOT used by route requests.
  const cells = splitBBoxGrid(paddedBounds, ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_GRID);
  const sharedStats = {
    requestCount: 0,
    splitCount: 0,
    maxDepthUsed: 0,
    failures: [],
    successfulPieces: 0,
    startMode: `${ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_GRID}x${ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_GRID}-grid`,
  };

  const results = await mapLimit(
    cells,
    ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API_CONCURRENCY,
    cell => fetchOsmMainApiBBoxAdaptive(cell, 0, sharedStats)
  );

  const pieces = [];
  for (const r of results) {
    if (r?.__error) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: 502,
        error: "osm_main_api_grid_worker_error",
        message: String(r.__error?.message || r.__error),
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
        adaptiveStats: sharedStats,
      };
    }
    if (!r?.ok) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        status: r?.status || 502,
        error: r?.error || "osm_main_api_grid_failed",
        message: r?.message || "Official OSM API grid fetch failed.",
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
        adaptiveStats: r?.stats || sharedStats,
      };
    }
    pieces.push(...(r.pieces || []));
  }

  const merged = mergeOsmMainApiResults(pieces);

  return {
    ok: true,
    endpoint: ROAD_GRAPH_OSM_API,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    responseBytes: merged.responseBytes,
    raw: merged.raw,
    adaptiveStats: {
      ...sharedStats,
      pieceCount: pieces.length,
      uniqueNodes: merged.uniqueNodes,
      uniqueWays: merged.uniqueWays,
    },
  };
}

function buildCarGraphTileFromOsm(raw, tileInfo, ownershipBounds = null) {
  const ownedBounds = ownershipBounds || tileInfo.bounds;
  const elements = Array.isArray(raw?.elements) ? raw.elements : [];
  const nodes = new Map();
  const ways = [];

  for (const el of elements) {
    if (el?.type !== "way" || !Array.isArray(el.nodes)) continue;

    ways.push(el);

    // Overpass `out body geom` keeps OSM node IDs in `nodes` and returns a
    // geometry array in the same order. This avoids a huge separate node list.
    const ids = el.nodes;
    const geom = Array.isArray(el.geometry) ? el.geometry : [];

    const n = Math.min(ids.length, geom.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const id = String(ids[i]);
      const lat = Number(geom[i]?.lat);
      const lon = Number(geom[i]?.lon);
      if (!id || !Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
      if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, { id, lat, lon });
    }
  }

  const edgeRows = [];
  const usedNodeIds = new Set();
  let acceptedWays = 0;
  let skippedWays = 0;
  let missingNodePairs = 0;

  function emitEdge(fromId, toId, way, tags, highway, speedKph) {
    const a = nodes.get(String(fromId));
    const b = nodes.get(String(toId));
    if (!a || !b) {
      missingNodePairs++;
      return;
    }

    // Ownership rule: outgoing edge is stored in the tile containing its FROM node.
    if (!pointInsideGraphTile(a.lat, a.lon, ownedBounds)) return;

    const lengthM = graphEdgeLengthM(a, b);
    if (!(lengthM > 0.2 && lengthM < 10000)) return;

    const wayId = String(way.id);
    const name = compactOsmRoadName(tags);

    edgeRows.push([
      String(fromId),
      String(toId),
      Math.round(lengthM * 10) / 10,
      speedKph,
      highway,
      name,
      wayId,
    ]);

    usedNodeIds.add(String(fromId));
    usedNodeIds.add(String(toId));
  }

  for (const way of ways) {
    const tags = way.tags || {};
    if (!isOsmWayDrivableCar(tags)) {
      skippedWays++;
      continue;
    }

    const highway = String(tags.highway || "").toLowerCase();
    const speedKph = graphDefaultSpeedKph(tags);
    const direction = osmWayDirection(tags);
    const ids = way.nodes.map(x => String(x));

    let emittedForWay = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = ids[i];
      const b = ids[i + 1];

      const before = edgeRows.length;

      if (direction >= 0) emitEdge(a, b, way, tags, highway, speedKph);
      if (direction <= 0) emitEdge(b, a, way, tags, highway, speedKph);

      if (edgeRows.length > before) emittedForWay = true;
    }

    if (emittedForWay) acceptedWays++;
  }

  const nodeRows = [];
  for (const id of usedNodeIds) {
    const n = nodes.get(id);
    if (!n) continue;
    nodeRows.push([id, n.lat, n.lon]);
  }

  nodeRows.sort((a, b) => Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]));
  edgeRows.sort((a, b) => {
    const da = Number(a[0]), db = Number(b[0]);
    if (da !== db) return da - db;
    return Number(a[1]) - Number(b[1]);
  });

  return {
    schemaVersion: ROAD_GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    engine: "osm-car-directed-graph",
    country: "TW",
    tileId: tileInfo.tileId,
    bounds: tileInfo.bounds,
    paddedBounds: tileInfo.paddedBounds,
    builtAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    source: "OpenStreetMap main API /map JSON",
    ownership: "edge stored in tile containing FROM node",
    nodeColumns: ["id", "lat", "lon"],
    edgeColumns: ["from", "to", "lengthM", "defaultSpeedKph", "highway", "name", "osmWayId"],
    nodes: nodeRows,
    edges: edgeRows,
    stats: {
      osmElements: elements.length,
      osmNodes: nodes.size,
      osmWays: ways.length,
      acceptedWays,
      skippedWays,
      nodeCount: nodeRows.length,
      edgeCount: edgeRows.length,
      missingNodePairs,
    },
  };
}


function graphShardCoreBounds(tileInfo, partIndex) {
  const grid = ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID;
  const i = Math.max(0, Math.min(grid * grid - 1, Number(partIndex) || 0));
  const row = Math.floor(i / grid);
  const col = i % grid;

  const latStep = (tileInfo.bounds.north - tileInfo.bounds.south) / grid;
  const lonStep = (tileInfo.bounds.east - tileInfo.bounds.west) / grid;

  return {
    south: tileInfo.bounds.south + row * latStep,
    west: tileInfo.bounds.west + col * lonStep,
    north: row === grid - 1
      ? tileInfo.bounds.north
      : tileInfo.bounds.south + (row + 1) * latStep,
    east: col === grid - 1
      ? tileInfo.bounds.east
      : tileInfo.bounds.west + (col + 1) * lonStep,
  };
}

function padGraphBounds(bounds, padM) {
  const midLat = (bounds.south + bounds.north) / 2;
  const latPad = padM / 111320;
  const lonPad = padM / metersPerDegLon(midLat);
  return {
    south: bounds.south - latPad,
    west: bounds.west - lonPad,
    north: bounds.north + latPad,
    east: bounds.east + lonPad,
  };
}

function graphShardKey(tileInfo, partIndex) {
  return `${ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_PREFIX}/${tileInfo.tileId}/part-${String(partIndex).padStart(2, "0")}.json`;
}

function graphManifestParts(tileInfo) {
  const total = ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID * ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID;
  return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => graphShardKey(tileInfo, i));
}

async function roadGraphObjectExists(bucket, key) {
  if (bucket && typeof bucket.head === "function") {
    try {
      return !!(await bucket.head(key));
    } catch (_) {}
  }
  try {
    return !!(await bucket.get(key));
  } catch (_) {
    return false;
  }
}

async function graphShardProgress(env, tileInfo) {
  const parts = graphManifestParts(tileInfo);
  const checks = await mapLimit(parts, 4, async (key, index) => ({
    key,
    index,
    exists: await roadGraphObjectExists(env.ROAD_GRAPH, key),
  }));

  const done = checks.filter(x => !x?.__error && x.exists).map(x => x.index);
  const missing = checks.filter(x => x?.__error || !x.exists).map(x => x.index);

  return {
    parts,
    done,
    missing,
    complete: missing.length === 0,
    completedCount: done.length,
    totalCount: parts.length,
  };
}

async function buildOneOsmGraphShard(env, tileInfo, partIndex, force = false) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const coreBounds = graphShardCoreBounds(tileInfo, partIndex);
  const queryBounds = padGraphBounds(coreBounds, ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_QUERY_PAD_M);
  const key = graphShardKey(tileInfo, partIndex);

  if (!force && await roadGraphObjectExists(env.ROAD_GRAPH, key)) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      cacheHit: true,
      partIndex,
      key,
      coreBounds,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    };
  }

  const stats = {
    requestCount: 0,
    splitCount: 0,
    maxDepthUsed: 0,
    failures: [],
    successfulPieces: 0,
    startMode: "single-shard",
  };

  const fetched = await fetchOsmMainApiBBoxAdaptive(queryBounds, 0, stats);
  if (!fetched?.ok) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      status: fetched?.status || 502,
      error: fetched?.error || "osm_main_api_shard_failed",
      message: fetched?.message || "Unable to fetch OSM data for graph shard.",
      partIndex,
      key,
      coreBounds,
      queryBounds,
      fetchStats: fetched?.stats || stats,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    };
  }

  const merged = mergeOsmMainApiResults(fetched.pieces || []);
  const graph = buildCarGraphTileFromOsm(merged.raw, tileInfo, coreBounds);

  // Keep shard metadata explicit; the logical tile manifest is written only
  // after all 16 shards exist.
  graph.sharded = true;
  graph.partIndex = partIndex;
  graph.partCount = ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID * ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID;
  graph.coreBounds = coreBounds;
  graph.queryBounds = queryBounds;
  graph.source = "OpenStreetMap main API /map JSON — sharded build";
  graph.builtAt = new Date().toISOString();

  const body = JSON.stringify(graph);
  await env.ROAD_GRAPH.put(key, body, {
    httpMetadata: { contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8" },
    customMetadata: {
      schemaVersion: String(ROAD_GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION),
      engine: "osm-car-directed-graph-shard",
      country: "TW",
      tileId: tileInfo.tileId,
      partIndex: String(partIndex),
    },
  });

  return {
    ok: true,
    cacheHit: false,
    partIndex,
    key,
    coreBounds,
    queryBounds,
    bytesStored: body.length,
    stats: graph.stats,
    fetchStats: {
      ...(fetched.stats || stats),
      pieceCount: (fetched.pieces || []).length,
      uniqueNodes: merged.uniqueNodes,
      uniqueWays: merged.uniqueWays,
      responseBytes: merged.responseBytes,
    },
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
  };
}

async function writeGraphShardManifest(env, tileInfo, progress) {
  const manifest = {
    schemaVersion: ROAD_GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    engine: "osm-car-directed-graph-sharded",
    sharded: true,
    complete: true,
    country: "TW",
    tileId: tileInfo.tileId,
    bounds: tileInfo.bounds,
    builtAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    source: "OpenStreetMap main API /map JSON",
    partCount: progress.totalCount,
    parts: progress.parts,
  };

  await env.ROAD_GRAPH.put(
    tileInfo.key,
    JSON.stringify(manifest),
    {
      httpMetadata: { contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8" },
      customMetadata: {
        schemaVersion: String(ROAD_GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION),
        engine: "osm-car-directed-graph-sharded",
        country: "TW",
        tileId: tileInfo.tileId,
      },
    }
  );

  return manifest;
}

async function buildAndStoreOsmCarGraphTile(env, { lat, lon, force = false, part = null }) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const tile = graphTileInfo(lat, lon);

  // Existing monolithic tiles from v2.5.x/v2.6.x remain valid.
  if (!force && part == null) {
    const existing = await env.ROAD_GRAPH.get(tile.key);
    if (existing) {
      let existingData = null;
      try { existingData = await existing.json(); } catch (_) {}

      if (existingData && Array.isArray(existingData.nodes) && Array.isArray(existingData.edges)) {
        return {
          ok: true,
          version: "3.7.1",
          source: "R2",
          cacheHit: true,
          complete: true,
          storageMode: "monolithic",
          tileId: tile.tileId,
          key: tile.key,
          bounds: tile.bounds,
          stats: existingData.stats || null,
          builtAt: existingData.builtAt || null,
          elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
          ts: Date.now(),
        };
      }

      if (existingData?.sharded && existingData?.complete) {
        return {
          ok: true,
          version: "3.7.1",
          source: "R2",
          cacheHit: true,
          complete: true,
          storageMode: "sharded",
          tileId: tile.tileId,
          key: tile.key,
          bounds: tile.bounds,
          partCount: Number(existingData.partCount) || (existingData.parts || []).length,
          builtAt: existingData.builtAt || null,
          elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
          ts: Date.now(),
        };
      }
    }
  }

  let progress = await graphShardProgress(env, tile);

  if (progress.complete) {
    const manifest = await writeGraphShardManifest(env, tile, progress);
    return {
      ok: true,
      version: "3.7.1",
      source: "R2_SHARDS",
      cacheHit: true,
      complete: true,
      storageMode: "sharded",
      tileId: tile.tileId,
      key: tile.key,
      bounds: tile.bounds,
      completedParts: progress.completedCount,
      totalParts: progress.totalCount,
      manifest,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
      ts: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  let requestedPart = part == null ? null : Number(part);
  if (requestedPart != null && (!Number.isInteger(requestedPart) || requestedPart < 0 || requestedPart >= progress.totalCount)) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      version: "3.7.1",
      status: 400,
      error: "invalid_graph_part",
      allowed: `0-${progress.totalCount - 1}`,
      ts: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  // Default: build exactly ONE missing shard per HTTP request.
  // Prefer the shard containing the requested lat/lon on the first call.
  let buildPart = requestedPart;
  if (buildPart == null) {
    const grid = ROAD_GRAPH_SHARD_GRID;
    const latPos = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.999999,
      (lat - tile.bounds.south) / (tile.bounds.north - tile.bounds.south)
    ));
    const lonPos = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.999999,
      (lon - tile.bounds.west) / (tile.bounds.east - tile.bounds.west)
    ));
    const row = Math.floor(latPos * grid);
    const col = Math.floor(lonPos * grid);
    const preferred = row * grid + col;
    buildPart = progress.missing.includes(preferred) ? preferred : progress.missing[0];
  }

  const built = await buildOneOsmGraphShard(env, tile, buildPart, force);
  if (!built?.ok) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      version: "3.7.1",
      status: built?.status || 502,
      error: built?.error || "graph_shard_build_failed",
      message: built?.message || null,
      tileId: tile.tileId,
      key: tile.key,
      storageMode: "sharded",
      builtPart: buildPart,
      shard: built,
      completedParts: progress.completedCount,
      totalParts: progress.totalCount,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
      ts: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  progress = await graphShardProgress(env, tile);

  let manifest = null;
  if (progress.complete) {
    manifest = await writeGraphShardManifest(env, tile, progress);
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    version: "3.7.1",
    source: built.cacheHit ? "R2_SHARD" : "OSM_MAIN_API",
    cacheHit: !!built.cacheHit,
    complete: progress.complete,
    storageMode: "sharded",
    tileId: tile.tileId,
    key: tile.key,
    bounds: tile.bounds,
    builtPart: buildPart,
    shardKey: built.key,
    shardBytesStored: built.bytesStored || null,
    shardStats: built.stats || null,
    shardFetchStats: built.fetchStats || null,
    completedParts: progress.completedCount,
    totalParts: progress.totalCount,
    remainingParts: progress.missing,
    nextPart: progress.missing.length ? progress.missing[0] : null,
    manifestWritten: !!manifest,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    ts: Date.now(),
  };
}

/* =========================
 * Custom car A* router — v2.7.0
 * Baseline/default edge speeds only in this first routing milestone.
 * Traffic matching will be layered on after graph routing is proven correct.
 * ========================= */

const CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_TILE_LOADS = 140;
const CUSTOM_CAR_AUTO_BUILD_MISSING = false;
const CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_AUTO_BUILDS = 0;
const CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 140000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_HEURISTIC_MAX_KPH = 160;
const CUSTOM_CAR_SNAP_NEIGHBOR_TILES = 1; // 3x3 around start/end
const CUSTOM_CAR_SNAP_MAX_M = 2500; // legacy node fallback guard
const CUSTOM_CAR_EDGE_SNAP_MAX_M = 300;

function parseRoutePointInput(v) {
  if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length >= 2) {
    const lat = Number(v[0]), lon = Number(v[1]);
    return Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) ? [lat, lon] : null;
  }
  if (v && typeof v === "object") {
    const lat = Number(v.lat ?? v.latitude);
    const lon = Number(v.lon ?? v.lng ?? v.longitude);
    return Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) ? [lat, lon] : null;
  }
  if (typeof v === "string") {
    const m = v.trim().split(",").map(Number);
    if (m.length >= 2 && Number.isFinite(m[0]) && Number.isFinite(m[1])) {
      return [m[0], m[1]];
    }
  }
  return null;
}

class CustomCarMinHeap {
  constructor() { this.a = []; }
  get size() { return this.a.length; }
  push(x) {
    const a = this.a;
    a.push(x);
    let i = a.length - 1;
    while (i > 0) {
      const p = (i - 1) >> 1;
      if (a[p].f <= x.f) break;
      a[i] = a[p];
      i = p;
    }
    a[i] = x;
  }
  pop() {
    const a = this.a;
    if (!a.length) return null;
    const root = a[0];
    const last = a.pop();
    if (!a.length) return root;
    let i = 0;
    while (true) {
      let l = i * 2 + 1, r = l + 1;
      if (l >= a.length) break;
      let c = (r < a.length && a[r].f < a[l].f) ? r : l;
      if (a[c].f >= last.f) break;
      a[i] = a[c];
      i = c;
    }
    a[i] = last;
    return root;
  }
}

class CustomCarGraphStore {
  constructor(env) {
    this.env = env;
    this.tileCache = new Map();     // key -> tile|null
    this.tileInflight = new Map();  // key -> Promise, prevents duplicate concurrent R2 reads
    this.nodeMap = new Map();       // id -> {id,lat,lon}
    this.adj = new Map();           // from id -> array edge objects
    this.inDegree = new Map();      // node id -> incoming edge count
    this.wayEdges = new Map();      // OSM way id -> loaded directed edges, for HERE chain evidence
