    this.loadedKeys = [];
    this.loadedKeySet = new Set();
    this.missingKeys = new Set();
    this.autoBuildCount = 0;
    this.autoBuiltKeys = [];
    this.autoBuildFailures = [];
    this.failedBuildKeys = new Set();
  }

  ingestTile(key, tile) {
    if (!tile || !Array.isArray(tile.nodes) || !Array.isArray(tile.edges)) return null;

    this.tileCache.set(key, tile);
    this.missingKeys.delete(key);

    if (!this.loadedKeySet.has(key)) {
      this.loadedKeySet.add(key);
      this.loadedKeys.push(key);
    }

    for (const row of tile.nodes) {
      if (!Array.isArray(row) || row.length < 3) continue;
      const id = String(row[0]);
      const lat = Number(row[1]), lon = Number(row[2]);
      if (!id || !Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
      if (!this.nodeMap.has(id)) this.nodeMap.set(id, { id, lat, lon });
    }

    for (const row of tile.edges) {
      if (!Array.isArray(row) || row.length < 5) continue;
      const from = String(row[0]);
      const to = String(row[1]);
      const lengthM = Number(row[2]);
      const speedKph = Number(row[3]);
      if (!from || !to || !(lengthM > 0) || !(speedKph > 0)) continue;

      const edge = {
        from,
        to,
        lengthM,
        speedKph,
        highway: String(row[4] || ""),
        name: String(row[5] || ""),
        osmWayId: row[6] != null ? String(row[6]) : null,
      };

      if (!this.adj.has(from)) this.adj.set(from, []);
      const list = this.adj.get(from);

      // Adjacent/padded graph tiles can reference the same OSM edge. Keep one copy.
      const duplicate = list.some(e =>
        e.to === edge.to &&
        e.osmWayId === edge.osmWayId &&
        Math.abs(e.lengthM - edge.lengthM) < 0.2
      );
      if (!duplicate) {
        list.push(edge);
        this.inDegree.set(to, (this.inDegree.get(to) || 0) + 1);

        if (edge.osmWayId) {
          if (!this.wayEdges.has(edge.osmWayId)) this.wayEdges.set(edge.osmWayId, []);
          this.wayEdges.get(edge.osmWayId).push(edge);
        }
      }
    }

    return tile;
  }

  async loadTileInfo(info, { buildIfMissing = false } = {}) {
    const key = info.key;

    if (this.tileCache.has(key)) {
      const cached = this.tileCache.get(key);
      if (cached) return cached;
      if (!buildIfMissing) return null;
      // cached null may later be promoted by lazy auto-build
    }

    if (this.tileInflight.has(key)) {
      return await this.tileInflight.get(key);
    }

    const p = (async () => {
      if (this.loadedKeySet.size >= CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_TILE_LOADS) {
        throw new Error("custom_car_tile_load_limit");
      }

      let obj = await this.env.ROAD_GRAPH.get(key);

      if (!obj && buildIfMissing && CUSTOM_CAR_AUTO_BUILD_MISSING) {
        if (this.failedBuildKeys.has(key)) {
          this.missingKeys.add(key);
          return null;
        }
        if (this.autoBuildCount >= CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_AUTO_BUILDS) {
          this.missingKeys.add(key);
          return null;
        }

        this.autoBuildCount++;
        const centerLat = (info.bounds.south + info.bounds.north) / 2;
        const centerLon = (info.bounds.west + info.bounds.east) / 2;

        const built = await buildAndStoreOsmCarGraphTile(this.env, {
          lat: centerLat,
          lon: centerLon,
          force: false,
        });

        if (!built?.ok) {
          this.autoBuildFailures.push({
            key,
            error: built?.error || "graph_auto_build_failed",
            message: built?.message || null,
            elapsedMs: built?.elapsedMs || null,
          });
          this.failedBuildKeys.add(key);
          this.tileCache.set(key, null);
          this.missingKeys.add(key);
          return null;
        }

        this.autoBuiltKeys.push(key);
        obj = await this.env.ROAD_GRAPH.get(key);
      }

      if (!obj) {
        this.tileCache.set(key, null);
        this.missingKeys.add(key);
        return null;
      }

      let tile = null;
      try { tile = await obj.json(); } catch (_) {}

      if (tile?.sharded && tile?.complete && Array.isArray(tile.parts)) {
        const partResults = await mapLimit(tile.parts, 4, async partKey => {
          const partObj = await this.env.ROAD_GRAPH.get(partKey);
          if (!partObj) return null;
          try { return await partObj.json(); } catch (_) { return null; }
        });

        const merged = {
          nodes: [],
          edges: [],
          stats: { nodeCount: 0, edgeCount: 0 },
        };

        for (const part of partResults) {
          if (!part || part.__error || !Array.isArray(part.nodes) || !Array.isArray(part.edges)) {
            this.tileCache.set(key, null);
            this.missingKeys.add(key);
            return null;
          }
          for (const row of part.nodes) merged.nodes.push(row);
          for (const row of part.edges) merged.edges.push(row);
          merged.stats.nodeCount += part.nodes.length;
          merged.stats.edgeCount += part.edges.length;
        }

        tile = merged;
      }

      if (!tile || !Array.isArray(tile.nodes) || !Array.isArray(tile.edges)) {
        this.tileCache.set(key, null);
        this.missingKeys.add(key);
        return null;
      }

      return this.ingestTile(key, tile);
    })();

    this.tileInflight.set(key, p);
    try {
      return await p;
    } finally {
      if (this.tileInflight.get(key) === p) this.tileInflight.delete(key);
    }
  }

  async loadAroundPoint(point, neighborTiles = CUSTOM_CAR_SNAP_NEIGHBOR_TILES) {
    const [lat, lon] = point;
    const base = graphTileInfo(lat, lon);
    const centerSouth = base.bounds.south;
    const centerWest = base.bounds.west;
    const jobs = [];

    for (let dy = -neighborTiles; dy <= neighborTiles; dy++) {
      for (let dx = -neighborTiles; dx <= neighborTiles; dx++) {
        const qLat = centerSouth + dy * ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG + ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG * 0.5;
        const qLon = centerWest + dx * ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG + ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG * 0.5;
        // Initial snap neighborhood reads R2 only; it does NOT build all 8 neighbors.
        jobs.push(this.loadTileInfo(graphTileInfo(qLat, qLon), { buildIfMissing: false }));
      }
    }

    await Promise.all(jobs);
  }

  async ensureCenterTile(point, buildIfMissing = false) {
    return await this.loadTileInfo(
      graphTileInfo(point[0], point[1]),
      { buildIfMissing }
    );
  }

  async ensureOutgoingForNode(node) {
    if (!node) return [];
    const info = graphTileInfo(node.lat, node.lon);

    // Route requests are intentionally R2-only. Public Overpass is a graph
    // provisioning dependency, not a latency-critical routing dependency.
    await this.loadTileInfo(info, { buildIfMissing: false });
    return this.adj.get(node.id) || [];
  }

  nearestLoadedNode(point, role = "any") {
    let best = null;
    for (const node of this.nodeMap.values()) {
      if (role === "start" && !(this.adj.get(node.id)?.length > 0)) continue;
      if (role === "end" && !((this.inDegree.get(node.id) || 0) > 0)) continue;

      const d = haversineM(point, [node.lat, node.lon]);
      if (!best || d < best.distanceM) best = { node, distanceM: d };
    }
    return best;
  }
}



function customCarProjectPointToSegment(point, a, b) {
  const lat0 = ((Number(point[0]) + Number(a.lat) + Number(b.lat)) / 3) * Math.PI / 180;
  const mx = 111320 * Math.cos(lat0);
  const my = 111320;

  const px = Number(point[1]) * mx;
  const py = Number(point[0]) * my;
  const ax = Number(a.lon) * mx;
  const ay = Number(a.lat) * my;
  const bx = Number(b.lon) * mx;
  const by = Number(b.lat) * my;

  const vx = bx - ax;
  const vy = by - ay;
  const vv = vx * vx + vy * vy;
  let t = vv > 0 ? ((px - ax) * vx + (py - ay) * vy) / vv : 0;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));

  const qx = ax + t * vx;
  const qy = ay + t * vy;
  const dx = px - qx;
  const dy = py - qy;

  return {
    t,
    lat: qy / my,
    lon: qx / mx,
    distanceM: Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy),
  };
}

function customCarFindReverseEdge(graph, edge) {
  const list = graph.adj.get(String(edge.to)) || [];
  return list.find(e =>
    String(e.to) === String(edge.from) &&
    (
      (edge.osmWayId && e.osmWayId === edge.osmWayId) ||
      (!edge.osmWayId && !e.osmWayId)
    )
  ) || null;
}

function customCarNearestLoadedEdge(graph, point) {
  let best = null;

  for (const [fromId, list] of graph.adj.entries()) {
    const a = graph.nodeMap.get(String(fromId));
    if (!a) continue;

    for (const edge of list || []) {
      const b = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edge.to));
      if (!b) continue;

      const p = customCarProjectPointToSegment(point, a, b);
      if (!best || p.distanceM < best.distanceM) {
        best = {
          edge,
          fromNode: a,
          toNode: b,
          t: p.t,
          lat: p.lat,
          lon: p.lon,
          distanceM: p.distanceM,
        };
      }
    }
  }

  return best;
}

function customCarAddAdjEdge(graph, edge) {
  if (!graph.adj.has(edge.from)) graph.adj.set(edge.from, []);
  graph.adj.get(edge.from).push(edge);
  graph.inDegree.set(edge.to, (graph.inDegree.get(edge.to) || 0) + 1);
}

function customCarInstallVirtualSnap(graph, snap, role, virtualId) {
  const edge = snap.edge;
  const reverse = customCarFindReverseEdge(graph, edge);

  const node = {
    id: virtualId,
    lat: snap.lat,
    lon: snap.lon,
    virtual: true,
  };
  graph.nodeMap.set(virtualId, node);

  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(snap.t) || 0));
  const fwdSpeed = Math.max(5, Number(edge.speedKph) || 30);

  if (role === "start") {
    // Forward legal direction: projected point -> original edge TO node.
    const remain = Math.max(0.01, Number(edge.lengthM) * (1 - t));
    customCarAddAdjEdge(graph, {
      from: virtualId,
      to: String(edge.to),
      lengthM: remain,
      speedKph: fwdSpeed,
      highway: edge.highway,
      name: edge.name,
      osmWayId: edge.osmWayId,
      virtualSnapEdge: true,
    });

    // If the same OSM segment is legally traversable in reverse, allow
    // projected point -> original edge FROM node too.
    if (reverse) {
      const backLen = Math.max(0.01, Number(reverse.lengthM) * t);
      customCarAddAdjEdge(graph, {
        from: virtualId,
        to: String(edge.from),
        lengthM: backLen,
        speedKph: Math.max(5, Number(reverse.speedKph) || fwdSpeed),
        highway: reverse.highway,
        name: reverse.name,
        osmWayId: reverse.osmWayId,
        virtualSnapEdge: true,
      });
    }
  } else if (role === "end") {
    // Forward legal direction can terminate at the projected point.
    const intoEnd = Math.max(0.01, Number(edge.lengthM) * t);
    customCarAddAdjEdge(graph, {
      from: String(edge.from),
      to: virtualId,
      lengthM: intoEnd,
      speedKph: fwdSpeed,
      highway: edge.highway,
      name: edge.name,
      osmWayId: edge.osmWayId,
      virtualSnapEdge: true,
    });

    // Reverse legal direction can also terminate at the projected point.
    if (reverse) {
      const reverseIntoEnd = Math.max(0.01, Number(reverse.lengthM) * (1 - t));
      customCarAddAdjEdge(graph, {
        from: String(edge.to),
        to: virtualId,
        lengthM: reverseIntoEnd,
        speedKph: Math.max(5, Number(reverse.speedKph) || fwdSpeed),
        highway: reverse.highway,
        name: reverse.name,
        osmWayId: reverse.osmWayId,
        virtualSnapEdge: true,
      });
    }
  }

  return node;
}

function graphTileCenterFromKey(key) {
  const m = String(key || "").match(/\/(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)_(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\.json$/);
  if (!m) return null;
  const south = Number(m[1]), west = Number(m[2]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(south) || !Number.isFinite(west)) return null;
  return [
    south + ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG * 0.5,
    west + ROAD_GRAPH_TILE_DEG * 0.5,
  ];
}

function pointToRouteCorridorScoreM(point, start, end) {
  // Local equirectangular projection is sufficient for tile prioritization.
  const lat0 = ((start[0] + end[0] + point[0]) / 3) * Math.PI / 180;
  const mx = 111320 * Math.cos(lat0);
  const my = 111320;

  const ax = start[1] * mx, ay = start[0] * my;
  const bx = end[1] * mx, by = end[0] * my;
  const px = point[1] * mx, py = point[0] * my;

  const vx = bx - ax, vy = by - ay;
  const vv = vx * vx + vy * vy;
  let t = vv > 0 ? ((px - ax) * vx + (py - ay) * vy) / vv : 0;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  const qx = ax + t * vx, qy = ay + t * vy;
  const dx = px - qx, dy = py - qy;
  return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

function prioritizeMissingGraphTiles(keys, start, end) {
  return [...new Set(keys || [])]
    .map(key => {
      const center = graphTileCenterFromKey(key);
      if (!center) return { key, score: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY };
      const corridorM = pointToRouteCorridorScoreM(center, start, end);
      const goalM = haversineM(center, end);
      return { key, score: corridorM * 2 + goalM * 0.15 };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score)
    .map(x => x.key);
}

function customCarHeuristicSec(node, goal) {
  const d = haversineM([node.lat, node.lon], [goal.lat, goal.lon]);
  return d / (CUSTOM_CAR_HEURISTIC_MAX_KPH / 3.6);
}

function customCarBaselineEdgeCostSec(edge) {
  return edge.lengthM / (Math.max(5, Number(edge.speedKph) || 30) / 3.6);
}

function reconstructCustomCarRoute(goalId, came, graph) {
  const ids = [goalId];
  const edges = [];
  let cur = goalId;

  while (came.has(cur)) {
    const step = came.get(cur);
    edges.push(step.edge);
    cur = step.prev;
    ids.push(cur);
  }

  ids.reverse();
  edges.reverse();

  const geometry = [];
  for (const id of ids) {
    const n = graph.nodeMap.get(id);
    if (n) geometry.push([n.lat, n.lon]);
  }

  const distanceM = edges.reduce((s, e) => s + (Number(e.lengthM) || 0), 0);
  return { ids, edges, geometry, distanceM };
}


/* =========================
 * Custom-car live traffic cost — v3.0.0
 * ========================= */

function customCarBool(v, fallback = true) {
  if (v == null || v === "") return fallback;
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v;
  const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (["0","false","off","no"].includes(s)) return false;
  if (["1","true","on","yes"].includes(s)) return true;
  return fallback;
}

function customCarTrafficEdgeView(edge, fromNode, toNode) {
  const a = [fromNode.lat, fromNode.lon];
  const b = [toNode.lat, toNode.lon];
  const highway = String(edge?.highway || "").toLowerCase();
  const navExpressway = ["motorway","motorway_link","trunk","trunk_link"].includes(highway);
  const wayId = edge?.osmWayId ? String(edge.osmWayId) : "";
  const dirKey = `${String(edge?.from || fromNode.id)}>${String(edge?.to || toNode.id)}`;

  return {
    a,
    b,
    midpoint: interpolateLatLon(a, b, 0.5),
    heading: bearingDeg(a, b),
    lengthM: Number(edge?.lengthM) || haversineM(a, b),
    navRoad: String(edge?.name || ""),
    navExpressway,
    navLinkId: wayId ? `OSM:${wayId}:${dirKey}` : `OSMLOCAL:${dirKey}`,
    roadChainId: wayId ? `OSMWAY:${wayId}` : `OSMLOCAL:${dirKey}`,
    navNodeA: String(fromNode.id),
    navNodeB: String(toNode.id),
  };
}

function customCarTrafficAdjustedCandidate(edgeView, seg, scored, {
  allowHereNameMismatch = false,
  selectionReason = null,
  chainAliasEvidence = null,
} = {}) {
  if (!seg || !scored) return null;

  const closed = seg?.closed === true || Number(seg?.trafficLevel) === -1;
  const speed = Number(seg?.speedKph);
  if (!closed && !(Number.isFinite(speed) && speed > 0 && speed <= 160)) return null;

  const roadCompat = roadNameCompatibility(edgeView.navRoad, seg.road);
  const fc = Number(seg.functionalClass);

  // Same hard wall used by the existing matcher to reduce bridge/ground mixups.
  if (
    edgeView.navExpressway &&
    seg.provider === "HERE" &&
    Number.isFinite(fc) &&
    fc >= 4
  ) return null;

  // v3.0.1: HERE geometry alone is NOT enough at intersections / stacked roads.
  // If both sides have a usable road name and they clearly disagree, reject it.
  // Examples this prevents:
  //   雙十路二段 -> 莊敬路
  //   文聖街     -> 文化路二段
  //
  // Do NOT apply this hard gate to TDX because TDX may call the same physical
  // road by a route number such as "台3線" while OSM names it "文化路二段".
  if (
    seg.provider === "HERE" &&
    edgeView.navRoad &&
    seg.road &&
    roadCompat === 0 &&
    !allowHereNameMismatch
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  let adjustedScore = Number(scored.score) || 0;
  if (roadCompat >= 0.70) adjustedScore += 0.070;
  else if (roadCompat > 0) adjustedScore += 0.030 * roadCompat;

  if (edgeView.navExpressway && Number(seg.functionalClass) <= 2) adjustedScore += 0.035;
  if (edgeView.navExpressway && Number(seg.functionalClass) >= 3) adjustedScore -= 0.060;

  adjustedScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, adjustedScore));

  return {
    provider: seg.provider,
    sourceType: seg.sourceType || seg.provider,
    trafficId: seg.id || null,
    sectionId: seg.sectionId || null,
    scope: seg.scope || "",
    trafficRoad: seg.road || "",
    speedKph: closed ? null : speed,
    closed,
    freeFlowKph: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.freeFlowKph)) ? Number(seg.freeFlowKph) : null,
    jamFactor: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.jamFactor)) ? Number(seg.jamFactor) : null,
    confidence: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.confidence)) ? Number(seg.confidence) : null,
    trafficLevel: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.trafficLevel)) ? Number(seg.trafficLevel) : 0,
    functionalClass: seg.functionalClass ?? null,
    dataTime: seg.dataTime || null,
    matchScore: adjustedScore,
    rawMatchScore: scored.score,
    matchDistanceM: scored.distanceM,
    avgDistanceM: scored.avgDistanceM,
    headingDeltaDeg: scored.headingDeltaDeg,
    overlapRatio: scored.overlapRatio,
    roadNameCompatibility: roadCompat,
    selectionReason: selectionReason || (seg.provider === "HERE" ? "HERE_NAME_GUARDED" : "TDX_STANDARD"),
    hereChainAlias: !!chainAliasEvidence,
    chainAliasEvidence: chainAliasEvidence || null,
    _trafficSeg: seg,
  };
}

function customCarBestTrafficMatch(edgeView, segments, index, provider, {
  allowHereNameMismatch = false,
  requiredHereRoadToken = null,
  selectionReason = null,
  chainAliasEvidence = null,
} = {}) {
  if (!Array.isArray(segments) || !segments.length || !index) return null;

  const maxSearchM = provider === "TDX"
    ? Math.max(MATCH_TDX_FREEWAY_MAX_M, MATCH_TDX_HIGHWAY_MAX_M)
    : MATCH_HERE_MAX_M;

  const candidateIdx = candidateIndexesNear(index, edgeView.midpoint, maxSearchM);
  let best = null;
  let second = null;

  for (const si of candidateIdx) {
    const seg = segments[si];
    if (!seg || seg.provider !== provider) continue;

    const th = thresholdsForTrafficSegment(seg);
    const scored = scoreTrafficCandidate(
      edgeView,
      seg,
      th.maxDistM,
      null,
      th.headingRejectDeg
    );
    if (!scored) continue;

    if (
      provider === "HERE" &&
      requiredHereRoadToken &&
      normalizeRoadToken(seg.road) !== requiredHereRoadToken
    ) continue;

    const candidate = customCarTrafficAdjustedCandidate(edgeView, seg, scored, {
      allowHereNameMismatch,
      selectionReason,
      chainAliasEvidence,
    });
    if (!candidate || candidate.matchScore < th.minScore) continue;

    if (!best || candidate.matchScore > best.matchScore) {
      second = best;
      best = candidate;
    } else if (!second || candidate.matchScore > second.matchScore) {
      second = candidate;
    }
  }

  if (!best) return null;

  // If two HERE roads are nearly indistinguishable, baseline is safer than
  // assigning traffic from the wrong stacked/parallel road.
  if (provider === "HERE" && second && second.trafficId !== best.trafficId) {
    const gap = best.matchScore - second.matchScore;
    if (
      gap <= NAVLINK_AMBIGUITY_SCORE_GAP &&
      Number(best.matchDistanceM) <= NAVLINK_AMBIGUITY_MAX_DIST_M &&
      Number(second.matchDistanceM) <= NAVLINK_AMBIGUITY_MAX_DIST_M &&
      Number(best.headingDeltaDeg) <= 28 &&
      Number(second.headingDeltaDeg) <= 28
    ) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  return best;
}


const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_M = 80;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_EDGES = 3;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_RATIO = 0.55;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_AVG_DIST_M = 8;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_AVG_HEADING_DEG = 16;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_DIST_M = 18;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_HEADING_DEG = 24;
const CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_SAMPLES = 14;

function customCarHereFcCompatible(osmHighway, hereFc) {
  const fc = Number(hereFc);
  if (!Number.isFinite(fc)) return true;

  const h = String(osmHighway || "").toLowerCase();
  if (h === "motorway" || h === "motorway_link") return fc <= 2;
  if (h === "trunk" || h === "trunk_link") return fc <= 3;
  if (h === "primary" || h === "primary_link") return fc <= 4;
  if (h === "secondary" || h === "secondary_link") return fc >= 2 && fc <= 4;
  if (h === "tertiary" || h === "tertiary_link") return fc >= 3 && fc <= 5;
  if (h === "unclassified") return fc >= 3;
  return fc >= 4;
}

function customCarHereChainEligibleHighway(highway) {
  return new Set([
    "motorway","motorway_link",
    "trunk","trunk_link",
    "primary","primary_link",
    "secondary","secondary_link",
    "tertiary","tertiary_link",
    "unclassified"
  ]).has(String(highway || "").toLowerCase());
}

function customCarEvenSamples(arr, maxCount) {
  if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length <= maxCount) return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < maxCount; i++) {
    const idx = Math.round(i * (arr.length - 1) / (maxCount - 1));
    out.push(arr[idx]);
  }
  return out;
}

function customCarBuildHereChainAliasHint(graph, edge, trafficSnapshot) {
  if (!graph || !edge?.osmWayId || !trafficSnapshot?.hereIndex) return null;
  if (!customCarHereChainEligibleHighway(edge.highway)) return null;

  const wayId = String(edge.osmWayId);
  if (!trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache) trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache = new Map();
  if (!trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats) {
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats = { evaluated:0, accepted:0, rejected:0 };
  }

  if (trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache.has(wayId)) {
    return trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache.get(wayId);
  }

  trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats.evaluated++;

  const allEdges = graph.wayEdges?.get(wayId) || [];
  const totalLen = allEdges.reduce((s, e) => s + (Number(e?.lengthM) || 0), 0);

  if (allEdges.length < CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_EDGES || totalLen < CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_M) {
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats.rejected++;
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache.set(wayId, null);
    return null;
  }

  const sampleEdges = customCarEvenSamples(allEdges, CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_SAMPLES);
  const groups = new Map();

  for (const sampleEdge of sampleEdges) {
    const fromNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(sampleEdge.from));
    const toNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(sampleEdge.to));
    if (!fromNode || !toNode) continue;

    const view = customCarTrafficEdgeView(sampleEdge, fromNode, toNode);
    const candidateIdx = candidateIndexesNear(
      trafficSnapshot.hereIndex,
      view.midpoint,
      CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_DIST_M
    );

    let bestForEdge = null;

    for (const si of candidateIdx) {
      const seg = trafficSnapshot.hereSegments[si];
      if (!seg || seg.provider !== "HERE" || !seg.road) continue;
      if (!customCarHereFcCompatible(sampleEdge.highway, seg.functionalClass)) continue;

      // Alias mode is only for a genuine road-name mismatch.
      if (roadNameCompatibility(view.navRoad, seg.road) > 0) continue;

      const scored = scoreTrafficCandidate(
        view,
        seg,
        CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_DIST_M,
        null,
        CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_HEADING_DEG
      );
      if (!scored) continue;
      if (scored.distanceM > CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_AVG_DIST_M * 1.5) continue;
      if (scored.avgDistanceM > CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_DIST_M) continue;
      if (scored.headingDeltaDeg > CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_HEADING_DEG) continue;
      if (scored.overlapRatio < 0.66) continue;

      const quality =
        (Number(scored.score) || 0) +
        Math.max(0, 1 - scored.distanceM / CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_STRICT_DIST_M) * 0.08;

      if (!bestForEdge || quality > bestForEdge.quality) {
        bestForEdge = { seg, scored, quality };
      }
    }

    if (!bestForEdge) continue;

    const token = normalizeRoadToken(bestForEdge.seg.road);
    if (!token) continue;

    if (!groups.has(token)) {
      groups.set(token, {
        token,
        road: bestForEdge.seg.road,
        supportM: 0,
        edgeCount: 0,
        weightedDistance: 0,
        weightedHeading: 0,
        trafficIds: new Set(),
      });
    }

    const g = groups.get(token);
    const len = Number(sampleEdge.lengthM) || 0;
    g.supportM += len;
    g.edgeCount++;
    g.weightedDistance += bestForEdge.scored.distanceM * Math.max(1, len);
    g.weightedHeading += bestForEdge.scored.headingDeltaDeg * Math.max(1, len);
    if (bestForEdge.seg.id) g.trafficIds.add(String(bestForEdge.seg.id));
  }

  const ranked = [...groups.values()].sort((a,b) => b.supportM - a.supportM);
  const winner = ranked[0];

  if (!winner) {
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats.rejected++;
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache.set(wayId, null);
    return null;
  }

  const sampledLen = sampleEdges.reduce((s,e) => s + (Number(e?.lengthM) || 0), 0);
  const supportRatio = sampledLen > 0 ? winner.supportM / sampledLen : 0;
  const avgDist = winner.supportM > 0 ? winner.weightedDistance / winner.supportM : Infinity;
  const avgHeading = winner.supportM > 0 ? winner.weightedHeading / winner.supportM : Infinity;
  const secondSupportM = ranked[1]?.supportM || 0;

  const clearlyDominant =
    secondSupportM === 0 ||
    winner.supportM >= secondSupportM * 1.45 ||
    (winner.supportM - secondSupportM) >= 50;

  const accepted = (
    winner.edgeCount >= CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_EDGES &&
    winner.supportM >= CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_M &&
    supportRatio >= CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MIN_SUPPORT_RATIO &&
    avgDist <= CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_AVG_DIST_M &&
    avgHeading <= CUSTOM_HERE_CHAIN_MAX_AVG_HEADING_DEG &&
    clearlyDominant
  );

  if (!accepted) {
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats.rejected++;
    trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache.set(wayId, null);
    return null;
  }

  const hint = {
    osmWayId: wayId,
    osmRoad: String(edge.name || ""),
    hereRoad: winner.road,
    hereRoadToken: winner.token,
    supportM: Math.round(winner.supportM * 10) / 10,
    supportEdges: winner.edgeCount,
    supportRatio: Math.round(supportRatio * 1000) / 1000,
    avgDistanceM: Math.round(avgDist * 10) / 10,
    avgHeadingDeltaDeg: Math.round(avgHeading * 10) / 10,
    distinctHereTrafficIds: winner.trafficIds.size,
  };

  trafficSnapshot.chainAliasStats.accepted++;
  trafficSnapshot.chainAliasCache.set(wayId, hint);
  return hint;
}


function customCarTrafficRadiusForTrip(distanceM) {
  const d = Math.max(0, Number(distanceM) || 0);

  // Old behavior fetched R50 even for a route only a few metres long.
  // Keep enough search margin around the straight-line trip while avoiding
  // tens of thousands of irrelevant HERE segments for local routing.
  //
  // Examples:
  //   0 km  -> 1.5 km radius
  //   2 km  -> 3.0 km radius
  //  10 km  -> 7.0 km radius
  //  30 km  -> 17.0 km radius
  //  80 km  -> 42.0 km radius
  // >=96 km -> capped at 50 km
  const marginM = Math.max(1500, Math.min(5000, d * 0.10));
  const radiusM = (d / 2) + marginM;
  return Math.max(1500, Math.min(TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_RADIUS_M, Math.round(radiusM)));
}

async function buildCustomCarTrafficSnapshot(env, ctx, startPoint, endPoint) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const includeHere = !!ctx?.hereKey;
  const includeTdx = !!(env?.TDX_CLIENT_ID && env?.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET);

  if (!includeHere && !includeTdx) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      degraded: true,
      reason: "no_traffic_credentials",
      tdxSegments: [],
      hereSegments: [],
      tdxIndex: null,
      hereIndex: null,
      matchCache: new Map(),
      chainAliasCache: new Map(),
      chainAliasStats: { evaluated:0, accepted:0, rejected:0 },
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
      providerStats: { tdx:0, here:0, total:0 },
      areaSummary: null,
    };
  }

  const directDistanceM = haversineM(startPoint, endPoint);
  const preloadRadiusM = directDistanceM <= TRAFFIC_AREA_SHORT_TRIP_MAX_M
    ? customCarTrafficRadiusForTrip(directDistanceM)
    : TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_RADIUS_M;

  const built = buildTrafficAreaCenters(
    startPoint,
    endPoint,
    preloadRadiusM,
    TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_SPACING_M
  );

  const cfg = {
    ok: true,
    mode: built.mode,
    directDistanceM: built.directDistanceM,
    radiusM: preloadRadiusM,
    spacingM: TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_SPACING_M,
    centers: built.centers,
    functionalClasses: [1,2,3,4,5],
    includeHere,
    includeTdx,
  };

  const area = await getTrafficArea(env, { hereKey: ctx?.hereKey || null }, cfg);
  const segments = Array.isArray(area?.segments) ? area.segments : [];
  const tdxSegments = segments.filter(x => x?.provider === "TDX");
  const hereSegments = segments.filter(x => x?.provider === "HERE");

  return {
    ok: segments.length > 0,
    degraded: segments.length === 0,
    reason: segments.length ? null : (
      area?.tdxError ||
      (includeHere && Number(area?.hereAreaSucceeded || 0) === 0 ? "HERE_area_failed" : "no_traffic_segments")
    ),
    tdxSegments,
    hereSegments,
    tdxIndex: tdxSegments.length ? buildTrafficGrid(tdxSegments) : null,
    hereIndex: hereSegments.length ? buildTrafficGrid(hereSegments) : null,
    matchCache: new Map(),
    chainAliasCache: new Map(),
    chainAliasStats: { evaluated:0, accepted:0, rejected:0 },
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    providerStats: area?.providerStats || {
      tdx: tdxSegments.length,
      here: hereSegments.length,
      total: segments.length,
    },
    areaSummary: {
      mode: area?.mode || built.mode,
      directDistanceM: Number(area?.directDistanceM ?? built.directDistanceM),
      centerCount: Number(area?.centerCount ?? built.centers.length),
      centers: area?.centers || built.centers,
      radiusM: area?.radiusM || preloadRadiusM,
      hereAreaRequested: area?.hereAreaRequested ?? null,
      hereAreaSucceeded: area?.hereAreaSucceeded ?? null,
      tdxUnavailable: area?.tdxUnavailable === true,
      tdxError: area?.tdxError || null,
      tdxStaleScopes: area?.tdxStaleScopes || [],
      areas: Array.isArray(area?.areas)
        ? area.areas.map(x => ({
            provider: x?.provider,
            ok: x?.ok,
            centerIndex: x?.centerIndex,
            resultCount: x?.resultCount,
            cacheHit: x?.cacheHit,
            elapsedMs: x?.elapsedMs,
            error: x?.error || null,
          }))
        : [],
    },
  };
}

function customCarLiveEdgeCost(
  edge,
  fromNode,
  toNode,
  trafficSnapshot,
  graph = null,
  { allowChainAlias = true } = {}
) {
  const baselineSpeedKph = Math.max(5, Number(edge?.speedKph) || 30);
  const baselineSec = Number(edge?.lengthM) / (baselineSpeedKph / 3.6);

  if (!trafficSnapshot?.ok) {
    return {
      sec: baselineSec,
      baselineSec,
      baselineSpeedKph,
      usedSpeedKph: baselineSpeedKph,
      source: "BASELINE",
      match: null,
      closed: false,
    };
  }

  const cacheKey = `${String(edge.from)}>${String(edge.to)}|${edge.osmWayId || ""}|${allowChainAlias ? "alias" : "strict"}`;
  if (trafficSnapshot.matchCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    return trafficSnapshot.matchCache.get(cacheKey);
  }

  const edgeView = customCarTrafficEdgeView(edge, fromNode, toNode);

  // Car policy: TDX first -> HERE -> graph baseline.
  let match = customCarBestTrafficMatch(
    edgeView,
    trafficSnapshot.tdxSegments,
    trafficSnapshot.tdxIndex,
    "TDX"
  );

  if (!match) {
    match = customCarBestTrafficMatch(
      edgeView,
      trafficSnapshot.hereSegments,
      trafficSnapshot.hereIndex,
      "HERE"
    );
  }

  // v3.0.2: strict name guard remains the default. A different HERE road name
  // is admitted only after the same alias proves stable across this OSM way.
  if (!match && allowChainAlias && graph && edge.osmWayId) {
    const aliasHint = customCarBuildHereChainAliasHint(graph, edge, trafficSnapshot);
    if (aliasHint) {
      match = customCarBestTrafficMatch(
        edgeView,
        trafficSnapshot.hereSegments,
        trafficSnapshot.hereIndex,
        "HERE",
        {
          allowHereNameMismatch: true,
          requiredHereRoadToken: aliasHint.hereRoadToken,
          selectionReason: "HERE_CHAIN_ALIAS",
          chainAliasEvidence: aliasHint,
        }
      );
    }
  }

  let out;
  if (match?.closed) {
    out = {
      sec: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
      baselineSec,
      baselineSpeedKph,
      usedSpeedKph: null,
      source: match.provider,
      match,
      closed: true,
    };
  } else if (match && Number.isFinite(Number(match.speedKph)) && Number(match.speedKph) > 0) {
    const usedSpeedKph = Math.max(2, Math.min(160, Number(match.speedKph)));
    out = {
      sec: Number(edge.lengthM) / (usedSpeedKph / 3.6),
      baselineSec,
      baselineSpeedKph,
      usedSpeedKph,
      source: match.provider,
      match,
      closed: false,
    };
  } else {
    out = {
      sec: baselineSec,
      baselineSec,
      baselineSpeedKph,
      usedSpeedKph: baselineSpeedKph,
      source: "BASELINE",
      match: null,
      closed: false,
    };
  }

  trafficSnapshot.matchCache.set(cacheKey, out);
  return out;
}

function summarizeCustomCarRouteTraffic(routeEdges, graph, trafficSnapshot, debug = false) {
  const stats = {
    edgeCount: routeEdges.length,
    tdxEdges: 0,
    hereEdges: 0,
    hereChainAliasEdges: 0,
    baselineEdges: 0,
    trafficMatchedEdges: 0,
    trafficMatchedDistanceM: 0,
    totalDistanceM: 0,
    chosenRouteBaselineSec: 0,
    chosenRouteTrafficSec: 0,
    fallbackBaselineSec: 0,
    fallbackBaselineDistanceM: 0,
  };
  const edgeDebug = [];

  for (const edge of routeEdges) {
    const fromNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edge.from));
    const toNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edge.to));
    if (!fromNode || !toNode) continue;

    const c = customCarLiveEdgeCost(
      edge,
      fromNode,
      toNode,
      trafficSnapshot,
      graph,
      { allowChainAlias:true }
    );
    const len = Number(edge.lengthM) || 0;

    stats.totalDistanceM += len;
    stats.chosenRouteBaselineSec += Number(c.baselineSec) || 0;
    stats.chosenRouteTrafficSec += Number.isFinite(c.sec) ? c.sec : 0;

    if (c.source === "TDX") stats.tdxEdges++;
    else if (c.source === "HERE") {
      stats.hereEdges++;
      if (c.match?.hereChainAlias) stats.hereChainAliasEdges++;
    }
    else {
      stats.baselineEdges++;
      stats.fallbackBaselineSec += Number(c.sec) || 0;
      stats.fallbackBaselineDistanceM += len;
    }

    if (c.source === "TDX" || c.source === "HERE") {
      stats.trafficMatchedEdges++;
      stats.trafficMatchedDistanceM += len;
    }

    if (debug) {
      edgeDebug.push({
        from: String(edge.from),
        to: String(edge.to),
        road: edge.name || "",
        highway: edge.highway || "",
        osmWayId: edge.osmWayId || null,
        lengthM: Math.round(len * 10) / 10,
        baselineSpeedKph: Math.round(Number(c.baselineSpeedKph) * 10) / 10,
        usedSpeedKph: c.usedSpeedKph == null ? null : Math.round(Number(c.usedSpeedKph) * 10) / 10,
        source: c.source,
        costSec: Number.isFinite(c.sec) ? Math.round(c.sec * 10) / 10 : null,
        trafficRoad: c.match?.trafficRoad || null,
        trafficId: c.match?.trafficId || null,
        scope: c.match?.scope || null,
        matchScore: c.match ? Math.round(Number(c.match.matchScore) * 1000) / 1000 : null,
        matchDistanceM: c.match ? Math.round(Number(c.match.matchDistanceM) * 10) / 10 : null,
        roadNameCompatibility: c.match?.roadNameCompatibility ?? null,
        selectionReason: c.match?.selectionReason || null,
        hereChainAlias: c.match?.hereChainAlias === true,
        chainAliasEvidence: c.match?.chainAliasEvidence || null,
        jamFactor: c.match?.jamFactor ?? null,
        trafficLevel: c.match?.trafficLevel ?? null,
        dataTime: c.match?.dataTime || null,
      });
    }
  }

  stats.trafficCoverageDistanceRatio = stats.totalDistanceM > 0
    ? stats.trafficMatchedDistanceM / stats.totalDistanceM
    : 0;
  stats.trafficDelayOnChosenRouteSec =
    stats.chosenRouteTrafficSec - stats.chosenRouteBaselineSec;

  return { stats, edgeDebug };
}


/* =========================
 * Custom-car signal + turn aware A* — v3.1.0
 * Reuses the same SIGNALS R2 tiles and residual-delay profiles already used
 * by the existing navigation ETA pipeline.
 * ========================= */

function customCarEdgeStateId(edge) {
  if (!edge) return "__START__";
  return [
    String(edge.from ?? ""),
    String(edge.to ?? ""),
    String(edge.osmWayId ?? ""),
    String(edge.highway ?? ""),
    String(edge.name ?? "")
  ].join("|");
}

function customCarSearchStateKey(nodeId, incomingEdge) {
  return `${String(nodeId)}@@${customCarEdgeStateId(incomingEdge)}`;
}

function customCarIsExpresswayEdge(edge) {
  const h = String(edge?.highway || "").toLowerCase();
  return h === "motorway" || h === "motorway_link" ||
         h === "trunk" || h === "trunk_link";
}

function customCarTurnGeometry(incomingEdge, outgoingEdge, currentNode, graph) {
  if (!incomingEdge || !outgoingEdge || !currentNode || !graph) {
    return { isTurn:false, angleDeg:0, incomingBearing:null, outgoingBearing:null };
  }

  const prevNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(incomingEdge.from));
  const nextNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(outgoingEdge.to));
  if (!prevNode || !nextNode) {
    return { isTurn:false, angleDeg:0, incomingBearing:null, outgoingBearing:null };
  }

  const current = [Number(currentNode.lat), Number(currentNode.lon)];
  const prev = [Number(prevNode.lat), Number(prevNode.lon)];
  const next = [Number(nextNode.lat), Number(nextNode.lon)];
  if (![...prev, ...current, ...next].every(Number.isFinite)) {
    return { isTurn:false, angleDeg:0, incomingBearing:null, outgoingBearing:null };
  }

  const incomingBearing = bearingDeg(prev, current);
  const outgoingBearing = bearingDeg(current, next);
  const angleDeg = angleDeltaDeg(incomingBearing, outgoingBearing);

  // Same geometric thresholds used by the old/front-end intersection logic:
  // <=24° straight, >=38° turn. With no maneuver instruction available in
  // graph-A*, the ambiguous 24–38° band stays "straight".
  return {
    isTurn: angleDeg >= 38,
    angleDeg,
    incomingBearing,
    outgoingBearing,
  };
}

class CustomCarSignalResolver {
  constructor(env) {
    this.env = env;
    this.bound = !!(env?.SIGNALS && typeof env.SIGNALS.get === "function");
    this.tilePromises = new Map();
    this.nodeCache = new Map();
    this.stats = {
      bound: this.bound,
      nodeQueries: 0,
      signalTrue: 0,
      signalFalse: 0,
      unknown: 0,
      requestedTiles: 0,
      loadedTiles: 0,
      cacheHitTiles: 0,
      r2HitTiles: 0,
      missingTiles: 0,
      tileErrors: 0,
      prefetchMs: 0,
    };
  }

  _tileKey(x, y, z = SIGNAL_TILE_Z) {
    return `${z}/${x}/${y}`;
  }

  async _loadTile(x, y, z = SIGNAL_TILE_Z) {
    const k = this._tileKey(x, y, z);
    if (this.tilePromises.has(k)) return this.tilePromises.get(k);

    this.stats.requestedTiles++;
    const p = loadSignalTileFromR2(this.env, x, y, z)
      .then(r => {
        if (r?.ok) {
          this.stats.loadedTiles++;
          if (r.cacheHit) this.stats.cacheHitTiles++;
          if (r.r2Hit) this.stats.r2HitTiles++;
        } else if (r?.missing) {
          this.stats.missingTiles++;
        } else {
          this.stats.tileErrors++;
        }
        return r;
      })
      .catch(err => {
        this.stats.tileErrors++;
        return {
          ok:false,
          missing:false,
          nodes:[],
          reason:"tile-read-error",
          error:String(err?.message || err),
          cacheHit:false,
          r2Hit:false,
        };
      });

    this.tilePromises.set(k, p);
    return p;
  }

  async _neighborhood(lat, lon) {
    const t = signalTileXY(lat, lon, SIGNAL_TILE_Z);
    const jobs = [];
    const n = 2 ** SIGNAL_TILE_Z;

    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
        const x = t.x + dx;
        const y = t.y + dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= n || y >= n) continue;
        jobs.push(this._loadTile(x, y, SIGNAL_TILE_Z));
      }
    }
    return Promise.all(jobs);
  }

  async prefetchPoints(points) {
    if (!this.bound) return;
    const t0 = Date.now();
    const jobs = [];
    for (const p of points || []) {
      if (!Array.isArray(p) || p.length < 2) continue;
      const lat = Number(p[0]), lon = Number(p[1]);
      if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
      jobs.push(this._neighborhood(lat, lon));
    }
    await Promise.all(jobs);
    this.stats.prefetchMs += Date.now() - t0;
  }

  async resolveNode(node) {
    const id = String(node?.id ?? `${node?.lat},${node?.lon}`);
    if (this.nodeCache.has(id)) return this.nodeCache.get(id);

    const promise = this._resolveNodeUncached(node);
    this.nodeCache.set(id, promise);
    return promise;
  }

  async _resolveNodeUncached(node) {
    this.stats.nodeQueries++;

    const lat = Number(node?.lat), lon = Number(node?.lon);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon) || !this.bound) {
      this.stats.unknown++;
      return { hasSignal:null, status:this.bound ? "invalid-node" : "unknown-r2-binding-missing", nearestM:null };
    }

    const tiles = await this._neighborhood(lat, lon);
    let anyKnown = false;
    let nearestM = Infinity;
    let nearestId = null;
    const seen = new Set();

    for (const tile of tiles) {
      if (tile?.ok) anyKnown = true;
      for (const s of (tile?.nodes || [])) {
        const sid = String(s?.id ?? `${s?.lat},${s?.lon}`);
        if (seen.has(sid)) continue;
        seen.add(sid);
        const d = haversineM([lat, lon], [Number(s.lat), Number(s.lon)]);
        if (d < nearestM) {
          nearestM = d;
          nearestId = sid;
        }
      }
    }

    if (nearestM <= NAVLINK_SIGNAL_INTERSECTION_MAX_M) {
      this.stats.signalTrue++;
      return {
        hasSignal:true,
        status:"confirmed-r2",
        nearestM,
        nearestSignalId:nearestId,
      };
    }

    if (anyKnown) {
      this.stats.signalFalse++;
      return {
        hasSignal:false,
        status:"confirmed-none-r2",
        nearestM:Number.isFinite(nearestM) ? nearestM : null,
        nearestSignalId:nearestId,
      };
    }

    this.stats.unknown++;
    return {
      hasSignal:null,
      status:"unknown-r2-uncovered",
      nearestM:Number.isFinite(nearestM) ? nearestM : null,
      nearestSignalId:nearestId,
    };
  }

  snapshot() {
    return { ...this.stats };
  }
}

function customCarIntersectionRule({
  incomingEdge,
  outgoingEdge,
  currentNode,
  graph,
  signalInfo,
  outgoingTrafficCost,
}) {
  if (!incomingEdge || !outgoingEdge) {
    return {
      rawSec:0,
      kind:"start",
      profile:"none",
      isTurn:false,
      turnAngleDeg:0,
      hasSignal:null,
      signalStatus:"not-applicable",
    };
  }

  if (customCarIsExpresswayEdge(incomingEdge) || customCarIsExpresswayEdge(outgoingEdge)) {
    return {
      rawSec:0,
      kind:"expressway-skip",
      profile:"none",
      isTurn:false,
      turnAngleDeg:0,
      hasSignal:null,
      signalStatus:"expressway-skip",
    };
  }

  const turn = customCarTurnGeometry(incomingEdge, outgoingEdge, currentNode, graph);
  const trafficBacked =
    outgoingTrafficCost?.source === "TDX" ||
    outgoingTrafficCost?.source === "HERE";
  const profile = trafficBacked ? SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_LIVE : SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_BASELINE;
  const hasSignal = signalInfo?.hasSignal ?? null;

  if (hasSignal === true) {
    return {
      rawSec: turn.isTurn ? profile.signalTurnSec : profile.signalStraightSec,
      kind: turn.isTurn ? "signal-turn" : "signal-straight",
      profile: trafficBacked ? "live-residual" : "baseline-residual",
      isTurn: turn.isTurn,
      turnAngleDeg: turn.angleDeg,
      hasSignal:true,
      signalStatus:signalInfo?.status || "confirmed-r2",
      signalDistanceM:signalInfo?.nearestM ?? null,
      trafficProvider:outgoingTrafficCost?.source || "BASELINE",
    };
  }

  if (hasSignal === false) {
    return {
      rawSec: turn.isTurn ? profile.noSignalTurnSec : profile.noSignalStraightSec,
      kind: turn.isTurn ? "nosignal-turn" : "nosignal-straight",
      profile: trafficBacked ? "live-residual" : "baseline-residual",
      isTurn: turn.isTurn,
      turnAngleDeg: turn.angleDeg,
      hasSignal:false,
      signalStatus:signalInfo?.status || "confirmed-none-r2",
      signalDistanceM:signalInfo?.nearestM ?? null,
      trafficProvider:outgoingTrafficCost?.source || "BASELINE",
    };
  }

  return {
    rawSec:0,
    kind:"unknown",
    profile:"unknown",
    isTurn:turn.isTurn,
    turnAngleDeg:turn.angleDeg,
    hasSignal:null,
    signalStatus:signalInfo?.status || "unknown-r2-uncovered",
    signalDistanceM:signalInfo?.nearestM ?? null,
    trafficProvider:outgoingTrafficCost?.source || "BASELINE",
  };
}

function customCarEffectiveIntersectionDelaySec(drivingSec, rawIntersectionSec) {
  const drive = Math.max(0, Number(drivingSec) || 0);
  const raw = Math.max(0, Number(rawIntersectionSec) || 0);
  return Math.min(raw, drive * SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION);
}

function reconstructCustomCarStateRoute(goalStateKey, came, graph) {
  const rev = [];
  let stateKey = goalStateKey;

  while (came.has(stateKey)) {
    const step = came.get(stateKey);
    rev.push(step);
    stateKey = step.prevStateKey;
  }
  rev.reverse();

  const edges = rev.map(x => x.edge);
  const ids = [];
  const geometry = [];
  const turnEvents = [];
  let distanceM = 0;

  if (edges.length) {
    ids.push(String(edges[0].from));
    const first = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edges[0].from));
    if (first) geometry.push([first.lat, first.lon]);
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const edge = edges[i];
    const step = rev[i];
    const routeM = distanceM;

    if (step?.turnEvent && step.turnEvent.rawSec > 0) {
      turnEvents.push({
        ...step.turnEvent,
        edgeIndex:i,
        routeM,
      });
    } else if (step?.turnEvent && step.turnEvent.kind !== "start") {
      // Keep zero-second events in debug so we can inspect signal/turn matching.
      turnEvents.push({
        ...step.turnEvent,
        edgeIndex:i,
        routeM,
      });
    }

    distanceM += Number(edge.lengthM) || 0;
    ids.push(String(edge.to));
    const n = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edge.to));
    if (n) {
      const last = geometry[geometry.length - 1];
      if (!last || Math.abs(last[0] - n.lat) > 1e-10 || Math.abs(last[1] - n.lon) > 1e-10) {
        geometry.push([n.lat, n.lon]);
      }
    }
  }

  return { ids, edges, geometry, distanceM, turnEvents };
}

function summarizeCustomCarIntersectionDelay(route, goalRecord, signalResolver, debug = false) {
  const rawTotalSec = Math.max(0, Number(goalRecord?.rawIntersectionSec) || 0);
  const drivingDurationSec = Math.max(0, Number(goalRecord?.drivingSec) || 0);
  const capSec = drivingDurationSec * SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION;
  const totalSec = Math.min(rawTotalSec, capSec);
  const scale = rawTotalSec > 0 ? totalSec / rawTotalSec : 1;

  const counts = {
    signalStraight:0,
    signalTurn:0,
    noSignalStraight:0,
    noSignalTurn:0,
    unknown:0,
    expresswaySkipped:0,
  };

  const events = [];
  for (const ev of (route?.turnEvents || [])) {
    if (ev.kind === "signal-straight") counts.signalStraight++;
    else if (ev.kind === "signal-turn") counts.signalTurn++;
    else if (ev.kind === "nosignal-straight") counts.noSignalStraight++;
    else if (ev.kind === "nosignal-turn") counts.noSignalTurn++;
    else if (ev.kind === "expressway-skip") counts.expresswaySkipped++;
    else if (ev.kind === "unknown") counts.unknown++;

    if (debug || Number(ev.rawSec) > 0) {
      events.push({
        ...ev,
        delaySec: Math.round((Number(ev.rawSec || 0) * scale) * 100) / 100,
      });
    }
  }

  return {
    totalSec:Math.round(totalSec * 10) / 10,
    rawTotalSec:Math.round(rawTotalSec * 10) / 10,
    capSec:Math.round(capSec * 10) / 10,
    capApplied:rawTotalSec > capSec + 1e-9,
    delayScale:Math.round(scale * 10000) / 10000,
    maxDrivingFraction:SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION,
    ...counts,
    events,
    signalLookup:signalResolver?.snapshot?.() || null,
    rules:{
      liveTraffic:{...SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_LIVE},
      baseline:{...SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_BASELINE},
      unknownSec:0,
      expresswaySec:0,
      turnGeometry:"<=24deg straight, >=38deg turn, 24-38deg treated as straight",
      capFractionOfDrivingTime:SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION,
    },
  };
}



const CUSTOM_CAR_BASELINE_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT = 1.35;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LIVE_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT = 1.20;
const CUSTOM_CAR_BASELINE_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 120000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LIVE_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 70000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_TRAFFIC_CORRIDOR_MIN_M = 1200;
const CUSTOM_CAR_TRAFFIC_CORRIDOR_MAX_M = 3500;

const CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG = 0.01;
const CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_SAMPLE_M = 400;

function customCarCorridorCellId(lat, lon) {
  const y = Math.floor((Number(lat) + 90) / CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG);
  const x = Math.floor((Number(lon) + 180) / CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG);
  return `${y}:${x}`;
}

function customCarBuildCorridorCellSet(geometry, corridorM) {
  const set = new Set();
  if (!Array.isArray(geometry) || geometry.length < 2) return set;

  const avgLat = geometry.reduce((s,p) => s + Number(p?.[0] || 0), 0) / geometry.length;
  const latCellM = 110540 * CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG;
  const lonCellM = 111320 * Math.cos(avgLat * Math.PI / 180) * CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG;
  const ry = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Number(corridorM) / Math.max(1, latCellM)));
  const rx = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Number(corridorM) / Math.max(1, lonCellM)));

  const addAround = (lat, lon) => {
    const cy = Math.floor((Number(lat) + 90) / CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG);
    const cx = Math.floor((Number(lon) + 180) / CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_CELL_DEG);
    for (let dy = -ry; dy <= ry; dy++) {
      for (let dx = -rx; dx <= rx; dx++) {
        // Elliptical neighborhood keeps the set smaller than a full rectangle.
        const ny = dy / Math.max(1, ry);
        const nx = dx / Math.max(1, rx);
        if ((nx * nx + ny * ny) > 1.35) continue;
        set.add(`${cy + dy}:${cx + dx}`);
      }
    }
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < geometry.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = geometry[i];
    const b = geometry[i + 1];
    if (!Array.isArray(a) || !Array.isArray(b)) continue;

    const segM = haversineM(a, b);
    const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(segM / CUSTOM_CAR_CORRIDOR_SAMPLE_M));

    for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {
      const t = s / steps;
      const lat = Number(a[0]) + (Number(b[0]) - Number(a[0])) * t;
      const lon = Number(a[1]) + (Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1])) * t;
      addAround(lat, lon);
    }
  }

  return set;
}

function customCarTrafficCorridorWidthM(routeDistanceM) {
  const d = Math.max(0, Number(routeDistanceM) || 0);
  return Math.max(
    CUSTOM_CAR_TRAFFIC_CORRIDOR_MIN_M,
    Math.min(CUSTOM_CAR_TRAFFIC_CORRIDOR_MAX_M, d * 0.14)
  );
}


const CUSTOM_CAR_EXPRESSWAY_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 110000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_EXPRESSWAY_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT = 1.55;

const CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE = Object.freeze({
  signalStraightSec: 14,
  signalTurnSec: 20,
  noSignalTurnSec: 4,
  unknownTurnSec: 2,
  maxDrivingFraction: 0.55,
});

function customCarCandidateBaselineCostSec(edge, profile = "normal") {
  const base = customCarBaselineEdgeCostSec(edge);
  if (profile !== "expressway") return base;

  // Candidate-generation only. The final ranking never uses these factors.
  // Their sole purpose is to expose a plausible longer/faster-road alternative.
  const h = String(edge?.highway || "").toLowerCase();
  const factor = {
    motorway:0.70,
    motorway_link:0.78,
    trunk:0.76,
    trunk_link:0.84,
    primary:0.94,
    primary_link:0.96,
    secondary:1.00,
    secondary_link:1.00,
    tertiary:1.04,
    tertiary_link:1.04,
    unclassified:1.07,
    residential:1.12,
    living_street:1.18,
    service:1.18,
    road:1.08,
  }[h] ?? 1.04;

  return base * factor;
}

function customCarRouteEdgeToken(edge) {
  return `${String(edge?.from || "")}>${String(edge?.to || "")}|${String(edge?.osmWayId || "")}`;
}

function customCarRouteEdgeSet(route) {
  return new Set((route?.edges || []).map(customCarRouteEdgeToken));
}

function customCarRouteOverlapRatio(a, b) {
  const A = customCarRouteEdgeSet(a);
  const B = customCarRouteEdgeSet(b);
  if (!A.size || !B.size) return 0;
  let common = 0;
  for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) common++;
  return common / Math.min(A.size, B.size);
}

function customCarPushDistinctCandidate(arr, candidate) {
  if (!candidate?.route?.edges?.length) return false;
  for (const existing of arr) {
    if (customCarRouteOverlapRatio(existing.route, candidate.route) >= 0.94) return false;
  }
  arr.push(candidate);
  return true;
}

async function customCarPostRouteSignalScore({
  route,
  graph,
  signalResolver,
  drivingSec,
  enabled = true,
  debug = false,
}) {
  if (!enabled || !signalResolver?.bound || !route?.edges?.length) {
    return {
      enabled:false,
      totalSec:0,
      rawSec:0,
      capSec:0,
      capApplied:false,
      uniqueSignals:0,
      signalStraight:0,
      signalTurn:0,
      noSignalTurn:0,
      unknownTurn:0,
      events:[],
      signalLookup:signalResolver?.snapshot?.() || null,
      profile:CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE,
    };
  }

  const tasks = [];
  let routeM = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < route.edges.length; i++) {
    const edge = route.edges[i];
    const edgeLen = Number(edge?.lengthM) || 0;

    if (i > 0) {
      const incomingEdge = route.edges[i - 1];
      const outgoingEdge = edge;
      const node = graph.nodeMap.get(String(incomingEdge.to));

      if (
        node &&
        !customCarIsExpresswayEdge(incomingEdge) &&
        !customCarIsExpresswayEdge(outgoingEdge)
      ) {
        const turn = customCarTurnGeometry(incomingEdge, outgoingEdge, node, graph);
        tasks.push({
          i,
          node,
          routeM,
          incomingEdge,
          outgoingEdge,
          turn,
        });
      }
    }

    routeM += edgeLen;
  }

  const resolved = await mapLimit(tasks, 12, async item => ({
    ...item,
    signalInfo: await signalResolver.resolveNode(item.node),
  }));

  const seenSignals = new Set();
  const events = [];
  let rawSec = 0;
  let signalStraight = 0;
  let signalTurn = 0;
  let noSignalTurn = 0;
  let unknownTurn = 0;

  for (const item of resolved) {
    if (!item || item.__error) continue;
    const info = item.signalInfo || {};
    const turn = item.turn || { isTurn:false, angleDeg:0 };
    let addSec = 0;
    let kind = "none";

    if (info.hasSignal === true) {
      const dedupeKey = info.nearestSignalId
        ? `sig:${info.nearestSignalId}`
        : `loc:${Math.round(Number(item.node.lat)*100000)}:${Math.round(Number(item.node.lon)*100000)}`;

      if (seenSignals.has(dedupeKey)) continue;
      seenSignals.add(dedupeKey);

      if (turn.isTurn) {
        addSec = CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE.signalTurnSec;
        signalTurn++;
        kind = "signal-turn";
      } else {
        addSec = CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE.signalStraightSec;
        signalStraight++;
        kind = "signal-straight";
      }
    } else if (info.hasSignal === false && turn.isTurn) {
      addSec = CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE.noSignalTurnSec;
      noSignalTurn++;
      kind = "nosignal-turn";
    } else if (info.hasSignal == null && turn.isTurn) {
      addSec = CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE.unknownTurnSec;
      unknownTurn++;
      kind = "unknown-turn";
    }

    if (addSec > 0) {
      rawSec += addSec;
      if (debug) {
        events.push({
          nodeId:String(item.node.id),
          lat:Number(item.node.lat),
          lon:Number(item.node.lon),
          routeM:Math.round(item.routeM),
          kind,
          delaySec:addSec,
          turnAngleDeg:Math.round(Number(turn.angleDeg || 0) * 10) / 10,
          incomingRoad:item.incomingEdge?.name || null,
          outgoingRoad:item.outgoingEdge?.name || null,
          signalStatus:info.status || null,
          signalDistanceM:Number.isFinite(Number(info.nearestM))
            ? Math.round(Number(info.nearestM) * 10) / 10
            : null,
          nearestSignalId:info.nearestSignalId || null,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  const drive = Math.max(0, Number(drivingSec) || 0);
  const capSec = drive * CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE.maxDrivingFraction;
  const totalSec = Math.min(rawSec, capSec);

  return {
    enabled:true,
    totalSec:Math.round(totalSec * 10) / 10,
    rawSec:Math.round(rawSec * 10) / 10,
    capSec:Math.round(capSec * 10) / 10,
    capApplied:rawSec > capSec + 1e-9,
    uniqueSignals:seenSignals.size,
    signalStraight,
    signalTurn,
    noSignalTurn,
    unknownTurn,
    events,
    signalLookup:signalResolver.snapshot(),
    profile:CUSTOM_CAR_POST_SIGNAL_PROFILE,
  };
}

async function customCarScoreCompletedCandidate({
  candidate,
  graph,
  trafficSnapshot,
  signalResolver,
  usePostSignals,
  debug,
}) {
  const routeTraffic = summarizeCustomCarRouteTraffic(
    candidate.route.edges,
    graph,
    trafficSnapshot,
    debug
  );

  const trafficSec = trafficSnapshot?.ok
    ? Number(routeTraffic.stats.chosenRouteTrafficSec)
    : Number(routeTraffic.stats.chosenRouteBaselineSec);

  const postSignal = await customCarPostRouteSignalScore({
    route:candidate.route,
    graph,
    signalResolver,
    drivingSec:trafficSec,
    enabled:usePostSignals,
    debug,
  });

  const finalEtaSec = Math.max(0, trafficSec) + Math.max(0, Number(postSignal.totalSec) || 0);

  return {
    ...candidate,
    routeTraffic,
    postSignal,
    trafficSec,
    finalEtaSec,
  };
}


const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD_M = 60000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_ADAPTIVE_LONG_DISTANCE_M = 35000;

function customCarShouldUseLongDistanceMode(startPoint, endPoint, directDistanceM) {
  if (directDistanceM >= CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD_M) return true;

  const sLat = Number(startPoint?.[0]), sLon = Number(startPoint?.[1]);
  const eLat = Number(endPoint?.[0]), eLon = Number(endPoint?.[1]);
  if (![sLat,sLon,eLat,eLon].every(Number.isFinite)) return false;

  // Taiwan east-side / mountain-crossing trips can have deceptively short
  // air distance while the legal road route is much longer. Treat these as
  // long-distance earlier so they use the segmented/gateway backend.
  const oneOnEast = sLon >= 121.45 || eLon >= 121.45;
  const otherTowardInteriorOrWest = sLon <= 121.38 || eLon <= 121.38;
  const latOverlapTaiwan = Math.min(sLat,eLat) <= 24.9 && Math.max(sLat,eLat) >= 22.0;

  if (
    directDistanceM >= CUSTOM_CAR_ADAPTIVE_LONG_DISTANCE_M &&
    oneOnEast && otherTowardInteriorOrWest && latOverlapTaiwan
  ) return true;

  // Also promote medium-long east-coast/northeast trips where 60 km was too
  // late and the ordinary multi-candidate baseline can explode first.
  const bothEastOrNortheast = sLon >= 121.45 && eLon >= 121.35;
  if (directDistanceM >= 45000 && bothEastOrNortheast) return true;

  return false;
}
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ACCESS_RADIUS_M = 1800;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 115000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT = 2.15;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_RADIUS_M = 12000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_SPACING_M = 22000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_MAX_TRAFFIC_CENTERS = 12;


const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_SAME_SIDE_MAX_LON_DELTA = 0.55;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ENVELOPE_LAT_MARGIN_DEG = 0.12;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ENVELOPE_LON_MARGIN_DEG = 0.22;


const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ANCHOR_SEARCH_MAX_M = 9000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_BOX_MARGIN_DEG = 0.075;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 30000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT = 1.45;

function customCarBuildLocalGeoEnvelope(a, b, marginDeg = CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_BOX_MARGIN_DEG) {
  const aLat = Number(a?.[0]), aLon = Number(a?.[1]);
  const bLat = Number(b?.[0]), bLon = Number(b?.[1]);
  if (![aLat,aLon,bLat,bLon].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;
  return {
    minLat:Math.min(aLat,bLat) - marginDeg,
    maxLat:Math.max(aLat,bLat) + marginDeg,
    minLon:Math.min(aLon,bLon) - marginDeg,
    maxLon:Math.max(aLon,bLon) + marginDeg,
  };
}

function customCarBuildMajorIncomingSet(graph) {
  const incoming = new Set();
  for (const edges of graph.adj.values()) {
    for (const edge of edges || []) {
      if (customCarIsLongMajorEdge(edge)) incoming.add(String(edge.to));
    }
  }
  return incoming;
}

function customCarFindMajorAnchor(graph, point, role = "start") {
  const incomingMajor = role === "end" ? customCarBuildMajorIncomingSet(graph) : null;
  let best = null;

  for (const node of graph.nodeMap.values()) {
    let eligible = false;

    if (role === "start") {
      const edges = graph.adj.get(String(node.id)) || [];
      eligible = edges.some(customCarIsLongMajorEdge);
    } else {
      eligible = incomingMajor.has(String(node.id));
    }

    if (!eligible) continue;

    const d = haversineM(point, [Number(node.lat), Number(node.lon)]);
    if (d > CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ANCHOR_SEARCH_MAX_M) continue;

    if (!best || d < best.distanceM) {
      best = { node, distanceM:d };
    }
  }

  return best;
}

function customCarCombineRouteSegments(parts) {
  const valid = (parts || []).filter(p => p?.route?.edges?.length || p?.route?.ids?.length);
  const ids = [];
  const edges = [];
  const geometry = [];
  let distanceM = 0;
  let searchDurationSec = 0;

  for (const part of valid) {
    const r = part.route;
    if (!r) continue;

    for (const id of r.ids || []) {
      if (!ids.length || ids[ids.length - 1] !== id) ids.push(id);
    }

    for (const edge of r.edges || []) edges.push(edge);

    for (const p of r.geometry || []) {
      const last = geometry[geometry.length - 1];
      if (
        !last ||
        Math.abs(Number(last[0]) - Number(p[0])) > 1e-9 ||
        Math.abs(Number(last[1]) - Number(p[1])) > 1e-9
      ) {
        geometry.push(p);
      }
    }

    distanceM += Number(r.distanceM) || 0;
    searchDurationSec += Number(part.durationSec) || 0;
  }

  return { ids, edges, geometry, distanceM, searchDurationSec };
}


const CUSTOM_CAR_TW_EAST_LON = 121.25;
const CUSTOM_CAR_TW_WEST_LON = 121.15;
const CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_MIN_SEPARATION_M = 12000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_TILE_NEIGHBORS = 1;
const CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_SEGMENT_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 14000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_SEGMENT_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT = 2.85;

function customCarIsTaiwanPoint(p) {
  const lat = Number(p?.[0]), lon = Number(p?.[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) &&
    lat >= 21.7 && lat <= 25.4 && lon >= 119.9 && lon <= 122.1;
}

function customCarPolylineAirDistance(points) {
  let m = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) m += haversineM(points[i-1], points[i]);
  return m;
}

function customCarCompactGatewayPoints(points, startPoint, endPoint) {
  const out = [];
  for (const p of points || []) {
    if (!Array.isArray(p) || p.length < 2) continue;
    if (haversineM(startPoint, p) < CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_MIN_SEPARATION_M) continue;
    if (haversineM(endPoint, p) < CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_MIN_SEPARATION_M) continue;
    const prev = out[out.length - 1];
    if (prev && haversineM(prev, p) < CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_MIN_SEPARATION_M) continue;
    out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}

function customCarBuildTaiwanGatewayPlan(startPoint, endPoint) {
  if (!customCarIsTaiwanPoint(startPoint) || !customCarIsTaiwanPoint(endPoint)) return null;

  const sLat = Number(startPoint[0]), sLon = Number(startPoint[1]);
  const eLat = Number(endPoint[0]), eLon = Number(endPoint[1]);

  const sEast = sLon >= CUSTOM_CAR_TW_EAST_LON;
  const eEast = eLon >= CUSTOM_CAR_TW_EAST_LON;
  const sWest = sLon <= CUSTOM_CAR_TW_WEST_LON;
  const eWest = eLon <= CUSTOM_CAR_TW_WEST_LON;

  // Hualien/east-coast <-> north Taiwan:
  // always segment through Su'ao / Yilan so the long A* never has to fan-search
  // the whole east-coast + Taipei basin in a single pass.
  const hualienToNorth =
    ((sLat <= 24.35 && sLon >= 121.45) && (eLat >= 24.75 && eLon >= 121.35)) ||
    ((eLat <= 24.35 && eLon >= 121.45) && (sLat >= 24.75 && sLon >= 121.35));

  if (hualienToNorth && !((sEast && eWest) || (sWest && eEast))) {
    const destination = sLat <= 24.35 && sLon >= 121.45 ? endPoint : startPoint;
    const dLat = Number(destination[0]);
    const dLon = Number(destination[1]);

    let pts;

    // Interior / mountain destination west of Yilan:
    // finish via Yilan -> Sanxing -> Datong/upper Lanyang instead of forcing
    // one large Taipei-southeast -> mountain endpoint search.
    if (dLon < 121.50 && dLat < 24.95) {
      pts = [
        [24.60, 121.84],  // Su'ao / north Suhua corridor
        [24.74, 121.75],  // Luodong / south Yilan
        [24.69, 121.62],  // Sanxing / west Yilan
        [24.67, 121.51],  // Datong / upper Lanyang approach
      ];
    } else {
      pts = [
        [24.60, 121.84],  // Su'ao / north Suhua corridor
        [24.85, 121.78],  // Yilan / Toucheng corridor
        [24.97, 121.64],  // Taipei southeast approach
      ];
    }

    if (sLat > eLat) pts = pts.reverse();
    pts = customCarCompactGatewayPoints(pts, startPoint, endPoint);

    return {
      mode:"taiwan-regional-gateway",
      selectedName:(dLon < 121.50 && dLat < 24.95)
        ? "east-yilan-mountain-transfer"
        : "east-north-coastal-transfer",
      northAirDistanceM:Math.round(customCarPolylineAirDistance([startPoint, ...pts, endPoint])),
      southAirDistanceM:null,
      waypoints:pts,
    };
  }

  // Cross-island east <-> west.
  if (!((sEast && eWest) || (sWest && eEast))) return null;

  const eastPoint = sEast ? startPoint : endPoint;
  const westPoint = sEast ? endPoint : startPoint;
  const westLat = Number(westPoint[0]);

  const north = [
    [24.60, 121.84],  // Su'ao
    [24.85, 121.78],  // Yilan / Toucheng
    [24.97, 121.53],  // Taipei basin
  ];
  if (westLat < 24.70) north.push([24.82, 121.03]); // Hsinchu / west corridor
  if (westLat < 24.30) north.push([24.35, 120.68]); // Miaoli / north Taichung corridor

  const south = [
    [22.76, 121.14],  // Taitung
    [22.34, 120.89],  // Dawu
    [22.37, 120.59],  // Fangliao
    [22.67, 120.49],  // Pingtung
  ];
  if (westLat > 23.05) south.push([23.48, 120.44]); // Chiayi

  const northPts = customCarCompactGatewayPoints(north, eastPoint, westPoint);
  const southPts = customCarCompactGatewayPoints(south, eastPoint, westPoint);

  const northScore = customCarPolylineAirDistance([eastPoint, ...northPts, westPoint]);
  const southScore = customCarPolylineAirDistance([eastPoint, ...southPts, westPoint]);

  let selectedName = northScore <= southScore ? "north-transfer" : "south-transfer";
  let selected = northScore <= southScore ? northPts : southPts;

  if (!sEast) selected = [...selected].reverse();

  return {
    mode:"taiwan-regional-gateway",
    selectedName,
    northAirDistanceM:Math.round(northScore),
    southAirDistanceM:Math.round(southScore),
    waypoints:selected,
  };
}

function customCarFindIntermediateMajorAnchor(graph, point) {
  let best = null;

  for (const node of graph.nodeMap.values()) {
    const outgoing = graph.adj.get(String(node.id)) || [];
    if (!outgoing.some(customCarIsLongMajorEdge)) continue;

    const d = haversineM(point, [Number(node.lat), Number(node.lon)]);
    if (d > CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ANCHOR_SEARCH_MAX_M) continue;

    if (!best || d < best.distanceM) best = { node, distanceM:d };
  }

  return best;
}

async function customCarLoadGatewayAnchors(graph, gatewayPlan) {
  const hits = [];
  for (const point of gatewayPlan?.waypoints || []) {
    await graph.loadAroundPoint(point, CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_TILE_NEIGHBORS);
    const hit = customCarFindIntermediateMajorAnchor(graph, point);
    if (!hit) {
      return { ok:false, error:"gateway_major_anchor_not_found", point, hits };
    }
    hits.push({
      point,
      node:hit.node,
      distanceM:hit.distanceM,
    });
  }
  return { ok:true, hits };
}

function customCarBuildLongGeoEnvelope(startPoint, endPoint) {
  const sLat = Number(startPoint?.[0]), sLon = Number(startPoint?.[1]);
  const eLat = Number(endPoint?.[0]), eLon = Number(endPoint?.[1]);
  if (![sLat,sLon,eLat,eLon].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;

  // Same-side intercity routes (e.g. Hualien↔Taipei) have a predictable
  // north/south corridor. Cross-island routes deliberately skip this envelope.
  if (Math.abs(sLon - eLon) > CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_SAME_SIDE_MAX_LON_DELTA) return null;

  return {
    minLat:Math.min(sLat,eLat) - CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ENVELOPE_LAT_MARGIN_DEG,
    maxLat:Math.max(sLat,eLat) + CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ENVELOPE_LAT_MARGIN_DEG,
    minLon:Math.min(sLon,eLon) - CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ENVELOPE_LON_MARGIN_DEG,
    maxLon:Math.max(sLon,eLon) + CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ENVELOPE_LON_MARGIN_DEG,
  };
}

function customCarNodeInsideLongGeoEnvelope(node, envlp) {
  if (!envlp || !node) return true;
  const lat = Number(node.lat), lon = Number(node.lon);
  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) return false;
  return (
    lat >= envlp.minLat && lat <= envlp.maxLat &&
    lon >= envlp.minLon && lon <= envlp.maxLon
  );
}

function customCarIsLongMajorEdge(edge) {
  const h = String(edge?.highway || "").toLowerCase();
  return new Set([
    "motorway","motorway_link",
    "trunk","trunk_link",
    "primary","primary_link",
    "secondary","secondary_link",
    "tertiary","tertiary_link"
  ]).has(h);
}

function customCarNearEitherEndpoint(node, startNode, goalNode, radiusM) {
  if (!node) return false;
  const p = [Number(node.lat), Number(node.lon)];
  return (
    haversineM(p, [Number(startNode.lat), Number(startNode.lon)]) <= radiusM ||
    haversineM(p, [Number(goalNode.lat), Number(goalNode.lon)]) <= radiusM
  );
}

function customCarSampleRouteCenters(geometry, spacingM, radiusM, maxCenters) {
  if (!Array.isArray(geometry) || geometry.length < 2) return [];
  const out = [];
  let carried = 0;
  let last = geometry[0];

  const push = p => {
    if (!Array.isArray(p) || p.length < 2) return;
    const lat = Number(p[0]), lon = Number(p[1]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) return;
    const prev = out[out.length - 1];
    if (prev && haversineM([prev.lat, prev.lon], [lat, lon]) < spacingM * 0.35) return;
    out.push({ lat, lon, radiusM });
  };

  push(geometry[0]);

  for (let i = 1; i < geometry.length; i++) {
    const cur = geometry[i];
    const seg = haversineM(last, cur);
    carried += seg;
    if (carried >= spacingM) {
      push(cur);
      carried = 0;
    }
    last = cur;
  }

  push(geometry[geometry.length - 1]);

  if (out.length <= maxCenters) return out;

  // Evenly downsample while always keeping first/last.
  const sampled = [out[0]];
  const slots = Math.max(0, maxCenters - 2);
  for (let i = 1; i <= slots; i++) {
    const idx = Math.round(i * (out.length - 1) / (slots + 1));
    sampled.push(out[idx]);
  }
  sampled.push(out[out.length - 1]);
  return sampled;
}

async function buildCustomCarTrafficSnapshotAlongRoute(env, ctx, route) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const includeHere = !!ctx?.hereKey;
  const includeTdx = !!(env?.TDX_CLIENT_ID && env?.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET);

  if (!includeHere && !includeTdx) {
    return {
      ok:false, degraded:true, reason:"no_traffic_credentials",
      tdxSegments:[], hereSegments:[], tdxIndex:null, hereIndex:null,
      matchCache:new Map(), chainAliasCache:new Map(),
      chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      providerStats:{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
      areaSummary:null,
    };
  }

  const centers = customCarSampleRouteCenters(
    route?.geometry || [],
    CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_SPACING_M,
    CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_RADIUS_M,
    CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_MAX_TRAFFIC_CENTERS
  );

  const cfg = {
    ok:true,
    mode:"route-corridor",
    directDistanceM:Number(route?.distanceM) || null,
    radiusM:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_RADIUS_M,
    spacingM:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_SPACING_M,
    centers,
    functionalClasses:[1,2,3,4,5],
    includeHere,
    includeTdx,
  };

  const area = await getTrafficArea(env, { hereKey:ctx?.hereKey || null }, cfg);
  const segments = Array.isArray(area?.segments) ? area.segments : [];
  const tdxSegments = segments.filter(x => x?.provider === "TDX");
  const hereSegments = segments.filter(x => x?.provider === "HERE");

  return {
    ok:segments.length > 0,
    degraded:segments.length === 0,
    reason:segments.length ? null : (
      area?.tdxError ||
      (includeHere && Number(area?.hereAreaSucceeded || 0) === 0
        ? "HERE_route_corridor_failed"
        : "no_traffic_segments")
    ),
    tdxSegments,
    hereSegments,
    tdxIndex:tdxSegments.length ? buildTrafficGrid(tdxSegments) : null,
    hereIndex:hereSegments.length ? buildTrafficGrid(hereSegments) : null,
    matchCache:new Map(),
    chainAliasCache:new Map(),
    chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
    elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
    providerStats:area?.providerStats || {
      tdx:tdxSegments.length,
      here:hereSegments.length,
      total:segments.length,
    },
    areaSummary:{
      mode:"route-corridor",
      directDistanceM:Number(route?.distanceM) || null,
      centerCount:centers.length,
      centers,
      radiusM:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_TRAFFIC_RADIUS_M,
      hereAreaRequested:area?.hereAreaRequested ?? null,
      hereAreaSucceeded:area?.hereAreaSucceeded ?? null,
      tdxUnavailable:area?.tdxUnavailable === true,
      tdxError:area?.tdxError || null,
      tdxStaleScopes:area?.tdxStaleScopes || [],
      areas:Array.isArray(area?.areas) ? area.areas.map(x => ({
        provider:x?.provider,
        ok:x?.ok,
        centerIndex:x?.centerIndex,
        resultCount:x?.resultCount,
        cacheHit:x?.cacheHit,
        elapsedMs:x?.elapsedMs,
        error:x?.error || null,
      })) : [],
    },
  };
}

async function customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
  graph,
  startNode,
  goalNode,
  trafficSnapshot,
  useLiveTraffic,
  corridorCellSet = null,
  corridorM = null,
  maxExpansions = CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_EXPANSIONS,
  heuristicWeight = 1,
  baselineProfile = "normal",
  longMajorOnly = false,
  endpointAccessRadiusM = CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_ACCESS_RADIUS_M,
  geoEnvelope = null,
}) {
  const open = new CustomCarMinHeap();
  const g = new Map();
  const came = new Map();
  const closed = new Set();

  const startId = String(startNode.id);
  const goalId = String(goalNode.id);

  g.set(startId, 0);
  open.push({
    id:startId,
    g:0,
    f:customCarHeuristicSec(startNode, goalNode) * heuristicWeight,
  });

  let expansions = 0;
  let relaxed = 0;
  let corridorRejected = 0;

  while (open.size) {
    const curEntry = open.pop();
    if (!curEntry) break;

    const curId = String(curEntry.id);
    const bestKnown = g.get(curId);
    if (!Number.isFinite(bestKnown)) continue;
    if (curEntry.g > bestKnown + 1e-9) continue;
    if (closed.has(curId)) continue;

    const current = graph.nodeMap.get(curId);
    if (!current) continue;

    if (
      geoEnvelope &&
      curId !== startId &&
      curId !== goalId &&
      !customCarNodeInsideLongGeoEnvelope(current, geoEnvelope)
    ) {
      closed.add(curId);
      continue;
    }

    if (curId === goalId) {
      return {
        ok:true,
        durationSec:bestKnown,
        route:reconstructCustomCarRoute(curId, came, graph),
        expansions,
        relaxed,
        corridorRejected,
      };
    }

    closed.add(curId);
    expansions++;

    if (expansions > maxExpansions) {
      return {
        ok:false,
        error:"astar_expansion_limit",
        expansions,
        relaxed,
        corridorRejected,
      };
    }

    let outgoing;
    try {
      outgoing = await graph.ensureOutgoingForNode(current);
    } catch (err) {
      if (String(err?.message || err) === "custom_car_tile_load_limit") {
        return {
          ok:false,
          error:"graph_tile_load_limit",
          expansions,
          relaxed,
          corridorRejected,
        };
      }
      throw err;
    }

    for (const edge of outgoing) {
      const next = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edge.to));
      if (!next) continue;

      if (
        geoEnvelope &&
        String(next.id) !== goalId &&
        !customCarNodeInsideLongGeoEnvelope(next, geoEnvelope)
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (
        longMajorOnly &&
        !customCarIsLongMajorEdge(edge) &&
        !customCarNearEitherEndpoint(current, startNode, goalNode, endpointAccessRadiusM) &&
        !customCarNearEitherEndpoint(next, startNode, goalNode, endpointAccessRadiusM)
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (
        corridorCellSet &&
        corridorCellSet.size &&
        String(next.id) !== goalId
      ) {
        const cellId = customCarCorridorCellId(next.lat, next.lon);
        if (!corridorCellSet.has(cellId)) {
          corridorRejected++;
          continue;
        }
      }

      let costSec;
      if (useLiveTraffic) {
        const liveCost = customCarLiveEdgeCost(
          edge,
          current,
          next,
          trafficSnapshot,
          graph,
          { allowChainAlias:false }
        );
        if (!Number.isFinite(liveCost.sec)) continue;
        costSec = liveCost.sec;
      } else {
        costSec = customCarCandidateBaselineCostSec(edge, baselineProfile);
      }

      const tentative = bestKnown + costSec;
      const nextId = String(next.id);
      const prevBest = g.get(nextId);

      if (Number.isFinite(prevBest) && tentative >= prevBest - 1e-9) continue;

      g.set(nextId, tentative);
      came.set(nextId, { prev:curId, edge });
      relaxed++;

      open.push({
        id:nextId,
        g:tentative,
        f:tentative + customCarHeuristicSec(next, goalNode) * heuristicWeight,
      });
    }
  }

  return {
    ok:false,
    error:"route_not_found_with_loaded_graph",
    expansions,
    relaxed,
    corridorRejected,
  };
}



async function customCarResolveNodeInFreshGraph(env, sourceNode) {
  const fresh = new CustomCarGraphStore(env);
  const p = [Number(sourceNode.lat), Number(sourceNode.lon)];

  await fresh.ensureCenterTile(p, false);
  await fresh.loadAroundPoint(p);

  let node = fresh.nodeMap.get(String(sourceNode.id)) || null;

  // Gateway anchors are real OSM nodes and should normally be found directly.
  // If not, fall back to the nearest loaded major-road node around the same point.
  if (!node) {
    let best = null;
    for (const candidate of fresh.nodeMap.values()) {
      const edges = fresh.adj.get(String(candidate.id)) || [];
      if (!edges.some(customCarIsLongMajorEdge)) continue;
      const d = haversineM(p, [Number(candidate.lat), Number(candidate.lon)]);
      if (!best || d < best.distanceM) best = { node:candidate, distanceM:d };
    }
    node = best?.node || null;
  }

  return { graph:fresh, node };
}

async function customCarRunIsolatedBackboneSegment({
  env,
  segStart,
  segEnd,
  geoEnvelope,
  maxExpansions,
  heuristicWeight,
}) {
  // Use a fresh graph store for each long leg. This is the key memory isolation:
  // a 5-leg Taiwan route no longer retains all tiles/nodes from prior legs.
