  const startResolved = await customCarResolveNodeInFreshGraph(env, segStart);
  const graph = startResolved.graph;

  const endPoint = [Number(segEnd.lat), Number(segEnd.lon)];
  await graph.ensureCenterTile(endPoint, false);
  await graph.loadAroundPoint(endPoint);

  let startNode = startResolved.node;
  let endNode = graph.nodeMap.get(String(segEnd.id)) || null;

  if (!startNode) {
    return { ok:false, error:"isolated_segment_start_anchor_missing", expansions:0, relaxed:0, graph };
  }

  if (!endNode) {
    let best = null;
    for (const candidate of graph.nodeMap.values()) {
      const edges = graph.adj.get(String(candidate.id)) || [];
      const incoming = graph.inDegree.get(String(candidate.id)) || 0;
      if (!edges.some(customCarIsLongMajorEdge) && incoming <= 0) continue;
      const d = haversineM(endPoint, [Number(candidate.lat), Number(candidate.lon)]);
      if (!best || d < best.distanceM) best = { node:candidate, distanceM:d };
    }
    endNode = best?.node || null;
  }

  if (!endNode) {
    return { ok:false, error:"isolated_segment_end_anchor_missing", expansions:0, relaxed:0, graph };
  }

  const result = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
    graph,
    startNode,
    goalNode:endNode,
    trafficSnapshot:null,
    useLiveTraffic:false,
    corridorCellSet:null,
    corridorM:null,
    maxExpansions,
    heuristicWeight,
    baselineProfile:"expressway",
    longMajorOnly:true,
    endpointAccessRadiusM:0,
    geoEnvelope,
  });

  result.isolatedLoadedGraphTileCount = graph.loadedKeys.length;
  return result;
}

async function routeCustomCarLongDistancePrepared({
  env,
  ctx,
  graph,
  startNode,
  goalNode,
  startPoint,
  endPoint,
  startEdgeSnap,
  endEdgeSnap,
  useTraffic,
  debug,
  t0,
}) {
  // Initial snap neighborhoods are already loaded. Pick nearby major-road
  // anchors so the long backbone never has to search deep into dense local grids.
  const startAnchorHit = customCarFindMajorAnchor(graph, startPoint, "start");
  const endAnchorHit = customCarFindMajorAnchor(graph, endPoint, "end");

  if (!startAnchorHit || !endAnchorHit) {
    return {
      ok:false,
      version:"3.7.1",
      status:503,
      error:"long_distance_major_anchor_not_found",
      startAnchorDistanceM:startAnchorHit?.distanceM ?? null,
      endAnchorDistanceM:endAnchorHit?.distanceM ?? null,
      loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
      loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      ts:Date.now(),
    };
  }

  const startAnchor = startAnchorHit.node;
  const endAnchor = endAnchorHit.node;

  // Cross-island trips need regional topology before node-level A*.
  // This changes only graph search; traffic acquisition/scoring remains exactly as before.
  const gatewayPlan = customCarBuildTaiwanGatewayPlan(
    [Number(startAnchor.lat), Number(startAnchor.lon)],
    [Number(endAnchor.lat), Number(endAnchor.lon)]
  );

  // Overall envelope is diagnostic only. Segmented gateway routing uses its
  // own per-segment envelopes below.
  const geoEnvelope = gatewayPlan
    ? null
    : customCarBuildLongGeoEnvelope(
        [Number(startAnchor.lat), Number(startAnchor.lon)],
        [Number(endAnchor.lat), Number(endAnchor.lon)]
      );

  let gatewayAnchors = [];
  if (gatewayPlan?.waypoints?.length) {
    const loadedGateways = await customCarLoadGatewayAnchors(graph, gatewayPlan);
    if (!loadedGateways.ok) {
      return {
        ok:false,
        version:"3.7.1",
        status:503,
        error:loadedGateways.error,
        phase:"regional-gateway-anchor",
        gatewayPlan,
        failedGatewayPoint:loadedGateways.point || null,
        loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
        loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
        elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
        ts:Date.now(),
      };
    }
    gatewayAnchors = loadedGateways.hits;
  }

  const startLocalEnvelope = customCarBuildLocalGeoEnvelope(
    startPoint,
    [startAnchor.lat, startAnchor.lon]
  );
  const endLocalEnvelope = customCarBuildLocalGeoEnvelope(
    [endAnchor.lat, endAnchor.lon],
    endPoint
  );

  // 1) Local start connector: normal-road A* in a tight box.
  const startLocal = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
    graph,
    startNode,
    goalNode:startAnchor,
    trafficSnapshot:null,
    useLiveTraffic:false,
    corridorCellSet:null,
    corridorM:null,
    maxExpansions:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_MAX_EXPANSIONS,
    heuristicWeight:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT,
    baselineProfile:"normal",
    longMajorOnly:false,
    geoEnvelope:startLocalEnvelope,
  });

  if (!startLocal.ok) {
    return {
      ok:false,
      version:"3.7.1",
      status:503,
      error:startLocal.error || "long_distance_start_connector_failed",
      phase:"local-start",
      startAnchor:{
        id:startAnchor.id,
        lat:startAnchor.lat,
        lon:startAnchor.lon,
        distanceM:startAnchorHit.distanceM,
      },
      expansions:startLocal.expansions,
      relaxed:startLocal.relaxed,
      loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      ts:Date.now(),
    };
  }

  // 2) Long major-road backbone.
  // Same-side trips remain one segment. Cross-island trips are split through
  // regional gateways so every segment gets a bounded geographic envelope.
  const backboneNodes = [
    startAnchor,
    ...gatewayAnchors.map(x => x.node),
    endAnchor,
  ];

  const backboneParts = [];
  let backboneExpansions = 0;
  let backboneRelaxed = 0;
  let backboneDurationSec = 0;
  const backboneSegmentStats = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < backboneNodes.length - 1; i++) {
    const segStart = backboneNodes[i];
    const segEnd = backboneNodes[i + 1];
    const segStartPoint = [Number(segStart.lat), Number(segStart.lon)];
    const segEndPoint = [Number(segEnd.lat), Number(segEnd.lon)];

    // Every gateway segment is short enough that this envelope is safe.
    // For the ordinary same-side case it behaves like v3.5.
    let segEnvelope = customCarBuildLongGeoEnvelope(segStartPoint, segEndPoint);
    if (!segEnvelope) {
      // Fallback generous segment envelope; unlike the old whole-trip null
      // envelope, this is limited to one regional leg.
      const segMargin =
        gatewayPlan?.selectedName === "east-yilan-mountain-transfer" ? 0.16 : 0.12;
      segEnvelope = {
        minLat:Math.min(segStartPoint[0],segEndPoint[0]) - segMargin,
        maxLat:Math.max(segStartPoint[0],segEndPoint[0]) + segMargin,
        minLon:Math.min(segStartPoint[1],segEndPoint[1]) - segMargin,
        maxLon:Math.max(segStartPoint[1],segEndPoint[1]) + segMargin,
      };
    }

    const segMaxExpansions = gatewayPlan
      ? (
          gatewayPlan.selectedName === "east-yilan-mountain-transfer"
            ? 18000
            : CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_SEGMENT_MAX_EXPANSIONS
        )
      : CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_MAX_EXPANSIONS;

    const segHeuristicWeight = gatewayPlan
      ? CUSTOM_CAR_GATEWAY_SEGMENT_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT
      : CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT;

    const seg = gatewayPlan
      ? await customCarRunIsolatedBackboneSegment({
          env,
          segStart,
          segEnd,
          geoEnvelope:segEnvelope,
          maxExpansions:segMaxExpansions,
          heuristicWeight:segHeuristicWeight,
        })
      : await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
          graph,
          startNode:segStart,
          goalNode:segEnd,
          trafficSnapshot:null,
          useLiveTraffic:false,
          corridorCellSet:null,
          corridorM:null,
          maxExpansions:segMaxExpansions,
          heuristicWeight:segHeuristicWeight,
          baselineProfile:"expressway",
          longMajorOnly:true,
          endpointAccessRadiusM:0,
          geoEnvelope:segEnvelope,
        });

    if (!seg.ok) {
      return {
        ok:false,
        version:"3.7.1",
        status:503,
        error:seg.error || "long_distance_backbone_segment_failed",
        phase:"long-distance-backbone",
        directDistanceM:Math.round(haversineM(startPoint,endPoint)),
        gatewayPlan,
        gatewayAnchors:gatewayAnchors.map(x => ({
          point:x.point,
          id:x.node.id,
          lat:x.node.lat,
          lon:x.node.lon,
          distanceM:Math.round(x.distanceM),
        })),
        failedSegmentIndex:i,
        failedSegment:{
          start:[segStart.lat,segStart.lon],
          end:[segEnd.lat,segEnd.lon],
          geoEnvelope:segEnvelope,
        },
        startAnchor:{
          id:startAnchor.id,
          lat:startAnchor.lat,
          lon:startAnchor.lon,
          distanceM:startAnchorHit.distanceM,
        },
        endAnchor:{
          id:endAnchor.id,
          lat:endAnchor.lat,
          lon:endAnchor.lon,
          distanceM:endAnchorHit.distanceM,
        },
        expansions:backboneExpansions + (seg.expansions || 0),
        relaxed:backboneRelaxed + (seg.relaxed || 0),
        searchStateMode:"node-only-regional-gateway-backbone",
        loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
        loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
        failedSegmentIsolatedLoadedGraphTileCount:seg.isolatedLoadedGraphTileCount ?? null,
        missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
        elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
        ts:Date.now(),
      };
    }

    backboneParts.push({ route:seg.route, durationSec:seg.durationSec });
    backboneExpansions += Number(seg.expansions) || 0;
    backboneRelaxed += Number(seg.relaxed) || 0;
    backboneDurationSec += Number(seg.durationSec) || 0;
    backboneSegmentStats.push({
      index:i,
      expansions:seg.expansions,
      relaxed:seg.relaxed,
      costSec:seg.durationSec,
      distanceM:Math.round(seg.route?.distanceM || 0),
      isolatedLoadedGraphTileCount:seg.isolatedLoadedGraphTileCount ?? null,
      start:[segStart.lat,segStart.lon],
      end:[segEnd.lat,segEnd.lon],
    });
  }

  const backboneCombined = customCarCombineRouteSegments(backboneParts);
  const backbone = {
    ok:true,
    route:{
      ids:backboneCombined.ids,
      edges:backboneCombined.edges,
      geometry:backboneCombined.geometry,
      distanceM:backboneCombined.distanceM,
    },
    durationSec:backboneDurationSec,
    expansions:backboneExpansions,
    relaxed:backboneRelaxed,
  };

  // 3) Local end connector: major-road anchor -> exact virtual end.
  const endLocal = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
    graph,
    startNode:endAnchor,
    goalNode,
    trafficSnapshot:null,
    useLiveTraffic:false,
    corridorCellSet:null,
    corridorM:null,
    maxExpansions:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_MAX_EXPANSIONS,
    heuristicWeight:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_LOCAL_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT,
    baselineProfile:"normal",
    longMajorOnly:false,
    geoEnvelope:endLocalEnvelope,
  });

  if (!endLocal.ok) {
    return {
      ok:false,
      version:"3.7.1",
      status:503,
      error:endLocal.error || "long_distance_end_connector_failed",
      phase:"local-end",
      endAnchor:{
        id:endAnchor.id,
        lat:endAnchor.lat,
        lon:endAnchor.lon,
        distanceM:endAnchorHit.distanceM,
      },
      expansions:endLocal.expansions,
      relaxed:endLocal.relaxed,
      loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      ts:Date.now(),
    };
  }

  const combined = customCarCombineRouteSegments([
    { route:startLocal.route, durationSec:startLocal.durationSec },
    { route:backbone.route, durationSec:backbone.durationSec },
    { route:endLocal.route, durationSec:endLocal.durationSec },
  ]);

  const route = {
    ids:combined.ids,
    edges:combined.edges,
    geometry:combined.geometry,
    distanceM:combined.distanceM,
  };

  const trafficSnapshot = useTraffic
    ? await buildCustomCarTrafficSnapshotAlongRoute(env, ctx, route)
    : {
        ok:false, degraded:true, reason:"traffic_disabled",
        tdxSegments:[], hereSegments:[], tdxIndex:null, hereIndex:null,
        matchCache:new Map(), chainAliasCache:new Map(),
        chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
        elapsedMs:0,
        providerStats:{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
        areaSummary:null,
      };

  const routeTraffic = summarizeCustomCarRouteTraffic(
    route.edges,
    graph,
    trafficSnapshot,
    debug
  );

  const drivingSec = trafficSnapshot?.ok
    ? Number(routeTraffic.stats.chosenRouteTrafficSec)
    : Number(routeTraffic.stats.chosenRouteBaselineSec);

  return {
    ok:true,
    version:"3.7.1",
    engine:"custom-car-a-star-v3.7.1-adaptive-long-mode",
    longDistanceMode:true,
    longDistanceTrigger:{
      directDistanceM:Math.round(haversineM(startPoint,endPoint)),
      baseThresholdM:CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD_M,
      adaptiveThresholdM:CUSTOM_CAR_ADAPTIVE_LONG_DISTANCE_M,
    },
    endpointAnchorMode:true,
    regionalGatewayMode:gatewayPlan?.mode || "not-needed",
    gatewayPlan,
    gatewayAnchors:gatewayAnchors.map(x => ({
      point:x.point,
      id:x.node.id,
      lat:x.node.lat,
      lon:x.node.lon,
      distanceM:Math.round(x.distanceM),
    })),
    trafficMode:useTraffic
      ? (trafficSnapshot?.ok ? "HERE_TDX_ROUTE_CORRIDOR_POST_SCORE" : "BASELINE_DEGRADED")
      : "BASELINE_ONLY",
    signalTurnMode:"LONG_DISTANCE_POST_SIGNALS_DISABLED",
    graphProvisioningMode:"r2-only",
    start:startPoint,
    end:endPoint,
    snapMode:"edge-virtual-node",
    snappedStart:{
      id:startNode.id,
      lat:startNode.lat,
      lon:startNode.lon,
      distanceM:startEdgeSnap.distanceM,
      road:startEdgeSnap.edge.name || null,
      highway:startEdgeSnap.edge.highway || null,
      osmWayId:startEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId || null
    },
    snappedEnd:{
      id:goalNode.id,
      lat:goalNode.lat,
      lon:goalNode.lon,
      distanceM:endEdgeSnap.distanceM,
      road:endEdgeSnap.edge.name || null,
      highway:endEdgeSnap.edge.highway || null,
      osmWayId:endEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId || null
    },
    startAnchor:{
      id:startAnchor.id,
      lat:startAnchor.lat,
      lon:startAnchor.lon,
      distanceM:Math.round(startAnchorHit.distanceM),
    },
    endAnchor:{
      id:endAnchor.id,
      lat:endAnchor.lat,
      lon:endAnchor.lon,
      distanceM:Math.round(endAnchorHit.distanceM),
    },
    geoEnvelope,
    selectedCandidate:gatewayPlan ? "regional-gateway-backbone" : "long-backbone-anchored",
    candidateRoutes:[{
      rank:1,
      id:gatewayPlan ? "regional-gateway-backbone" : "long-backbone-anchored",
      generation:gatewayPlan
        ? "local-start + segmented regional-gateway backbone + local-end"
        : "local-start + major-road-backbone + local-end",
      selected:true,
      distanceM:Math.round(route.distanceM),
      trafficDrivingSec:Math.round(drivingSec),
      postSignalDelaySec:0,
      finalEtaSec:Math.round(drivingSec),
      finalEtaMin:Math.round(drivingSec / 6) / 10,
      trafficCoverageDistanceRatio:
        Math.round(Number(routeTraffic?.stats?.trafficCoverageDistanceRatio || 0) * 1000) / 1000,
      fallbackBaselineSec:Math.round(Number(routeTraffic?.stats?.fallbackBaselineSec || 0)),
      signalCount:0,
      generationSearch:{
        localStartExpansions:startLocal.expansions,
        backboneExpansions:backbone.expansions,
        backboneSegments:backboneSegmentStats,
        localEndExpansions:endLocal.expansions,
        localStartCostSec:startLocal.durationSec,
        backboneCostSec:backbone.durationSec,
        localEndCostSec:endLocal.durationSec,
      },
    }],
    distanceM:Math.round(route.distanceM),
    drivingDurationSec:Math.round(drivingSec),
    intersectionDelaySec:0,
    durationSec:Math.round(drivingSec),
    geometry:route.geometry,
    trafficSnapshot:{
      ok:trafficSnapshot?.ok === true,
      degraded:trafficSnapshot?.degraded === true,
      reason:trafficSnapshot?.reason || null,
      elapsedMs:trafficSnapshot?.elapsedMs ?? null,
      providerStats:trafficSnapshot?.providerStats || {tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
      hereChainAliasStats:trafficSnapshot?.chainAliasStats || {evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
      area:trafficSnapshot?.areaSummary || null,
    },
    trafficRouteStats:routeTraffic.stats,
    postRouteIntersectionDelay:{
      enabled:false,
      totalSec:0,
      reason:"disabled_in_long_distance_mode_v3.5.0"
    },
    routeEdgeDebug:debug ? routeTraffic.edgeDebug : undefined,
    pathNodeCount:route.ids.length,
    pathEdgeCount:route.edges.length,
    expansions:startLocal.expansions + backbone.expansions + endLocal.expansions,
    relaxed:startLocal.relaxed + backbone.relaxed + endLocal.relaxed,
    searchStateMode:gatewayPlan ? "node-only-regional-gateway-backbone" : "node-only-long-backbone-anchored",
    loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
    loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
    autoBuildCount:graph.autoBuildCount,
    autoBuiltGraphTiles:graph.autoBuiltKeys,
    autoBuildFailures:graph.autoBuildFailures,
    missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
    elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
    ts:Date.now(),
  };
}


const CUSTOM_CAR_SHORT_EXTRA_CANDIDATE_BASELINE_EXPANSIONS = 26000;

function customCarBuildShortMediumEnvelope(startPoint, endPoint) {
  const d = haversineM(startPoint, endPoint);
  const sLat = Number(startPoint?.[0]), sLon = Number(startPoint?.[1]);
  const eLat = Number(endPoint?.[0]), eLon = Number(endPoint?.[1]);
  if (![sLat,sLon,eLat,eLon].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;

  // Dense urban routes need a hard spatial bound before any extra R2 tile is
  // loaded. Margins remain generous enough for normal expressway detours.
  let marginDeg;
  if (d <= 12000) marginDeg = 0.035;       // ~3.5 km
  else if (d <= 25000) marginDeg = 0.055;  // ~5.5 km
  else if (d <= 45000) marginDeg = 0.085;  // ~8.5 km
  else marginDeg = 0.12;

  return {
    minLat:Math.min(sLat,eLat) - marginDeg,
    maxLat:Math.max(sLat,eLat) + marginDeg,
    minLon:Math.min(sLon,eLon) - marginDeg,
    maxLon:Math.max(sLon,eLon) + marginDeg,
  };
}

async function routeCustomCarNodeOnlyPrepared({
  graph,
  startNode,
  goalNode,
  startPoint,
  endPoint,
  startEdgeSnap,
  endEdgeSnap,
  trafficPromise,
  useTraffic,
  debug,
  signalResolver,
  usePostSignals,
  t0,
}) {
  const shortMediumEnvelope = customCarBuildShortMediumEnvelope(startPoint, endPoint);

  // Candidate A: normal baseline route.
  const baselinePass = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
    graph,
    startNode,
    goalNode,
    trafficSnapshot:null,
    useLiveTraffic:false,
    corridorCellSet:null,
    corridorM:null,
    maxExpansions:Math.min(CUSTOM_CAR_BASELINE_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS, 60000),
    heuristicWeight:CUSTOM_CAR_BASELINE_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT,
    baselineProfile:"normal",
    geoEnvelope:shortMediumEnvelope,
  });

  if (!baselinePass.ok) {
    return {
      ok:false,
      version:"3.7.1",
      status:503,
      error:baselinePass.error || "baseline_route_failed",
      phase:"baseline-pass",
      expansions:baselinePass.expansions,
      relaxed:baselinePass.relaxed,
      searchStateMode:"node-only-multi-candidate",
      loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
      loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
      missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      ts:Date.now(),
    };
  }

  const allowExtraCandidates =
    baselinePass.expansions <= CUSTOM_CAR_SHORT_EXTRA_CANDIDATE_BASELINE_EXPANSIONS;

  // Candidate B: deliberately expose a longer major-road / expressway option.
  // These factors are NOT used to choose the winner; they only create diversity.
  const expresswayPass = allowExtraCandidates
    ? await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
    graph,
    startNode,
    goalNode,
    trafficSnapshot:null,
    useLiveTraffic:false,
    corridorCellSet:null,
    corridorM:null,
    maxExpansions:Math.min(CUSTOM_CAR_EXPRESSWAY_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS, 45000),
    heuristicWeight:CUSTOM_CAR_EXPRESSWAY_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT,
    baselineProfile:"expressway",
    geoEnvelope:shortMediumEnvelope,
  })
    : null;

  // Traffic fetch has been running since the route request started.
  const trafficSnapshot = useTraffic
    ? await trafficPromise
    : {
        ok:false,
        degraded:true,
        reason:"traffic_disabled",
        tdxSegments:[],
        hereSegments:[],
        tdxIndex:null,
        hereIndex:null,
        matchCache:new Map(),
        chainAliasCache:new Map(),
        chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
        elapsedMs:0,
        providerStats:{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
        areaSummary:null,
      };

  // Candidate C: traffic-aware refinement around the normal route.
  let livePass = null;
  let liveRetry = null;
  let corridorM = null;
  let corridorCellSet = null;

  if (allowExtraCandidates && useTraffic && trafficSnapshot?.ok) {
    corridorM = customCarTrafficCorridorWidthM(baselinePass.route.distanceM);
    corridorCellSet = customCarBuildCorridorCellSet(
      baselinePass.route.geometry,
      corridorM
    );

    livePass = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
      graph,
      startNode,
      goalNode,
      trafficSnapshot,
      useLiveTraffic:true,
      corridorCellSet,
      corridorM,
      maxExpansions:Math.min(CUSTOM_CAR_LIVE_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS, 40000),
      heuristicWeight:CUSTOM_CAR_LIVE_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT,
      baselineProfile:"normal",
      geoEnvelope:shortMediumEnvelope,
    });

    if (!livePass.ok && livePass.error !== "graph_tile_load_limit") {
      const widerCorridorM = Math.min(
        CUSTOM_CAR_TRAFFIC_CORRIDOR_MAX_M * 1.6,
        corridorM * 1.8
      );
      const widerSet = customCarBuildCorridorCellSet(
        baselinePass.route.geometry,
        widerCorridorM
      );

      liveRetry = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
        graph,
        startNode,
        goalNode,
        trafficSnapshot,
        useLiveTraffic:true,
        corridorCellSet:widerSet,
        corridorM:widerCorridorM,
        maxExpansions:Math.min(CUSTOM_CAR_LIVE_PASS_MAX_EXPANSIONS, 40000),
        heuristicWeight:CUSTOM_CAR_LIVE_HEURISTIC_WEIGHT,
        baselineProfile:"normal",
        geoEnvelope:shortMediumEnvelope,
      });

      if (liveRetry.ok) {
        corridorM = widerCorridorM;
        corridorCellSet = widerSet;
      }
    }
  }

  const rawCandidates = [];
  customCarPushDistinctCandidate(rawCandidates, {
    id:"normal",
    generation:"baseline-normal",
    route:baselinePass.route,
    search:{
      expansions:baselinePass.expansions,
      relaxed:baselinePass.relaxed,
      searchCostSec:baselinePass.durationSec,
    },
  });

  if (expresswayPass?.ok) {
    customCarPushDistinctCandidate(rawCandidates, {
      id:"expressway",
      generation:"expressway-biased-candidate",
      route:expresswayPass.route,
      search:{
        expansions:expresswayPass.expansions,
        relaxed:expresswayPass.relaxed,
        searchCostSec:expresswayPass.durationSec,
      },
    });
  }

  const bestLive = liveRetry?.ok ? liveRetry : (livePass?.ok ? livePass : null);
  if (bestLive) {
    customCarPushDistinctCandidate(rawCandidates, {
      id:"live-corridor",
      generation:"traffic-corridor",
      route:bestLive.route,
      search:{
        expansions:bestLive.expansions,
        relaxed:bestLive.relaxed,
        searchCostSec:bestLive.durationSec,
        corridorM:corridorM == null ? null : Math.round(corridorM),
        corridorCellCount:corridorCellSet?.size || 0,
      },
    });
  }

  // Full traffic matcher + signals are applied only to completed routes.
  const scored = [];
  for (const candidate of rawCandidates) {
    scored.push(await customCarScoreCompletedCandidate({
      candidate,
      graph,
      trafficSnapshot,
      signalResolver,
      usePostSignals,
      debug,
    }));
  }

  scored.sort((a,b) => a.finalEtaSec - b.finalEtaSec);
  const best = scored[0];

  if (!best) {
    return {
      ok:false,
      version:"3.7.1",
      status:503,
      error:"no_candidate_route",
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      ts:Date.now(),
    };
  }

  const route = best.route;
  const routeTraffic = best.routeTraffic;
  const postSignal = best.postSignal;

  const candidateRoutes = scored.map((c, index) => ({
    rank:index + 1,
    id:c.id,
    generation:c.generation,
    selected:c === best,
    distanceM:Math.round(c.route.distanceM),
    trafficDrivingSec:Math.round(c.trafficSec),
    postSignalDelaySec:Math.round(Number(c.postSignal?.totalSec) || 0),
    finalEtaSec:Math.round(c.finalEtaSec),
    finalEtaMin:Math.round(c.finalEtaSec / 6) / 10,
    trafficCoverageDistanceRatio:
      Math.round(Number(c.routeTraffic?.stats?.trafficCoverageDistanceRatio || 0) * 1000) / 1000,
    fallbackBaselineSec:
      Math.round(Number(c.routeTraffic?.stats?.fallbackBaselineSec || 0)),
    signalCount:Number(c.postSignal?.uniqueSignals || 0),
    generationSearch:c.search,
  }));

  return {
    ok:true,
    version:"3.7.1",
    engine:"custom-car-a-star-v3.7.1-adaptive-long-mode",
    trafficMode:useTraffic
      ? (trafficSnapshot?.ok ? "HERE_TDX_POST_ROUTE_SCORING" : "BASELINE_DEGRADED")
      : "BASELINE_ONLY",
    signalTurnMode:usePostSignals
      ? "POST_ROUTE_R2_SIGNALS"
      : "DISABLED",
    graphProvisioningMode:"r2-only",
    start:startPoint,
    end:endPoint,
    snapMode:"edge-virtual-node",
    snappedStart:{
      id:startNode.id,
      lat:startNode.lat,
      lon:startNode.lon,
      distanceM:startEdgeSnap.distanceM,
      road:startEdgeSnap.edge.name || null,
      highway:startEdgeSnap.edge.highway || null,
      osmWayId:startEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId || null
    },
    snappedEnd:{
      id:goalNode.id,
      lat:goalNode.lat,
      lon:goalNode.lon,
      distanceM:endEdgeSnap.distanceM,
      road:endEdgeSnap.edge.name || null,
      highway:endEdgeSnap.edge.highway || null,
      osmWayId:endEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId || null
    },
    selectedCandidate:best.id,
    candidateRoutes,
    distanceM:Math.round(route.distanceM),
    drivingDurationSec:Math.round(best.trafficSec),
    intersectionDelaySec:Math.round(Number(postSignal.totalSec) || 0),
    durationSec:Math.round(best.finalEtaSec),
    geometry:route.geometry,
    trafficSnapshot:{
      ok:trafficSnapshot?.ok === true,
      degraded:trafficSnapshot?.degraded === true,
      reason:trafficSnapshot?.reason || null,
      elapsedMs:trafficSnapshot?.elapsedMs ?? null,
      providerStats:trafficSnapshot?.providerStats || {tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
      hereChainAliasStats:trafficSnapshot?.chainAliasStats || {evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
      area:trafficSnapshot?.areaSummary || null
    },
    trafficRouteStats:routeTraffic.stats,
    postRouteIntersectionDelay:postSignal,
    routeEdgeDebug:debug ? routeTraffic.edgeDebug : undefined,
    pathNodeCount:route.ids.length,
    pathEdgeCount:route.edges.length,
    expansions:
      baselinePass.expansions +
      (expresswayPass?.expansions || 0) +
      (livePass?.expansions || 0) +
      (liveRetry?.expansions || 0),
    relaxed:
      baselinePass.relaxed +
      (expresswayPass?.relaxed || 0) +
      (livePass?.relaxed || 0) +
      (liveRetry?.relaxed || 0),
    searchStateMode:"node-only-multi-candidate",
    shortMediumEnvelope,
    extraCandidatePassesEnabled:allowExtraCandidates,
    loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
    loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
    autoBuildCount:graph.autoBuildCount,
    autoBuiltGraphTiles:graph.autoBuiltKeys,
    autoBuildFailures:graph.autoBuildFailures,
    missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
    elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
    ts:Date.now(),
  };
}


const CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_KEY = "graph/v4/TW/overlay.json";
const CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_MAX_SNAP_M = 30000;
const CUSTOM_CAR_V4_DETAIL_MAX_EXPANSIONS = 70000;
let CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_CACHE = null;
let CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_PROMISE = null;

async function customCarV4LoadOverlay(env) {
  if (CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_CACHE) return CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_CACHE;
  if (CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_PROMISE) return await CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_PROMISE;

  CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_PROMISE = (async () => {
    const obj = await env.ROAD_GRAPH.get(CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_KEY);
    if (!obj) throw new Error("v4_overlay_missing");
    const x = await obj.json();
    if (
      !x || Number(x.schemaVersion) !== 4 ||
      !Array.isArray(x.latE5) || !Array.isArray(x.lonE5) ||
      !Array.isArray(x.firstEdge) || !Array.isArray(x.edgeTo) ||
      !Array.isArray(x.edgeCostDs) || !Array.isArray(x.edgeShapesE5)
    ) throw new Error("v4_overlay_invalid");
    if (x.firstEdge.length !== x.latE5.length + 1) throw new Error("v4_overlay_csr_invalid");
    CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_CACHE = x;
    return x;
  })();

  try {
    return await CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_PROMISE;
  } finally {
    CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_PROMISE = null;
  }
}

function customCarV4OverlayCellId(overlay, lat, lon) {
  const deg = Number(overlay?.cellDeg) || 0.10;
  const y = Math.floor((Number(lat) + 90) / deg);
  const x = Math.floor((Number(lon) + 180) / deg);
  return `${y}:${x}`;
}

function customCarV4NodePoint(overlay, idx) {
  return [Number(overlay.latE5[idx]) / 1e5, Number(overlay.lonE5[idx]) / 1e5];
}

function customCarV4NearestOverlayNode(overlay, point) {
  const deg = Number(overlay?.cellDeg) || 0.10;
  const baseY = Math.floor((Number(point[0]) + 90) / deg);
  const baseX = Math.floor((Number(point[1]) + 180) / deg);
  const index = overlay.cellIndex || {};
  let best = null;

  for (let radius = 0; radius <= 4; radius++) {
    let foundThisRadius = false;
    for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++) {
      for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
        if (radius > 0 && Math.abs(dx) !== radius && Math.abs(dy) !== radius) continue;
        const ids = index[`${baseY + dy}:${baseX + dx}`];
        if (!Array.isArray(ids)) continue;
        for (const rawIdx of ids) {
          const i = Number(rawIdx);
          if (!Number.isInteger(i) || i < 0 || i >= overlay.latE5.length) continue;
          const p = customCarV4NodePoint(overlay, i);
          const d = haversineM(point, p);
          if (!best || d < best.distanceM) best = { index:i, point:p, distanceM:d };
          foundThisRadius = true;
        }
      }
    }
    if (foundThisRadius && best && best.distanceM < Math.max(3500, radius * deg * 111000 * 0.75)) break;
  }
  return best;
}

function customCarV4OverlayHeuristicSec(overlay, aIdx, bIdx) {
  const a = customCarV4NodePoint(overlay, aIdx);
  const b = customCarV4NodePoint(overlay, bIdx);
  return haversineM(a, b) / (130000 / 3600);
}

function customCarV4OverlaySearch(overlay, startIdx, goalIdx) {
  const n = overlay.latE5.length;
  if (!(n > 0) || startIdx < 0 || goalIdx < 0 || startIdx >= n || goalIdx >= n) {
    return { ok:false, error:"v4_overlay_bad_endpoint", expansions:0 };
  }

  const g = new Float64Array(n);
  g.fill(Infinity);
  const cameNode = new Int32Array(n);
  const cameEdge = new Int32Array(n);
  cameNode.fill(-1);
  cameEdge.fill(-1);
  const closed = new Uint8Array(n);
  const open = new CustomCarMinHeap();

  g[startIdx] = 0;
  open.push({ id:startIdx, g:0, f:customCarV4OverlayHeuristicSec(overlay, startIdx, goalIdx) });
  let expansions = 0;
  let relaxed = 0;

  while (open.size) {
    const cur = open.pop();
    if (!cur) break;
    const u = Number(cur.id);
    if (closed[u]) continue;
    if (cur.g > g[u] + 1e-9) continue;
    if (u === goalIdx) {
      const nodes = [];
      const edges = [];
      let x = goalIdx;
      while (x >= 0) {
        nodes.push(x);
        const e = cameEdge[x];
        if (e >= 0) edges.push(e);
        if (x === startIdx) break;
        x = cameNode[x];
      }
      if (nodes[nodes.length - 1] !== startIdx) return {ok:false,error:"v4_overlay_reconstruct_failed",expansions,relaxed};
      nodes.reverse(); edges.reverse();
      return { ok:true, nodes, edges, costSec:g[goalIdx], expansions, relaxed };
    }
    closed[u] = 1;
    expansions++;
    const e0 = Number(overlay.firstEdge[u]);
    const e1 = Number(overlay.firstEdge[u+1]);
    for (let e = e0; e < e1; e++) {
      const v = Number(overlay.edgeTo[e]);
      if (closed[v]) continue;
      const sec = Number(overlay.edgeCostDs[e]) / 10;
      if (!(sec > 0)) continue;
      const ng = g[u] + sec;
      if (ng >= g[v] - 1e-9) continue;
      g[v] = ng;
      cameNode[v] = u;
      cameEdge[v] = e;
      relaxed++;
      open.push({ id:v, g:ng, f:ng + customCarV4OverlayHeuristicSec(overlay, v, goalIdx) * 1.08 });
    }
  }
  return { ok:false, error:"v4_overlay_route_not_found", expansions, relaxed };
}

function customCarV4OverlayGeometry(overlay, result) {
  const out = [];
  const push = p => {
    if (!Array.isArray(p) || p.length < 2) return;
    const lat = Number(p[0]), lon = Number(p[1]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) return;
    const last = out[out.length - 1];
    if (last && Math.abs(last[0]-lat) < 1e-8 && Math.abs(last[1]-lon) < 1e-8) return;
    out.push([lat, lon]);
  };

  if (result?.nodes?.length) push(customCarV4NodePoint(overlay, result.nodes[0]));
  for (const edgeIdx of result?.edges || []) {
    const shp = overlay.edgeShapesE5?.[edgeIdx];
    if (Array.isArray(shp) && shp.length >= 4) {
      for (let i = 0; i + 1 < shp.length; i += 2) push([Number(shp[i])/1e5, Number(shp[i+1])/1e5]);
    } else {
      const to = Number(overlay.edgeTo?.[edgeIdx]);
      if (Number.isInteger(to)) push(customCarV4NodePoint(overlay, to));
    }
  }
  return out;
}

async function routeCustomCarHierarchicalV4(env, input, ctx = {}) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const startPoint = parseRoutePointInput(input?.start ?? input?.origin);
  const endPoint = parseRoutePointInput(input?.end ?? input?.destination);
  if (!startPoint) return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:400,error:"invalid_start"};
  if (!endPoint) return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:400,error:"invalid_end"};

  const useTraffic = customCarBool(input?.traffic, true);
  const debug = customCarBool(input?.debug, false);
  const usePostSignals = customCarBool(input?.postsignals, true);
  const signalResolver = new CustomCarSignalResolver(env);

  const overlay = await customCarV4LoadOverlay(env);
  const startOverlay = customCarV4NearestOverlayNode(overlay, startPoint);
  const endOverlay = customCarV4NearestOverlayNode(overlay, endPoint);
  if (!startOverlay || startOverlay.distanceM > CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_MAX_SNAP_M) {
    return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:503,error:"v4_start_overlay_too_far",distanceM:startOverlay?.distanceM ?? null,elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
  }
  if (!endOverlay || endOverlay.distanceM > CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_MAX_SNAP_M) {
    return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:503,error:"v4_end_overlay_too_far",distanceM:endOverlay?.distanceM ?? null,elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
  }

  const overlayRoute = customCarV4OverlaySearch(overlay, startOverlay.index, endOverlay.index);
  if (!overlayRoute.ok) return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:503,error:overlayRoute.error,phase:"overlay",overlayExpansions:overlayRoute.expansions,elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};

  const overlayGeometry = customCarV4OverlayGeometry(overlay, overlayRoute);
  const guideGeometry = [startPoint, ...overlayGeometry, endPoint];
  const directDistanceM = haversineM(startPoint, endPoint);
  const corridorM = directDistanceM <= 15000 ? 4500 : (directDistanceM <= 60000 ? 6500 : 9000);
  const corridorCellSet = customCarBuildCorridorCellSet(guideGeometry, corridorM);

  const graph = new CustomCarGraphStore(env);
  await Promise.all([graph.ensureCenterTile(startPoint,false), graph.ensureCenterTile(endPoint,false)]);
  await Promise.all([graph.loadAroundPoint(startPoint), graph.loadAroundPoint(endPoint)]);

  const startEdgeSnap = customCarNearestLoadedEdge(graph, startPoint);
  const endEdgeSnap = customCarNearestLoadedEdge(graph, endPoint);
  if (!startEdgeSnap || startEdgeSnap.distanceM > CUSTOM_CAR_EDGE_SNAP_MAX_M) {
    return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:409,error:"start_graph_missing_or_too_far",nearestDistanceM:startEdgeSnap?.distanceM ?? null,elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
  }
  if (!endEdgeSnap || endEdgeSnap.distanceM > CUSTOM_CAR_EDGE_SNAP_MAX_M) {
    return {ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:409,error:"end_graph_missing_or_too_far",nearestDistanceM:endEdgeSnap?.distanceM ?? null,elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
  }

  const startNode = customCarInstallVirtualSnap(graph,startEdgeSnap,"start","__custom_car_start__");
  const goalNode = customCarInstallVirtualSnap(graph,endEdgeSnap,"end","__custom_car_end__");

  const detailed = await customCarNodeOnlySearchCore({
    graph,
    startNode,
    goalNode,
    trafficSnapshot:null,
    useLiveTraffic:false,
    corridorCellSet,
    corridorM,
    maxExpansions:CUSTOM_CAR_V4_DETAIL_MAX_EXPANSIONS,
    heuristicWeight:1.22,
    baselineProfile:"normal",
  });

  if (!detailed.ok) {
    return {
      ok:false,version:"4.0.0",status:503,error:detailed.error,phase:"detail-corridor",
      overlayExpansions:overlayRoute.expansions,detailExpansions:detailed.expansions,
      corridorM,corridorCellCount:corridorCellSet.size,
      loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
      missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys],startPoint,endPoint),
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,ts:Date.now()
    };
  }

  const route = detailed.route;
  const trafficSnapshot = useTraffic
    ? (route.distanceM >= CUSTOM_CAR_LONG_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD_M
        ? await buildCustomCarTrafficSnapshotAlongRoute(env,ctx,route)
        : await buildCustomCarTrafficSnapshot(env,ctx,startPoint,endPoint))
    : {ok:false,degraded:true,reason:"traffic_disabled",tdxSegments:[],hereSegments:[],tdxIndex:null,hereIndex:null,matchCache:new Map(),chainAliasCache:new Map(),chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},elapsedMs:0,providerStats:{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},areaSummary:null};

  const scored = await customCarScoreCompletedCandidate({
    candidate:{id:"hierarchical",generation:"v4-overlay-corridor",route,search:{expansions:detailed.expansions,relaxed:detailed.relaxed,searchCostSec:detailed.durationSec}},
    graph,trafficSnapshot,signalResolver,usePostSignals,debug
  });

  return {
    ok:true,
    version:"4.0.0",
    engine:"custom-car-hierarchical-overlay-v4.0.0",
    hierarchyMode:"precomputed-major-road-overlay + single-detail-corridor",
    trafficMode:useTraffic ? (trafficSnapshot?.ok ? "HERE_TDX_POST_ROUTE_SCORING" : "BASELINE_DEGRADED") : "BASELINE_ONLY",
    signalTurnMode:usePostSignals ? "POST_ROUTE_R2_SIGNALS" : "DISABLED",
    graphProvisioningMode:"r2-v1-detail + r2-v4-overlay",
    start:startPoint,end:endPoint,
    snapMode:"edge-virtual-node",
    snappedStart:{id:startNode.id,lat:startNode.lat,lon:startNode.lon,distanceM:startEdgeSnap.distanceM,road:startEdgeSnap.edge.name||null,highway:startEdgeSnap.edge.highway||null,osmWayId:startEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId||null},
    snappedEnd:{id:goalNode.id,lat:goalNode.lat,lon:goalNode.lon,distanceM:endEdgeSnap.distanceM,road:endEdgeSnap.edge.name||null,highway:endEdgeSnap.edge.highway||null,osmWayId:endEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId||null},
    selectedCandidate:"hierarchical",
    candidateRoutes:[{rank:1,id:"hierarchical",generation:"v4-overlay-corridor",selected:true,distanceM:Math.round(route.distanceM),trafficDrivingSec:Math.round(scored.trafficSec),postSignalDelaySec:Math.round(Number(scored.postSignal?.totalSec)||0),finalEtaSec:Math.round(scored.finalEtaSec),finalEtaMin:Math.round(scored.finalEtaSec/6)/10,trafficCoverageDistanceRatio:Math.round(Number(scored.routeTraffic?.stats?.trafficCoverageDistanceRatio||0)*1000)/1000,fallbackBaselineSec:Math.round(Number(scored.routeTraffic?.stats?.fallbackBaselineSec||0)),signalCount:Number(scored.postSignal?.uniqueSignals||0),generationSearch:{overlayExpansions:overlayRoute.expansions,overlayRelaxed:overlayRoute.relaxed,detailExpansions:detailed.expansions,detailRelaxed:detailed.relaxed,detailSearchCostSec:detailed.durationSec,corridorM,corridorCellCount:corridorCellSet.size}}],
    distanceM:Math.round(route.distanceM),
    drivingDurationSec:Math.round(scored.trafficSec),
    intersectionDelaySec:Math.round(Number(scored.postSignal?.totalSec)||0),
    durationSec:Math.round(scored.finalEtaSec),
    geometry:route.geometry,
    overlay:{key:CUSTOM_CAR_V4_OVERLAY_KEY,nodeCount:overlay.latE5.length,edgeCount:overlay.edgeTo.length,startSnapM:Math.round(startOverlay.distanceM),endSnapM:Math.round(endOverlay.distanceM),expansions:overlayRoute.expansions,relaxed:overlayRoute.relaxed,guidePointCount:overlayGeometry.length},
    trafficSnapshot:{ok:trafficSnapshot?.ok===true,degraded:trafficSnapshot?.degraded===true,reason:trafficSnapshot?.reason||null,elapsedMs:trafficSnapshot?.elapsedMs??null,providerStats:trafficSnapshot?.providerStats||{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},hereChainAliasStats:trafficSnapshot?.chainAliasStats||{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},area:trafficSnapshot?.areaSummary||null},
    trafficRouteStats:scored.routeTraffic.stats,
    postRouteIntersectionDelay:scored.postSignal,
    routeEdgeDebug:debug ? scored.routeTraffic.edgeDebug : undefined,
    pathNodeCount:route.ids.length,pathEdgeCount:route.edges.length,
    expansions:detailed.expansions,relaxed:detailed.relaxed,
    searchStateMode:"v4-overlay-then-single-node-detail",
    corridorM,corridorCellCount:corridorCellSet.size,
    loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
    missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys],startPoint,endPoint),
    elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,ts:Date.now()
  };
}

async function routeCustomCarAStar(env, input, ctx = {}) {
  if (String(input?.engine || "").toLowerCase() === "v3") {
    return await routeCustomCarAStarV371(env,input,ctx);
  }
  try {
    return await routeCustomCarHierarchicalV4(env,input,ctx);
  } catch (err) {
    const reason = String(err?.message || err);
    if (reason === "v4_overlay_missing" || reason === "v4_overlay_invalid" || reason === "v4_overlay_csr_invalid") {
      const fallback = await routeCustomCarAStarV371(env,input,ctx);
      if (fallback && typeof fallback === "object") {
        fallback.v4Fallback = true;
        fallback.v4FallbackReason = reason;
      }
      return fallback;
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

async function routeCustomCarAStarV371(env, input, ctx = {}) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const startPoint = parseRoutePointInput(input?.start ?? input?.origin);
  const endPoint = parseRoutePointInput(input?.end ?? input?.destination);

  if (!startPoint) return { ok:false, version:"3.7.1", status:400, error:"invalid_start" };
  if (!endPoint) return { ok:false, version:"3.7.1", status:400, error:"invalid_end" };

  const graph = new CustomCarGraphStore(env);
  const directDistanceM = haversineM(startPoint, endPoint);
  const longDistanceMode = customCarShouldUseLongDistanceMode(startPoint, endPoint, directDistanceM);
  const useTraffic = customCarBool(input?.traffic, true);
  const debug = customCarBool(input?.debug, false);
  const useSignals = customCarBool(input?.signals, false);
  const usePostSignals = customCarBool(input?.postsignals, true);
  const signalResolver = new CustomCarSignalResolver(env);

  // Traffic, graph and the first SIGNALS neighborhoods are fetched in parallel.
  const trafficPromise = (useTraffic && !longDistanceMode)
    ? buildCustomCarTrafficSnapshot(env, ctx, startPoint, endPoint)
        .catch(err => ({
          ok:false,
          degraded:true,
          reason:String(err?.message || err),
          tdxSegments:[],
          hereSegments:[],
          tdxIndex:null,
          hereIndex:null,
          matchCache:new Map(),
          chainAliasCache:new Map(),
          chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
          elapsedMs:null,
          providerStats:{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
          areaSummary:null,
        }))
    : Promise.resolve({
        ok:false,
        degraded:true,
        reason:"traffic_disabled",
        tdxSegments:[],
        hereSegments:[],
        tdxIndex:null,
        hereIndex:null,
        matchCache:new Map(),
        chainAliasCache:new Map(),
        chainAliasStats:{evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
        elapsedMs:0,
        providerStats:{tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
        areaSummary:null,
      });

  const signalWarmPromise = useSignals
    ? signalResolver.prefetchPoints([startPoint, endPoint]).catch(() => {})
    : Promise.resolve();

  await Promise.all([
    graph.ensureCenterTile(startPoint, false),
    graph.ensureCenterTile(endPoint, false),
  ]);

  await Promise.all([
    graph.loadAroundPoint(startPoint),
    graph.loadAroundPoint(endPoint),
  ]);

  await signalWarmPromise;

  const startEdgeSnap = customCarNearestLoadedEdge(graph, startPoint);
  const endEdgeSnap = customCarNearestLoadedEdge(graph, endPoint);

  if (!startEdgeSnap || startEdgeSnap.distanceM > CUSTOM_CAR_EDGE_SNAP_MAX_M) {
    return {
      ok:false, version:"3.7.1", status:409, error:"start_graph_missing_or_too_far",
      start:startPoint, nearestDistanceM:startEdgeSnap?.distanceM ?? null,
      snapMode:"edge",
      missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0, ts:Date.now()
    };
  }

  if (!endEdgeSnap || endEdgeSnap.distanceM > CUSTOM_CAR_EDGE_SNAP_MAX_M) {
    return {
      ok:false, version:"3.7.1", status:409, error:"end_graph_missing_or_too_far",
      end:endPoint, nearestDistanceM:endEdgeSnap?.distanceM ?? null,
      snapMode:"edge",
      missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0, ts:Date.now()
    };
  }

  const startNode = customCarInstallVirtualSnap(graph, startEdgeSnap, "start", "__custom_car_start__");
  const goalNode = customCarInstallVirtualSnap(graph, endEdgeSnap, "end", "__custom_car_end__");

  if (longDistanceMode) {
    return routeCustomCarLongDistancePrepared({
      env,
      ctx,
      graph,
      startNode,
      goalNode,
      startPoint,
      endPoint,
      startEdgeSnap,
      endEdgeSnap,
      useTraffic,
      debug,
      t0,
    });
  }

  // Short/medium routes keep v3.3 multi-candidate behavior.
  // edge is irrelevant. Use one state per node to avoid state explosion.
  if (!useSignals) {
    return routeCustomCarNodeOnlyPrepared({
      graph,
      startNode,
      goalNode,
      startPoint,
      endPoint,
      startEdgeSnap,
      endEdgeSnap,
      trafficPromise,
      useTraffic,
      debug,
      signalResolver,
      usePostSignals,
      t0,
    });
  }

  // Signal-aware mode keeps state = (node, incoming edge).
  const trafficSnapshot = await trafficPromise;
  // leaving an intersection depends on how we entered it.
  const startStateKey = customCarSearchStateKey(startNode.id, null);
  const open = new CustomCarMinHeap();
  const records = new Map();
  const came = new Map();
  const closed = new Set();

  records.set(startStateKey, {
    stateKey:startStateKey,
    nodeId:startNode.id,
    incomingEdge:null,
    drivingSec:0,
    rawIntersectionSec:0,
    g:0,
  });

  open.push({
    id:startStateKey,
    nodeId:startNode.id,
    f:customCarHeuristicSec(startNode, goalNode),
    g:0,
  });

  let expansions = 0;
  let relaxed = 0;

  while (open.size) {
    const curEntry = open.pop();
    if (!curEntry) break;

    const rec = records.get(curEntry.id);
    if (!rec) continue;
    if (curEntry.g > rec.g + 1e-9) continue;
    if (closed.has(curEntry.id)) continue;

    const current = graph.nodeMap.get(String(rec.nodeId));
    if (!current) continue;

    if (String(rec.nodeId) === String(goalNode.id)) {
      const route = reconstructCustomCarStateRoute(curEntry.id, came, graph);
      const routeTraffic = summarizeCustomCarRouteTraffic(route.edges, graph, trafficSnapshot, debug);
      const intersectionDelay = useSignals
        ? summarizeCustomCarIntersectionDelay(route, rec, signalResolver, debug)
        : {
            totalSec:0,
            rawTotalSec:0,
            capSec:0,
            capApplied:false,
            delayScale:1,
            maxDrivingFraction:SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION,
            events:[],
            signalLookup:signalResolver.snapshot(),
            disabled:true,
          };

      return {
        ok:true,
        version:"3.7.1",
        engine:"custom-car-a-star-v3.7.1-adaptive-long-mode",
        trafficMode:useTraffic
          ? (trafficSnapshot?.ok ? "HERE_TDX_LIVE" : "BASELINE_DEGRADED")
          : "BASELINE_ONLY",
        signalTurnMode:useSignals ? "R2_RESIDUAL_IN_ASTAR" : "DISABLED",
        graphProvisioningMode:"r2-only",
        start:startPoint,
        end:endPoint,
        snapMode:"edge-virtual-node",
        snappedStart:{
          id:startNode.id,
          lat:startNode.lat,
          lon:startNode.lon,
          distanceM:startEdgeSnap.distanceM,
          road:startEdgeSnap.edge.name || null,
          highway:startEdgeSnap.edge.highway || null,
          osmWayId:startEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId || null
        },
        snappedEnd:{
          id:goalNode.id,
          lat:goalNode.lat,
          lon:goalNode.lon,
          distanceM:endEdgeSnap.distanceM,
          road:endEdgeSnap.edge.name || null,
          highway:endEdgeSnap.edge.highway || null,
          osmWayId:endEdgeSnap.edge.osmWayId || null
        },
        distanceM:Math.round(route.distanceM),
        drivingDurationSec:Math.round(rec.drivingSec),
        intersectionDelaySec:Math.round(intersectionDelay.totalSec),
        durationSec:Math.round(rec.g),
        geometry:route.geometry,
        trafficSnapshot:{
          ok:trafficSnapshot?.ok === true,
          degraded:trafficSnapshot?.degraded === true,
          reason:trafficSnapshot?.reason || null,
          elapsedMs:trafficSnapshot?.elapsedMs ?? null,
          providerStats:trafficSnapshot?.providerStats || {tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
          hereChainAliasStats:trafficSnapshot?.chainAliasStats || {evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
          area:trafficSnapshot?.areaSummary || null
        },
        trafficRouteStats:routeTraffic.stats,
        intersectionDelay,
        routeEdgeDebug:debug ? routeTraffic.edgeDebug : undefined,
        pathNodeCount:route.ids.length,
        pathEdgeCount:route.edges.length,
        expansions,
        relaxed,
        searchStateMode:"node+incoming-edge",
        loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
        loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
        autoBuildCount:graph.autoBuildCount,
        autoBuiltGraphTiles:graph.autoBuiltKeys,
        autoBuildFailures:graph.autoBuildFailures,
        missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
        elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
        ts:Date.now(),
      };
    }

    closed.add(curEntry.id);
    expansions++;

    if (expansions > CUSTOM_CAR_MAX_EXPANSIONS) {
      return {
        ok:false, version:"3.7.1", status:503, error:"astar_expansion_limit",
        expansions, loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
        signalLookup:signalResolver.snapshot(),
        missingGraphTiles:[...graph.missingKeys],
        elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0, ts:Date.now()
      };
    }

    let outgoing;
    try {
      outgoing = await graph.ensureOutgoingForNode(current);
    } catch (err) {
      if (String(err?.message || err) === "custom_car_tile_load_limit") {
        return {
          ok:false, version:"3.7.1", status:503, error:"graph_tile_load_limit",
          expansions, loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
          signalLookup:signalResolver.snapshot(),
          autoBuildCount:graph.autoBuildCount,
          autoBuiltGraphTiles:graph.autoBuiltKeys,
          autoBuildFailures:graph.autoBuildFailures,
          missingGraphTiles:prioritizeMissingGraphTiles([...graph.missingKeys], startPoint, endPoint),
          elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0, ts:Date.now()
        };
      }
      throw err;
    }

    // Resolve the physical signal status once for this graph node. It is cached
    // by node id, even when several incoming-edge states reach the same node.
    let signalInfo = {hasSignal:null,status:"disabled",nearestM:null};
    if (useSignals && rec.incomingEdge && !customCarIsExpresswayEdge(rec.incomingEdge)) {
      signalInfo = await signalResolver.resolveNode(current);
    }

    for (const edge of outgoing) {
      const next = graph.nodeMap.get(String(edge.to));
      if (!next) continue;

      const liveCost = customCarLiveEdgeCost(edge, current, next, trafficSnapshot, graph);
      if (!Number.isFinite(liveCost.sec)) continue; // hard closure

      const turnEvent = useSignals
        ? customCarIntersectionRule({
            incomingEdge:rec.incomingEdge,
            outgoingEdge:edge,
            currentNode:current,
            graph,
            signalInfo,
            outgoingTrafficCost:liveCost,
          })
        : {
            rawSec:0,
            kind:"disabled",
            profile:"none",
            isTurn:false,
            turnAngleDeg:0,
            hasSignal:null,
            signalStatus:"disabled",
          };

      const nextDrivingSec = rec.drivingSec + liveCost.sec;
      const nextRawIntersectionSec = rec.rawIntersectionSec + (Number(turnEvent.rawSec) || 0);
      const nextEffectiveIntersectionSec = useSignals
        ? customCarEffectiveIntersectionDelaySec(nextDrivingSec, nextRawIntersectionSec)
        : 0;
      const tentative = nextDrivingSec + nextEffectiveIntersectionSec;

      const nextStateKey = customCarSearchStateKey(next.id, edge);
      const prev = records.get(nextStateKey);
      if (prev && tentative >= prev.g - 1e-9) continue;

      records.set(nextStateKey, {
        stateKey:nextStateKey,
        nodeId:next.id,
        incomingEdge:edge,
        drivingSec:nextDrivingSec,
        rawIntersectionSec:nextRawIntersectionSec,
        g:tentative,
      });

      came.set(nextStateKey, {
        prevStateKey:curEntry.id,
        edge,
        turnEvent:{
          ...turnEvent,
          nodeId:String(current.id),
          lat:Number(current.lat),
          lon:Number(current.lon),
          incomingRoad:rec.incomingEdge?.name || null,
          outgoingRoad:edge?.name || null,
          incomingHighway:rec.incomingEdge?.highway || null,
          outgoingHighway:edge?.highway || null,
        },
      });

      relaxed++;

      open.push({
        id:nextStateKey,
        nodeId:next.id,
        g:tentative,
        f:tentative + customCarHeuristicSec(next, goalNode),
      });
    }
  }

  return {
    ok:false,
    version:"3.7.1",
    status:409,
    error:"route_not_found_with_loaded_graph",
    message:"A* exhausted the prebuilt R2 graph. Route requests do not build graph tiles; build the first prioritized missingGraphTiles entry, then retry.",
    start:startPoint,
    end:endPoint,
    snappedStart:{id:startNode.id,distanceM:startEdgeSnap.distanceM},
    snappedEnd:{id:goalNode.id,distanceM:endEdgeSnap.distanceM},
    expansions,
    relaxed,
    searchStateMode:"node+incoming-edge",
    loadedGraphTileCount:graph.loadedKeys.length,
    loadedGraphTiles:graph.loadedKeys,
    trafficSnapshot:{
      ok:trafficSnapshot?.ok === true,
      degraded:trafficSnapshot?.degraded === true,
      reason:trafficSnapshot?.reason || null,
      elapsedMs:trafficSnapshot?.elapsedMs ?? null,
      providerStats:trafficSnapshot?.providerStats || {tdx:0,here:0,total:0},
      hereChainAliasStats:trafficSnapshot?.chainAliasStats || {evaluated:0,accepted:0,rejected:0},
      area:trafficSnapshot?.areaSummary || null
    },
    signalLookup:signalResolver.snapshot(),
    autoBuildCount:graph.autoBuildCount,
    autoBuiltGraphTiles:graph.autoBuiltKeys,
    autoBuildFailures:graph.autoBuildFailures,
    missingGraphTiles:[...graph.missingKeys],
    elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
    ts:Date.now(),
  };
}

function getGoogleKey(env) {
  const v = env && (env.GOOGLE_MAPS_KEY || env.GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY);
  return (v || "").trim();
}

function getHereKey(env) {
  const v = env && (env.HERE_API_KEY || env.HERE_KEY);
  return (v || "").trim();
}

function cors(resp) {
  const h = new Headers(resp.headers);
  h.set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");
  h.set("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET,POST,OPTIONS");
  h.set("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type,Authorization");
  h.set("Access-Control-Max-Age", "86400");
  return new Response(resp.body, { status: resp.status, headers: h });
}

function json(obj, status = 200) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(obj), {
    status,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8" },
  });
}

const cache = caches.default;

async function cacheGetJSON(keyUrl) {
  const key = new Request(keyUrl, { method: "GET" });
  const hit = await cache.match(key);
  if (!hit) return null;
  return await hit.json();
}

async function cachePutJSON(keyUrl, data, ttlSec) {
  const key = new Request(keyUrl, { method: "GET" });
  const resp = new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
      "Cache-Control": `max-age=${ttlSec}`,
    },
  });
  await cache.put(key, resp);
}

async function safeOptional(loader, fallback, label) {
  try {
    return await loader();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`Optional TDX dependency failed: ${label || "unknown"}`, err?.message || err);
    return fallback;
  }
}

function withStaleMarker(data, reason) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return data;
  if (Array.isArray(data)) return data;
  return {
    ...data,
    __stale: true,
    __staleReason: String(reason?.message || reason || "refresh_failed").slice(0, 500),
  };
}


/* =========================
 * Traffic area preload — v2.4.0
 *
 * Policy:
 * - radius defaults to 50 km and never exceeds 50 km per HERE circle query.
 * - direct distance <= 100 km: one midpoint query.
 * - direct distance > 100 km: centers approximately every 75 km, including both ends.
 * - A* can later request another single R50 center when its search frontier leaves coverage.
 * ========================= */

const TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_RADIUS_M = 50000;
const TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_RADIUS_M = 50000;
const TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_SPACING_M = 75000;
const TRAFFIC_AREA_SHORT_TRIP_MAX_M = 100000;
const TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_CENTERS = 12;
const TRAFFIC_AREA_HERE_CONCURRENCY = 2;

function trafficAreaBool(v, fallback = true) {
  if (v == null || v === "") return fallback;
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v;
  const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (["0", "false", "no", "off"].includes(s)) return false;
  if (["1", "true", "yes", "on"].includes(s)) return true;
  return fallback;
}

function trafficAreaLatLon(v) {
  if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length >= 2) {
    const lat = Number(v[0]), lon = Number(v[1]);
    if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) &&
        lat >= -90 && lat <= 90 && lon >= -180 && lon <= 180) {
      return [lat, lon];
    }
    return null;
  }

  if (v && typeof v === "object") {
    const lat = Number(v.lat ?? v.latitude);
    const lon = Number(v.lon ?? v.lng ?? v.longitude);
    if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon) &&
        lat >= -90 && lat <= 90 && lon >= -180 && lon <= 180) {
      return [lat, lon];
    }
    return null;
  }

  if (typeof v === "string" && v.trim()) {
    const p = v.split(",").map(x => Number(x.trim()));
    if (p.length >= 2 && Number.isFinite(p[0]) && Number.isFinite(p[1]) &&
        p[0] >= -90 && p[0] <= 90 && p[1] >= -180 && p[1] <= 180) {
      return [p[0], p[1]];
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function buildTrafficAreaCenters(start, end, radiusM, spacingM) {
  const distanceM = haversineM(start, end);

  // User-selected first version: for <=100 km, only preload one R50 midpoint.
  if (distanceM <= TRAFFIC_AREA_SHORT_TRIP_MAX_M) {
    const p = interpolateLatLon(start, end, 0.5);
    return {
      mode: "midpoint",
      directDistanceM: distanceM,
      centers: [{ lat: p[0], lon: p[1], radiusM }],
    };
  }

  // Long trip: e.g. 300 km => ~0/75/150/225/300 => 5 centers.
  let segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(distanceM / spacingM));
  segments = Math.min(TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_CENTERS - 1, segments);

  const centers = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
    const p = interpolateLatLon(start, end, i / segments);
    centers.push({ lat: p[0], lon: p[1], radiusM });
  }

  return {
    mode: "line-centers",
    directDistanceM: distanceM,
    centers,
  };
}

function normalizeTrafficAreaRequest(input) {
  const body = input && typeof input === "object" ? input : {};

  const radiusRaw = Number(body.radiusM ?? body.radius ?? TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_RADIUS_M);
  const radiusM = Math.round(radiusRaw);
  if (!Number.isFinite(radiusM) || radiusM < 100 || radiusM > TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_RADIUS_M) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "invalid_radius",
      message: `radius must be 100..${TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_RADIUS_M} meters`,
    };
  }

  const spacingRaw = Number(body.spacingM ?? body.spacing ?? TRAFFIC_AREA_DEFAULT_SPACING_M);
  const spacingM = Math.round(spacingRaw);
  if (!Number.isFinite(spacingM) || spacingM < 10000 || spacingM > 100000) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "invalid_spacing",
      message: "spacing must be 10000..100000 meters",
    };
  }

  const includeHere = trafficAreaBool(body.includeHere, true);
  const includeTdx = trafficAreaBool(body.includeTdx, true);

  let fcInput = body.functionalClasses ?? body.fc ?? "1,2,3,4,5";
  if (Array.isArray(fcInput)) fcInput = fcInput.join(",");
  const functionalClasses = normalizeHereFunctionalClasses(fcInput);

  let mode = "single";
  let directDistanceM = null;
  let centers = [];

  if (Array.isArray(body.centers) && body.centers.length) {
    mode = "manual-centers";
    for (const raw of body.centers.slice(0, TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_CENTERS)) {
      const p = trafficAreaLatLon(raw);
      if (!p) continue;
      const rRaw = Number(raw?.radiusM ?? raw?.radius ?? radiusM);
      const r = Math.round(rRaw);
      if (!Number.isFinite(r) || r < 100 || r > TRAFFIC_AREA_MAX_RADIUS_M) continue;
      centers.push({ lat: p[0], lon: p[1], radiusM: r });
    }
  } else {
    const start = trafficAreaLatLon(body.start);
    const end = trafficAreaLatLon(body.end);

    if (start && end) {
      const built = buildTrafficAreaCenters(start, end, radiusM, spacingM);
      mode = built.mode;
      directDistanceM = built.directDistanceM;
      centers = built.centers;
    } else {
      const p = trafficAreaLatLon({
        lat: body.lat,
        lon: body.lon ?? body.lng,
      });
      if (p) centers = [{ lat: p[0], lon: p[1], radiusM }];
    }
  }

  if (!centers.length) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "traffic_area_location_required",
      message: "provide lat/lon, start+end, or centers[]",
    };
  }

  // Remove practically identical centers.
  const deduped = [];
  for (const c of centers) {
    const duplicate = deduped.some(x =>
      Math.abs(x.radiusM - c.radiusM) < 1 &&
      haversineM([x.lat, x.lon], [c.lat, c.lon]) < 500
    );
    if (!duplicate) deduped.push(c);
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    mode,
    directDistanceM,
    radiusM,
    spacingM,
    centers: deduped,
    functionalClasses,
    includeHere,
    includeTdx,
  };
}

function trafficAreaSegmentTouchesCenter(seg, center) {
  const pts = seg?.points;
  if (!Array.isArray(pts) || pts.length < 2) return false;

  const radiusM = Number(center?.radiusM);
  const lat = Number(center?.lat);
  const lon = Number(center?.lon);
  if (!(Number.isFinite(radiusM) && Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon))) return false;

  const bbox = seg?.bbox || segmentBBox(pts);
  if (bbox) {
    const latPad = radiusM / 111320;
    const lonPad = radiusM / metersPerDegLon(lat);
    if (
      bbox.maxLat < lat - latPad ||
      bbox.minLat > lat + latPad ||
      bbox.maxLon < lon - lonPad ||
      bbox.minLon > lon + lonPad
    ) return false;
  }

  const nearest = nearestOnPolyline([lat, lon], pts);
  return !!(nearest && nearest.distanceM <= radiusM);
}

function trafficAreaSegmentTouchesAnyCenter(seg, centers) {
  for (const c of centers || []) {
    if (trafficAreaSegmentTouchesCenter(seg, c)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function trafficAreaHereKey(seg) {
  const pts = Array.isArray(seg?.points) ? seg.points : [];
  const a = pts[0] || [];
  const b = pts[pts.length - 1] || [];
  const mid = pts.length ? pts[(pts.length / 2) | 0] : [];
  const road = normalizeRoadToken(seg?.road || "");
  const len = Math.round(Number(seg?.lengthM) || 0);
  return [
    road,
    Number(a[0]).toFixed(5), Number(a[1]).toFixed(5),
    Number(mid[0]).toFixed(5), Number(mid[1]).toFixed(5),
    Number(b[0]).toFixed(5), Number(b[1]).toFixed(5),
    len,
    seg?.functionalClass ?? "",
  ].join("|");
}

function dedupeTrafficAreaSegments(segments) {
  const out = [];
  const seen = new Set();

  for (const raw of segments || []) {
    if (!raw) continue;

    let key;
    if (raw.provider === "TDX" && raw.sectionId) {
      key = `TDX:${raw.scope || ""}:${raw.sectionId}`;
    } else if (raw.provider === "HERE") {
      key = `HERE:${trafficAreaHereKey(raw)}`;
    } else {
      key = `${raw.provider || "?"}:${raw.id || out.length}`;
    }

    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);

    if (raw.provider === "HERE") {
      out.push({
        ...raw,
        id: `here-area:${simpleStringHash(key)}`,
        bbox: raw.bbox || segmentBBox(raw.points),
      });
    } else {
      out.push(raw);
    }
  }

  return out;
}

async function getTrafficArea(env, ctx, cfg) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const centers = cfg.centers || [];
  const hereSegments = [];
  const areaResults = [];

  // Start TDX immediately so it overlaps with HERE network/JSON work.
  const tdxPromise = (async () => {
    let tdxSegments = [];
    let tdxUnavailable = false;
    let tdxError = null;
    let tdxStaleScopes = [];

    if (cfg.includeTdx) {
      if (env.TDX_CLIENT_ID && env.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET) {
        const allTdx = await getTdxTrafficSegmentsForMatcher(env, false);
        tdxUnavailable = allTdx?.tdxUnavailable === true;
        tdxError = allTdx?.tdxError || null;
        tdxStaleScopes = Array.isArray(allTdx?.tdxStaleScopes) ? allTdx.tdxStaleScopes : [];
        tdxSegments = (allTdx || []).filter(seg =>
          trafficAreaSegmentTouchesAnyCenter(seg, centers)
        );
      } else {
        tdxUnavailable = true;
        tdxError = "TDX_credentials_missing";
      }
    }

    return { tdxSegments, tdxUnavailable, tdxError, tdxStaleScopes };
  })();

  if (cfg.includeHere) {
    const hereResults = await mapLimit(
      centers,
      TRAFFIC_AREA_HERE_CONCURRENCY,
      async (center, index) => {
        const t = Date.now();
        const out = await getHereFlowCached(
          { hereKey: ctx.hereKey },
          {
            lat: center.lat,
            lon: center.lon,
            radius: center.radiusM,
            functionalClasses: cfg.functionalClasses,
          }
        );

        return {
          index,
          center,
          elapsedMs: Date.now() - t,
          out,
        };
      }
    );

    for (const item of hereResults) {
      if (!item || item.__error) {
        areaResults.push({
          ok: false,
          provider: "HERE",
          error: item?.__error ? String(item.__error?.message || item.__error) : "unknown_here_area_error",
        });
        continue;
      }

      const out = item.out;
      areaResults.push({
        ok: out?.ok === true,
        provider: "HERE",
        centerIndex: item.index,
        center: item.center,
        resultCount: Number(out?.resultCount) || 0,
        cacheHit: out?.cacheHit === true,
        sourceUpdated: out?.sourceUpdated || null,
        elapsedMs: item.elapsedMs,
        error: out?.ok === true ? null : (out?.error || out?.message || "HERE_failed"),
      });

      if (out?.ok && Array.isArray(out.segments)) {
        const normalized = normalizeHereSegmentsForMatcher(out.segments);
        hereSegments.push(...normalized);
      }
    }
  }

  const {
    tdxSegments,
    tdxUnavailable,
    tdxError,
    tdxStaleScopes,
  } = await tdxPromise;

  const segments = dedupeTrafficAreaSegments([
    ...tdxSegments,
    ...hereSegments,
  ]);

  const providerStats = {
    tdx: segments.filter(x => x?.provider === "TDX").length,
    here: segments.filter(x => x?.provider === "HERE").length,
    total: segments.length,
  };

  const successfulHereAreas = areaResults.filter(x => x?.provider === "HERE" && x?.ok).length;
  const hereRequested = cfg.includeHere ? centers.length : 0;

  return {
    ok: cfg.includeHere ? successfulHereAreas > 0 : true,
    version: "3.7.1",
    feature: "TRAFFIC_AREA_PRELOAD",
    mode: cfg.mode,
    ts: Date.now(),
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    directDistanceM: Number.isFinite(cfg.directDistanceM) ? cfg.directDistanceM : null,
    radiusM: cfg.radiusM,
    spacingM: cfg.spacingM,
    centerCount: centers.length,
    centers,
    functionalClasses: cfg.functionalClasses,
    includeHere: cfg.includeHere,
    includeTdx: cfg.includeTdx,
    hereAreaRequested: hereRequested,
    hereAreaSucceeded: successfulHereAreas,
    tdxUnavailable,
    tdxError,
    tdxStaleScopes,
    providerStats,
    areas: areaResults,
    segments,
  };
}


/* =========================
 * HERE Real-Time Traffic v7
 * ========================= */

const HERE_FLOW_CACHE_SEC = 120;

function normalizeHereFunctionalClasses(v) {
  const raw = String(v || "1,2,3,4,5")
    .split(",")
    .map((x) => Number(x.trim()))
    .filter((x) => Number.isInteger(x) && x >= 1 && x <= 5);

  const uniq = [...new Set(raw)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  return uniq.length ? uniq : [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
}

function hereMpsToKph(v) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.round(n * 3.6 * 10) / 10 : null;
}

function flattenHereShapePoints(shape) {
  const out = [];
  const links = Array.isArray(shape?.links) ? shape.links : [];

  for (const link of links) {
    const pts = Array.isArray(link?.points) ? link.points : [];
    for (const p of pts) {
      const lat = Number(p?.lat);
      const lon = Number(p?.lng ?? p?.lon);
      if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;

      const last = out[out.length - 1];
      if (last && Math.abs(last[0] - lat) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(last[1] - lon) < 1e-9) {
        continue;
      }
      // 統一成 [lat, lon]，方便你目前 Leaflet / route snapping 使用
      out.push([lat, lon]);
    }
  }

  return out;
}

function getHereFunctionalClasses(shape) {
  const links = Array.isArray(shape?.links) ? shape.links : [];
  const set = new Set();

  for (const link of links) {
    const fc = Number(link?.functionalClass);
    if (Number.isInteger(fc) && fc >= 1 && fc <= 5) set.add(fc);
  }

  return [...set].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

function normalizeHereFlowResult(it, index) {
  const flow = it?.currentFlow || {};
  const shape = it?.location?.shape || {};
  const points = flattenHereShapePoints(shape);
  const functionalClasses = getHereFunctionalClasses(shape);

  const subs = Array.isArray(flow?.subSegments)
    ? flow.subSegments.map((s) => ({
        lengthM: toNumberOrNull(s?.length),
        speedKph: hereMpsToKph(s?.speed),
        speedUncappedKph: hereMpsToKph(s?.speedUncapped),
        freeFlowKph: hereMpsToKph(s?.freeFlow),
        trafficLevel: trafficLevelFromHere(s?.jamFactor, s?.traversability),
        jamFactor: toNumberOrNull(s?.jamFactor),
        confidence: toNumberOrNull(s?.confidence),
        traversability: s?.traversability || null,
        closed: isHereClosed(s?.jamFactor, s?.traversability),
      }))
    : [];

  return {
    provider: "HERE",
    scope: "here",
    id: `here_${index}`,
    road: it?.location?.description || "",
    lengthM: toNumberOrNull(it?.location?.length),
    functionalClass: functionalClasses.length === 1 ? functionalClasses[0] : null,
    functionalClasses,
    speedKph: hereMpsToKph(flow?.speed),
    speedUncappedKph: hereMpsToKph(flow?.speedUncapped),
    freeFlowKph: hereMpsToKph(flow?.freeFlow),
    trafficLevel: trafficLevelFromHere(flow?.jamFactor, flow?.traversability),
    jamFactor: toNumberOrNull(flow?.jamFactor),
    jamTendency: toNumberOrNull(flow?.jamTendency),
    confidence: toNumberOrNull(flow?.confidence),
    traversability: flow?.traversability || null,
    closed: isHereClosed(flow?.jamFactor, flow?.traversability),
    points,
    subSegments: subs,
  };
}

async function getHereFlowCached(ctx, { lat, lon, radius, functionalClasses, bypassCache = false }) {
  const fc = Array.isArray(functionalClasses) && functionalClasses.length
    ? functionalClasses
    : [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];

  // 不把 API Key 放進 cache key。
  const cacheKey =
    "https://cache.internal/here/flow?v=3" +
    "&lat=" + encodeURIComponent(lat.toFixed(5)) +
    "&lon=" + encodeURIComponent(lon.toFixed(5)) +
    "&r=" + encodeURIComponent(String(radius)) +
    "&fc=" + encodeURIComponent(fc.join(","));

  const safeApi =
    "https://data.traffic.hereapi.com/v7/flow" +
    "?in=" + encodeURIComponent(`circle:${lat},${lon};r=${radius}`) +
    "&locationReferencing=shape" +
    "&functionalClasses=" + encodeURIComponent(fc.join(","));

  if (!bypassCache) {
    const cached = await cacheGetJSON(cacheKey);
    if (cached?.ok) {
      return {
        ...cached,
        cacheHit: true,
        ts: Date.now(),
        fetchDebug: {
          source: "CACHE",
          cacheHit: true,
          bypassCache: false,
          urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
          status: null,
          ok: true,
          responseBytes: null,
          apiResultCount: null,
          normalizedSegmentCount: Number(cached?.resultCount) || 0,
          sourceUpdated: cached?.sourceUpdated || null,
          errorText: null,
        },
      };
    }
  }

  const api = safeApi + "&apiKey=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx.hereKey);
  let r;
  let text = "";
  let raw = null;

  try {
    r = await fetch(api, {
      method: "GET",
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
    });
    text = await r.text();
    try {
      raw = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
    } catch (_) {
      raw = null;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      provider: "HERE",
      error: "here_traffic_fetch_exception",
      status: 0,
      message: String(err?.message || err),
      ts: Date.now(),
      fetchDebug: {
        source: "NETWORK",
        cacheHit: false,
        bypassCache: !!bypassCache,
        urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
        status: 0,
        ok: false,
        responseBytes: 0,
        apiResultCount: null,
        normalizedSegmentCount: 0,
        sourceUpdated: null,
        errorText: String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 1000),
      },
    };
  }

  if (!r.ok || !raw) {
    const message = raw?.error_description || raw?.title || raw?.error || `HTTP ${r.status}`;
    const out = {
      ok: false,
      provider: "HERE",
      error: "here_traffic_failed",
      status: r.status,
      message,
      raw: raw || null,
      ts: Date.now(),
      fetchDebug: {
        source: "NETWORK",
        cacheHit: false,
        bypassCache: !!bypassCache,
        urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
        status: r.status,
        ok: r.ok,
        responseBytes: text.length,
        apiResultCount: Array.isArray(raw?.results) ? raw.results.length : null,
        normalizedSegmentCount: 0,
        sourceUpdated: raw?.sourceUpdated || null,
        contentType: r.headers.get("content-type"),
        errorText: (r.ok ? null : text.slice(0, 1000)),
      },
    };
    if (!bypassCache) await cachePutJSON(cacheKey, out, 10);
    return out;
  }

  const results = Array.isArray(raw?.results) ? raw.results : [];
  const segments = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
    const seg = normalizeHereFlowResult(results[i], i);

    // 沒有 geometry 或任何可用速度的項目，對 route matching 沒價值。
    if (!Array.isArray(seg.points) || seg.points.length < 2) continue;
    if (!seg.closed && !(Number.isFinite(seg.speedKph) || Number.isFinite(seg.freeFlowKph))) continue;

    segments.push(seg);
  }

  const out = {
    ok: true,
    provider: "HERE",
    sourceUpdated: raw?.sourceUpdated || null,
    ts: Date.now(),
    cacheHit: false,
    updateIntervalSec: HERE_FLOW_CACHE_SEC,
    query: {
      lat,
      lon,
      radius,
      functionalClasses: fc,
    },
    resultCount: segments.length,
    segments,
    fetchDebug: {
      source: "NETWORK",
      cacheHit: false,
      bypassCache: !!bypassCache,
      urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
      status: r.status,
      ok: r.ok,
      responseBytes: text.length,
      apiResultCount: results.length,
      normalizedSegmentCount: segments.length,
      sourceUpdated: raw?.sourceUpdated || null,
      contentType: r.headers.get("content-type"),
      errorText: null,
    },
  };

  if (!bypassCache) await cachePutJSON(cacheKey, out, HERE_FLOW_CACHE_SEC);
  return out;
}



/* =========================
 * HERE corridor flow — v1.6
 *
 * Main route matcher uses corridor requests instead of a chain of circle requests.
 * HERE Traffic API corridor limit: <= 300 polyline points.
 * We keep 280 points as a safety margin.
 * ========================= */

const HERE_CORRIDOR_RADIUS_M = 180;
const HERE_CORRIDOR_MAX_SPAN_M = 50000;
const HERE_CORRIDOR_MAX_POINTS = 280;
const HERE_CORRIDOR_ROUTE_PADDING_M = 600;

const FLEXIBLE_POLYLINE_CHARS =
  "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_";

function flexEncodeUnsigned(value) {
  let v = Math.floor(Number(value));
  if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v < 0) throw new Error("invalid_flexible_polyline_unsigned");

  let out = "";
  do {
    let chunk = v % 32;
    v = Math.floor(v / 32);
    if (v > 0) chunk |= 0x20;
    out += FLEXIBLE_POLYLINE_CHARS[chunk];
  } while (v > 0);

  return out;
}

function flexEncodeSigned(value) {
  const n = Math.trunc(Number(value));
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) throw new Error("invalid_flexible_polyline_signed");
  const mapped = n < 0 ? (-n * 2) - 1 : n * 2;
  return flexEncodeUnsigned(mapped);
}

function encodeHereFlexiblePolyline(points, precision = 5) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(points);
  if (pts.length < 2) throw new Error("flexible_polyline_requires_2_points");

  // Flexible Polyline v1:
  // first header = format version (1)
  // second header = precision + third-dimension flags.
  // No third dimension => second header is simply precision.
  let out = flexEncodeUnsigned(1) + flexEncodeUnsigned(precision);

  const factor = 10 ** precision;
  let lastLat = 0;
  let lastLon = 0;

  for (const p of pts) {
    const lat = Math.round(Number(p[0]) * factor);
    const lon = Math.round(Number(p[1]) * factor);

    out += flexEncodeSigned(lat - lastLat);
    out += flexEncodeSigned(lon - lastLon);

    lastLat = lat;
    lastLon = lon;
  }

  return out;
}

function rdpSimplifyLatLon(points, epsilonM) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(points);
  if (pts.length <= 2) return pts;

  const eps = Math.max(0.1, Number(epsilonM) || 0.1);
  const keep = new Uint8Array(pts.length);
  keep[0] = 1;
  keep[pts.length - 1] = 1;

  const stack = [[0, pts.length - 1]];

  while (stack.length) {
    const pair = stack.pop();
    const start = pair[0];
    const end = pair[1];
    if (end <= start + 1) continue;

    let bestIndex = -1;
    let bestDistance = -1;

    for (let i = start + 1; i < end; i++) {
      const d = pointToSegmentDistanceLocalM(pts[i], pts[start], pts[end]).distanceM;
      if (d > bestDistance) {
        bestDistance = d;
        bestIndex = i;
      }
    }

    if (bestIndex >= 0 && bestDistance > eps) {
      keep[bestIndex] = 1;
      stack.push([start, bestIndex], [bestIndex, end]);
    }
  }

  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
