    if (keep[i]) out.push(pts[i]);
  }
  return out;
}

function limitHereCorridorPoints(points, maxPoints = HERE_CORRIDOR_MAX_POINTS) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(points);
  const maxPts = Math.max(2, Math.min(300, Number(maxPoints) || HERE_CORRIDOR_MAX_POINTS));
  if (pts.length <= maxPts) return pts;

  // Increase RDP tolerance until the route fits under HERE's point limit.
  let low = 0.5;
  let high = 20;
  let candidate = rdpSimplifyLatLon(pts, high);

  while (candidate.length > maxPts && high < 5000) {
    low = high;
    high *= 2;
    candidate = rdpSimplifyLatLon(pts, high);
  }

  if (candidate.length <= maxPts) {
    let best = candidate;
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
      const mid = (low + high) / 2;
      const test = rdpSimplifyLatLon(pts, mid);
      if (test.length <= maxPts) {
        best = test;
        high = mid;
      } else {
        low = mid;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  // Extreme fallback: preserve endpoints and uniformly sample.
  const out = [pts[0]];
  const last = pts.length - 1;
  for (let i = 1; i < maxPts - 1; i++) {
    const idx = Math.round((i / (maxPts - 1)) * last);
    const p = pts[idx];
    const prev = out[out.length - 1];
    if (!prev || prev[0] !== p[0] || prev[1] !== p[1]) out.push(p);
  }
  const tail = pts[last];
  const prev = out[out.length - 1];
  if (!prev || prev[0] !== tail[0] || prev[1] !== tail[1]) out.push(tail);
  return out;
}

function routePointsFromEdgeRange(edges, startEdge, endEdge) {
  if (!Array.isArray(edges) || !edges.length) return [];

  const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(edges.length - 1, Number(startEdge) || 0));
  const b = Math.max(a, Math.min(edges.length - 1, Number(endEdge) || a));
  if (!edges[a]?.a || !edges[b]?.b) return [];

  const out = [edges[a].a];
  for (let i = a; i <= b; i++) {
    if (edges[i]?.b) out.push(edges[i].b);
  }
  return normalizeRouteInput(out);
}

function edgeIndexAtOrBeforeRouteM(edges, routeM, fallbackIndex = 0) {
  const m = Number(routeM);
  if (!Array.isArray(edges) || !edges.length || !Number.isFinite(m)) return fallbackIndex;

  let lo = 0;
  let hi = edges.length - 1;
  let ans = 0;

  while (lo <= hi) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
    if (Number(edges[mid]?.routeStartM) <= m) {
      ans = mid;
      lo = mid + 1;
    } else {
      hi = mid - 1;
    }
  }
  return ans;
}

function edgeIndexAtOrAfterRouteM(edges, routeM, fallbackIndex = null) {
  const m = Number(routeM);
  if (!Array.isArray(edges) || !edges.length || !Number.isFinite(m)) {
    return fallbackIndex == null ? Math.max(0, (edges?.length || 1) - 1) : fallbackIndex;
  }

  let lo = 0;
  let hi = edges.length - 1;
  let ans = edges.length - 1;

  while (lo <= hi) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
    if (Number(edges[mid]?.routeEndM) >= m) {
      ans = mid;
      hi = mid - 1;
    } else {
      lo = mid + 1;
    }
  }
  return ans;
}

function buildHereCorridorQueries(edges, chunks) {
  const queries = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < (chunks || []).length; i++) {
    const chunk = chunks[i];
    if (!chunk) continue;

    const paddedStartM = Math.max(0, Number(chunk.startM || 0) - HERE_CORRIDOR_ROUTE_PADDING_M);
    const paddedEndM = Number(chunk.endM || 0) + HERE_CORRIDOR_ROUTE_PADDING_M;

    const startEdge = edgeIndexAtOrBeforeRouteM(edges, paddedStartM, chunk.startEdge);
    const endEdge = edgeIndexAtOrAfterRouteM(edges, paddedEndM, chunk.endEdge);

    const rawPoints = routePointsFromEdgeRange(edges, startEdge, endEdge);
    if (rawPoints.length < 2) continue;

    const corridorPoints = limitHereCorridorPoints(rawPoints, HERE_CORRIDOR_MAX_POINTS);
    if (corridorPoints.length < 2) continue;

    queries.push({
      queryIndex: queries.length,
      startEdge,
      endEdge,
      chunkStartEdge: chunk.startEdge,
      chunkEndEdge: chunk.endEdge,
      startM: chunk.startM,
      endM: chunk.endM,
      needM: Number(chunk.needM) || Math.max(0, Number(chunk.endM) - Number(chunk.startM)),
      routeSpanM: Math.max(0, Number(chunk.endM) - Number(chunk.startM)),
      rawPointCount: rawPoints.length,
      pointCount: corridorPoints.length,
      points: corridorPoints,
      radius: HERE_CORRIDOR_RADIUS_M,
    });
  }

  // Route order matters for long trips: if the HERE budget is exhausted,
  // query the roads the driver will reach first.
  queries.sort((a, b) => Number(a.startM) - Number(b.startM));
  queries.forEach((q, i) => { q.queryIndex = i; });
  return queries;
}

async function getHereFlowCorridorCached(
  ctx,
  {
    points,
    radius = HERE_CORRIDOR_RADIUS_M,
    functionalClasses,
    bypassCache = false,
  }
) {
  const fc = Array.isArray(functionalClasses) && functionalClasses.length
    ? functionalClasses
    : [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];

  const corridorPoints = limitHereCorridorPoints(points, HERE_CORRIDOR_MAX_POINTS);
  if (corridorPoints.length < 2) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      provider: "HERE",
      error: "invalid_corridor_points",
      status: 400,
      ts: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  const rMeters = Math.max(20, Math.min(5000, Math.round(Number(radius) || HERE_CORRIDOR_RADIUS_M)));
  const encoded = encodeHereFlexiblePolyline(corridorPoints, 5);
  const corridorHash = simpleStringHash(encoded);

  const cacheKey =
    "https://cache.internal/here/flow_corridor?v=1" +
    "&h=" + encodeURIComponent(corridorHash) +
    "&n=" + encodeURIComponent(String(corridorPoints.length)) +
    "&el=" + encodeURIComponent(String(encoded.length)) +
    "&r=" + encodeURIComponent(String(rMeters)) +
    "&fc=" + encodeURIComponent(fc.join(","));

  const safeApi =
    "https://data.traffic.hereapi.com/v7/flow" +
    "?POST_CORRIDOR_HASH=" + encodeURIComponent(corridorHash) +
    "&radius=" + encodeURIComponent(String(rMeters)) +
    "&points=" + encodeURIComponent(String(corridorPoints.length)) +
    "&locationReferencing=shape" +
    "&functionalClasses=" + encodeURIComponent(fc.join(","));

  if (!bypassCache) {
    const cached = await cacheGetJSON(cacheKey);
    if (cached?.ok) {
      return {
        ...cached,
        cacheHit: true,
        ts: Date.now(),
        fetchDebug: {
          source: "CACHE",
          cacheHit: true,
          bypassCache: false,
          queryType: "corridor",
          urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
          status: null,
          ok: true,
          responseBytes: null,
          apiResultCount: null,
          normalizedSegmentCount: Number(cached?.resultCount) || 0,
          sourceUpdated: cached?.sourceUpdated || null,
          corridorPointCount: corridorPoints.length,
          corridorEncodedLength: encoded.length,
          corridorRadiusM: rMeters,
          errorText: null,
        },
      };
    }
  }

  const api = "https://data.traffic.hereapi.com/v7/flow?apiKey=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx.hereKey);
  const body = {
    in: {
      type: "corridor",
      corridor: encoded,
      radius: rMeters,
    },
    locationReferencing: ["shape"],
    functionalClasses: fc,
  };

  let response;
  let responseText = "";
  let raw = null;

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);
  try {
    response = await fetch(api, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Accept": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });

    responseText = await response.text();
    try {
      raw = responseText ? JSON.parse(responseText) : null;
    } catch (_) {
      raw = null;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      provider: "HERE",
      error: controller.signal.aborted ? "here_corridor_timeout" : "here_corridor_fetch_exception",
      status: 0,
      message: String(err?.message || err),
      ts: Date.now(),
      fetchDebug: {
        source: "NETWORK",
        cacheHit: false,
        bypassCache: !!bypassCache,
        queryType: "corridor",
        urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
        status: 0,
        ok: false,
        responseBytes: 0,
        apiResultCount: null,
        normalizedSegmentCount: 0,
        sourceUpdated: null,
        corridorPointCount: corridorPoints.length,
        corridorEncodedLength: encoded.length,
        corridorRadiusM: rMeters,
        errorText: String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 1000),
      },
    };
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }

  if (!response.ok || !raw) {
    const message =
      raw?.error_description ||
      raw?.title ||
      raw?.error ||
      `HTTP ${response.status}`;

    const out = {
      ok: false,
      provider: "HERE",
      error: "here_corridor_failed",
      status: response.status,
      message,
      raw: raw || null,
      ts: Date.now(),
      fetchDebug: {
        source: "NETWORK",
        cacheHit: false,
        bypassCache: !!bypassCache,
        queryType: "corridor",
        urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
        status: response.status,
        ok: response.ok,
        responseBytes: responseText.length,
        apiResultCount: Array.isArray(raw?.results) ? raw.results.length : null,
        normalizedSegmentCount: 0,
        sourceUpdated: raw?.sourceUpdated || null,
        contentType: response.headers.get("content-type"),
        corridorPointCount: corridorPoints.length,
        corridorEncodedLength: encoded.length,
        corridorRadiusM: rMeters,
        errorText: response.ok ? null : responseText.slice(0, 1000),
      },
    };

    if (!bypassCache) await cachePutJSON(cacheKey, out, 10);
    return out;
  }

  const results = Array.isArray(raw?.results) ? raw.results : [];
  const segments = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
    const seg = normalizeHereFlowResult(results[i], i);
    if (!Array.isArray(seg.points) || seg.points.length < 2) continue;
    if (!seg.closed && !(Number.isFinite(seg.speedKph) || Number.isFinite(seg.freeFlowKph))) continue;
    segments.push(seg);
  }

  const out = {
    ok: true,
    provider: "HERE",
    sourceUpdated: raw?.sourceUpdated || null,
    ts: Date.now(),
    cacheHit: false,
    updateIntervalSec: HERE_FLOW_CACHE_SEC,
    query: {
      type: "corridor",
      radius: rMeters,
      pointCount: corridorPoints.length,
      encodedLength: encoded.length,
      corridorHash,
      functionalClasses: fc,
    },
    resultCount: segments.length,
    segments,
    fetchDebug: {
      source: "NETWORK",
      cacheHit: false,
      bypassCache: !!bypassCache,
      queryType: "corridor",
      urlWithoutApiKey: safeApi,
      status: response.status,
      ok: response.ok,
      responseBytes: responseText.length,
      apiResultCount: results.length,
      normalizedSegmentCount: segments.length,
      sourceUpdated: raw?.sourceUpdated || null,
      contentType: response.headers.get("content-type"),
      corridorPointCount: corridorPoints.length,
      corridorEncodedLength: encoded.length,
      corridorRadiusM: rMeters,
      errorText: null,
    },
  };

  if (!bypassCache) await cachePutJSON(cacheKey, out, HERE_FLOW_CACHE_SEC);
  return out;
}


/* ==========================================================
 * Route ↔ Traffic Matcher v1.1 — provider-aware
 *
 * 目標：
 *   - 不再用「route point 找最近交通線」直接套速度。
 *   - 先把 route 重採樣成短 edge。
 *   - 用 grid spatial index 找候選。
 *   - 距離 + 方向 + overlap + continuity 綜合評分。
 *   - 依 provider/scope 使用不同規則：freeway / highway / taipei / HERE。
 *   - Freeway TDX 有有效 live speed 時優先，允許較大的 geometry offset。
 *   - Highway/Taipei 仍要求較好的幾何匹配。
 *   - HERE 主要補 TDX 缺口；高速公路上也可作 conflict 驗證，但不輕易覆蓋有效 TDX。
 *
 * v1 暫時沒有 OSM layer/bridge/tunnel metadata；
 * 後續可在 matcher score 補上 layer / road class。
 * ========================================================== */

const MATCH_GRID_M = 220;

// to re-route sparse waypoints. This is specifically to stop bridge/ground or

// Full node-backed OSRM Match is valuable near the vehicle for bridge/ground
// protection, but doing it for a 100-400 km route creates tens/hundreds of

const LONG_ROUTE_TRUE_ROADLINK_MAX_M = 30000;

  signalStraightSec: 3,

  noSignalStraightSec: 0,
  noSignalTurnSec: 2,
});

  signalStraightSec: 8,
  signalTurnSec: 12,
  noSignalStraightSec: 1,
  noSignalTurnSec: 3,
});

const HERE_GAP_RADIUS_MAX_M = 20000;

// 9 km 在省額度與 Worker CPU/回傳量之間較平衡；硬上限仍保留原本 20 km。
const HERE_BUNDLE_TARGET_RADIUS_M = 9000;
const HERE_BUNDLE_MAX_RADIUS_M = 12000;
const HERE_BUNDLE_MAX_ROUTE_SPAN_M = 42000;

function normalizeRouteInput(route) {
  if (!Array.isArray(route)) return [];
  const out = [];

  for (const p of route) {
    let lat, lon;

    if (Array.isArray(p)) {
      lat = Number(p[0]);
      lon = Number(p[1]);
    } else if (p && typeof p === "object") {
      lat = Number(p.lat ?? p.latitude);
      lon = Number(p.lon ?? p.lng ?? p.longitude);
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
    if (lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lon < -180 || lon > 180) continue;

    const last = out[out.length - 1];
    if (last && Math.abs(last[0] - lat) < 1e-10 && Math.abs(last[1] - lon) < 1e-10) continue;
    out.push([lat, lon]);
  }

  return out;
}

function haversineM(a, b) {
  const R = 6371000;
  const lat1 = a[0] * Math.PI / 180;
  const lat2 = b[0] * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLat = (b[0] - a[0]) * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLon = (b[1] - a[1]) * Math.PI / 180;

  const s1 = Math.sin(dLat / 2);
  const s2 = Math.sin(dLon / 2);
  const h = s1 * s1 + Math.cos(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * s2 * s2;
  return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h)));
}

function bearingDeg(a, b) {
  const lat1 = a[0] * Math.PI / 180;
  const lat2 = b[0] * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLon = (b[1] - a[1]) * Math.PI / 180;
  const y = Math.sin(dLon) * Math.cos(lat2);
  const x = Math.cos(lat1) * Math.sin(lat2) -
    Math.sin(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * Math.cos(dLon);
  const d = Math.atan2(y, x) * 180 / Math.PI;
  return (d + 360) % 360;
}

function angleDeltaDeg(a, b) {
  let d = Math.abs(Number(a) - Number(b)) % 360;
  if (d > 180) d = 360 - d;
  return d;
}

function interpolateLatLon(a, b, t) {
  return [
    a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t,
    a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t,
  ];
}

function resampleRoute(route, maxEdgeM = MATCH_ROUTE_EDGE_M) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(route);
  if (pts.length < 2) return pts;

  const maxM = Math.max(15, Math.min(100, Number(maxEdgeM) || MATCH_ROUTE_EDGE_M));
  const out = [pts[0]];

  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = pts[i];
    const b = pts[i + 1];
    const d = haversineM(a, b);
    if (!(d > 0)) continue;

    const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(d / maxM));
    for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) {
      out.push(interpolateLatLon(a, b, k / n));
    }
  }

  return out;
}


function routeLengthM(route) {
  let total = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < route.length - 1; i++) total += haversineM(route[i], route[i + 1]);
  return total;
}

/*
 * Performance simplifier for matcher input.
 * Keeps corners, but avoids feeding every dense OSRM polyline vertex into the matcher.
 * This changes only matcher sampling density, not the original routing geometry itself.
 */
function simplifyRouteForMatcher(route, spacingM = 35, turnKeepDeg = 16) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(route);
  if (pts.length <= 2) return pts;

  const spacing = Math.max(18, Math.min(90, Number(spacingM) || 35));
  const turnDeg = Math.max(8, Math.min(45, Number(turnKeepDeg) || 16));
  const out = [pts[0]];
  let lastKept = pts[0];
  let accum = 0;

  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const prev = pts[i - 1], cur = pts[i], next = pts[i + 1];
    accum += haversineM(prev, cur);
    const b1 = bearingDeg(prev, cur);
    const b2 = bearingDeg(cur, next);
    const turn = angleDeltaDeg(b1, b2);

    if (accum >= spacing || turn >= turnDeg || haversineM(lastKept, cur) >= spacing * 1.35) {
      out.push(cur);
      lastKept = cur;
      accum = 0;
    }
  }

  const last = pts[pts.length - 1];
  const tail = out[out.length - 1];
  if (!tail || haversineM(tail, last) > 0.5) out.push(last);
  return out;
}

async function mapLimit(items, limit, fn) {
  const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  if (!arr.length) return [];
  const results = new Array(arr.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const workerCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(limit) || 1, arr.length));

  async function worker() {
    while (true) {
      const i = cursor++;
      if (i >= arr.length) return;
      try { results[i] = await fn(arr[i], i); }
      catch (error) { results[i] = { __error: error }; }
    }
  }

  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()));
  return results;
}

function buildRouteEdges(routePoints) {
  const edges = [];
  let cumM = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < routePoints.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = routePoints[i];
    const b = routePoints[i + 1];
    const lengthM = haversineM(a, b);
    if (!(lengthM > 0.2)) continue;

    const midpoint = interpolateLatLon(a, b, 0.5);
    edges.push({
      edgeIndex: edges.length,
      pointIndexA: i,
      pointIndexB: i + 1,
      a,
      b,
      midpoint,
      heading: bearingDeg(a, b),
      lengthM,
      routeStartM: cumM,
      routeEndM: cumM + lengthM,
      navLinkId: null,
      navNodeA: null,
      navNodeB: null,
      navRoad: "",
      navExpressway: false,
    });
    cumM += lengthM;
  }

  return { edges, totalDistanceM: cumM };
}

function normalizeRoadToken(v) {
  return String(v || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[\s\-_/\\.,，。・·()（）\[\]【】]+/g, "")
    .replace(/northbound|southbound|eastbound|westbound|北向|南向|東向|西向/g, "")
    .trim();
}

function roadNameCompatibility(a, b) {
  const x = normalizeRoadToken(a), y = normalizeRoadToken(b);
  if (!x || !y) return 0;
  if (x === y) return 1;
  if (x.includes(y) || y.includes(x)) return 0.72;
  // Common suffix/prefix overlap catches e.g. "信義路三段" vs "信義路".
  const min = Math.min(x.length, y.length);
  let same = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < min; i++) if (x[i] === y[i]) same++; else break;
  return same >= 3 ? Math.min(0.55, same / Math.max(x.length, y.length)) : 0;
}

function normalizeNavLinkHints(body, totalDistanceM) {
  const raw = Array.isArray(body?.navLinkHints) ? body.navLinkHints : [];
  const clean = raw.map((x, i) => {
    const startM = Number(x?.startM);
    const endM = Number(x?.endM);
    if (!(Number.isFinite(startM) && Number.isFinite(endM) && endM > startM)) return null;
    return {
      navLinkId: String(x?.navLinkId || x?.id || `NLK:LOCAL:${i}`),
      startM, endM,
      nodeA: x?.nodeA != null ? String(x.nodeA) : null,
      nodeB: x?.nodeB != null ? String(x.nodeB) : null,
      road: String(x?.road || ""),
      isExpressway: x?.isExpressway === true,
    };
  }).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>a.startM-b.startM);
  if (!clean.length) return [];
  const hintTotal = Math.max(...clean.map(x => x.endM));
  const scale = hintTotal > 0 && totalDistanceM > 0 ? totalDistanceM / hintTotal : 1;
  return clean.map(x => ({ ...x, startM:x.startM*scale, endM:x.endM*scale }));
}


function roadLinkHash32(s) {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
  const str = String(s || "");
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);
}

function sampledRouteDeviation(candidateRoute, referenceRoute, maxSamples = 72) {
  const a = normalizeRouteInput(candidateRoute);
  const b = normalizeRouteInput(referenceRoute);
  if (a.length < 2 || b.length < 2) return { meanM: Infinity, p90M: Infinity, maxM: Infinity };
  const n = Math.min(maxSamples, a.length);
  const vals = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const idx = Math.round((i / Math.max(1, n - 1)) * (a.length - 1));
    const p = a[idx];
    const proj = projectPointToRouteM(p, b);
    if (proj && Number.isFinite(proj.distanceM)) vals.push(proj.distanceM);
  }
  if (!vals.length) return { meanM: Infinity, p90M: Infinity, maxM: Infinity };
  vals.sort((x, y) => x - y);
  const meanM = vals.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / vals.length;
  const p90M = vals[Math.min(vals.length - 1, Math.floor(vals.length * 0.90))];
  const maxM = vals[vals.length - 1];
  return { meanM, p90M, maxM };
}

function deriveSyntheticRoadLinkHints(baselineProfile, totalDistanceM) {
  const steps = Array.isArray(baselineProfile?.steps) ? baselineProfile.steps : [];
  if (!steps.length || !(baselineProfile.totalDistanceM > 0) || !(totalDistanceM > 0)) return [];
  const scale = totalDistanceM / baselineProfile.totalDistanceM;
  const out = [];
  for (let si = 0; si < steps.length; si++) {
    const st = steps[si];
    const a = st.startM * scale;
    const b = st.endM * scale;
    const len = Math.max(0, b - a);
    if (!(len > 0)) continue;
    const target = st.isExpressway ? ROADLINK_SYNTH_EXPRESSWAY_CHUNK_M : ROADLINK_SYNTH_LOCAL_CHUNK_M;
    const chunks = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(len / target));
    const roadHash = roadLinkHash32(`${st.road}|${st.isExpressway ? 1 : 0}`);
    for (let k = 0; k < chunks; k++) {
      const startM = a + len * (k / chunks);
      const endM = a + len * ((k + 1) / chunks);
      out.push({ navLinkId:`NLK:SYN:${roadHash}:${si}:${k}`, startM, endM, nodeA:null, nodeB:null, road:st.road||"", isExpressway:st.isExpressway===true });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(totalDistanceM, startM = 0, isExpressway = false) {
  const total = Number(totalDistanceM) || 0;
  const from = Math.max(0, Math.min(total, Number(startM) || 0));
  if (!(total > from)) return [];

  const target = isExpressway ? LONG_ROUTE_GENERIC_SYNTH_EXPRESSWAY_M : LONG_ROUTE_GENERIC_SYNTH_LOCAL_M;
  const out = [];
  let k = 0;
  for (let a = from; a < total - 0.5; a += target) {
    const b = Math.min(total, a + target);
    out.push({
      navLinkId: `NLK:SYN:FAR:${k++}`,
      startM: a,
      endM: b,
      nodeA: null,
      nodeB: null,
      road: "",
      isExpressway: !!isExpressway,
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function clipRoadLinkHintsToRange(hints, startM, endM) {
  const a0 = Math.max(0, Number(startM) || 0);
  const b0 = Math.max(a0, Number(endM) || a0);
  const out = [];
  for (const h of Array.isArray(hints) ? hints : []) {
    const a = Math.max(a0, Number(h.startM) || 0);
    const b = Math.min(b0, Number(h.endM) || 0);
    if (!(b > a)) continue;
    out.push({ ...h, startM: a, endM: b });
  }
  return out;
}

function fillRoadLinkHintGaps(hints, totalDistanceM, genericStartM = 0) {
  const total = Number(totalDistanceM) || 0;
  const sorted = (Array.isArray(hints) ? hints : [])
    .filter(h => Number(h.endM) > Number(h.startM))
    .map(h => ({...h}))
    .sort((a,b) => a.startM - b.startM || a.endM - b.endM);

  const out = [];
  let cursor = Math.max(0, Number(genericStartM) || 0);

  for (const h of sorted) {
    if (h.endM <= cursor) continue;
    if (h.startM > cursor + 1) {
      out.push(...genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(h.startM, cursor, false));
    }
    out.push(h);
    cursor = Math.max(cursor, h.endM);
  }
  if (cursor < total - 1) out.push(...genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(total, cursor, false));
  return out.sort((a,b) => a.startM - b.startM || a.endM - b.endM);
}

async function deriveHybridRoadLinkHints(rawRoute, totalDistanceM, baselineProfile, {
  fastTrafficOnly = false,
  motoHere = false,
} = {}) {
  const total = Number(totalDistanceM) || 0;
  const baselineSynthetic = deriveSyntheticRoadLinkHints(baselineProfile, total);

  // Motorcycle already uses Google geometry + baseline links and intentionally
  // does not perform car-oriented OSRM node matching.
  if (motoHere) {
    const hints = baselineSynthetic.length
      ? baselineSynthetic
      : genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(total, 0, false);
    return {
      ok: true,
      hints,
      source: baselineSynthetic.length ? "google-baseline-synthetic-roadlinks" : "google-generic-synthetic-roadlinks",
      longRouteMode: total > LONG_ROUTE_TRUE_ROADLINK_MAX_M,
      trueRoadLinkSpanM: 0,
      farSyntheticHintCount: hints.length,
      fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: true,
    };
  }

  // Long-route FAST pass: HERE must not wait for dozens of OSRM Match calls.
  // Use synthetic links immediately; Full pass can upgrade the near 30 km.
  if (fastTrafficOnly) {
    const hints = baselineSynthetic.length
      ? baselineSynthetic
      : genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(total, 0, false);
    return {
      ok: true,
      hints,
      source: baselineSynthetic.length ? "long-fast-synthetic-roadlinks" : "long-fast-generic-roadlinks",
      longRouteMode: total > LONG_ROUTE_TRUE_ROADLINK_MAX_M,
      trueRoadLinkSpanM: 0,
      farSyntheticHintCount: hints.length,
      fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: true,
    };
  }

  // Short routes retain the full v2.2.1 true RoadLink behavior.
  if (total <= LONG_ROUTE_TRUE_ROADLINK_MAX_M) {
    const auto = await deriveOsrmRoadLinkHintsCached(rawRoute, total);
    if (auto?.ok && Array.isArray(auto.hints) && auto.hints.length) {
      return {
        ...auto,
        longRouteMode: false,
        trueRoadLinkSpanM: total,
        farSyntheticHintCount: 0,
        fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: false,
      };
    }
    const hints = baselineSynthetic.length
      ? baselineSynthetic
      : genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(total, 0, false);
    return {
      ...(auto || {}),
      ok: true,
      hints,
      source: baselineSynthetic.length ? "baseline-synthetic-roadlinks" : "generic-synthetic-roadlinks",
      autoRejectReason: auto?.source || "no-auto-result",
      longRouteMode: false,
      trueRoadLinkSpanM: 0,
      farSyntheticHintCount: hints.length,
      fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: false,
    };
  }

  // Long-route Full pass: true node-backed RoadLinks only for the near 30 km.
  const nearSpanM = Math.min(total, LONG_ROUTE_TRUE_ROADLINK_MAX_M);
  const nearRoute = sliceRouteByM(rawRoute, 0, nearSpanM);
  let nearAuto = null;
  if (nearRoute.length >= 2) {
    const nearActualM = routeLengthM(nearRoute);
    nearAuto = await deriveOsrmRoadLinkHintsCached(nearRoute, nearActualM);
  }

  let nearHints = [];
  if (nearAuto?.ok && Array.isArray(nearAuto.hints) && nearAuto.hints.length) {
    const nearAutoDistance = Math.max(...nearAuto.hints.map(h => Number(h.endM) || 0), 0);
    const scale = nearAutoDistance > 0 ? nearSpanM / nearAutoDistance : 1;
    nearHints = nearAuto.hints
      .map(h => ({...h, startM:(Number(h.startM)||0)*scale, endM:(Number(h.endM)||0)*scale}))
      .filter(h => h.endM > h.startM && h.startM < nearSpanM + 1);
  }

  // Prefer baseline-derived synthetic RoadLinks for the far route because they
  // carry road/class information. Fall back to generic distance chunks only for
  // uncovered gaps.
  let farHints = clipRoadLinkHintsToRange(baselineSynthetic, nearSpanM, total);
  if (!farHints.length) farHints = genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(total, nearSpanM, false);

  let hints = [...nearHints, ...farHints].sort((a,b) => a.startM - b.startM || a.endM - b.endM);

  // If near OSRM Match failed, do not fail the whole traffic request. Fill the
  // near area synthetically and let HERE still run for the entire route.
  if (!nearHints.length) {
    const nearFallback = baselineSynthetic.length
      ? clipRoadLinkHintsToRange(baselineSynthetic, 0, nearSpanM)
      : genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(nearSpanM, 0, false);
    hints = [...nearFallback, ...farHints].sort((a,b) => a.startM - b.startM || a.endM - b.endM);
  }

  hints = fillRoadLinkHintGaps(hints, total, 0);

  return {
    ...(nearAuto || {}),
    ok: true,
    hints,
    source: nearHints.length ? "long-near-osrm-node+far-synthetic" : "long-all-synthetic-roadlinks",
    autoRejectReason: nearHints.length ? null : (nearAuto?.source || "near-auto-failed"),
    longRouteMode: true,
    trueRoadLinkSpanM: nearHints.length ? nearSpanM : 0,
    farSyntheticHintCount: hints.filter(h => !h.nodeA || !h.nodeB).length,
    fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: false,
    nearAutoSource: nearAuto?.source || null,
  };
}

function compactRoadLinkHttpMessage(v, maxLen = 220) {
  const s = String(v || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return s.length > maxLen ? s.slice(0, maxLen) + "…" : s;
}

function buildRoadLinkMatchTrace(route, spacingM = ROADLINK_MATCH_TRACE_SPACING_M) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(route);
  const totalM = routeLengthM(pts);
  if (pts.length < 2 || !(totalM > 0)) return [];
  const spacing = Math.max(25, Math.min(90, Number(spacingM) || ROADLINK_MATCH_TRACE_SPACING_M));
  const count = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(totalM / spacing) + 1);
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const routeM = i === count - 1 ? totalM : Math.min(totalM, i * spacing);
    const point = pointAtRouteM(pts, routeM);
    if (!point) continue;
    const before = pointAtRouteM(pts, Math.max(0, routeM - 16));
    const after = pointAtRouteM(pts, Math.min(totalM, routeM + 16));
    const bearing = before && after ? bearingDeg(before, after) : 0;
    out.push({point, routeM, bearing:Number.isFinite(bearing)?bearing:0});
  }
  if (out.length && Math.abs(out[out.length-1].routeM-totalM)>1) {
    const point = pointAtRouteM(pts,totalM);
    const before = pointAtRouteM(pts,Math.max(0,totalM-16));
    if (point) out.push({point,routeM:totalM,bearing:before?bearingDeg(before,point):out[out.length-1].bearing});
  }
  return out;
}

function buildRoadLinkMatchChunks(trace) {
  const pts = Array.isArray(trace) ? trace : [];
  if (pts.length < 2) return [];
  const maxPts = ROADLINK_MATCH_CHUNK_MAX_POINTS;
  const overlap = Math.max(2,Math.min(20,ROADLINK_MATCH_CHUNK_OVERLAP_POINTS));
  const stride = Math.max(2,maxPts-overlap);
  const chunks=[];
  for(let start=0;start<pts.length-1;start+=stride){
    const endExclusive=Math.min(pts.length,start+maxPts);
    const chunkPts=pts.slice(start,endExclusive);
    if(chunkPts.length<2) break;
    chunks.push({index:chunks.length,startIndex:start,endIndex:endExclusive-1,points:chunkPts,emitStartM:chunkPts[0].routeM,emitEndM:chunkPts[chunkPts.length-1].routeM});
    if(endExclusive>=pts.length) break;
  }
  for(let i=0;i<chunks.length-1;i++){
    const a=chunks[i],b=chunks[i+1];
    const boundary=(b.points[0].routeM+a.points[a.points.length-1].routeM)/2;
    a.emitEndM=boundary; b.emitStartM=boundary;
  }
  return chunks;
}


function splitRoadLinkMatchChunkForTooBig(chunk){
  const pts=Array.isArray(chunk?.points)?chunk.points:[];
  if(pts.length<ROADLINK_MATCH_TOO_BIG_SPLIT_MIN_POINTS)return[];

  const overlap=Math.max(4,Math.min(8,Math.floor(pts.length/5)));
  const mid=Math.floor(pts.length/2);
  const leftEnd=Math.min(pts.length,mid+Math.ceil(overlap/2));
  const rightStart=Math.max(0,mid-Math.floor(overlap/2));

  const leftPts=pts.slice(0,leftEnd);
  const rightPts=pts.slice(rightStart);

  if(leftPts.length<2||rightPts.length<2)return[];

  const boundary=(leftPts[leftPts.length-1].routeM+rightPts[0].routeM)/2;

  return[
    {
      ...chunk,
      index:`${chunk.index}a`,
      points:leftPts,
      startIndex:chunk.startIndex,
      endIndex:(chunk.startIndex||0)+leftEnd-1,
      emitStartM:chunk.emitStartM,
      emitEndM:boundary,
      splitDepth:(chunk.splitDepth||0)+1,
    },
    {
      ...chunk,
      index:`${chunk.index}b`,
      points:rightPts,
      startIndex:(chunk.startIndex||0)+rightStart,
      endIndex:chunk.endIndex,
      emitStartM:boundary,
      emitEndM:chunk.emitEndM,
      splitDepth:(chunk.splitDepth||0)+1,
    }
  ];
}

async function fetchRoadLinkOsrmMatchChunkAdaptive(chunk,relaxed=false,depth=0){
  const fetched=await fetchRoadLinkOsrmMatchChunk(chunk,relaxed);
  const isTooBig=
    !fetched?.ok &&
    fetched?.status===400 &&
    String(fetched?.osrmCode||"").toLowerCase()==="toobig";

  if(
    !isTooBig ||
    depth>=ROADLINK_MATCH_TOO_BIG_MAX_DEPTH ||
    (chunk?.points?.length||0)<ROADLINK_MATCH_TOO_BIG_SPLIT_MIN_POINTS
  ){
    return[{chunk,fetched,splitDepth:depth}];
  }

  const children=splitRoadLinkMatchChunkForTooBig(chunk);
  if(children.length!==2)return[{chunk,fetched,splitDepth:depth}];

  const nested=await Promise.all(children.map(c=>
    fetchRoadLinkOsrmMatchChunkAdaptive(c,relaxed,depth+1)
  ));

  return nested.flat();
}

function sliceRouteByM(route,startM,endM){
  const pts=normalizeRouteInput(route);
  if(pts.length<2) return pts;
  const a=Math.max(0,Number(startM)||0), b=Math.max(a,Number(endM)||a);
  const out=[]; const p0=pointAtRouteM(pts,a); if(p0) out.push(p0);
  let cum=0;
  for(let i=1;i<pts.length-1;i++){
    cum+=haversineM(pts[i-1],pts[i]);
    if(cum>a+0.5&&cum<b-0.5) out.push(pts[i]);
    if(cum>=b) break;
  }
  const p1=pointAtRouteM(pts,b); if(p1) out.push(p1);
  return out.length>=2?out:[p0,p1].filter(Boolean);
}

function routeHintCoverageRatio(hints,totalDistanceM){
  if(!(totalDistanceM>0)) return 0;
  const ranges=(hints||[]).map(h=>[Math.max(0,Number(h.startM)||0),Math.min(totalDistanceM,Number(h.endM)||0)]).filter(([a,b])=>b>a).sort((x,y)=>x[0]-y[0]);
  if(!ranges.length) return 0;
  let covered=0,[a,b]=ranges[0];
  for(let i=1;i<ranges.length;i++){const[c,d]=ranges[i];if(c<=b+2)b=Math.max(b,d);else{covered+=b-a;a=c;b=d;}}
  covered+=b-a; return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,covered/totalDistanceM));
}

function mergeDuplicateRoadLinkHints(hints){
  const arr=(hints||[]).filter(h=>Number.isFinite(Number(h.startM))&&Number.isFinite(Number(h.endM))&&Number(h.endM)>Number(h.startM)).sort((a,b)=>a.startM-b.startM||a.endM-b.endM);
  const out=[];
  for(const h of arr){const last=out[out.length-1];if(last&&last.navLinkId===h.navLinkId&&h.startM<=last.endM+3){last.endM=Math.max(last.endM,h.endM);continue;}out.push({...h});}
  return out;
}

async function fetchRoadLinkOsrmMatchChunk(chunk,relaxed=false){
  const t0=Date.now(); const pts=chunk?.points||[];
  if(pts.length<2) return {ok:false,source:"osrm-match-too-short",elapsedMs:0};
  const coordPath=pts.map(x=>`${x.point[1]},${x.point[0]}`).join(";");
  const radius=relaxed?ROADLINK_MATCH_RETRY_RADIUS_M:ROADLINK_MATCH_RADIUS_M;
  const bearingRange=relaxed?ROADLINK_MATCH_RETRY_BEARING_RANGE_DEG:ROADLINK_MATCH_BEARING_RANGE_DEG;
  const radiuses=pts.map(()=>String(radius)).join(";");
  const bearings=pts.map(x=>`${Math.round((Number(x.bearing)%360+360)%360)},${bearingRange}`).join(";");
  const waypoints=`0;${pts.length-1}`;
  const url=`${NAV_OSRM_DRIVE}/match/v1/driving/${coordPath}?overview=full&geometries=geojson&steps=true&annotations=true&gaps=ignore&tidy=false&generate_hints=false&radiuses=${radiuses}&bearings=${bearings}&waypoints=${waypoints}`;
  const controller=new AbortController(); const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort("roadlink_osrm_match_timeout"),ROADLINK_AUTO_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
  try{
    const r=await fetch(url,{headers:{Accept:"application/json","User-Agent":ROADLINK_AUTO_USER_AGENT},signal:controller.signal});
    const bodyText=await r.text().catch(()=>""); let j=null; try{j=bodyText?JSON.parse(bodyText):null;}catch{}
    if(!r.ok) return {ok:false,source:"osrm-match-http",status:r.status,osrmCode:j?.code||null,osrmMessage:compactRoadLinkHttpMessage(j?.message||bodyText),requestMode:relaxed?"match-relaxed":"match-strict",endpoint:"router.project-osrm.org",elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
    if(j?.code!=="Ok"||!Array.isArray(j?.matchings)||!j.matchings.length) return {ok:false,source:j?.code==="NoMatch"?"osrm-match-no-match":"osrm-match-api",status:r.status,osrmCode:j?.code||null,osrmMessage:compactRoadLinkHttpMessage(j?.message||""),requestMode:relaxed?"match-relaxed":"match-strict",endpoint:"router.project-osrm.org",elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
    return {ok:true,source:"osrm-match-http-ok",status:r.status,osrmCode:j.code,osrmMessage:null,requestMode:relaxed?"match-relaxed":"match-strict",endpoint:"router.project-osrm.org",matchings:j.matchings,tracepoints:Array.isArray(j.tracepoints)?j.tracepoints:[],elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
  }catch(err){
    const isAbort=err?.name==="AbortError"||String(err?.message||err).toLowerCase().includes("abort");
    return {ok:false,source:isAbort?"osrm-match-timeout":"osrm-match-fetch-error",status:null,osrmCode:null,osrmMessage:compactRoadLinkHttpMessage(err?.message||err),requestMode:relaxed?"match-relaxed":"match-strict",endpoint:"router.project-osrm.org",elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};
  }finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
}

function extractRoadLinkHintsFromMatchChunk(rawRoute,totalDistanceM,chunk,fetched){
  const matchings=fetched?.matchings||[], tracepoints=fetched?.tracepoints||[], hints=[], chunkMetrics=[];
  let rawNodePairs=0,acceptedMatchings=0,rejectedMatchings=0,ambiguityTotal=0,ambiguityCount=0,minConfidence=1;
  for(let mi=0;mi<matchings.length;mi++){
    const m=matchings[mi],assigned=[];
    for(let ti=0;ti<tracepoints.length&&ti<chunk.points.length;ti++){const tp=tracepoints[ti];if(tp&&Number(tp.matchings_index)===mi){assigned.push(ti);ambiguityTotal++;if(Number(tp.alternatives_count)>0)ambiguityCount++;}}
    if(assigned.length<2){rejectedMatchings++;continue;}
    const firstIdx=assigned[0],lastIdx=assigned[assigned.length-1],spanStartM=chunk.points[firstIdx].routeM,spanEndM=chunk.points[lastIdx].routeM,spanM=Math.max(0,spanEndM-spanStartM);
    if(!(spanM>5)){rejectedMatchings++;continue;}
    const coords=m?.geometry?.coordinates;
    const geometry=Array.isArray(coords)?coords.map(x=>[Number(x[1]),Number(x[0])]).filter(p=>Number.isFinite(p[0])&&Number.isFinite(p[1])):[];
    if(geometry.length<2){rejectedMatchings++;continue;}
    const reference=sliceRouteByM(rawRoute,spanStartM,spanEndM);
    const candidateDistanceM=Number(m?.distance)||routeLengthM(geometry);
    const distanceRatio=spanM>0?Math.abs(candidateDistanceM-spanM)/spanM:0;
    const forward=sampledRouteDeviation(geometry,reference,96), reverse=sampledRouteDeviation(reference,geometry,96);
    const p90M=Math.max(forward.p90M,reverse.p90M),maxM=Math.max(forward.maxM,reverse.maxM);
    const confidence=Number.isFinite(Number(m?.confidence))?Number(m.confidence):0; minConfidence=Math.min(minConfidence,confidence);
    const payload=osrmRoutePayloadForMatcher({geometry,legs:Array.isArray(m?.legs)?m.legs:[]});
    const localHints=Array.isArray(payload?.navLinkHints)?payload.navLinkHints:[];
    const localRawPairs=localHints.filter(h=>h?.nodeA!=null&&h?.nodeB!=null).length; rawNodePairs+=localRawPairs;
    const localTotalM=Math.max(Number(m?.distance)||0,...localHints.map(h=>Number(h.endM)||0),routeLengthM(geometry));
    const geometryOk=distanceRatio<=ROADLINK_AUTO_MAX_DISTANCE_RATIO&&p90M<=ROADLINK_AUTO_P90_DEVIATION_M&&maxM<=ROADLINK_AUTO_MAX_DEVIATION_M&&confidence>=ROADLINK_MATCH_MIN_CONFIDENCE&&localRawPairs>0&&localTotalM>0;
    chunkMetrics.push({matchingIndex:mi,confidence,distanceRatio,p90M,maxM,rawNodePairs:localRawPairs,accepted:geometryOk});
    if(!geometryOk){rejectedMatchings++;continue;}
    acceptedMatchings++;
    const emitStartM=Math.max(spanStartM,Number(chunk.emitStartM)||spanStartM),emitEndM=Math.min(spanEndM,Number(chunk.emitEndM)||spanEndM);
    for(const h of localHints){
      if(h?.nodeA==null||h?.nodeB==null)continue;
      const a=spanStartM+(Number(h.startM)/localTotalM)*spanM,b=spanStartM+(Number(h.endM)/localTotalM)*spanM;
      if(!(Number.isFinite(a)&&Number.isFinite(b)&&b>a))continue;
      const mid=(a+b)/2;if(mid<emitStartM||mid>emitEndM)continue;
      const startM=Math.max(emitStartM,a),endM=Math.min(emitEndM,b);if(!(endM>startM))continue;
      hints.push({navLinkId:`NLK:${h.nodeA}>${h.nodeB}`,startM,endM,nodeA:String(h.nodeA),nodeB:String(h.nodeB),road:String(h.road||""),isExpressway:h.isExpressway===true});
    }
  }
  const ambiguousFraction=ambiguityTotal>0?ambiguityCount/ambiguityTotal:0;
  const chunkOk=hints.length>0&&acceptedMatchings>0&&ambiguousFraction<=ROADLINK_MATCH_MAX_AMBIGUOUS_FRACTION;
  return {ok:chunkOk,source:chunkOk?"osrm-match-chunk-ok":"osrm-match-chunk-rejected",hints,rawNodePairs,acceptedMatchings,rejectedMatchings,ambiguousFraction,minConfidence:Number.isFinite(minConfidence)?minConfidence:null,metrics:chunkMetrics};
}

async function deriveOsrmRoadLinkHintsUncached(rawRoute,totalDistanceM,trace,fp){
  const t0=Date.now();
  const chunks=buildRoadLinkMatchChunks(trace);
  if(!chunks.length)return{ok:false,source:"osrm-match-no-chunks",hints:[],elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0};

  // Each top-level chunk may recursively split itself when the public OSRM
  // instance answers 400 TooBig. This makes the code robust to server-side
  // max-matching-size changes instead of relying on one hard-coded limit.
  const grouped=await mapLimit(chunks,ROADLINK_MATCH_CONCURRENCY,async chunk=>{
    let leaves=await fetchRoadLinkOsrmMatchChunkAdaptive(chunk,false,0);

    const out=[];
    for(const leaf of leaves){
      let fetched=leaf.fetched;

      if(!fetched?.ok&&["osrm-match-no-match","osrm-match-api"].includes(fetched?.source)){
        const relaxedLeaves=await fetchRoadLinkOsrmMatchChunkAdaptive(leaf.chunk,true,leaf.splitDepth||0);
        for(const rl of relaxedLeaves){
          out.push({
            chunk:rl.chunk,
            fetched:rl.fetched,
            parsed:rl.fetched?.ok?extractRoadLinkHintsFromMatchChunk(rawRoute,totalDistanceM,rl.chunk,rl.fetched):null,
            splitDepth:rl.splitDepth||0,
          });
        }
        continue;
      }

      out.push({
        chunk:leaf.chunk,
        fetched,
        parsed:fetched?.ok?extractRoadLinkHintsFromMatchChunk(rawRoute,totalDistanceM,leaf.chunk,fetched):null,
        splitDepth:leaf.splitDepth||0,
      });
    }
    return out;
  });

  const results=[];
  for(const g of grouped){
    if(g?.__error){results.push(g);continue;}
    if(Array.isArray(g))results.push(...g);
  }

  let hints=[],rawNodePairs=0,matchedChunkCount=0,failedChunkCount=0,relaxedChunkCount=0,
      maxDistanceRatio=0,maxP90M=0,maxDeviationM=0,minConfidence=1,
      ambiguityWeighted=0,ambiguityWeight=0,firstFailure=null,totalHttpMs=0,
      splitLeafCount=0,maxSplitDepth=0,tooBigLeafCount=0;

  for(const r of results){
    if(r?.__error){
      failedChunkCount++;
      firstFailure||={source:"osrm-match-worker-error",message:String(r.__error?.message||r.__error)};
      continue;
    }

    splitLeafCount++;
    maxSplitDepth=Math.max(maxSplitDepth,Number(r?.splitDepth)||0);
    totalHttpMs+=Number(r?.fetched?.elapsedMs)||0;
    if(r?.fetched?.requestMode==="match-relaxed")relaxedChunkCount++;
    if(r?.fetched?.status===400&&String(r?.fetched?.osrmCode||"").toLowerCase()==="toobig")tooBigLeafCount++;

    if(!r?.fetched?.ok||!r?.parsed?.ok){
      failedChunkCount++;
      firstFailure||={
        source:r?.parsed?.source||r?.fetched?.source||"osrm-match-chunk-failed",
        status:r?.fetched?.status??null,
        code:r?.fetched?.osrmCode||null,
        message:r?.fetched?.osrmMessage||null
      };

      if(r?.parsed){
        rawNodePairs+=Number(r.parsed.rawNodePairs)||0;
        ambiguityWeighted+=(Number(r.parsed.ambiguousFraction)||0)*Math.max(1,r.chunk?.points?.length||1);
        ambiguityWeight+=Math.max(1,r.chunk?.points?.length||1);
        for(const m of r.parsed.metrics||[]){
          maxDistanceRatio=Math.max(maxDistanceRatio,Number(m.distanceRatio)||0);
          maxP90M=Math.max(maxP90M,Number(m.p90M)||0);
          maxDeviationM=Math.max(maxDeviationM,Number(m.maxM)||0);
          if(Number.isFinite(Number(m.confidence)))minConfidence=Math.min(minConfidence,Number(m.confidence));
        }
      }
      continue;
    }

    matchedChunkCount++;
    hints.push(...r.parsed.hints);
    rawNodePairs+=Number(r.parsed.rawNodePairs)||0;
    ambiguityWeighted+=(Number(r.parsed.ambiguousFraction)||0)*Math.max(1,r.chunk?.points?.length||1);
    ambiguityWeight+=Math.max(1,r.chunk?.points?.length||1);

    for(const m of r.parsed.metrics||[]){
      maxDistanceRatio=Math.max(maxDistanceRatio,Number(m.distanceRatio)||0);
      maxP90M=Math.max(maxP90M,Number(m.p90M)||0);
      maxDeviationM=Math.max(maxDeviationM,Number(m.maxM)||0);
      if(Number.isFinite(Number(m.confidence)))minConfidence=Math.min(minConfidence,Number(m.confidence));
    }
  }

  hints=mergeDuplicateRoadLinkHints(hints);
  const coverageRatio=routeHintCoverageRatio(hints,totalDistanceM);
  const ambiguousFraction=ambiguityWeight>0?ambiguityWeighted/ambiguityWeight:0;

  if(!hints.length||rawNodePairs<=0||coverageRatio<ROADLINK_MATCH_MIN_COVERAGE_RATIO){
    return{
      ok:false,
      source:"osrm-match-coverage-rejected",
      hints:[],
      tracePointCount:trace.length,
      chunkCount:chunks.length,
      splitLeafCount,
      maxSplitDepth,
      tooBigLeafCount,
      matchedChunkCount,
      failedChunkCount,
      relaxedChunkCount,
      rawNodePairs,
      coverageRatio,
      distanceRatio:maxDistanceRatio,
      p90M:maxP90M,
      maxM:maxDeviationM,
      minConfidence:Number.isFinite(minConfidence)?minConfidence:null,
      ambiguousFraction,
      httpStatus:firstFailure?.status??200,
      osrmCode:firstFailure?.code||(matchedChunkCount?"Ok":null),
      osrmMessage:firstFailure?.message||null,
      endpoint:"router.project-osrm.org",
      requestMode:"match-annotations-true-radii-bearings-adaptive-chunk",
      elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
      httpElapsedMs:totalHttpMs
    };
  }

  const data={
    ok:true,
    source:"osrm-match-node-auto",
    hints,
    tracePointCount:trace.length,
    chunkCount:chunks.length,
    splitLeafCount,
    maxSplitDepth,
    tooBigLeafCount,
    matchedChunkCount,
    failedChunkCount,
    relaxedChunkCount,
    rawNodePairs,
    coverageRatio,
    distanceRatio:maxDistanceRatio,
    p90M:maxP90M,
    maxM:maxDeviationM,
    minConfidence:Number.isFinite(minConfidence)?minConfidence:null,
    ambiguousFraction,
    httpStatus:200,
    osrmCode:"Ok",
    osrmMessage:null,
    endpoint:"router.project-osrm.org",
    requestMode:"match-annotations-true-radii-bearings-adaptive-chunk",
    elapsedMs:Date.now()-t0,
    httpElapsedMs:totalHttpMs,
    cacheHit:false
  };

  const cacheUrl=`https://cache.internal/roadlink-auto/v215/${fp}`;
  await cachePutJSON(cacheUrl,data,ROADLINK_AUTO_CACHE_TTL_SEC);
  return data;
}
async function deriveOsrmRoadLinkHintsCached(rawRoute,totalDistanceM){
  const trace=buildRoadLinkMatchTrace(rawRoute,ROADLINK_MATCH_TRACE_SPACING_M);
  if(trace.length<2)return{ok:false,source:"route-too-short",hints:[],tracePointCount:trace.length};
  const fpRoute=simplifyRouteForMatcher(rawRoute,Math.max(90,totalDistanceM/120),10);
  const fp=roadLinkHash32(fpRoute.map(p=>`${p[0].toFixed(5)},${p[1].toFixed(5)}`).join("|")+`|${Math.round(totalDistanceM)}|v215match`);
  const cacheUrl=`https://cache.internal/roadlink-auto/v215/${fp}`;const cached=await cacheGetJSON(cacheUrl);
  if(cached?.ok&&Array.isArray(cached?.hints)&&cached.hints.length)return{...cached,source:"osrm-match-node-auto-cache",cacheHit:true};
  if(ROADLINK_AUTO_INFLIGHT.has(fp)){try{const shared=await ROADLINK_AUTO_INFLIGHT.get(fp);return{...shared,sharedInflight:true};}catch{}}
  const p=deriveOsrmRoadLinkHintsUncached(rawRoute,totalDistanceM,trace,fp);ROADLINK_AUTO_INFLIGHT.set(fp,p);
  try{return await p;}finally{if(ROADLINK_AUTO_INFLIGHT.get(fp)===p)ROADLINK_AUTO_INFLIGHT.delete(fp);}
}

function annotateEdgesWithNavLinks(edges, hints) {
  const hs = Array.isArray(hints) ? hints : [];
  let hi = 0;
  for (const e of edges) {
    const mid = (e.routeStartM + e.routeEndM) / 2;
    while (hi + 1 < hs.length && hs[hi].endM <= mid) hi++;
    let h = hs[hi];
    if (!h || mid < h.startM || mid > h.endM) {
      h = hs.find(x => mid >= x.startM && mid <= x.endM) || null;
    }
    if (h) {
      e.navLinkId = h.navLinkId;
      e.navNodeA = h.nodeA;
      e.navNodeB = h.nodeB;
      e.navRoad = h.road || "";
      e.navExpressway = h.isExpressway === true;
    } else {
      // Deterministic within this matcher request. Full navigation requests normally
      // receive OSRM node-pair IDs and therefore use globally stable IDs.
      e.navLinkId = `NLK:LOCAL:${e.edgeIndex}`;
    }
  }
  return edges;
}


function buildRoadLinkChains(edges) {
  let chainIndex = -1;
  let chainLengthM = 0;
  let prev = null;

  const stats = {
    chainCount: 0,
    hardBreakCount: 0,
    roadBreakCount: 0,
    headingBreakCount: 0,
    classBreakCount: 0,
    maxLengthBreakCount: 0,
  };

  for (const e of edges || []) {
    const maxChainM = e.navExpressway ? HERE_CHAIN_MAX_EXPRESSWAY_M : HERE_CHAIN_MAX_LOCAL_M;
    let shouldBreak = !prev;
    let breakReason = "start";

    if (prev) {
      const classBreak = !!(prev.navExpressway !== e.navExpressway);
      const headingJump = angleDeltaDeg(prev.heading, e.heading);
      const headingBreak = headingJump > HERE_CHAIN_MAX_HEADING_JUMP_DEG;

      const prevRoad = String(prev.navRoad || "").trim();
      const road = String(e.navRoad || "").trim();
      const roadCompat = roadNameCompatibility(prevRoad, road);
      const roadBreak = !!(
        prevRoad && road &&
        roadCompat < HERE_CHAIN_MIN_ROAD_COMPAT
      );

      const maxLengthBreak = chainLengthM + e.lengthM > maxChainM;

      if (classBreak || headingBreak || roadBreak || maxLengthBreak) {
        shouldBreak = true;
        if (classBreak) { stats.classBreakCount++; breakReason = "class"; }
        else if (headingBreak) { stats.headingBreakCount++; breakReason = "heading"; }
        else if (roadBreak) { stats.roadBreakCount++; breakReason = "road"; }
        else { stats.maxLengthBreakCount++; breakReason = "length"; }
      }
    }

    if (shouldBreak) {
      if (prev) stats.hardBreakCount++;
      chainIndex++;
      chainLengthM = 0;
    }

    e.roadChainId = `RLC:${chainIndex}`;
    e.roadChainBreakReason = shouldBreak ? breakReason : null;
    e.roadChainStartM = e.routeStartM;
    chainLengthM += e.lengthM;
    e.roadChainLengthFromStartM = chainLengthM;

    prev = e;
  }

  stats.chainCount = chainIndex + 1;
  return stats;
}

function projectPointToRouteM(point, route) {
  const pts = normalizeRouteInput(route);
  if (pts.length < 2) return null;
  let best = null, cum = 0;
  for (let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++) {
    const a=pts[i], b=pts[i+1];
    const segM=haversineM(a,b);
    if (!(segM>0)) continue;
    const d=pointToSegmentDistanceLocalM(point,a,b);
    const routeM=cum + Math.max(0,Math.min(1,d.t))*segM;
    if (!best || d.distanceM < best.distanceM) best={distanceM:d.distanceM,routeM,segIndex:i,t:d.t};
    cum += segM;
  }
  return best;
}

function pointAtRouteM(route, targetM) {
  const pts=normalizeRouteInput(route);
  if (!pts.length) return null;
  let cum=0;
  const t=Math.max(0,Number(targetM)||0);
  for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){
    const d=haversineM(pts[i],pts[i+1]);
    if (cum+d>=t && d>0) return interpolateLatLon(pts[i],pts[i+1],(t-cum)/d);
    cum+=d;
  }
  return pts[pts.length-1];
}

function geometricTurnAtRouteM(route, routeM, fallback=false) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(Number(routeM))) return !!fallback;
  const m=Number(routeM), before=pointAtRouteM(route, Math.max(0,m-28));
  const center=pointAtRouteM(route,m), after=pointAtRouteM(route,m+28);
  if (!before || !center || !after) return !!fallback;
  const delta=angleDeltaDeg(bearingDeg(before,center),bearingDeg(center,after));
  if (delta <= 24) return false;
  if (delta >= 38) return true;
  return !!fallback;
}

function parseWktPoints(wkt) {
  const s = String(wkt || "").trim();
  if (!s) return [];

  // TDX 常見 LINESTRING (lon lat, lon lat, ...)
  // MULTILINESTRING 也採「照出現順序攤平」供 matcher 使用。
  const nums = s.match(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g);
  if (!nums || nums.length < 4) return [];

  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < nums.length; i += 2) {
    const lon = Number(nums[i]);
    const lat = Number(nums[i + 1]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
    if (lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lon < -180 || lon > 180) continue;

    const last = out[out.length - 1];
    if (last && Math.abs(last[0] - lat) < 1e-10 && Math.abs(last[1] - lon) < 1e-10) continue;
    out.push([lat, lon]);
  }
  return out;
}

function trafficDataAgeMin(dataTime) {
  if (!dataTime) return null;
  const t = Date.parse(dataTime);
  if (!Number.isFinite(t)) return null;
  return (Date.now() - t) / 60000;
}

function isUsableTdxSpeed(speed, dataTime) {
  const v = Number(speed);
  if (!(Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0 && v <= 160)) return false;

  // 來源 timestamp 能解析時，15 分鐘以上視為 stale。
  // 解析不了則保留，避免因格式差異把資料全丟掉。
  const age = trafficDataAgeMin(dataTime);
  if (Number.isFinite(age) && (age < -3 || age > 15)) return false;
  return true;
}

function segmentBBox(points) {
  let minLat = Infinity, maxLat = -Infinity, minLon = Infinity, maxLon = -Infinity;
  for (const p of points || []) {
    if (!Array.isArray(p) || p.length < 2) continue;
    if (p[0] < minLat) minLat = p[0];
    if (p[0] > maxLat) maxLat = p[0];
    if (p[1] < minLon) minLon = p[1];
    if (p[1] > maxLon) maxLon = p[1];
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(minLat)) return null;
  return { minLat, maxLat, minLon, maxLon };
}

function normalizeTdxTrafficSegment(scope, live, geomPoints) {
  const speedKph = Number(live?.speedKph);
  const status = resolveTdxTrafficStatus(scope, speedKph, live?.level);
  if (!status.closed && !status.validTraffic) return null;
  if (!Array.isArray(geomPoints) || geomPoints.length < 2) return null;
  if (!status.closed && !isUsableTdxSpeed(speedKph, live?.dataTime)) return null;
  return {
    provider:"TDX", sourceType:status.closed ? "TDX_CLOSED" : "TDX_LIVE", scope,
    id:`tdx:${scope}:${live.sectionId}`, sectionId:live.sectionId, road:live.road || "", direction:live.direction || "",
    speedKph:status.closed ? null : speedKph, freeFlowKph:null, confidence:1, jamFactor:null,
    level:live.level ?? null, trafficLevel:status.trafficLevel, validTraffic:status.validTraffic,
    closed:status.closed, insufficient:status.insufficient, fallbackToHere:status.fallbackToHere,
    dataTime:live.dataTime || null, points:geomPoints, bbox:segmentBBox(geomPoints)
  };
}

async function getTdxTrafficSegmentsForMatcher(env, highwayOnly = false) {
  const scopeErrors = {};

  async function buildScope(scope) {
    try {
      let geomArr, raw, meta = {}, stdMap = {};

      if (scope === "freeway") {
        [geomArr, raw] = await Promise.all([
          getFreewayGeometryCached(env),
          getFreewayLiveCached(env),
        ]);
        meta = await safeOptional(() => getFreewaySectionMetaCached(env), {}, "freeway section metadata");
      } else if (scope === "highway") {
        [geomArr, raw] = await Promise.all([
          getHighwayGeometryCached(env),
          getHighwayLiveCached(env),
        ]);
        [meta, stdMap] = await Promise.all([
          safeOptional(() => getHighwaySectionMetaCached(env), {}, "highway section metadata"),
          safeOptional(() => getHighwayStandardCached(env), {}, "highway congestion standard"),
        ]);
      } else {
        [geomArr, raw] = await Promise.all([
          getTaipeiGeometryCached(env),
          getTaipeiLiveCached(env),
        ]);
        meta = await safeOptional(() => getTaipeiSectionMetaCached(env), {}, "taipei section metadata");
      }

      const geomMap = new Map();
      for (const g of geomArr || []) {
        const sid = String(g?.SectionID || "");
        if (!sid) continue;
        const pts = parseWktPoints(g?.Geometry);
        if (pts.length >= 2) geomMap.set(sid, pts);
      }

      const out = [];
      for (const s of raw?.LiveTraffics || []) {
        const sid = String(s?.SectionID || "");
        if (!sid) continue;
        const m = meta?.[sid] || {};
        const speed = toNumberOrNull(s.TravelSpeed);
        let level = 0;

        if (scope === "freeway") {
          level = normalizeLevelNumber(s.CongestionLevel);
        } else if (scope === "highway") {
          level = resolveHighwayLevel({
            congestionLevel: s.CongestionLevel,
            congestionLevelID: s.CongestionLevelID,
            speedKph: speed,
          }, stdMap);
        } else {
          level = normalizeTaipeiLevel(s.CongestionLevel);
        }

        const live = {
          sectionId: sid,
          road: m.road || "",
          direction: m.directionLabel || "",
          speedKph: speed,
          level,
          dataTime: s.DataCollectTime || null,
        };

        const seg = normalizeTdxTrafficSegment(scope, live, geomMap.get(sid));
        if (seg) out.push(seg);
      }

      out.stale = raw?.__stale === true;
      out.staleReason = raw?.__staleReason || null;
      return out;
    } catch (err) {
      scopeErrors[scope] = err?.message || String(err);
      console.warn(`TDX matcher scope=${scope} unavailable; other scopes continue`, scopeErrors[scope]);
      return [];
    }
  }

  // v1.5: Taipei TDX is intentionally excluded from routing/matcher because the feed is unstable.
  // Taipei/local roads fall through directly to HERE, then OSRM baseline.
  const [freeway, highway] = await Promise.all([
    highwayOnly ? Promise.resolve([]) : buildScope("freeway"),
    buildScope("highway"),
  ]);

  const out = [...freeway, ...highway];
  out.tdxUnavailable = out.length === 0 && Object.keys(scopeErrors).length === (highwayOnly ? 1 : 2);
  out.tdxError = Object.keys(scopeErrors).length ? JSON.stringify(scopeErrors) : null;
  out.tdxScopeErrors = scopeErrors;
  out.tdxStaleScopes = [
    freeway.stale ? "freeway" : null,
    highway.stale ? "highway" : null,
  ].filter(Boolean);
  return out;
}

function metersPerDegLon(lat) {
  return Math.max(1000, 111320 * Math.cos(Number(lat) * Math.PI / 180));
}

function gridKeyForPoint(p, cellM = MATCH_GRID_M) {
  const lat = Number(p[0]);
  const lon = Number(p[1]);
  const y = Math.floor((lat * 111320) / cellM);
  const x = Math.floor((lon * metersPerDegLon(lat)) / cellM);
  return `${x}:${y}`;
}

function gridXYForPoint(p, cellM = MATCH_GRID_M) {
  const lat = Number(p[0]);
  const lon = Number(p[1]);
  return {
    x: Math.floor((lon * metersPerDegLon(lat)) / cellM),
    y: Math.floor((lat * 111320) / cellM),
  };
}


const HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M = 220;
const HERE_DEBUG_SAMPLE_M = 120;
const HERE_DEBUG_MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SEG = 180;

function hereDebugMercatorXY(lat, lon) {
  const R = 6378137;
  const x = R * (Number(lon) * Math.PI / 180);
  const clampedLat = Math.max(-85.05112878, Math.min(85.05112878, Number(lat)));
  const y = R * Math.log(Math.tan(Math.PI / 4 + (clampedLat * Math.PI / 180) / 2));
  return [x, y];
}

function hereDebugGridKey(p) {
  const [x, y] = hereDebugMercatorXY(p[0], p[1]);
  return `${Math.floor(x / HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M)}:${Math.floor(y / HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M)}`;
}

function hereDebugNearbyKeys(p, radiusM) {
  const [x, y] = hereDebugMercatorXY(p[0], p[1]);
  const gx = Math.floor(x / HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M);
  const gy = Math.floor(y / HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M);
  const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(radiusM / HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M));
  const keys = [];
  for (let dx = -n; dx <= n; dx++) {
    for (let dy = -n; dy <= n; dy++) {
      keys.push(`${gx + dx}:${gy + dy}`);
    }
  }
  return keys;
}

function hereDebugSamplePolyline(points) {
  if (!Array.isArray(points) || points.length < 2) return [];
  const out = [];
  const totalPts = points.length;

  // 先均勻保留原始 shape vertices，避免超長 polyline 產生過多 sample。
  const stride = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalPts / 90));
  const base = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < totalPts; i += stride) base.push(points[i]);
  if (base[base.length - 1] !== points[totalPts - 1]) base.push(points[totalPts - 1]);

  for (let i = 0; i < base.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = base[i], b = base[i + 1];
    if (!out.length) out.push(a);
    const d = haversineM(a, b);
    const n = Math.min(8, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(d / HERE_DEBUG_SAMPLE_M)));
    for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) {
      out.push(interpolateLatLon(a, b, k / n));
      if (out.length >= HERE_DEBUG_MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SEG) return out;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function buildHereDebugGrid(segments) {
  const grid = new Map();

  for (let si = 0; si < (segments || []).length; si++) {
    const seg = segments[si];
    if (!Array.isArray(seg?.points) || seg.points.length < 2) continue;

    const seen = new Set();
    const samples = hereDebugSamplePolyline(seg.points);
    for (const p of samples) {
      const key = hereDebugGridKey(p);
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      if (!grid.has(key)) grid.set(key, []);
      grid.get(key).push(si);
    }
  }

  return grid;
}

function hereDebugCandidates(grid, p, radiusM) {
  const out = new Set();
  for (const key of hereDebugNearbyKeys(p, radiusM)) {
    const arr = grid.get(key);
    if (!arr) continue;
    for (const i of arr) out.add(i);
  }
  return [...out];
}

function buildTrafficGrid(segments, cellM = MATCH_GRID_M) {
  const grid = new Map();

  for (let si = 0; si < (segments || []).length; si++) {
    const seg = segments[si];
    const pts = seg?.points;
    if (!Array.isArray(pts) || pts.length < 2) continue;

    // 沿 geometry 線逐小段採樣，讓長線段不會只落進 vertices 的格子。
    const keys = new Set();

    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = pts[i];
      const b = pts[i + 1];
      const d = haversineM(a, b);
      const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(d / (cellM * 0.65)));
      for (let k = 0; k <= n; k++) {
        keys.add(gridKeyForPoint(interpolateLatLon(a, b, k / n), cellM));
      }
    }

    for (const key of keys) {
      if (!grid.has(key)) grid.set(key, []);
      grid.get(key).push(si);
    }
  }

  return { grid, segments, cellM };
}

function candidateIndexesNear(index, p, maxDistM) {
  const { grid, cellM } = index;
  const xy = gridXYForPoint(p, cellM);
  const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(maxDistM / cellM) + 1);
  const set = new Set();

  for (let dx = -n; dx <= n; dx++) {
    for (let dy = -n; dy <= n; dy++) {
      const arr = grid.get(`${xy.x + dx}:${xy.y + dy}`);
      if (!arr) continue;
      for (const si of arr) set.add(si);
    }
  }

  return [...set];
}

function projectPointLocalMeters(p, refLat) {
  return {
    x: p[1] * metersPerDegLon(refLat),
    y: p[0] * 111320,
  };
}

function pointToSegmentDistanceLocalM(p, a, b) {
  const refLat = p[0];
  const P = projectPointLocalMeters(p, refLat);
  const A = projectPointLocalMeters(a, refLat);
  const B = projectPointLocalMeters(b, refLat);

  const vx = B.x - A.x;
  const vy = B.y - A.y;
  const wx = P.x - A.x;
  const wy = P.y - A.y;
  const vv = vx * vx + vy * vy;

  let t = vv > 0 ? (wx * vx + wy * vy) / vv : 0;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));

  const qx = A.x + vx * t;
  const qy = A.y + vy * t;
  const dx = P.x - qx;
  const dy = P.y - qy;

  return {
    distanceM: Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy),
    t,
  };
}

// Weak references let per-geometry indexes be reclaimed after the request.
// The BVH keeps every original segment; it does not simplify roads or relax matching thresholds.
const trafficPolylineIndexes = new WeakMap();
function trafficPolylineIndex(points) {
  let tree=trafficPolylineIndexes.get(points);
  if(tree) return tree;
  function build(lo,hi){
    const node={lo,hi,minLat:Infinity,maxLat:-Infinity,minLon:Infinity,maxLon:-Infinity};
    if(hi-lo<=8){
      for(let i=lo;i<=hi;i++){
        const p=points[i];node.minLat=Math.min(node.minLat,p[0]);node.maxLat=Math.max(node.maxLat,p[0]);
        node.minLon=Math.min(node.minLon,p[1]);node.maxLon=Math.max(node.maxLon,p[1]);
      }
    }else{
      const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;node.left=build(lo,mid);node.right=build(mid,hi);
      node.minLat=Math.min(node.left.minLat,node.right.minLat);node.maxLat=Math.max(node.left.maxLat,node.right.maxLat);
      node.minLon=Math.min(node.left.minLon,node.right.minLon);node.maxLon=Math.max(node.left.maxLon,node.right.maxLon);
    }
    return node;
  }
  tree=build(0,points.length-1);trafficPolylineIndexes.set(points,tree);return tree;
}
function nearestOnPolyline(p, points) {
  if(!Array.isArray(points)||points.length<2)return null;
  const sx=metersPerDegLon(p[0]),px=p[1]*sx,py=p[0]*111320;
  let bestSq=Infinity,bestIndex=-1;
  function scan(lo,hi){
    for(let i=lo;i<hi;i++){
      const a=points[i],b=points[i+1];
      const ax=a[1]*sx,ay=a[0]*111320,bx=b[1]*sx,by=b[0]*111320;
      const vx=bx-ax,vy=by-ay,wx=px-ax,wy=py-ay,vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0));
      const dx=px-(ax+vx*t),dy=py-(ay+vy*t),sq=dx*dx+dy*dy;
      if(sq<bestSq||(sq===bestSq&&(bestIndex<0||i<bestIndex))){bestSq=sq;bestIndex=i;}
    }
  }
  function lowerBound(node){
    const dx=Math.max(node.minLon*sx-px,0,px-node.maxLon*sx);
    const dy=Math.max(node.minLat*111320-py,0,py-node.maxLat*111320);
    return dx*dx+dy*dy;
  }
  function visit(node){
    // Floating-point cushion preserves candidates on exact bounding-box edges.
    if(lowerBound(node)>bestSq+1e-6)return;
    if(!node.left){scan(node.lo,node.hi);return;}
    if(lowerBound(node.left)<=lowerBound(node.right)){visit(node.left);visit(node.right);}
    else{visit(node.right);visit(node.left);}
  }
  if(points.length<=24)scan(0,points.length-1);else visit(trafficPolylineIndex(points));
  return bestIndex<0?null:{distanceM:Math.sqrt(bestSq),segIndex:bestIndex,segmentHeading:bearingDeg(points[bestIndex],points[bestIndex+1])};
}

function scoreTrafficCandidate(edge, trafficSeg, maxDistM, prevChoice, headingRejectDeg = MATCH_HEADING_REJECT_DEG) {
  // Reject on the midpoint/heading before spending two more nearest-segment searches.
  const centerNearest=nearestOnPolyline(interpolateLatLon(edge.a,edge.b,0.5),trafficSeg.points);
  if(!centerNearest||centerNearest.distanceM>maxDistM)return null;
  const headingDelta=angleDeltaDeg(edge.heading,centerNearest.segmentHeading);
  if(headingDelta>headingRejectDeg)return null;
  const first=nearestOnPolyline(edge.a,trafficSeg.points),last=nearestOnPolyline(edge.b,trafficSeg.points);
  if(!first||!last)return null;
  const samples=[edge.a,edge.midpoint,edge.b];
  const distSum=first.distanceM+centerNearest.distanceM+last.distanceM;
  const within=Number(first.distanceM<=maxDistM)+1+Number(last.distanceM<=maxDistM);

  const avgDist = distSum / samples.length;
  const distanceScore = Math.max(0, 1 - avgDist / maxDistM);
  const headingScore = Math.max(0, 1 - headingDelta / headingRejectDeg);
  const overlapScore = within / samples.length;

  let continuityScore = 0;
  if (prevChoice) {
    if (prevChoice.id === trafficSeg.id) continuityScore = 1;
    else if (
      prevChoice.provider === trafficSeg.provider &&
      prevChoice.road &&
      trafficSeg.road &&
      prevChoice.road === trafficSeg.road
    ) continuityScore = 0.60;
    else if (
      prevChoice.provider === "TDX" &&
      trafficSeg.provider === "TDX" &&
      prevChoice.scope === trafficSeg.scope
    ) continuityScore = 0.20;
  }

  let score;
  if (trafficSeg.provider === "TDX" && trafficSeg.scope === "freeway") {
    score =
      distanceScore * 0.20 +
      headingScore * 0.35 +
      overlapScore * 0.20 +
      continuityScore * 0.25;
    score += 0.03;
  } else if (trafficSeg.provider === "TDX" && trafficSeg.scope === "highway") {
    score =
      distanceScore * 0.30 +
      headingScore * 0.30 +
      overlapScore * 0.25 +
      continuityScore * 0.15;
    score += 0.02;
  } else if (trafficSeg.provider === "TDX" && trafficSeg.scope === "taipei") {
    score =
      distanceScore * 0.38 +
      headingScore * 0.30 +
      overlapScore * 0.22 +
      continuityScore * 0.10;
    score += 0.015;
  } else {
    score =
      distanceScore * 0.35 +
      headingScore * 0.30 +
      overlapScore * 0.25 +
      continuityScore * 0.10;
  }

  return {
    score: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, score)),
    distanceM: centerNearest.distanceM,
    avgDistanceM: avgDist,
    headingDeltaDeg: headingDelta,
    overlapRatio: overlapScore,
  };
}

function thresholdsForTrafficSegment(seg) {
  if (seg?.provider === "TDX" && seg?.scope === "freeway") {
    return {
      maxDistM: MATCH_TDX_FREEWAY_MAX_M,
      minScore: MATCH_TDX_FREEWAY_MIN_SCORE,
      headingRejectDeg: MATCH_FREEWAY_HEADING_REJECT_DEG,
    };
  }
  if (seg?.provider === "TDX" && seg?.scope === "highway") {
    return {
      maxDistM: MATCH_TDX_HIGHWAY_MAX_M,
      minScore: MATCH_TDX_HIGHWAY_MIN_SCORE,
      headingRejectDeg: MATCH_HEADING_REJECT_DEG,
    };
  }
  if (seg?.provider === "TDX" && seg?.scope === "taipei") {
    return {
      maxDistM: MATCH_TDX_TAIPEI_MAX_M,
      minScore: MATCH_TDX_TAIPEI_MIN_SCORE,
      headingRejectDeg: 60,
    };
  }
  return {
    maxDistM: MATCH_HERE_MAX_M,
    minScore: MATCH_HERE_MIN_SCORE,
    headingRejectDeg: MATCH_HEADING_REJECT_DEG,
  };
}

function matchEdgesAgainstTraffic(edges, trafficSegments, {
  onlyUnmatched = false,
  existing = null,
  providerFilter = null,
  onIndexBuilt = null,
} = {}) {
  const maxSearchM = Math.max(
    MATCH_TDX_FREEWAY_MAX_M,
    MATCH_TDX_HIGHWAY_MAX_M,
    MATCH_TDX_TAIPEI_MAX_M,
    MATCH_HERE_MAX_M
  );

  const index = buildTrafficGrid(trafficSegments || []);
  if(onIndexBuilt)onIndexBuilt();
  const matches = existing
    ? existing.map((x) => x ? { ...x } : null)
    : new Array(edges.length).fill(null);

  let prevChoice = null;
  let prevChainId = null;

  for (let ei = 0; ei < edges.length; ei++) {
    const edge = edges[ei];
    const chainId = edge.roadChainId || edge.navLinkId || `RLC:LOCAL:${ei}`;

    // v2.2.1: continuity resets at a RoadLink CHAIN boundary, not at every
    // node-to-node OSM RoadLink. This keeps HERE locked to the same physical
    // road while still creating a hard wall at road/class/direction changes.
    if (prevChainId !== null && chainId !== prevChainId) prevChoice = null;
    prevChainId = chainId;

    if (onlyUnmatched && matches[ei]) {
      prevChoice = matches[ei]?._trafficSeg || null;
      continue;
    }

    const candidateIdx = candidateIndexesNear(index, edge.midpoint, maxSearchM);
    let best = null;
    let second = null;
    let stickyCandidate = null;

    for (const si of candidateIdx) {
      const seg = trafficSegments[si];
      if (providerFilter && !providerFilter(seg)) continue;

      const closed = seg?.closed === true || Number(seg?.trafficLevel) === -1;
      const speed = Number(seg?.speedKph);
      if (!closed && !(Number.isFinite(speed) && speed > 0 && speed <= 160)) continue;

      const th = thresholdsForTrafficSegment(seg);
      const scored = scoreTrafficCandidate(
        edge, seg, th.maxDistM, prevChoice, th.headingRejectDeg
      );
      if (!scored) continue;

      const roadCompat = roadNameCompatibility(edge.navRoad, seg.road);
      const fc = Number(seg.functionalClass);

      // Hard class wall for the most dangerous bridge/ground mistake.
      if (edge.navExpressway && seg.provider === "HERE" && Number.isFinite(fc) && fc >= 4) continue;

      let adjustedScore = scored.score;
      if (roadCompat >= 0.70) adjustedScore += 0.055;
      else if (roadCompat > 0) adjustedScore += 0.025 * roadCompat;
      if (edge.navExpressway && Number(seg.functionalClass) <= 2) adjustedScore += 0.035;
      if (edge.navExpressway && Number(seg.functionalClass) >= 3) adjustedScore -= 0.060;
      if (edge.navRoad && seg.road && roadCompat === 0) adjustedScore -= 0.025;

      const isStickyHere = !!(
        seg.provider === "HERE" &&
        prevChoice?.provider === "HERE" &&
        prevChoice.id === seg.id &&
        scored.distanceM <= HERE_STICKY_MAX_DIST_M &&
        scored.headingDeltaDeg <= HERE_STICKY_MAX_HEADING_DEG &&
        scored.overlapRatio >= HERE_STICKY_MIN_OVERLAP
      );

      if (isStickyHere) adjustedScore += HERE_STICKY_SCORE_BONUS;

      adjustedScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, adjustedScore));
      if (adjustedScore < th.minScore) continue;

      const candidate = {
        provider: seg.provider,
        sourceType: seg.sourceType || seg.provider,
        trafficId: seg.id,
        sectionId: seg.sectionId || null,
        road: seg.road || "",
        scope: seg.scope || "",
        speedKph: closed ? null : speed,
        closed,
        traversability: seg.traversability || null,
        freeFlowKph: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.freeFlowKph)) ? Number(seg.freeFlowKph) : null,
        jamFactor: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.jamFactor)) ? Number(seg.jamFactor) : null,
        confidence: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.confidence)) ? Number(seg.confidence) : null,
        trafficLevel: Number.isFinite(Number(seg.trafficLevel)) ? Number(seg.trafficLevel) : 0,
        level: seg.level ?? null,
        dataTime: seg.dataTime || null,
        matchScore: adjustedScore,
        rawMatchScore: scored.score,
        matchDistanceM: scored.distanceM,
        avgDistanceM: scored.avgDistanceM,
        headingDeltaDeg: scored.headingDeltaDeg,
        overlapRatio: scored.overlapRatio,
        functionalClass: seg.functionalClass ?? null,
        navLinkId: edge.navLinkId,
        roadChainId: chainId,
        navNodeA: edge.navNodeA,
        navNodeB: edge.navNodeB,
        navRoad: edge.navRoad || "",
        stickyEligible: isStickyHere,
        _trafficSeg: seg,
      };

      if (isStickyHere) stickyCandidate = candidate;

      if (!best || candidate.matchScore > best.matchScore) {
        second = best;
        best = candidate;
      } else if (!second || candidate.matchScore > second.matchScore) {
        second = candidate;
      }
    }

    // Sequence preference: if the previous HERE segment still fits this edge well,
    // do not switch just because another candidate wins by a tiny score margin.
    if (
      stickyCandidate &&
      best &&
      best.trafficId !== stickyCandidate.trafficId &&
      (best.matchScore - stickyCandidate.matchScore) < HERE_STICKY_SWITCH_MARGIN
    ) {
      second = best;
      best = { ...stickyCandidate, stickyRetained: true };
    }

    if (best) {
      const gap = second ? best.matchScore - second.matchScore : null;

      // Sticky continuity is itself evidence. At a chain start there is no sticky
      // evidence, so a true stacked-road tie is still marked ambiguous.
      const stackedAmbiguous = !!(
        best.provider === "HERE" &&
        !best.stickyRetained &&
        second &&
        second.trafficId !== best.trafficId &&
        (
          gap <= NAVLINK_AMBIGUITY_SCORE_GAP ||
          (
            Number(best.matchDistanceM) <= 10 &&
            Number(second.matchDistanceM) <= 10 &&
            Number(best.headingDeltaDeg) <= 18 &&
            Number(second.headingDeltaDeg) <= 18
          )
        ) &&
        Number(best.matchDistanceM) <= NAVLINK_AMBIGUITY_MAX_DIST_M &&
        Number(second.matchDistanceM) <= NAVLINK_AMBIGUITY_MAX_DIST_M &&
        Number(best.headingDeltaDeg) <= 28 &&
        Number(second.headingDeltaDeg) <= 28
      );

      best.ambiguousMatch = stackedAmbiguous;
      best.ambiguityScoreGap = gap;
      best.ambiguityOtherTrafficId = stackedAmbiguous ? second.trafficId : null;
      matches[ei] = best;
      prevChoice = best._trafficSeg;
    } else {
      prevChoice = null;
    }
  }

  return matches;
}

function stabilizeMatchesByNavLink(edges, matches, provider) {
  const groups = new Map();
  for (let i=0;i<edges.length;i++) {
    const id=edges[i].navLinkId || `NLK:LOCAL:${i}`;
    if (!groups.has(id)) groups.set(id,[]);
    groups.get(id).push(i);
  }
  let ambiguousNavLinks=0, stabilizedEdges=0;
  const out=matches.map(x=>x ? {...x}:null);

  for (const [navLinkId, idxs] of groups) {
    const votes=new Map();
    let totalLen=0, ambiguousLen=0;
    for (const i of idxs) {
      const e=edges[i], m=out[i]; totalLen+=e.lengthM;
      if (!m || (provider && m.provider!==provider)) continue;
      if (m.ambiguousMatch) ambiguousLen+=e.lengthM;
      const key=m.trafficId || `${m.provider}:${m.road}:${m.speedKph}`;
      const v=votes.get(key) || {weight:0,match:m};
      v.weight += e.lengthM * Math.max(0.01,Number(m.matchScore)||0.01);
      if ((Number(m.matchScore)||0) > (Number(v.match.matchScore)||0)) v.match=m;
      votes.set(key,v);
    }
    const ranked=[...votes.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.weight-a.weight);
    if (!ranked.length) continue;

    // If most of a NAVLINK is geometrically indistinguishable between parallel
    // HERE links, do not guess. OSRM baseline is safer than a wrong stacked road.
    const ambiguous = provider === "HERE" && totalLen>0 && ambiguousLen/totalLen >= 0.45;
    if (ambiguous) {
      ambiguousNavLinks++;
      for (const i of idxs) if (!provider || out[i]?.provider===provider) out[i]=null;
      continue;
    }

    const winner=ranked[0].match;
    for (const i of idxs) {
      if (!provider || !out[i] || out[i].provider===provider) {
        if (out[i]?.provider===provider && out[i].trafficId!==winner.trafficId) stabilizedEdges++;
        if (out[i]?.provider===provider) out[i]={...winner, navLinkId, selectionReason:`${winner.selectionReason||provider}_NAVLINK_STABLE`};
      }
    }
  }
  return {matches:out, ambiguousNavLinks, stabilizedEdges};
}


function stabilizeHereMatchesByChain(edges, matches) {
  const out = matches.map(x => x ? { ...x } : null);
  const chains = new Map();

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const id = edges[i].roadChainId || edges[i].navLinkId || `RLC:LOCAL:${i}`;
    if (!chains.has(id)) chains.set(id, []);
    chains.get(id).push(i);
  }

  let ambiguousFallbackChains = 0;
  let ambiguousFallbackEdges = 0;
  let smoothedEdges = 0;
  let stickyRetainedEdges = out.filter(x => x?.stickyRetained).length;
  let nullGapFilledEdges = 0;

  const canReuse = (candidateMatch, edge) => {
    const seg = candidateMatch?._trafficSeg;
    if (!seg || seg.provider !== "HERE") return null;

    const th = thresholdsForTrafficSegment(seg);
    const scored = scoreTrafficCandidate(
      edge,
      seg,
      Math.min(th.maxDistM, HERE_STICKY_MAX_DIST_M),
      null,
      Math.min(th.headingRejectDeg, HERE_STICKY_MAX_HEADING_DEG)
    );
    if (!scored) return null;
    if (
      scored.distanceM > HERE_STICKY_MAX_DIST_M ||
      scored.headingDeltaDeg > HERE_STICKY_MAX_HEADING_DEG ||
      scored.overlapRatio < HERE_STICKY_MIN_OVERLAP
    ) return null;

    return {
      ...candidateMatch,
      navLinkId: edge.navLinkId,
      roadChainId: edge.roadChainId,
      navNodeA: edge.navNodeA,
      navNodeB: edge.navNodeB,
      navRoad: edge.navRoad || "",
      matchDistanceM: scored.distanceM,
      avgDistanceM: scored.avgDistanceM,
      headingDeltaDeg: scored.headingDeltaDeg,
      overlapRatio: scored.overlapRatio,
      selectionReason: "HERE_CHAIN_STICKY_SMOOTH",
      ambiguousMatch: false,
      stickyRetained: true,
    };
  };

  for (const [chainId, idxs] of chains) {
    const totalLen = idxs.reduce((s, i) => s + edges[i].lengthM, 0);
    const ambiguousLen = idxs.reduce(
      (s, i) => s + (out[i]?.provider === "HERE" && out[i]?.ambiguousMatch ? edges[i].lengthM : 0),
      0
    );

    // If an entire chain is still mostly ambiguous even after sticky sequence
    // matching, do not guess the stacked road. Only HERE matches in that chain
    // are cleared; OSRM baseline remains the safe fallback.
    if (
      totalLen > 0 &&
      ambiguousLen / totalLen >= HERE_CHAIN_AMBIGUOUS_FALLBACK_FRACTION
    ) {
      ambiguousFallbackChains++;
      for (const i of idxs) {
        if (out[i]?.provider === "HERE") {
          out[i] = null;
          ambiguousFallbackEdges++;
        }
      }
      continue;
    }

    // Any remaining isolated ambiguous edge is safer as baseline unless the
    // surrounding sequence can prove the same HERE segment on both sides.
    for (let p = 0; p < idxs.length; p++) {
      const i = idxs[p];
      if (!(out[i]?.provider === "HERE" && out[i]?.ambiguousMatch)) continue;

      const prevI = p > 0 ? idxs[p - 1] : null;
      const nextI = p + 1 < idxs.length ? idxs[p + 1] : null;
      const prev = prevI != null ? out[prevI] : null;
      const next = nextI != null ? out[nextI] : null;

      if (
        prev?.provider === "HERE" &&
        next?.provider === "HERE" &&
        prev.trafficId &&
        prev.trafficId === next.trafficId
      ) {
        const reused = canReuse(prev, edges[i]);
        if (reused) {
          out[i] = reused;
          smoothedEdges++;
          continue;
        }
      }
