      out[i] = null;
      ambiguousFallbackEdges++;
    }

    // Smooth one short wrong-ID run when the same HERE traffic segment appears
    // immediately before and after it, and that segment still geometrically fits.
    let p = 1;
    while (p < idxs.length - 1) {
      const leftI = idxs[p - 1];
      const left = out[leftI];
      if (!(left?.provider === "HERE" && left.trafficId)) { p++; continue; }

      let q = p;
      let runLen = 0;
      while (q < idxs.length) {
        const m = out[idxs[q]];
        if (m?.provider === "HERE" && m.trafficId === left.trafficId) break;
        runLen += edges[idxs[q]].lengthM;
        if (runLen > HERE_CHAIN_SMOOTH_MAX_M) break;
        q++;
      }

      if (
        q < idxs.length &&
        runLen > 0 &&
        runLen <= HERE_CHAIN_SMOOTH_MAX_M &&
        out[idxs[q]]?.provider === "HERE" &&
        out[idxs[q]].trafficId === left.trafficId
      ) {
        let allFit = true;
        const replacements = [];
        for (let k = p; k < q; k++) {
          const reused = canReuse(left, edges[idxs[k]]);
          if (!reused) { allFit = false; break; }
          replacements.push([idxs[k], reused]);
        }
        if (allFit) {
          for (const [ri, replacement] of replacements) {
            out[ri] = replacement;
            smoothedEdges++;
          }
          p = q + 1;
          continue;
        }
      }

      p++;
    }

    // Fill only a very short null gap between the same HERE segment.
    p = 1;
    while (p < idxs.length - 1) {
      if (out[idxs[p]]) { p++; continue; }

      const start = p;
      let gapLen = 0;
      while (p < idxs.length && !out[idxs[p]]) {
        gapLen += edges[idxs[p]].lengthM;
        // Always consume the entire missing run. Breaking before p++ can
        // loop forever when one unmatched edge is longer than the gap limit.
        p++;
      }

      const left = start > 0 ? out[idxs[start - 1]] : null;
      const right = p < idxs.length ? out[idxs[p]] : null;

      if (
        gapLen > 0 &&
        gapLen <= HERE_CHAIN_NULL_GAP_MAX_M &&
        left?.provider === "HERE" &&
        right?.provider === "HERE" &&
        left.trafficId &&
        left.trafficId === right.trafficId
      ) {
        let allFit = true;
        const replacements = [];
        for (let k = start; k < p; k++) {
          const reused = canReuse(left, edges[idxs[k]]);
          if (!reused) { allFit = false; break; }
          replacements.push([idxs[k], reused]);
        }
        if (allFit) {
          for (const [ri, replacement] of replacements) {
            out[ri] = replacement;
            nullGapFilledEdges++;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    matches: out,
    ambiguousNavLinks: ambiguousFallbackChains, // backward-compatible diagnostic name
    ambiguousFallbackChains,
    ambiguousFallbackEdges,
    stabilizedEdges: smoothedEdges + nullGapFilledEdges,
    smoothedEdges,
    nullGapFilledEdges,
    stickyRetainedEdges,
  };
}

function classifyHereSource(confidence) {
  const c = Number(confidence);
  if (!Number.isFinite(c)) return "HERE";
  if (c >= 0.75) return "HERE_LIVE";
  if (c >= 0.69) return "HERE_HISTORICAL";
  return "HERE_LOW_CONF";
}

function normalizeHereSegmentsForMatcher(segments) {
  const out = [];
  for (const s of segments || []) {
    const jamFactor = Number(s?.jamFactor);
    const traversability = s?.traversability || null;
    const closed = isHereClosed(jamFactor, traversability);
    const speed = Number(s?.speedKph);
    if (!closed && !(Number.isFinite(speed) && speed > 0 && speed <= 160)) continue;
    if (!Array.isArray(s?.points) || s.points.length < 2) continue;
    out.push({
      provider:"HERE", sourceType:closed ? "HERE_CLOSED" : classifyHereSource(s?.confidence), scope:"here",
      id:String(s?.id || `here:${out.length}`), sectionId:null, road:s?.road || "",
      speedKph:closed ? null : speed, freeFlowKph:Number.isFinite(Number(s?.freeFlowKph)) ? Number(s.freeFlowKph) : null,
      confidence:Number.isFinite(Number(s?.confidence)) ? Number(s.confidence) : null,
      jamFactor:Number.isFinite(jamFactor) ? jamFactor : null, traversability, closed,
      trafficLevel:trafficLevelFromHere(jamFactor, traversability), level:null, dataTime:null,
      functionalClass:s?.functionalClass ?? null, functionalClasses:Array.isArray(s?.functionalClasses)?s.functionalClasses:[], points:s.points, bbox:segmentBBox(s.points)
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function isStrongTdxLock(match) {
  if (!match || match.provider !== "TDX") return false;

  if (match.scope === "freeway") {
    return (
      Number(match.speedKph) > 0 &&
      Number(match.matchScore) >= FREEWAY_TDX_LOCK_SCORE &&
      Number(match.matchDistanceM) <= FREEWAY_TDX_LOCK_MAX_M
    );
  }

  return Number(match.matchScore) >= TDX_STRONG_SCORE;
}

function buildHereNeedChunks(edges, matches) {
  const chunks = [];
  let current = null;

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const m = matches[i];
    const needHere = !m || m.closed === true || !isStrongTdxLock(m);

    if (!needHere) {
      if (current) {
        chunks.push(current);
        current = null;
      }
      continue;
    }

    const e = edges[i];
    if (!current) {
      current = {
        startEdge: i,
        endEdge: i,
        startM: e.routeStartM,
        endM: e.routeEndM,
        needM: e.lengthM,
      };
    } else if (e.routeEndM - current.startM <= HERE_GAP_CHUNK_M) {
      current.endEdge = i;
      current.endM = e.routeEndM;
      current.needM += e.lengthM;
    } else {
      chunks.push(current);
      current = {
        startEdge: i,
        endEdge: i,
        startM: e.routeStartM,
        endM: e.routeEndM,
        needM: e.lengthM,
      };
    }
  }

  if (current) chunks.push(current);
  return chunks;
}

function chooseBetweenTdxAndHere(tdx, here) {
  const tdxClosed = tdx?.closed === true || Number(tdx?.trafficLevel) === -1;
  const hereClosed = here?.closed === true || Number(here?.trafficLevel) === -1;

  // v1.6.1 closure fallback policy:
  // TDX -1/black is NOT considered a definitive route closure.
  // Try HERE first; if HERE is also closed/unusable/missing, leave the edge
  // unmatched so finalizeEdgeMatches() falls back to OSRM baseline.
  if (tdxClosed) {
    if (here && !hereClosed && Number(here.speedKph) > 0) {
      return { chosen: here, reason: "TDX_CLOSED_FALLBACK_HERE" };
    }
    return { chosen: null, reason: "TDX_CLOSED_FALLBACK_OSRM" };
  }

  // v2.2: only HERE traversability=closed is a closure candidate; jamFactor=10 is level 5/purple.
  // Never turn it into "道路封閉 / 無法計算"; use TDX if valid, otherwise OSRM.
  if (hereClosed) {
    if (tdx && Number(tdx.speedKph) > 0) {
      return { chosen: tdx, reason: "HERE_CLOSED_FALLBACK_TDX" };
    }
    return { chosen: null, reason: "HERE_CLOSED_FALLBACK_OSRM" };
  }

  if (!tdx) return { chosen: here, reason: "HERE_ONLY" };
  if (!here) return { chosen: tdx, reason: "TDX_ONLY" };

  if (tdx.scope === "freeway" && isStrongTdxLock(tdx)) {
    return { chosen: tdx, reason: "TDX_FREEWAY_PRIORITY" };
  }

  if (isStrongTdxLock(tdx)) {
    return { chosen: tdx, reason: "TDX_STRONG_MATCH" };
  }

  const tdxScore = Number(tdx.matchScore) || 0;
  const hereScore = Number(here.matchScore) || 0;
  const tdxDist = Number(tdx.matchDistanceM);
  const hereDist = Number(here.matchDistanceM);

  if (
    hereScore >= tdxScore + 0.08 ||
    (
      Number.isFinite(hereDist) &&
      Number.isFinite(tdxDist) &&
      hereDist + 18 < tdxDist &&
      hereScore >= tdxScore - 0.02
    )
  ) {
    return { chosen: here, reason: "HERE_BETTER_MATCH" };
  }

  return { chosen: tdx, reason: "TDX_WEAK_BUT_BETTER" };
}

function buildUnmatchedChunks(edges, matches) {
  const chunks = [];
  let current = null;

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    if (matches[i]) {
      if (current) {
        chunks.push(current);
        current = null;
      }
      continue;
    }

    const e = edges[i];
    if (!current) {
      current = {
        startEdge: i,
        endEdge: i,
        startM: e.routeStartM,
        endM: e.routeEndM,
      };
    } else if (e.routeEndM - current.startM <= HERE_GAP_CHUNK_M) {
      current.endEdge = i;
      current.endM = e.routeEndM;
    } else {
      chunks.push(current);
      current = {
        startEdge: i,
        endEdge: i,
        startM: e.routeStartM,
        endM: e.routeEndM,
      };
    }
  }

  if (current) chunks.push(current);
  return chunks;
}

function hereQueryForChunk(edges, chunk) {
  let minLat = Infinity, maxLat = -Infinity, minLon = Infinity, maxLon = -Infinity;

  for (let i = chunk.startEdge; i <= chunk.endEdge; i++) {
    for (const p of [edges[i].a, edges[i].b]) {
      minLat = Math.min(minLat, p[0]);
      maxLat = Math.max(maxLat, p[0]);
      minLon = Math.min(minLon, p[1]);
      maxLon = Math.max(maxLon, p[1]);
    }
  }

  const lat = (minLat + maxLat) / 2;
  const lon = (minLon + maxLon) / 2;
  const corners = [
    [minLat, minLon], [minLat, maxLon],
    [maxLat, minLon], [maxLat, maxLon],
  ];

  let radius = HERE_GAP_PADDING_M;
  for (const c of corners) radius = Math.max(radius, haversineM([lat, lon], c) + HERE_GAP_PADDING_M);

  return {
    lat,
    lon,
    radius: Math.max(1200, Math.min(HERE_GAP_RADIUS_MAX_M, Math.ceil(radius / 100) * 100)),
  };
}


function hereQueryForChunks(edges, chunks) {
  let minLat = Infinity, maxLat = -Infinity, minLon = Infinity, maxLon = -Infinity;
  let needM = 0;
  let startEdge = Infinity;
  let endEdge = -Infinity;
  let startM = Infinity;
  let endM = -Infinity;

  for (const chunk of chunks || []) {
    needM += Number(chunk.needM) || Math.max(0, Number(chunk.endM) - Number(chunk.startM));
    startEdge = Math.min(startEdge, chunk.startEdge);
    endEdge = Math.max(endEdge, chunk.endEdge);
    startM = Math.min(startM, chunk.startM);
    endM = Math.max(endM, chunk.endM);

    for (let i = chunk.startEdge; i <= chunk.endEdge; i++) {
      for (const p of [edges[i].a, edges[i].b]) {
        minLat = Math.min(minLat, p[0]);
        maxLat = Math.max(maxLat, p[0]);
        minLon = Math.min(minLon, p[1]);
        maxLon = Math.max(maxLon, p[1]);
      }
    }
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(minLat)) return null;

  const lat = (minLat + maxLat) / 2;
  const lon = (minLon + maxLon) / 2;
  const corners = [
    [minLat, minLon], [minLat, maxLon],
    [maxLat, minLon], [maxLat, maxLon],
  ];

  let radius = HERE_GAP_PADDING_M;
  for (const c of corners) {
    radius = Math.max(
      radius,
      haversineM([lat, lon], c) + HERE_GAP_PADDING_M
    );
  }

  return {
    lat,
    lon,
    radius: Math.max(
      1200,
      Math.min(HERE_GAP_RADIUS_MAX_M, Math.ceil(radius / 100) * 100)
    ),
    needM,
    startEdge,
    endEdge,
    startM,
    endM,
    routeSpanM: Math.max(0, endM - startM),
  };
}

function buildBundledHereQueries(edges, chunks) {
  const remaining = (chunks || [])
    .map((chunk, index) => ({ ...chunk, _chunkIndex: index }))
    .sort((a, b) => (Number(b.needM) || 0) - (Number(a.needM) || 0));

  const packages = [];

  while (remaining.length) {
    const seed = remaining.shift();
    const selected = [seed];

    let currentQ = hereQueryForChunks(edges, selected);

    while (remaining.length) {
      let bestIdx = -1;
      let bestQ = null;
      let bestValue = -Infinity;

      for (let i = 0; i < remaining.length; i++) {
        const cand = remaining[i];
        const mergedQ = hereQueryForChunks(edges, [...selected, cand]);
        if (!mergedQ) continue;

        // 優先以 9 km 內完成；必要時可放寬到 12 km，
        // 但避免為了很小的缺口把 query 拉得過大。
        const targetOk = mergedQ.radius <= HERE_BUNDLE_TARGET_RADIUS_M;
        const hardOk =
          mergedQ.radius <= HERE_BUNDLE_MAX_RADIUS_M &&
          mergedQ.routeSpanM <= HERE_BUNDLE_MAX_ROUTE_SPAN_M;

        if (!targetOk && !hardOk) continue;

        const addNeedM = Number(cand.needM) || 0;
        const radiusPenalty = Math.max(0, mergedQ.radius - currentQ.radius);
        const routeGapM = Math.max(
          0,
          Math.max(cand.startM, currentQ.startM) -
            Math.min(cand.endM, currentQ.endM)
        );

        // 「能包大就包大」：增加的缺口公里數權重大；
        // 半徑擴張與非常遠的 route gap 才扣分。
        const value =
          addNeedM -
          radiusPenalty * 0.22 -
          routeGapM * 0.035;

        if (value > bestValue) {
          bestValue = value;
          bestIdx = i;
          bestQ = mergedQ;
        }
      }

      if (bestIdx < 0) break;

      const cand = remaining[bestIdx];
      const addNeedM = Number(cand.needM) || 0;

      // 小缺口若要把半徑拉到 target 之外，不值得浪費大包的回傳量。
      if (
        bestQ.radius > HERE_BUNDLE_TARGET_RADIUS_M &&
        addNeedM < 1200
      ) {
        break;
      }

      selected.push(cand);
      remaining.splice(bestIdx, 1);
      currentQ = bestQ;
    }

    packages.push({
      ...currentQ,
      chunks: selected
        .sort((a, b) => a.startEdge - b.startEdge)
        .map(c => ({
          startEdge: c.startEdge,
          endEdge: c.endEdge,
          startM: c.startM,
          endM: c.endM,
          needM: c.needM,
        })),
      chunkCount: selected.length,
    });
  }

  // Query 額度不足時：不是「從起點開始」，而是優先打能補最多缺口公里數的大包。
  packages.sort((a, b) =>
    (Number(b.needM) || 0) - (Number(a.needM) || 0)
  );

  return packages;
}



function namespaceHereSegments(segments, queryTag) {
  return (segments || []).map((seg, i) => ({
    ...seg,
    id: `HERE:${queryTag}:${seg?.id ?? i}`,
    _hereOriginalId: seg?.id ?? null,
    _hereQueryTag: String(queryTag),
  }));
}

function dedupeTrafficSegments(segments) {
  const map = new Map();
  for (const s of segments || []) {
    const key = `${s.provider}:${s.id}`;
    if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, s);
  }
  return [...map.values()];
}


function normalizeRouteIntersections(body) {
  const raw = Array.isArray(body?.routeIntersections) ? body.routeIntersections : [];
  const route = normalizeRouteInput(body?.route);
  const tmp=[];

  for (let i=0;i<raw.length;i++) {
    const x=raw[i]||{};
    const lat=Number(x.lat ?? x.latitude ?? x?.location?.lat ?? x?.location?.latitude);
    const lon=Number(x.lon ?? x.lng ?? x.longitude ?? x?.location?.lon ?? x?.location?.lng ?? x?.location?.longitude);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat)||!Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
    const id=String(x.id ?? x.nodeId ?? x.osmNodeId ?? '').trim();
    let hasSignal=null;
    if (x.hasSignal===true || x.hasTrafficSignal===true || x.signal===true || String(x.highway||'').toLowerCase()==='traffic_signals') hasSignal=true;
    else if (x.hasSignal===false || x.hasTrafficSignal===false || x.signal===false) hasSignal=false;
    const maneuverTurn = x.isTurn===true || x.turn===true || x.isUTurn===true || /left|right|turn|uturn|u-turn/i.test(String(x.maneuver||x.modifier||x.turnType||''));
    const isFreewayOrExpressway = x.isFreewayOrExpressway===true || x.isFreeway===true || x.isExpressway===true || x.expressway===true || /freeway|motorway|expressway|高速公路|快速公路|快速道路|高架道路|高架/i.test(String(x.roadClass||x.roadType||x.road||x.name||''));
    let routeM=Number(x.routeM);
    if (!Number.isFinite(routeM) && route.length>=2) {
      const p=projectPointToRouteM([lat,lon],route);
      if (p && p.distanceM<=45) routeM=p.routeM;
    }
    const isTurn = route.length>=2 && Number.isFinite(routeM) ? geometricTurnAtRouteM(route,routeM,maneuverTurn) : maneuverTurn;
    tmp.push({id:id||null,lat,lon,hasSignal,isTurn,isFreewayOrExpressway,routeM:Number.isFinite(routeM)?routeM:null});
  }

  // One physical route intersection can be emitted multiple times by OSRM step/intersection
  // boundaries. Collapse nearby route-M candidates before querying signals.
  tmp.sort((a,b)=>(Number.isFinite(a.routeM)?a.routeM:1e15)-(Number.isFinite(b.routeM)?b.routeM:1e15));
  const out=[];
  for (const x of tmp) {
    const last=out[out.length-1];
    const sameRoute = last && Number.isFinite(last.routeM) && Number.isFinite(x.routeM) && Math.abs(last.routeM-x.routeM)<=NAVLINK_INTERSECTION_DEDUPE_M;
    const sameSpace = last && haversineM([last.lat,last.lon],[x.lat,x.lon])<=8;
    if (last && (sameRoute || sameSpace)) {
      if (x.hasSignal===true) last.hasSignal=true;
      if (last.hasSignal==null && x.hasSignal===false) last.hasSignal=false;
      // geometry says straight wins over a noisy step-boundary turn if route-M is same
      last.isTurn = last.isTurn && x.isTurn;
      last.isFreewayOrExpressway = last.isFreewayOrExpressway || x.isFreewayOrExpressway;
      continue;
    }
    out.push({...x});
  }
  return out;
}

function simpleStringHash(str) {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }
  return h.toString(16);
}

function clusterSignalNodesOnRoute(nodes, route) {
  const projected=[];
  const seen=new Set();
  for (const n of nodes||[]) {
    const key=String(n.id ?? `${n.lat.toFixed(7)},${n.lon.toFixed(7)}`);
    if (seen.has(key)) continue; seen.add(key);
    const p=projectPointToRouteM([n.lat,n.lon],route);
    if (!p || p.distanceM>NAVLINK_SIGNAL_ROUTE_MAX_M) continue;
    projected.push({...n,routeM:p.routeM,routeDistanceM:p.distanceM});
  }
  projected.sort((a,b)=>a.routeM-b.routeM);
  const clusters=[];
  for (const n of projected) {
    let c=clusters[clusters.length-1];
    if (!c || Math.abs(n.routeM-c.routeM)>NAVLINK_SIGNAL_CLUSTER_ROUTE_M) {
      clusters.push({routeM:n.routeM,nodes:[n],lat:n.lat,lon:n.lon});
    } else {
      c.nodes.push(n);
      c.routeM=c.nodes.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.routeM,0)/c.nodes.length;
      c.lat=c.nodes.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.lat,0)/c.nodes.length;
      c.lon=c.nodes.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.lon,0)/c.nodes.length;
    }
  }
  return clusters;
}

const SIGNAL_TILE_Z = 13;
const SIGNAL_TILE_PREFIX = 'signals/v1/z13';
const SIGNAL_TILE_READ_CONCURRENCY = 12;
const SIGNAL_TILE_CACHE_TTL_SEC = 86400;
const SIGNAL_MANIFEST_CACHE_TTL_SEC = 21600;

function signalTileXY(lat, lon, z=SIGNAL_TILE_Z) {
  const n = 2 ** z;
  const clampedLat = Math.max(-85.05112878, Math.min(85.05112878, Number(lat)));
  const x = Math.floor((Number(lon) + 180) / 360 * n);
  const latRad = clampedLat * Math.PI / 180;
  const y = Math.floor((1 - Math.asinh(Math.tan(latRad)) / Math.PI) / 2 * n);
  return { x: Math.max(0, Math.min(n-1, x)), y: Math.max(0, Math.min(n-1, y)), z };
}

function signalTileKey(x, y, z=SIGNAL_TILE_Z) {
  return `signals/v1/z${z}/${x}/${y}.json`;
}

async function loadSignalDbManifest(env, regionId='taiwan') {
  if (!env?.SIGNALS || typeof env.SIGNALS.get !== 'function') {
    return { ok:false, source:'binding-missing', data:null };
  }

  const key = `_builder/regions/${regionId}.json`;
  const cacheUrl = `https://cache.internal/r2-signal-manifest/${encodeURIComponent(regionId)}`;
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(cacheUrl);
  if (cached && cached.complete === true) {
    return { ok:true, source:'cache', data:cached };
  }

  try {
    const obj = await env.SIGNALS.get(key);
    if (!obj) return { ok:false, source:'r2-missing', data:null };
    const data = await obj.json();
    if (!data || data.complete !== true) return { ok:false, source:'r2-incomplete', data:data || null };
    await cachePutJSON(cacheUrl, data, SIGNAL_MANIFEST_CACHE_TTL_SEC);
    return { ok:true, source:'r2', data };
  } catch (err) {
    return { ok:false, source:'r2-error', error:String(err?.message || err), data:null };
  }
}

async function loadSignalTileFromR2(env, x, y, z=SIGNAL_TILE_Z) {
  if (!env?.SIGNALS || typeof env.SIGNALS.get !== 'function') {
    return { ok:false, missing:true, nodes:[], reason:'binding-missing', cacheHit:false, r2Hit:false };
  }

  const key = signalTileKey(x,y,z);
  const cacheUrl = `https://cache.internal/r2-signal-tile/${z}/${x}/${y}`;

  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(cacheUrl);
  if (cached && Array.isArray(cached.signals)) {
    return {
      ok:true,
      missing:false,
      nodes:cached.signals
        .map(a=>({id:a[0],lat:Number(a[1]),lon:Number(a[2])}))
        .filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n.lat)&&Number.isFinite(n.lon)),
      key,
      source:'cache',
      cacheHit:true,
      r2Hit:false,
    };
  }

  try {
    const obj = await env.SIGNALS.get(key);
    if (!obj) {
      return { ok:false, missing:true, nodes:[], key, reason:'tile-missing', cacheHit:false, r2Hit:false };
    }

    const data = await obj.json();
    const arr = Array.isArray(data?.signals) ? data.signals : [];
    await cachePutJSON(cacheUrl, {signals:arr}, SIGNAL_TILE_CACHE_TTL_SEC);

    return {
      ok:true,
      missing:false,
      nodes:arr
        .map(a=>({id:a[0],lat:Number(a[1]),lon:Number(a[2])}))
        .filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n.lat)&&Number.isFinite(n.lon)),
      key,
      source:'r2',
      cacheHit:false,
      r2Hit:true,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      ok:false,
      missing:false,
      nodes:[],
      key,
      reason:'tile-read-error',
      error:String(err?.message || err),
      cacheHit:false,
      r2Hit:false,
    };
  }
}

async function lookupTrafficSignalsForIntersections(intersections, route, env) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const candidates=(intersections||[]).filter(
    x=>!x.isFreewayOrExpressway && x.hasSignal==null && Number.isFinite(x.lat)&&Number.isFinite(x.lon)
  );

  if (!candidates.length) {
    return {
      intersections,
      queried:0,
      signalNodes:0,
      signalClusters:0,
      missingTiles:0,
      loadedTiles:0,
      cacheHitTiles:0,
      r2HitTiles:0,
      tileErrors:0,
      unknownCount:0,
      tileLoadMs:0,
      source:'none-needed',
    };
  }

  if (!env?.SIGNALS || typeof env.SIGNALS.get !== 'function') {
    return {
      intersections:(intersections||[]).map(x =>
        (!x.isFreewayOrExpressway && x.hasSignal==null)
          ? {...x,hasSignal:null,signalStatus:'unknown-r2-binding-missing'}
          : x
      ),
      queried:candidates.length,
      signalNodes:0,
      signalClusters:0,
      missingTiles:0,
      loadedTiles:0,
      cacheHitTiles:0,
      r2HitTiles:0,
      tileErrors:0,
      unknownCount:candidates.length,
      tileLoadMs:Date.now()-t0,
      source:'r2-binding-missing',
    };
  }

  // Read a 3x3 neighborhood around each route-intersection tile.
  // z13 tiles are large, and tile keys are deduplicated across the whole route.
  const candidateTileSets=[];
  const tileMap=new Map();

  for (const x of candidates) {
    const t=signalTileXY(x.lat,x.lon,SIGNAL_TILE_Z);
    const keys=[];

    for (let dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++) {
      for (let dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++) {
        const tx=t.x+dx, ty=t.y+dy;
        const n = 2 ** SIGNAL_TILE_Z;
        if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= n || ty >= n) continue;
        const key=`${tx}/${ty}`;
        keys.push(key);
        if (!tileMap.has(key)) tileMap.set(key,{x:tx,y:ty});
      }
    }

    candidateTileSets.push(keys);
  }

  const tileEntries=[...tileMap.entries()];
  const loaded=new Map();

  const tileResults = await mapLimit(tileEntries, SIGNAL_TILE_READ_CONCURRENCY, async ([k,t]) => {
    const result = await loadSignalTileFromR2(env,t.x,t.y,SIGNAL_TILE_Z);
    return {k,result};
  });

  let tileErrors=0;
  for (const item of tileResults) {
    if (item?.__error) {
      tileErrors++;
      continue;
    }
    if (!item || !item.k) continue;
    loaded.set(item.k,item.result);
  }

  const signalNodes=[];
  const seenNodeIds=new Set();
  let missingTiles=0, loadedTiles=0, cacheHitTiles=0, r2HitTiles=0;

  for (const r of loaded.values()) {
    if (r?.ok) {
      loadedTiles++;
      if (r.cacheHit) cacheHitTiles++;
      if (r.r2Hit) r2HitTiles++;
    } else if (r?.missing) {
      missingTiles++;
    } else {
      tileErrors++;
    }

    for (const n of (r?.nodes||[])) {
      const nk=String(n.id ?? `${n.lat.toFixed(7)},${n.lon.toFixed(7)}`);
      if (seenNodeIds.has(nk)) continue;
      seenNodeIds.add(nk);
      signalNodes.push(n);
    }
  }

  const clusters=clusterSignalNodesOnRoute(signalNodes,route);
  const assigned=new Set();

  // A physical signal cluster can prove at most one route intersection.
  // This prevents one OSM signal group from inflating into several +30s delays.
  for (const c of clusters) {
    let best=null;

    for (let i=0;i<candidates.length;i++) {
      const x=candidates[i];
      const spatial=haversineM([x.lat,x.lon],[c.lat,c.lon]);
      const routeGap=Number.isFinite(x.routeM)?Math.abs(x.routeM-c.routeM):spatial;

      if (spatial>NAVLINK_SIGNAL_INTERSECTION_MAX_M || routeGap>36) continue;

      const score=spatial + routeGap*0.35;
      if (!best || score<best.score) best={i,score};
    }

    if(best) assigned.add(best.i);
  }

  let ci=0, unknownCount=0;

  const out=(intersections||[]).map(x=>{
    if(x.isFreewayOrExpressway || x.hasSignal!=null) return x;

    const localIndex=ci++;
    if(assigned.has(localIndex)) {
      return {...x,hasSignal:true,signalStatus:'confirmed-r2'};
    }

    // Builder v1 currently stores only non-empty z13 tiles.
    // If at least one neighboring tile exists, the local DB is known around this
    // candidate and no matching signal means "confirmed no signal".
    // If all surrounding tiles are absent, coverage is unknown -> +0s.
    const keys=candidateTileSets[localIndex]||[];
    const anyKnown=keys.some(k=>loaded.get(k)?.ok===true);

    if(!anyKnown) {
      unknownCount++;
      return {...x,hasSignal:null,signalStatus:'unknown-r2-uncovered'};
    }

    return {...x,hasSignal:false,signalStatus:'confirmed-none-r2'};
  });

  return {
    intersections:out,
    queried:candidates.length,
    signalNodes:seenNodeIds.size,
    signalClusters:clusters.length,
    requestedTiles:tileEntries.length,
    missingTiles,
    loadedTiles,
    cacheHitTiles,
    r2HitTiles,
    tileErrors,
    unknownCount,
    tileLoadMs:Date.now()-t0,
    source:'r2-signal-db-v1',
  };
}

function edgeContextAtRouteM(routeM, edges, edgeMatches) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(routeM) || !Array.isArray(edges) || !edges.length) {
    return { provider: "OSRM", sourceType: "OSRM_BASELINE", speedKph: null, edgeIndex: -1 };
  }
  let best = -1;
  let bestGap = Infinity;
  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const e = edges[i];
    if (routeM >= e.routeStartM - 1 && routeM <= e.routeEndM + 1) {
      best = i;
      break;
    }
    const mid = (e.routeStartM + e.routeEndM) / 2;
    const gap = Math.abs(routeM - mid);
    if (gap < bestGap) { bestGap = gap; best = i; }
  }
  const m = best >= 0 ? edgeMatches?.[best] : null;
  return {
    provider: String(m?.provider || "OSRM"),
    sourceType: String(m?.sourceType || "OSRM_BASELINE"),
    speedKph: Number.isFinite(Number(m?.speedKph)) ? Number(m.speedKph) : null,
    edgeIndex: best,
  };
}

function residualIntersectionRule(x, ctx) {
  const trafficBacked = ctx?.provider === "TDX" || ctx?.provider === "HERE";
  const profile = trafficBacked ? SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_LIVE : SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_BASELINE;

  if (x?.hasSignal === true) {
    return {
      rawSec: x.isTurn ? profile.signalTurnSec : profile.signalStraightSec,
      kind: x.isTurn ? "signal-turn" : "signal-straight",
      profile: trafficBacked ? "live-residual" : "baseline-residual",
    };
  }
  if (x?.hasSignal === false) {
    return {
      rawSec: x.isTurn ? profile.noSignalTurnSec : profile.noSignalStraightSec,
      kind: x.isTurn ? "nosignal-turn" : "nosignal-straight",
      profile: trafficBacked ? "live-residual" : "baseline-residual",
    };
  }
  return { rawSec: 0, kind: "unknown", profile: "unknown" };
}

async function calcIntersectionDelay(body, env, edges, edgeMatches, drivingDurationSec) {
  const normalized = normalizeRouteIntersections(body);
  const route = normalizeRouteInput(body?.route);
  const lookup = await lookupTrafficSignalsForIntersections(normalized, route, env);
  const intersections = lookup.intersections;

  let rawTotalSec = 0;
  let skippedExpressway = 0, signalStraight = 0, signalTurn = 0;
  let noSignalStraight = 0, noSignalTurn = 0, unknown = 0;
  let liveTrafficEvents = 0, baselineEvents = 0;
  const events = [];

  for (const x of intersections) {
    if (x.isFreewayOrExpressway) { skippedExpressway++; continue; }

    const ctx = edgeContextAtRouteM(Number(x.routeM), edges, edgeMatches);
    const rule = residualIntersectionRule(x, ctx);

    if (x.hasSignal === true) {
      if (x.isTurn) signalTurn++; else signalStraight++;
    } else if (x.hasSignal === false) {
      if (x.isTurn) noSignalTurn++; else noSignalStraight++;
    } else {
      unknown++;
    }

    if (rule.profile === "live-residual") liveTrafficEvents++;
    if (rule.profile === "baseline-residual") baselineEvents++;

    rawTotalSec += rule.rawSec;
    events.push({
      routeM: Number.isFinite(x.routeM) ? x.routeM : null,
      rawDelaySec: rule.rawSec,
      delaySec: rule.rawSec,
      hasSignal: x.hasSignal,
      isTurn: x.isTurn,
      kind: rule.kind,
      ruleProfile: rule.profile,
      trafficProvider: ctx.provider,
      trafficSourceType: ctx.sourceType,
      trafficSpeedKph: ctx.speedKph,
      edgeIndex: ctx.edgeIndex,
      lat: x.lat,
      lon: x.lon,
    });
  }

  const capSec = Number.isFinite(Number(drivingDurationSec)) && Number(drivingDurationSec) > 0
    ? Number(drivingDurationSec) * SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION
    : Infinity;
  const scale = rawTotalSec > 0 && Number.isFinite(capSec) && rawTotalSec > capSec
    ? capSec / rawTotalSec
    : 1;

  let totalSec = 0, counted = 0;
  for (const ev of events) {
    ev.delaySec = Math.round((ev.rawDelaySec * scale) * 100) / 100;
    if (ev.delaySec > 0) counted++;
    totalSec += ev.delaySec;
  }
  totalSec = Math.round(totalSec * 10) / 10;

  return {
    totalSec,
    rawTotalSec,
    capSec: Number.isFinite(capSec) ? Math.round(capSec * 10) / 10 : null,
    capApplied: scale < 0.999999,
    delayScale: Math.round(scale * 10000) / 10000,
    maxDrivingFraction: SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION,
    inputCount: intersections.length,
    counted,
    skippedExpressway,
    signalStraight,
    signalTurn,
    noSignalStraight,
    noSignalTurn,
    unknown,
    liveTrafficEvents,
    baselineEvents,
    events,
    signalLookup: {
      queried: lookup.queried,
      signalNodes: lookup.signalNodes,
      signalClusters: lookup.signalClusters,
      requestedTiles: lookup.requestedTiles || 0,
      loadedTiles: lookup.loadedTiles || 0,
      cacheHitTiles: lookup.cacheHitTiles || 0,
      r2HitTiles: lookup.r2HitTiles || 0,
      missingTiles: lookup.missingTiles || 0,
      tileErrors: lookup.tileErrors || 0,
      tileLoadMs: lookup.tileLoadMs || 0,
      failedBatches: 0,
      unknownCount: lookup.unknownCount,
      source: lookup.source,
    },
    rules: {
      liveTraffic: { ...SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_LIVE },
      osrmBaseline: { ...SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_BASELINE },
      unknownSec: 0,
      freewayExpresswaySec: 0,
      capFractionOfDrivingTime: SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION,
    },
  };
}

function normalizeBaselineSteps(body) {
  const raw = Array.isArray(body?.baselineSteps)
    ? body.baselineSteps
    : (Array.isArray(body?.routeSteps) ? body.routeSteps : []);

  const out = [];
  let totalDistanceM = 0;
  let totalDurationSec = 0;

  for (const x of raw) {
    const distanceM = Number(x?.distance ?? x?.distanceM ?? x?.lengthM);
    const durationSec = Number(x?.duration ?? x?.durationSec);
    if (!(Number.isFinite(distanceM) && distanceM > 0 && Number.isFinite(durationSec) && durationSec >= 0)) continue;
    out.push({
      startM: totalDistanceM, endM: totalDistanceM + distanceM, distanceM, durationSec,
      road: String(x?.road || x?.name || ""),
      isExpressway: x?.isExpressway === true || x?.isFreewayOrExpressway === true
    });
    totalDistanceM += distanceM;
    totalDurationSec += durationSec;
  }

  return { steps: out, totalDistanceM, totalDurationSec };
}

function baselineDurationForEdge(edge, totalRouteDistanceM, baseline) {
  if (!baseline?.steps?.length || !(baseline.totalDistanceM > 0) || !(totalRouteDistanceM > 0)) return null;

  const scale = baseline.totalDistanceM / totalRouteDistanceM;
  const a = edge.routeStartM * scale;
  const b = edge.routeEndM * scale;
  let sec = 0;
  let covered = 0;

  for (const st of baseline.steps) {
    const lo = Math.max(a, st.startM);
    const hi = Math.min(b, st.endM);
    if (hi <= lo) continue;
    const d = hi - lo;
    if (st.distanceM > 0) sec += st.durationSec * (d / st.distanceM);
    covered += d;
  }

  if (covered < Math.max(0.5, (b - a) * 0.5)) return null;
  return Number.isFinite(sec) ? sec : null;
}

function chooseFallbackSpeedKph(totalDistanceM, body) {
  const explicit = Number(body?.fallbackSpeedKph);
  if (Number.isFinite(explicit) && explicit >= 3 && explicit <= 130) return explicit;

  const durationSec = Number(
    body?.osrmDurationSec ??
    body?.baselineDurationSec ??
    body?.durationSec
  );

  if (Number.isFinite(durationSec) && durationSec > 0 && totalDistanceM > 0) {
    const kph = (totalDistanceM / durationSec) * 3.6;
    if (Number.isFinite(kph)) return Math.max(3, Math.min(130, kph));
  }

  // 沒有 OSRM baseline 時仍回 match 結果，但不假裝有精確 ETA。
  return null;
}

function publicMatch(m) {
  if (!m) return null;
  const { _trafficSeg, ...rest } = m;
  return rest;
}

function finalizeEdgeMatches(edges, matches, fallbackSpeedKph, baselineProfile, totalDistanceM) {
  const out = [];
  let durationSec = 0;
  let matchedDistanceM = 0;
  let baselineEdgeCount = 0;
  let globalFallbackEdgeCount = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const edge = edges[i];
    const m = matches[i];

    const matchedSpeedKph = m ? Number(m.speedKph) : null;
    const baselineEdgeSec = baselineDurationForEdge(edge, totalDistanceM, baselineProfile);

    let speedKph = null;
    let edgeDurationSec = null;
    let baselineSource = null;

    if (m && Number.isFinite(matchedSpeedKph) && matchedSpeedKph > 0) {
      speedKph = matchedSpeedKph;
      edgeDurationSec = edge.lengthM / (speedKph / 3.6);
      baselineSource = 'TRAFFIC_SPEED';
      matchedDistanceM += edge.lengthM;
    } else if (Number.isFinite(baselineEdgeSec) && baselineEdgeSec >= 0) {
      edgeDurationSec = baselineEdgeSec;
      speedKph = edgeDurationSec > 0 ? (edge.lengthM / edgeDurationSec) * 3.6 : null;
      baselineSource = 'OSRM_STEP_BASELINE';
      baselineEdgeCount++;
    } else {
      const fallback = Number(fallbackSpeedKph);
      if (Number.isFinite(fallback) && fallback > 0) {
        speedKph = fallback;
        edgeDurationSec = edge.lengthM / (fallback / 3.6);
        baselineSource = 'OSRM_GLOBAL_FALLBACK';
        globalFallbackEdgeCount++;
      }
    }

    if (Number.isFinite(edgeDurationSec)) durationSec += edgeDurationSec;

    out.push({
      ...publicMatch(m),
      provider: m?.provider || "OSRM",
      sourceType: m?.sourceType || "OSRM_BASELINE",
      trafficLevel: m ? (Number.isFinite(Number(m.trafficLevel)) ? Number(m.trafficLevel) : 0) : 0,
      closed: m?.closed === true || Number(m?.trafficLevel) === -1,
      traversability: m?.traversability || null,
      speedKph,
      lengthM: edge.lengthM,
      edgeDurationSec,
      baselineEdgeDurationSec: Number.isFinite(baselineEdgeSec) ? baselineEdgeSec : null,
      baselineSource,
      navLinkId: edge.navLinkId || null,
      navNodeA: edge.navNodeA || null,
      navNodeB: edge.navNodeB || null,
      navRoad: edge.navRoad || "",
    });
  }

  return {
    edgeMatches: out,
    durationSec: Number.isFinite(durationSec) ? durationSec : null,
    matchedDistanceM,
    baselineEdgeCount,
    globalFallbackEdgeCount,
  };
}

function mergeFinalEdges(edges, finalMatches) {
  const merged = [];

  function sameGroup(a, b) {
    if (!a || !b) return false;
    if (a.provider !== b.provider) return false;
    if (a.sourceType !== b.sourceType) return false;

    // 真正 match 到 traffic segment 時，只有同一 trafficId 才合併；
    // fallback 則可以連續合併。
    if (a.provider !== "OSRM") {
      return a.trafficId && a.trafficId === b.trafficId;
    }
    return true;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < finalMatches.length; i++) {
    const m = finalMatches[i];
    const e = edges[i];

    const last = merged[merged.length - 1];
    if (last && sameGroup(last, m) && last.endPointIndex === e.pointIndexA) {
      last.endEdge = i;
      last.endPointIndex = e.pointIndexB;
      last.lengthM += e.lengthM;
      if (Number.isFinite(m.edgeDurationSec)) {
        last.durationSec = (last.durationSec || 0) + m.edgeDurationSec;
      }
      last.maxMatchDistanceM = Math.max(
        Number(last.maxMatchDistanceM || 0),
        Number(m.matchDistanceM || 0)
      );
      if (Number.isFinite(m.matchScore)) {
        last.minMatchScore = Math.min(
          Number.isFinite(last.minMatchScore) ? last.minMatchScore : m.matchScore,
          m.matchScore
        );
      }
      if (m.navLinkId && !last.navLinkIds.includes(m.navLinkId)) last.navLinkIds.push(m.navLinkId);
      continue;
    }

    merged.push({
      startEdge: i,
      endEdge: i,
      startPointIndex: e.pointIndexA,
      endPointIndex: e.pointIndexB,
      lengthM: e.lengthM,
      durationSec: m.edgeDurationSec,
      provider: m.provider,
      sourceType: m.sourceType,
      trafficId: m.trafficId || null,
      sectionId: m.sectionId || null,
      road: m.road || "",
      scope: m.scope || "",
      speedKph: m.speedKph,
      freeFlowKph: m.freeFlowKph ?? null,
      jamFactor: m.jamFactor ?? null,
      confidence: m.confidence ?? null,
      trafficLevel: Number.isFinite(Number(m.trafficLevel)) ? Number(m.trafficLevel) : 0,
      closed: m.closed === true || Number(m.trafficLevel) === -1,
      traversability: m.traversability || null,
      level: m.level ?? null,
      dataTime: m.dataTime || null,
      selectionReason: m.selectionReason || null,
      providerConflict: m.providerConflict || null,
      minMatchScore: Number.isFinite(m.matchScore) ? m.matchScore : null,
      maxMatchDistanceM: Number.isFinite(m.matchDistanceM) ? m.matchDistanceM : null,
      navLinkIds: m.navLinkId ? [m.navLinkId] : [],
      navNodeA: m.navNodeA || null,
      navNodeB: m.navNodeB || null,
    });
  }

  return merged;
}

function coverageStats(edges, finalMatches, totalDistanceM) {
  const dist = {
    TDX: 0,
    HERE_LIVE: 0,
    HERE_HISTORICAL: 0,
    HERE_LOW_CONF: 0,
    HERE: 0,
    OSRM_BASELINE: 0,
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const key = finalMatches[i]?.sourceType || "OSRM_BASELINE";
    if (!(key in dist)) dist[key] = 0;
    dist[key] += edges[i].lengthM;
  }

  const pct = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(dist)) {
    pct[k] = totalDistanceM > 0 ? Math.round((v / totalDistanceM) * 1000) / 10 : 0;
  }

  return { distanceM: dist, percent: pct };
}

function isVerifiedRouteClosure(seg) {
  if (!seg || !(seg.closed === true || Number(seg.trafficLevel) === -1)) return false;

  const provider = String(seg.provider || "").toUpperCase();
  const score = Number(seg.minMatchScore);
  const dist = Number(seg.maxMatchDistanceM);
  const lengthM = Number(seg.lengthM) || 0;

  if (provider === "TDX") {
    return Number.isFinite(score) && score >= 0.72 &&
      Number.isFinite(dist) && dist <= 60 && lengthM >= 20;
  }

  if (provider === "HERE") {
    return Number.isFinite(score) && score >= 0.82 &&
      Number.isFinite(dist) && dist <= 40 && lengthM >= 80;
  }

  return false;
}


// Motorcycle-only conservative traffic denylist requested by the user.
// This filters TDX candidates only, never HERE, Google route geometry or car data.
function motoTrafficExclusionReason(seg) {
  if (seg?.scope === "freeway") return "freeway";
  const road = String(seg?.road || "").normalize("NFKC").replace(/臺/g, "台").trim();
  if (!road) return "missingRoadName";
  const route = /(?:台\s*|省道\s*|(?:provincial\s*)?(?:highway|route)\s*|\b(?:tw|ph)[-\s]*)(61|62|64|65|66|68|72|74|76|78|82|84|86|88)(?!\d)/i;
  const bareRoute = /^(61|62|64|65|66|68|72|74|76|78|82|84|86|88)(?:[甲乙丙丁戊]?線?|[AB])?$/i;
  if (route.test(road) || bareRoute.test(road)) return "listedProvincialRoute";
  if (/快速|高速|高架|國道|\b(?:expressway|freeway|motorway|elevated)\b/i.test(road)) return "expresswayName";
  if (/西部濱海|萬里瑞濱|基隆港東側聯外|八里新店|五股土城|觀音大溪|南寮竹東|後龍汶水|快官霧峰|芳苑草屯|台西古坑|東石嘉義|北門玉井|台南關廟|高雄潮州|環東大道|堤頂大道|新店環河|新北環河/.test(road)) return "listedRoadName";
  return null;
}
function filterMotoTrafficCandidates(segments, stats, provider) {
  return segments.filter(seg => {
    const reason = motoTrafficExclusionReason(seg);
    if (!reason) return true;
    stats[provider]++;
    stats.reasons[reason] = (stats.reasons[reason] || 0) + 1;
    return false;
  });
}

async function matchTrafficToRoute(env, ctx, body) {
  const motoHere = body?.etaPolicy === "HERE_GOOGLE_MOTO";
  const motoTrafficExclusions = { enabled:motoHere, appliesTo:"TDX_ONLY", TDX:0, HERE:0, reasons:{} };
  // Google supplies the motorcycle geometry and fallback durations; HERE supplies speed.
  // Keep Google motorcycle geometry; matched TDX highway/HERE speeds override baseline.

  const diagnosticId = String(body?.diagnosticId || Date.now()).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'').slice(0,64);
  const stageTimings = {};
  const startedAt = Date.now(); let stageAt = startedAt;
  const mark = (stage) => {
    const now=Date.now(); stageTimings[stage]=now-stageAt;stageAt=now;
    if(body?.debugTiming===true) console.log(JSON.stringify({type:'traffic-stage',id:diagnosticId,stage,elapsedMs:now-startedAt,stageMs:stageTimings[stage]}));
  };
  mark('start');
  const rawRoute = normalizeRouteInput(body?.route);
  if (rawRoute.length < 2) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, error: "route_requires_at_least_2_points" };
  }

  if (rawRoute.length > 25000) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, error: "route_too_many_points", maxPoints: 25000 };
  }

  const rawDistanceM = routeLengthM(rawRoute);
  const autoSpacingM = rawDistanceM <= 20000 ? 32 : rawDistanceM <= 80000 ? 42 : rawDistanceM <= 200000 ? 55 : 68;
  const matcherSpacingM = Math.max(18, Math.min(90, Number(body?.matcherSpacingM) || autoSpacingM));
  const simplifiedRoute = simplifyRouteForMatcher(rawRoute, matcherSpacingM, 16);
  const maxEdgeM = Number(body?.maxEdgeM) || Math.max(MATCH_ROUTE_EDGE_M, matcherSpacingM * 1.35);
  const routePoints = resampleRoute(simplifiedRoute, maxEdgeM);
  const built = buildRouteEdges(routePoints);
  const edges = built.edges;
  const totalDistanceM = built.totalDistanceM;
  const baselineProfile = normalizeBaselineSteps(body);

  // v2.3.1: TDX fetch overlaps with RoadLink enrichment; long routes cap true OSRM Match to near 30 km.
  // This keeps the extra OSRM node-pair lookup from needlessly delaying Fast ETA.
  mark('routePreparation');
  const tdxStartedAt=Date.now(); let tdxFetchMs=0;
  const tdxSegmentsPromise = getTdxTrafficSegmentsForMatcher(env, motoHere).then(segments=>{
    tdxFetchMs=Date.now()-tdxStartedAt;
    if(!motoHere) return segments;
    const highway=filterMotoTrafficCandidates(segments.filter(s=>s.scope==='highway'), motoTrafficExclusions, 'TDX');
    for(const key of ['tdxUnavailable','tdxError','tdxScopeErrors','tdxStaleScopes']) highway[key]=segments[key];
    return highway;
  }).catch(err=>{tdxFetchMs=Date.now()-tdxStartedAt;const empty=[];empty.tdxUnavailable=true;empty.tdxError=String(err?.message||err);return empty;});

  let navLinkHints = normalizeNavLinkHints(body, totalDistanceM);
  let navLinkMeta = {
    source: navLinkHints.length ? "provided-osrm-node-hints" : "none",
    hintCount: navLinkHints.length,
    cacheHit: false,
  };

  if (!navLinkHints.length) {
    const auto = await deriveHybridRoadLinkHints(rawRoute, totalDistanceM, baselineProfile, {
      fastTrafficOnly: body?.fastTrafficOnly === true,
      motoHere,
    });

    if (auto?.ok && Array.isArray(auto.hints) && auto.hints.length) {
      navLinkHints = auto.hints;
      navLinkMeta = {
        source: auto.source || "hybrid-roadlinks",
        hintCount: navLinkHints.length,
        cacheHit: auto.cacheHit === true,
        longRouteMode: auto.longRouteMode === true,
        trueRoadLinkSpanM: Number(auto.trueRoadLinkSpanM) || 0,
        farSyntheticHintCount: Number(auto.farSyntheticHintCount) || 0,
        fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: auto.fastSkippedTrueRoadLink === true,
        nearAutoSource: auto.nearAutoSource || null,
        autoRejectReason: auto.autoRejectReason || null,
        tracePointCount: Number(auto.tracePointCount) || 0,
        chunkCount: Number(auto.chunkCount) || 0,
        splitLeafCount: Number(auto.splitLeafCount) || 0,
        maxSplitDepth: Number(auto.maxSplitDepth) || 0,
        tooBigLeafCount: Number(auto.tooBigLeafCount) || 0,
        matchedChunkCount: Number(auto.matchedChunkCount) || 0,
        failedChunkCount: Number(auto.failedChunkCount) || 0,
        relaxedChunkCount: Number(auto.relaxedChunkCount) || 0,
        coverageRatio: Number.isFinite(auto.coverageRatio) ? auto.coverageRatio : null,
        distanceRatio: Number.isFinite(auto.distanceRatio) ? auto.distanceRatio : null,
        p90DeviationM: Number.isFinite(auto.p90M) ? auto.p90M : null,
        maxDeviationM: Number.isFinite(auto.maxM) ? auto.maxM : null,
        minConfidence: Number.isFinite(auto.minConfidence) ? auto.minConfidence : null,
        ambiguousFraction: Number.isFinite(auto.ambiguousFraction) ? auto.ambiguousFraction : null,
        deriveMs: Number(auto.elapsedMs) || 0,
        httpElapsedMs: Number(auto.httpElapsedMs) || 0,
        rawNodePairs: Number(auto.rawNodePairs) || 0,
        httpStatus: auto.httpStatus ?? null,
        osrmCode: auto.osrmCode ?? null,
        osrmMessage: auto.osrmMessage || null,
        endpoint: auto.endpoint || null,
        requestMode: auto.requestMode || null,
        sharedInflight: auto.sharedInflight === true,
      };
    } else {
      navLinkHints = deriveSyntheticRoadLinkHints(baselineProfile, totalDistanceM);
      if (!navLinkHints.length) navLinkHints = genericSyntheticRoadLinkHints(totalDistanceM, 0, false);
      navLinkMeta = {
        source: "last-resort-synthetic-roadlinks",
        hintCount: navLinkHints.length,
        cacheHit: false,
        longRouteMode: totalDistanceM > LONG_ROUTE_TRUE_ROADLINK_MAX_M,
        trueRoadLinkSpanM: 0,
        farSyntheticHintCount: navLinkHints.length,
        fastSkippedTrueRoadLink: body?.fastTrafficOnly === true,
        autoRejectReason: auto?.source || "no-hybrid-result",
      };
    }
  }

  annotateEdgesWithNavLinks(edges, navLinkHints);
  const roadLinkChainStats = buildRoadLinkChains(edges);

  if (!edges.length || !(totalDistanceM > 0)) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, error: "route_has_no_distance" };
  }

  mark("roadLinks");
  const tdxSegments = await tdxSegmentsPromise;
  mark("tdxWait");
  const tdxRawMatches = matchEdgesAgainstTraffic(edges, tdxSegments);
  const tdxStable = stabilizeMatchesByNavLink(edges, tdxRawMatches, "TDX");
  const tdxMatches = tdxStable.matches;

  const tdxMatchedEdges = tdxMatches.filter(Boolean).length;
  const tdxStrongLockedEdges = tdxMatches.filter(isStrongTdxLock).length;
  const weakTdxMatchCount = tdxMatches.filter((m) => m && !isStrongTdxLock(m)).length;

  mark("tdxMatching");
  const hereNeedChunks = buildHereNeedChunks(edges, tdxMatches);
  const hereCorridors = buildHereCorridorQueries(edges, hereNeedChunks);
  const hereQueries = [];
  const hereSegmentsAll = [];
  const hereFetchDebug = [];

  const maxHereQueries = Math.max(1, Math.min(20, Number(body?.maxHereQueries) || 12));
  const selectedHereQueries = hereCorridors.slice(0, maxHereQueries);

  // v1.6: contiguous TDX gaps are fetched as HERE corridor queries.
  // Requests run concurrently, while long-route budget is consumed from route start forward.
  selectedHereQueries.forEach((q, hereQueryIndex) => {
    hereQueries.push({
      queryIndex: hereQueryIndex,
      type: "corridor",
      radius: q.radius,
      startEdge: q.startEdge,
      endEdge: q.endEdge,
      chunkStartEdge: q.chunkStartEdge,
      chunkEndEdge: q.chunkEndEdge,
      needM: Math.round(q.needM),
      needKm: Math.round(q.needM / 10) / 100,
      routeSpanM: Math.round(q.routeSpanM),
      rawPointCount: q.rawPointCount,
      pointCount: q.pointCount,
    });
  });

  const hereConcurrency = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, Number(body?.hereConcurrency) || 4));
  mark("hereQueryPreparation");
  const hereResults = await mapLimit(selectedHereQueries, hereConcurrency, async (q, hereQueryIndex) => {
    const queryStarted=Date.now();
    const here = await getHereFlowCorridorCached(ctx, {
      points: q.points,
      radius: q.radius,
      functionalClasses: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
      bypassCache: body?.bypassHereCache === true,
    });
    return { here, q, hereQueryIndex, elapsedMs:Date.now()-queryStarted };
  });

  mark("hereFetch");
  for (const result of hereResults) {
    if (!result || result.__error) {
      hereFetchDebug.push({
        source: "NETWORK",
        queryType: "corridor",
        ok: false,
        errorText: String(result?.__error || "HERE fetch error"),
      });
      continue;
    }

    const { here, q, hereQueryIndex } = result;
    hereFetchDebug.push({
      queryIndex: hereQueryIndex,
      elapsedMs: result.elapsedMs,
      queryType: "corridor",
      radius: q.radius,
      routeSpanM: Math.round(q.routeSpanM),
      corridorPointCount: q.pointCount,
      ...((here && here.fetchDebug) || {
        source: "UNKNOWN",
        cacheHit: !!here?.cacheHit,
        bypassCache: body?.bypassHereCache === true,
        status: here?.status ?? null,
        ok: !!here?.ok,
        apiResultCount: null,
        normalizedSegmentCount: Number(here?.resultCount) || 0,
        errorText: here?.message || null,
      }),
    });

    if (!here?.ok) continue;
    const normalizedHere = normalizeHereSegmentsForMatcher(here.segments || []);
    hereSegmentsAll.push(
      ...namespaceHereSegments(normalizedHere, `corridor-${hereQueryIndex}`)
    );
  }

  const hereSegments = dedupeTrafficSegments(hereSegmentsAll);
  mark("hereNormalization");
  const hereRawMatches = hereSegments.length
    ? matchEdgesAgainstTraffic(edges, hereSegments, {onIndexBuilt:()=>mark("hereIndex")})
    : new Array(edges.length).fill(null);
  mark("hereScoring");
  const hereStable = stabilizeHereMatchesByChain(edges, hereRawMatches);
  const hereMatches = hereStable.matches;

  mark("hereMatching");
  const matches = new Array(edges.length).fill(null);
  let providerConflictCount = 0;
  let hereReplacedWeakTdxCount = 0;
  let freewayTdxPriorityCount = 0;
  let tdxClosedFallbackHereCount = 0;
  let tdxClosedFallbackOsrmCount = 0;
  let hereClosedFallbackTdxCount = 0;
  let hereClosedFallbackOsrmCount = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const tdx = tdxMatches[i];
    const here = hereMatches[i];

    if (tdx && here) {
      const dv = Math.abs(Number(tdx.speedKph) - Number(here.speedKph));
      if (Number.isFinite(dv) && dv >= PROVIDER_CONFLICT_KPH) {
        providerConflictCount++;
        tdx.providerConflict = {
          otherProvider: "HERE",
          speedDiffKph: Math.round(dv * 10) / 10,
          hereSpeedKph: here.speedKph,
          hereConfidence: here.confidence,
          hereMatchScore: here.matchScore,
          hereMatchDistanceM: here.matchDistanceM,
        };
        here.providerConflict = {
          otherProvider: "TDX",
          speedDiffKph: Math.round(dv * 10) / 10,
          tdxSpeedKph: tdx.speedKph,
          tdxScope: tdx.scope,
          tdxMatchScore: tdx.matchScore,
          tdxMatchDistanceM: tdx.matchDistanceM,
        };
      }
    }

    const decision = chooseBetweenTdxAndHere(tdx, here);
    matches[i] = decision.chosen ? { ...decision.chosen, selectionReason: decision.reason } : null;

    if (decision.reason === "HERE_BETTER_MATCH") hereReplacedWeakTdxCount++;
    if (decision.reason === "TDX_FREEWAY_PRIORITY") freewayTdxPriorityCount++;
    if (decision.reason === "TDX_CLOSED_FALLBACK_HERE") tdxClosedFallbackHereCount++;
    if (decision.reason === "TDX_CLOSED_FALLBACK_OSRM") tdxClosedFallbackOsrmCount++;
    if (decision.reason === "HERE_CLOSED_FALLBACK_TDX") hereClosedFallbackTdxCount++;
    if (decision.reason === "HERE_CLOSED_FALLBACK_OSRM") hereClosedFallbackOsrmCount++;
  }

  const fallbackSpeedKph = chooseFallbackSpeedKph(totalDistanceM, body);
  const finalized = finalizeEdgeMatches(edges, matches, fallbackSpeedKph, baselineProfile, totalDistanceM);
  if (motoHere) {
    for (const edge of finalized.edgeMatches) {
      if (edge.provider === "OSRM") {
        edge.provider = "GOOGLE";
        edge.sourceType = "GOOGLE_BASELINE";
        edge.baselineSource = "GOOGLE_DISTANCE_ALLOCATED_BASELINE";
      }
    }
  }
  const drivingDurationSec = finalized.durationSec;
  const fastTrafficOnly = body?.fastTrafficOnly === true;
  mark("etaCalculation");
  const intersectionDelay = (fastTrafficOnly || motoHere)
    ? {
        totalSec: 0, rawTotalSec: 0, capSec: Number.isFinite(drivingDurationSec) ? Math.round(drivingDurationSec * SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION * 10) / 10 : null,
        capApplied: false, delayScale: 1, maxDrivingFraction: SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION,
        inputCount: Array.isArray(body?.routeIntersections) ? body.routeIntersections.length : 0, counted: 0,
        skippedExpressway: 0, signalStraight: 0, signalTurn: 0, noSignalStraight: 0, noSignalTurn: 0, unknown: 0,
        liveTrafficEvents: 0, baselineEvents: 0, events: [],
        signalLookup: { queried: 0, signalNodes: 0, signalClusters: 0, requestedTiles: 0, loadedTiles: 0, cacheHitTiles: 0, r2HitTiles: 0, missingTiles: 0, tileErrors: 0, tileLoadMs: 0, failedBatches: 0, unknownCount: 0, source: 'skipped-fast-traffic' },
        rules: { liveTraffic:{...SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_LIVE}, osrmBaseline:{...SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_BASELINE}, unknownSec:0, freewayExpresswaySec:0, capFractionOfDrivingTime:SIGNAL_RESIDUAL_MAX_DRIVING_FRACTION }
      }
    : await calcIntersectionDelay(body, env, edges, finalized.edgeMatches, drivingDurationSec);
  mark("signals");
  const finalDurationSec = Number.isFinite(drivingDurationSec)
    ? drivingDurationSec + intersectionDelay.totalSec
    : null;
  const merged = mergeFinalEdges(edges, finalized.edgeMatches);

  // v2.2.1 timeline preserves spatial distribution of traffic and residual signal delays.
  const edgeTimeline = finalized.edgeMatches.map((m, i) => ({
    edgeIndex: i,
    startM: edges[i].routeStartM,
    endM: edges[i].routeEndM,
    lengthM: edges[i].lengthM,
    navLinkId: edges[i].navLinkId || null,
    roadChainId: edges[i].roadChainId || null,
    provider: m.provider,
    sourceType: m.sourceType,
    speedKph: m.speedKph,
    trafficLevel: m.trafficLevel,
    drivingDurationSec: Number(m.edgeDurationSec) || 0,
    intersectionDelaySec: 0,
    durationSec: Number(m.edgeDurationSec) || 0,
  }));
  for (const ev of (intersectionDelay.events || [])) {
    if (!(Number.isFinite(ev.routeM) && Number(ev.delaySec) > 0)) continue;
    let idx = edgeTimeline.findIndex(x => ev.routeM >= x.startM - 1 && ev.routeM <= x.endM + 1);
    if (idx < 0 && edgeTimeline.length) idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(edgeTimeline.length-1, Math.round((ev.routeM/Math.max(1,totalDistanceM))*(edgeTimeline.length-1))));
    if (idx >= 0) {
      edgeTimeline[idx].intersectionDelaySec += ev.delaySec;
      edgeTimeline[idx].durationSec += ev.delaySec;
    }
  }

  const coverageDistance = {
    TDX: 0,
    HERE_LIVE: 0,
    HERE_HISTORICAL: 0,
    HERE_LOW_CONF: 0,
    HERE: 0,
    OSRM_BASELINE: 0,
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < edges.length; i++) {
    const src = finalized.edgeMatches[i]?.sourceType || "OSRM_BASELINE";
    const key = src === "TDX_LIVE" ? "TDX" : src;
    if (!(key in coverageDistance)) coverageDistance[key] = 0;
    coverageDistance[key] += edges[i].lengthM;
  }

  const coveragePercent = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(coverageDistance)) {
    coveragePercent[k] = totalDistanceM > 0 ? Math.round((v / totalDistanceM) * 1000) / 10 : 0;
  }

  // v1.6.1: traffic "closed/black" values are only provider-level closure candidates.
  // Final route segments must have already fallen through to HERE/TDX/OSRM.
  // Therefore traffic data alone never makes the route uncomputable.
  const detectedClosures = [
    ...tdxMatches.filter((m) => m?.closed === true || Number(m?.trafficLevel) === -1),
    ...hereMatches.filter((m) => m?.closed === true || Number(m?.trafficLevel) === -1),
  ];
  const routeBlocked = false;
  const hasCompleteEta = finalized.edgeMatches.every((x) => Number.isFinite(x.edgeDurationSec));

  mark("responsePreparation");
  return {
    ok: true,
    provider: motoHere ? "TDX+HERE+GOOGLE" : "TDX+HERE",
    matcherVersion: "2.3.7-indexed-nearest",
    matcherNotes: {
      tdxPolicy: "TDX usable only when speed>0 and native level is valid; -99 falls through to HERE; -1/black is a closure candidate and falls through to HERE, then OSRM.",
      herePolicy: "HERE traffic uses 5 congestion levels aligned with freeway colors: jamFactor 0-<3=level1 green, 3-<5=level2 yellow, 5-<7=level3 orange, 7-<9=level4 red, 9-10=level5 purple. Only traversability=closed is treated as a closure candidate.",
      tdxReliability: "v1.3.4 stable TDX live/cache behavior retained.",
      etaPolicy: "v2.2.1 RoadLink Chain + Sticky HERE: node-pair RoadLinks are grouped into safe continuity chains; HERE stays on the previous segment while it still geometrically fits, and only real road/class/direction boundaries reset continuity. Signal ETA remains residual-only and capped at 20% of driving time.",
      intersectionDelayPolicy: "v2.2 keeps the residual-signal model unchanged: R2 signals; live TDX/HERE 3s/6s, OSRM fallback 8s/12s, max 20% of driving time.",
      roadLinkWallPolicy: "v2.3.1 long-route guard: full true OSRM node RoadLinks are used on short routes; on routes over 30 km, Fast traffic skips OSRM Match entirely and Full traffic upgrades only the near 30 km to true RoadLinks while the far route stays synthetic. HERE corridor matching still runs across the full route.",
    },
    ts: Date.now(),
    routeBlocked,
    blockedSegmentCount: 0,

    route: {
      inputPointCount: rawRoute.length,
      simplifiedPointCount: simplifiedRoute.length,
      matcherSpacingM,
      pointCount: routePoints.length,
      edgeCount: edges.length,
      distanceM: Math.round(totalDistanceM),
      routePoints,
      navLinkCount: new Set(edges.map(e => e.navLinkId)).size,
      roadLinkChainCount: roadLinkChainStats.chainCount,
    },

    navLinks: (() => {
      const out=[];
      for (const e of edges) {
        const last=out[out.length-1];
        if (last && last.navLinkId===e.navLinkId) { last.endM=e.routeEndM; last.endEdge=e.edgeIndex; last.lengthM+=e.lengthM; continue; }
        out.push({navLinkId:e.navLinkId,startM:e.routeStartM,endM:e.routeEndM,startEdge:e.edgeIndex,endEdge:e.edgeIndex,lengthM:e.lengthM,nodeA:e.navNodeA,nodeB:e.navNodeB,road:e.navRoad||"",isExpressway:e.navExpressway===true});
      }
      return out;
    })(),
    edgeTimeline,

    eta: {
      policy: motoHere ? "HERE_GOOGLE_MOTO" : "TDX_HERE_OSRM",
      baselineProvider: motoHere ? "GOOGLE" : "OSRM",
      complete: hasCompleteEta,
      provisional: fastTrafficOnly && !motoHere,
      blocked: routeBlocked,
      durationSec: hasCompleteEta ? Math.round(finalDurationSec) : null,
      durationMin: hasCompleteEta ? Math.round((finalDurationSec / 60) * 10) / 10 : null,
      drivingDurationSec: hasCompleteEta ? Math.round(drivingDurationSec) : null,
      drivingDurationMin: hasCompleteEta ? Math.round((drivingDurationSec / 60) * 10) / 10 : null,
      intersectionDelaySec: intersectionDelay.totalSec,
      intersectionDelayMin: Math.round((intersectionDelay.totalSec / 60) * 10) / 10,
      fallbackSpeedKph,
      note: motoHere ? "TDX highway/HERE matched speed -> distance/speed; missing or unusable HERE -> Google distance-allocated baseline from this route request. No additional signal delay."
        : (baselineProfile.steps.length
        ? "Unmatched edges preserve OSRM step-level baseline duration."
        : (fallbackSpeedKph
          ? "No baselineSteps supplied; unmatched edges use OSRM-derived/explicit global fallback speed."
          : "No baselineSteps or OSRM duration/fallbackSpeedKph supplied; unmatched edges have no ETA."))
        + (intersectionDelay.inputCount
          ? " Intersection delay applied from routeIntersections."
          : " No routeIntersections supplied; intersection delay = 0."),
    },

    coverage: {
      distanceM: coverageDistance,
      percent: coveragePercent,
    },

    diagnostics: {
      diagnosticId,
      timing: { stages:stageTimings,tdxFetchMs,totalWallMs:Date.now()-startedAt,note:'Wall-clock intervals; stages may overlap TDX fetch. Use Worker CPU logs to assess CPU cost.' },
      motoTdxEnabled:motoHere,
      motoTrafficExclusions,

      intersectionDelay,
      closure: {
        policy: "fallback_only",
        detectedCandidateCount: detectedClosures.length,
        routeBlocked: false,

      },
      baseline: {

        totalDistanceM: Math.round(baselineProfile.totalDistanceM || 0),
        totalDurationSec: Math.round(baselineProfile.totalDurationSec || 0),
        perEdgeBaselineCount: finalized.baselineEdgeCount,
        globalFallbackEdgeCount: finalized.globalFallbackEdgeCount,
      },
      signalDb: {
        binding: !!(env?.SIGNALS && typeof env.SIGNALS.get === "function"),
        tileZoom: SIGNAL_TILE_Z,
        tilePrefix: SIGNAL_TILE_PREFIX,
        source: intersectionDelay?.signalLookup?.source || null,
        requestedTiles: intersectionDelay?.signalLookup?.requestedTiles || 0,
        loadedTiles: intersectionDelay?.signalLookup?.loadedTiles || 0,
        cacheHitTiles: intersectionDelay?.signalLookup?.cacheHitTiles || 0,
        r2HitTiles: intersectionDelay?.signalLookup?.r2HitTiles || 0,
        missingTiles: intersectionDelay?.signalLookup?.missingTiles || 0,

      tdxMatchedEdges,
