async function debugHereRouteOnly(ctx, body) {
  const rawRoute = normalizeRouteInput(body?.route);
  if (rawRoute.length < 2) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, error: "route_requires_at_least_2_points" };
  }

  const routePoints = resampleRoute(rawRoute, Number(body?.maxEdgeM) || MATCH_ROUTE_EDGE_M);
  const built = buildRouteEdges(routePoints);
  const edges = built.edges;
  const totalDistanceM = built.totalDistanceM;

  const maxHereQueries = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, Number(body?.maxHereQueries) || 8));

  // HERE-only：把整條 route 依 10 km 左右切塊，完全不碰 TDX。
  const chunks = [];
  let startEdge = 0;
  while (startEdge < edges.length) {
    let endEdge = startEdge;
    const startM = edges[startEdge].routeStartM;
    while (
      endEdge + 1 < edges.length &&
      edges[endEdge + 1].routeEndM - startM <= 10000
    ) {
      endEdge++;
    }
    chunks.push({
      startEdge,
      endEdge,
      startM,
      endM: edges[endEdge].routeEndM,
    });
    startEdge = endEdge + 1;
  }

  const queries = [];
  const hereSegmentsAll = [];

  const activeChunks = chunks.slice(0, maxHereQueries);
  for (let qi = 0; qi < activeChunks.length; qi++) {
    const chunk = activeChunks[qi];
    const q = hereQueryForChunk(edges, chunk);
    const here = await getHereFlowCached(ctx, {
      lat: q.lat,
      lon: q.lon,
      radius: Math.min(9000, Math.max(1800, q.radius)),
      functionalClasses: [1,2,3,4,5],
    });

    queries.push({
      queryIndex: qi,
      startEdge: chunk.startEdge,
      endEdge: chunk.endEdge,
      lat: q.lat,
      lon: q.lon,
      radius: Math.min(9000, Math.max(1800, q.radius)),
      ok: !!here?.ok,
      returnedSegments: here?.segments?.length || 0,
    });

    if (here?.ok) {
      const normalizedHere = normalizeHereSegmentsForMatcher(here.segments || []);
      hereSegmentsAll.push(
        ...namespaceHereSegments(normalizedHere, `debug-${qi}`)
      );
    }
  }

  const hereSegments = dedupeTrafficSegments(hereSegmentsAll);
  const grid = buildHereDebugGrid(hereSegments);

  const rows = [];
  let matchedEdges = 0;
  let noCandidateEdges = 0;
  let distanceRejected = 0;
  let headingRejected = 0;
  let scoreRejected = 0;

  for (let ei = 0; ei < edges.length; ei++) {
    const edge = edges[ei];
    const candidateIdx = hereDebugCandidates(grid, edge.midpoint, MATCH_HERE_MAX_M);

    let best = null;
    let nearestRejected = null;

    if (!candidateIdx.length) noCandidateEdges++;

    for (const si of candidateIdx) {
      const seg = hereSegments[si];
      const speed = Number(seg?.speedKph);
      if (!(speed > 0 && speed <= 160)) continue;

      const center = nearestOnPolyline(edge.midpoint, seg.points);
      if (!center) continue;

      const headingDelta = angleDeltaDeg(edge.heading, center.segmentHeading);

      let rejectReason = null;
      if (center.distanceM > MATCH_HERE_MAX_M) {
        rejectReason = "DISTANCE_TOO_FAR";
      } else if (headingDelta > MATCH_HEADING_REJECT_DEG) {
        rejectReason = "HEADING_REJECTED";
      }

      const scored = scoreTrafficCandidate(
        edge,
        seg,
        MATCH_HERE_MAX_M,
        null
      );

      if (!rejectReason && (!scored || scored.score < MATCH_HERE_MIN_SCORE)) {
        rejectReason = "SCORE_TOO_LOW";
      }

      if (rejectReason) {
        if (rejectReason === "DISTANCE_TOO_FAR") distanceRejected++;
        if (rejectReason === "HEADING_REJECTED") headingRejected++;
        if (rejectReason === "SCORE_TOO_LOW") scoreRejected++;

        const cand = {
          road: seg.road || "",
          speedKph: speed,
          confidence: seg.confidence ?? null,
          distanceM: center.distanceM,
          headingDeltaDeg: headingDelta,
          score: scored?.score ?? null,
          rejectReason,
        };

        if (!nearestRejected || cand.distanceM < nearestRejected.distanceM) {
          nearestRejected = cand;
        }
        continue;
      }

      if (!best || scored.score > best.score) {
        best = {
          road: seg.road || "",
          speedKph: speed,
          confidence: seg.confidence ?? null,
          score: scored.score,
          distanceM: scored.distanceM,
          headingDeltaDeg: scored.headingDeltaDeg,
        };
      }
    }

    if (best) matchedEdges++;

    // 只保留北新路區域、unmatched、或每 25 edge 抽樣，控制 response 大小。
    const [lat, lon] = edge.midpoint;
    const inNorthXin =
      lat >= 25.17 && lat <= 25.245 &&
      lon >= 121.43 && lon <= 121.515;

    if (inNorthXin || !best || ei % 25 === 0) {
      rows.push({
        edgeIndex: ei,
        routeStartM: Math.round(edge.routeStartM),
        routeEndM: Math.round(edge.routeEndM),
        midpoint: edge.midpoint,
        candidateCount: candidateIdx.length,
        best,
        nearestRejected,
      });
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    matcherVersion: "1.2.5-here-only-debug",
    route: {
      pointCount: routePoints.length,
      edgeCount: edges.length,
      distanceM: Math.round(totalDistanceM),
    },
    queries,
    hereTrafficSegmentCountRaw: hereSegmentsAll.length,
    hereTrafficSegmentCount: hereSegments.length,
    stats: {
      matchedEdges,
      unmatchedEdges: edges.length - matchedEdges,
      noCandidateEdges,
      distanceRejected,
      headingRejected,
      scoreRejected,
    },
    spatialIndex: {
      projection: "WebMercator",
      gridCellM: HERE_DEBUG_GRID_M,
      sampleM: HERE_DEBUG_SAMPLE_M,
      maxSamplesPerSegment: HERE_DEBUG_MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SEG,
    },
    debugRows: rows.slice(0, 700),
  };
}


const TRAFFIC_SCOPES = ["freeway", "highway", "taipei"];

function isValidTrafficScope(scope) {
  return scope === "freeway" || scope === "highway" || scope === "taipei";
}

function toNumberOrNull(v) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

function normalizeLevelNumber(v) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

/* ==========================================================
 * Unified traffic status / trafficLevel
 *
 * -1 = provider closure candidate / black in raw source (final route falls through)
 *  0 = no usable data
 *  1 = green
 *  2 = yellow
 *  3 = orange
 *  4 = red
 *  5 = purple
 *
 * TDX valid traffic MUST satisfy:
 *   speed > 0 AND native level in the valid range.
 * Special values:
 *   -1  = closure candidate (do NOT block route; HERE then OSRM fallback)
 *   -99 = insufficient data (fall through to HERE, then OSRM)
 *
 * HERE uses native jamFactor mapped to the same 5-color scale as freeway:
 *   0 <= jf < 3  => level 1 green   (integer 0/1/2)
 *   3 <= jf < 5  => level 2 yellow  (integer 3/4)
 *   5 <= jf < 7  => level 3 orange  (integer 5/6)
 *   7 <= jf < 9  => level 4 red     (integer 7/8)
 *   9 <= jf <=10 => level 5 purple  (integer 9/10)
 *
 * Closure is determined by traversability=closed only.
 * jamFactor=10 by itself remains level 5/purple instead of being forced to OSRM.
 * ========================================================== */
function validTdxNativeLevel(scope, level) {
  const lv = Number(level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(lv)) return false;
  if (scope === "freeway") return lv >= 1 && lv <= 5;
  if (scope === "highway") return lv >= 1 && lv <= 3;
  if (scope === "taipei") return lv >= 1 && lv <= 6;
  return false;
}

function trafficLevelFromTdxNative(scope, level) {
  const lv = Number(level);
  if (!validTdxNativeLevel(scope, lv)) return 0;
  if (scope === "freeway") return lv;
  if (scope === "taipei") {
    if (lv === 1 || lv === 2) return 1;
    if (lv === 3 || lv === 4) return 3;
    if (lv === 5 || lv === 6) return 4;
    return 0;
  }
  if (scope === "highway") {
    if (lv === 1) return 1;
    if (lv === 2) return 3;
    if (lv === 3) return 4;
  }
  return 0;
}

function resolveTdxTrafficStatus(scope, speedKph, level) {
  const lv = Number(level);
  const speed = Number(speedKph);
  if (lv === -1) return { validTraffic:false, closed:true, insufficient:false, trafficLevel:-1, fallbackToHere:true };
  if (lv === -99) return { validTraffic:false, closed:false, insufficient:true, trafficLevel:0, fallbackToHere:true };
  const valid = Number.isFinite(speed) && speed > 0 && speed <= 160 && validTdxNativeLevel(scope, lv);
  return {
    validTraffic:valid, closed:false, insufficient:!valid,
    trafficLevel:valid ? trafficLevelFromTdxNative(scope, lv) : 0,
    fallbackToHere:!valid
  };
}

function trafficLevelFromTdx(scope, level, speedKph) {
  if (arguments.length >= 3) return resolveTdxTrafficStatus(scope, speedKph, level).trafficLevel;
  if (Number(level) === -1) return -1;
  if (Number(level) === -99) return 0;
  return trafficLevelFromTdxNative(scope, level);
}

function trafficLevelFromHere(jamFactor, traversability) {
  const trav = String(traversability || "").toLowerCase();
  const jf = Number(jamFactor);

  // A real closure still falls through to TDX/OSRM.
  // jamFactor=10 alone is NOT treated as closed in v2.1.6;
  // it is level 5/purple as requested.
  if (trav === "closed") return -1;

  if (!Number.isFinite(jf) || jf < 0) return 0;
  if (jf < 3) return 1;  // 0-2: green
  if (jf < 5) return 2;  // 3-4: yellow
  if (jf < 7) return 3;  // 5-6: orange
  if (jf < 9) return 4;  // 7-8: red
  if (jf <= 10) return 5; // 9-10: purple
  return 0;
}

function isHereClosed(jamFactor, traversability) {
  return trafficLevelFromHere(jamFactor, traversability) === -1;
}

const NAV_OSRM_DRIVE = "https://router.project-osrm.org";

function parseLatLonText(value) {
  const s = String(value || "").trim().replace(/，/g, ",");
  const m = s.match(/^\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*,\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$/);
  if (!m) return null;
  const lat = Number(m[1]);
  const lon = Number(m[2]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon) || lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lon < -180 || lon > 180) return null;
  return { lat, lon };
}

async function resolveNavigationWaypoint(ctx, value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value) && value.length >= 2) {
    const lat = Number(value[0]);
    const lon = Number(value[1]);
    if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon)) return { lat, lon, source: "coords" };
  }

  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    const lat = Number(value.lat ?? value.latitude);
    const lon = Number(value.lon ?? value.lng ?? value.longitude);
    if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lon)) return { lat, lon, source: "coords" };
  }

  const text = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!text) return null;
  const direct = parseLatLonText(text);
  if (direct) return { ...direct, source: "coords" };

  if (!ctx.googleKey) throw new Error("GOOGLE_MAPS_KEY_missing_for_text_geocode");
  const g = await googleGeocodeCompatCached({ googleKey: ctx.googleKey }, {
    q: text.replace(/[臺台]/g, "台"), region: "tw", lang: "zh-TW"
  });
  if (!g?.ok || !Number.isFinite(Number(g.lat)) || !Number.isFinite(Number(g.lon))) {
    throw new Error(`geocode_failed:${text}`);
  }
  return { lat: Number(g.lat), lon: Number(g.lon), source: "geocode", formatted: g.formatted || text };
}

async function resolveNavigationPoints(ctx, input) {
  if (Array.isArray(input?.points) && input.points.length >= 2) {
    const out = [];
    for (const p of input.points) {
      const r = await resolveNavigationWaypoint(ctx, p);
      if (!r) throw new Error("invalid_waypoint");
      out.push(r);
    }
    return out;
  }

  const startRaw = input?.start ?? input?.origin;
  const endRaw = input?.end ?? input?.destination;
  let viasRaw = input?.vias ?? input?.via ?? [];
  if (!Array.isArray(viasRaw)) viasRaw = [viasRaw];

  const start = await resolveNavigationWaypoint(ctx, startRaw);
  const end = await resolveNavigationWaypoint(ctx, endRaw);
  if (!start || !end) throw new Error("start_and_end_required");

  const vias = [];
  for (const v of viasRaw) {
    if (typeof v === "string" && v.includes("|")) {
      for (const part of v.split("|")) {
        if (!part.trim()) continue;
        const r = await resolveNavigationWaypoint(ctx, part.trim());
        if (r) vias.push(r);
      }
    } else if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== "") {
      const r = await resolveNavigationWaypoint(ctx, v);
      if (r) vias.push(r);
    }
  }
  return [start, ...vias, end];
}

function osrmStepLooksExpresswayBackend(step, intersection) {
  const text = `${step?.name || ""} ${step?.ref || ""}`;
  const classes = Array.isArray(intersection?.classes) ? intersection.classes.join(" ") : "";
  return /motorway|freeway|expressway|國道|快速公路|快速道路|環河快速|市民大道高架|建國高架|高架道路/i.test(text + " " + classes);
}

function osrmRoutePayloadForMatcher(osrmRoute) {
  const baselineSteps=[];
  const rawIntersections=[];
  let stepCum=0;
  for (const leg of osrmRoute?.legs || []) {
    for (const step of leg?.steps || []) {
      const distance=Number(step?.distance), duration=Number(step?.duration);
      const stepStart=stepCum;
      if (Number.isFinite(distance)&&distance>0&&Number.isFinite(duration)&&duration>=0) {
        baselineSteps.push({distance,duration,road:step?.name||step?.ref||"",isExpressway:osrmStepLooksExpresswayBackend(step,null)});
        stepCum+=distance;
      }
      const type=String(step?.maneuver?.type||"").toLowerCase();
      const mod=String(step?.maneuver?.modifier||"").toLowerCase();
      const fallbackTurn=type==='turn'||type==='uturn'||mod.includes('left')||mod.includes('right');
      for (const it of (Array.isArray(step?.intersections)?step.intersections:[])) {
        const loc=it?.location; if(!Array.isArray(loc)||loc.length<2) continue;
        const lon=Number(loc[0]),lat=Number(loc[1]); if(!Number.isFinite(lat)||!Number.isFinite(lon)) continue;
        const proj=projectPointToRouteM([lat,lon],osrmRoute?.geometry||[]);
        const routeM=proj && proj.distanceM<=45 ? proj.routeM : stepStart;
        rawIntersections.push({lat,lon,routeM,hasSignal:null,isTurn:geometricTurnAtRouteM(osrmRoute?.geometry||[],routeM,fallbackTurn),isFreewayOrExpressway:osrmStepLooksExpresswayBackend(step,it),maneuver:type,modifier:mod,road:step?.name||""});
      }
    }
  }

  // Build stable directed NAVLINK IDs from OSRM annotation OSM node pairs.
  const navLinkHints=[];
  let legCum=0;
  const stepRanges=[];
  let sc=0;
  for (const s of baselineSteps) { stepRanges.push({startM:sc,endM:sc+s.distance,road:s.road,isExpressway:s.isExpressway}); sc+=s.distance; }
  for (const leg of osrmRoute?.legs || []) {
    const ann=leg?.annotation||{};
    const nodes=Array.isArray(ann.nodes)?ann.nodes:[];
    const dist=Array.isArray(ann.distance)?ann.distance:[];
    let local=0;
    if (nodes.length>=2 && dist.length>=1) {
      for(let i=0;i<Math.min(dist.length,nodes.length-1);i++){
        const d=Number(dist[i]); if(!(Number.isFinite(d)&&d>0)) continue;
        const startM=legCum+local,endM=startM+d,mid=(startM+endM)/2;
        const st=stepRanges.find(x=>mid>=x.startM-2&&mid<=x.endM+2)||{};
        navLinkHints.push({navLinkId:`NLK:${nodes[i]}>${nodes[i+1]}`,startM,endM,nodeA:String(nodes[i]),nodeB:String(nodes[i+1]),road:st.road||"",isExpressway:st.isExpressway===true});
        local+=d;
      }
    }
    const legDist=Number(leg?.distance);
    legCum += Number.isFinite(legDist)&&legDist>0 ? legDist : local;
  }

  return {baselineSteps,routeIntersections:rawIntersections,navLinkHints};
}

async function fetchOsrmDrivingRouteBackend(points) {
  if (!Array.isArray(points) || points.length < 2) throw new Error("route_requires_at_least_2_points");
  const coordPath = points.map(p => `${Number(p.lon)},${Number(p.lat)}`).join(";");
  const url = `${NAV_OSRM_DRIVE}/route/v1/driving/${coordPath}?overview=full&geometries=geojson&steps=true&annotations=true&generate_hints=false`;
  const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } });
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`OSRM_HTTP_${r.status}`);
  const j = await r.json().catch(() => null);
  const rt = j?.routes?.[0];
  if (!rt) throw new Error("OSRM_no_route");

  const coords = rt?.geometry?.coordinates;
  if (!Array.isArray(coords) || coords.length < 2) throw new Error("OSRM_invalid_geometry");
  const geometry = coords
    .map(x => [Number(x[1]), Number(x[0])])
    .filter(x => Number.isFinite(x[0]) && Number.isFinite(x[1]));

  return {
    geometry,
    legs: Array.isArray(rt.legs) ? rt.legs : [],
    distanceM: Number(rt.distance) || 0,
    durationSec: Number(rt.duration) || 0,
  };
}

async function computeNavigationRouteV214(env, ctx, input) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const points = await resolveNavigationPoints(ctx, input || {});
  if (points.length < 2) return { ok: false, status: 400, error: "route_requires_at_least_2_points" };
  const tGeocode = Date.now();

  const osrm = await fetchOsrmDrivingRouteBackend(points);
  const tOsrm = Date.now();
  const matcherInput = osrmRoutePayloadForMatcher(osrm);

  const traffic = await matchTrafficToRoute(env, { hereKey: ctx.hereKey }, {
    route: osrm.geometry,
    osrmDurationSec: osrm.durationSec,
    baselineSteps: matcherInput.baselineSteps,
    routeIntersections: matcherInput.routeIntersections,
    navLinkHints: matcherInput.navLinkHints,
    maxHereQueries: Number(input?.maxHereQueries) || undefined,
    hereConcurrency: Number(input?.hereConcurrency) || 4,
    fastTrafficOnly: false,
  });
  const tTraffic = Date.now();

  if (!traffic?.ok) return traffic;

  return {
    ok: true,
    version: "2.4.0",
    pipeline: "V2_2_1_ROADLINK_CHAIN_STICKY_HERE_TDX_R2_RESIDUAL_SIGNALS",
    points: points.map(p => ({ lat: p.lat, lon: p.lon, source: p.source || "coords" })),
    route: {
      provider: "osrm",
      geometry: osrm.geometry,
      legs: osrm.legs,
      distanceM: osrm.distanceM,
      durationSec: osrm.durationSec,
    },
    osrm: {
      distanceM: osrm.distanceM,
      durationSec: osrm.durationSec,
      durationMin: Math.round((osrm.durationSec / 60) * 10) / 10,
    },
    routeBlocked: traffic.routeBlocked === true,
    eta: traffic.eta,
    coverage: traffic.coverage,
    segments: traffic.segments,
    navLinks: traffic.navLinks,
    edgeTimeline: traffic.edgeTimeline,
    trafficRoute: traffic.route,
    trafficDiagnostics: traffic.diagnostics,
    matcherVersion: traffic.matcherVersion,
    timings: {
      geocodeMs: tGeocode - t0,
      osrmMs: tOsrm - tGeocode,
      trafficAndSignalsMs: tTraffic - tOsrm,
      totalMs: tTraffic - t0,
    },
    ts: Date.now(),
  };
}

async function googleGeocodeCompatCached(ctx, { q, region = "tw", lang = "zh-TW" }) {
  const keyUrl =
    "https://cache.internal/google/geocode_compat?v=1&q=" +
    encodeURIComponent(q.trim().toLowerCase()) +
    "&region=" + encodeURIComponent(region) +
    "&lang=" + encodeURIComponent(lang);

  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached && cached.ok && Number.isFinite(cached.lat) && Number.isFinite(cached.lon)) return cached;

  const api =
    "https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/geocode/json" +
    "?address=" + encodeURIComponent(q) +
    "&region=" + encodeURIComponent(region) +
    "&language=" + encodeURIComponent(lang) +
    "&components=" + encodeURIComponent("country:TW") +
    "&key=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx.googleKey);

  const r = await fetch(api, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } });
  const j = await r.json().catch(() => null);

  if (!r.ok || !j) {
    const out = { ok: false, query: q, error: `Google geocode failed: HTTP ${r.status}` };
    await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, out, 300);
    return out;
  }

  if (j.status !== "OK" || !Array.isArray(j.results) || !j.results[0]) {
    const out = { ok: false, query: q, status: j.status || "UNKNOWN", error: "no_result", results: [] };
    await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, out, 3600);
    return out;
  }

  const results = j.results.slice(0, 5).map((it) => ({
    formatted: it.formatted_address || "",
    placeId: it.place_id || "",
    location: it.geometry?.location
      ? { lat: Number(it.geometry.location.lat), lon: Number(it.geometry.location.lng) }
      : null,
    types: it.types || [],
  }));

  const top = results[0];
  const loc = top?.location;

  const out = {
    ok: !!(loc && Number.isFinite(loc.lat) && Number.isFinite(loc.lon)),
    source: "geocode",
    query: q,
    formatted: top?.formatted || "",
    placeId: top?.placeId || "",
    lat: loc?.lat ?? null,
    lon: loc?.lon ?? null,
    results,
  };

  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, out, 30 * 86400);
  return out;
}

async function googleComputeRoutesRobust(ctx, body) {
  const travelMode = String(body.travelMode || "DRIVE").toUpperCase();
  const mode = String(body.mode || "FASTEST").toUpperCase();
  const languageCode = String(body.languageCode || "zh-TW");
  const regionCode = String(body.regionCode || "TW");
  const polylineQuality = String(body.polylineQuality || "HIGH_QUALITY").toUpperCase();

  const computeAlternativeRoutes = !!body.alternatives;

  const origin = normalizeWaypoint(body.origin);
  const destination = normalizeWaypoint(body.destination);
  const vias = Array.isArray(body.vias) ? body.vias.map(normalizeWaypoint).filter(Boolean) : [];

  if (!origin || !destination) {
    return { ok: false, error: "missing_origin_or_destination" };
  }

  let routingPreference = String(body.routingPreference || "").toUpperCase();

  if (!routingPreference) {
    if (mode === "SHORTEST" || mode === "BASELINE" || mode === "UNAWARE") {
      routingPreference = "TRAFFIC_UNAWARE";
    } else if (mode === "AWARE") {
      routingPreference = "TRAFFIC_AWARE";
    } else {
      routingPreference = "TRAFFIC_AWARE_OPTIMAL";
    }
  }

  const includeTrafficIntervals = body.includeTrafficIntervals === undefined ? false : !!body.includeTrafficIntervals;

  const routeModifiers = {};
  if (body.avoidTolls === true || mode === "NO_TOLLS") routeModifiers.avoidTolls = true;
  if (body.avoidHighways === true) routeModifiers.avoidHighways = true;
  if (body.avoidFerries === true) routeModifiers.avoidFerries = true;
  if (body.avoidIndoor === true) routeModifiers.avoidIndoor = true;

  if (travelMode === "TWO_WHEELER") {
    if (body.avoidHighways !== false) routeModifiers.avoidHighways = true;
    if (body.avoidTolls !== false) routeModifiers.avoidTolls = true;
    if (body.avoidFerries !== false) routeModifiers.avoidFerries = true;
  }

  const req = {
    origin,
    destination,
    intermediates: vias.length ? vias : undefined,
    travelMode,
    languageCode,
    regionCode,
    polylineQuality,
    polylineEncoding: "ENCODED_POLYLINE",
    computeAlternativeRoutes,
    routingPreference,
  };

  if (Object.keys(routeModifiers).length) req.routeModifiers = routeModifiers;
  if (includeTrafficIntervals) req.extraComputations = ["TRAFFIC_ON_POLYLINE"];

  const fieldMaskSafe =
    "routes.distanceMeters," +
    "routes.duration," +
    "routes.staticDuration," +
    "routes.polyline.encodedPolyline," +
    "routes.legs.distanceMeters," +
    "routes.legs.duration," +
    "routes.legs.staticDuration," +
    "routes.legs.steps.distanceMeters," +
    "routes.legs.steps.navigationInstruction.instructions" +
    (includeTrafficIntervals ? ",routes.routeTravelAdvisory.speedReadingIntervals" : "");

  const fieldMaskWithManeuver = fieldMaskSafe + ",routes.legs.steps.navigationInstruction.maneuver";

  const endpoint = "https://routes.googleapis.com/directions/v2:computeRoutes";

  const cacheKey =
    "https://cache.internal/google/routes?v=6" +
    "&mode=" + encodeURIComponent(mode) +
    "&tm=" + encodeURIComponent(travelMode) +
    "&rp=" + encodeURIComponent(routingPreference) +
    "&lc=" + encodeURIComponent(languageCode) +
    "&rc=" + encodeURIComponent(regionCode) +
    "&pq=" + encodeURIComponent(polylineQuality) +
    "&ti=" + (includeTrafficIntervals ? "1" : "0") +
    "&alt=" + (computeAlternativeRoutes ? "1" : "0") +
    "&body=" + encodeURIComponent(stableStringify(req));

  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(cacheKey);
  if (cached) return cached;

  let out = await callGoogleRoutesOnce(ctx.googleKey, endpoint, req, fieldMaskWithManeuver);
  if (!out.ok && out.status === 400) {
    out = await callGoogleRoutesOnce(ctx.googleKey, endpoint, req, fieldMaskSafe);
  }

  await cachePutJSON(cacheKey, out, out.ok ? 300 : 10);
  return out;
}

async function callGoogleRoutesOnce(googleKey, endpoint, req, fieldMask) {
  const r = await fetch(endpoint, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "X-Goog-Api-Key": googleKey,
      "X-Goog-FieldMask": fieldMask,
      Accept: "application/json",
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(req),
  });

  const j = await r.json().catch(() => null);

  if (!r.ok || !j) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "google_routes_failed",
      status: r.status,
      message: j?.error?.message || `HTTP ${r.status}`,
      details: j?.error?.details || null,
      requestUsed: {
        travelMode: req.travelMode,
        routingPreference: req.routingPreference,
        routeModifiers: req.routeModifiers || null,
        computeAlternativeRoutes: req.computeAlternativeRoutes,
      },
      fieldMaskUsed: fieldMask,
      raw: j,
    };
  }

  const routes = Array.isArray(j.routes) ? j.routes : [];
  if (!routes.length) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "no_routes",
      status: 200,
      requestUsed: {
        travelMode: req.travelMode,
        routingPreference: req.routingPreference,
        routeModifiers: req.routeModifiers || null,
        computeAlternativeRoutes: req.computeAlternativeRoutes,
      },
      fieldMaskUsed: fieldMask,
    };
  }

  function normalizeRoute(rt) {
    return {
      distanceMeters: rt.distanceMeters ?? null,
      duration: rt.duration ?? null,
      staticDuration: rt.staticDuration ?? null,
      polyline: rt.polyline?.encodedPolyline || "",
      trafficIntervals: normalizeSpeedIntervals(rt?.routeTravelAdvisory?.speedReadingIntervals || []),
      legs: (rt.legs || []).map((leg) => ({
        distanceMeters: leg.distanceMeters ?? null,
        duration: leg.duration ?? null,
        staticDuration: leg.staticDuration ?? null,
        steps: (leg.steps || []).map((st) => ({
          distanceMeters: st.distanceMeters ?? null,
          duration: null,
          staticDuration: null,
          polyline: "",
          instruction: st.navigationInstruction?.instructions || "",
          maneuver: st.navigationInstruction?.maneuver || null,
        })),
      })),
    };
  }

  const primary = normalizeRoute(routes[0]);

  return {
    ok: true,
    ts: Date.now(),
    source: "google_routes",
    requestUsed: {
      travelMode: req.travelMode,
      routingPreference: req.routingPreference,
      routeModifiers: req.routeModifiers || null,
      computeAlternativeRoutes: req.computeAlternativeRoutes,
    },
    fieldMaskUsed: fieldMask,
    route: primary,
    alternatives: routes.slice(1).map(normalizeRoute),
  };
}

function normalizeSpeedIntervals(arr) {
  if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return [];
  const out = [];
  for (const it of arr) {
    const s = Number(it?.startPolylinePointIndex);
    const e = Number(it?.endPolylinePointIndex);
    const sp = String(it?.speed || "").toUpperCase();
    if (!Number.isFinite(s) || !Number.isFinite(e) || e <= s) continue;
    out.push({ s, e, speed: sp || "UNKNOWN" });
  }
  return out;
}

function normalizeWaypoint(wp) {
  if (!wp) return null;

  const hasLat = wp.lat != null || wp.latitude != null;
  const hasLon = wp.lon != null || wp.lng != null || wp.longitude != null;

  if (hasLat && hasLon) {
    const lat = Number(wp.lat ?? wp.latitude);
    const lng = Number(wp.lon ?? wp.lng ?? wp.longitude);
    if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng)) return null;
    return { location: { latLng: { latitude: lat, longitude: lng } } };
  }

  if (wp.placeId) {
    const pid = String(wp.placeId).trim();
    if (!pid) return null;
    return { placeId: pid };
  }

  if (wp.address || wp.text) {
    const t = String(wp.address || wp.text).trim();
    if (!t) return null;
    return { address: t };
  }

  return null;
}

function stableStringify(obj) {
  return JSON.stringify(sortRec(obj));
}
function sortRec(x) {
  if (!x || typeof x !== "object") return x;
  if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.map(sortRec);
  const keys = Object.keys(x).sort();
  const out = {};
  for (const k of keys) out[k] = sortRec(x[k]);
  return out;
}

// TDX OAuth token cache
// - module memory: fastest path inside the same Worker isolate
// - Cache API: reuse across requests/isolates when Cloudflare cache has the entry
// - single-flight Promise: a cold matcher may call many TDX loaders in Promise.all; only ONE token request is allowed
let tdxTokenMemory = null;
let tdxTokenExpiresAt = 0;
let tdxTokenRefreshPromise = null;

function isTdxTransientError(err) {
  const m = String(err?.message || err || "");
  return /Token fetch failed:\\s*(429|500|502|503|504)/i.test(m) ||
         /TDX.*(?:429|rate limit|temporarily|timeout|timed out)/i.test(m);
}

async function getTokenCached(env) {
  const now = Date.now();

  // Keep a safety margin so an almost-expired token is never handed to a data request.
  if (tdxTokenMemory && tdxTokenExpiresAt > now + 90_000) {
    return tdxTokenMemory;
  }

  // IMPORTANT: all concurrent callers wait on the same cache-check/fetch operation.
  if (tdxTokenRefreshPromise) return await tdxTokenRefreshPromise;

  tdxTokenRefreshPromise = (async () => {
    const tokenCacheKey = "https://cache.internal/tdx/token_v2";

    const cached = await cacheGetJSON(tokenCacheKey).catch(() => null);
    if (cached?.access_token) {
      tdxTokenMemory = cached.access_token;
      // Cache API already enforces TTL. This local expiry just prevents repeated cache lookups.
      const cachedExp = Number(cached.expires_at);
      tdxTokenExpiresAt = Number.isFinite(cachedExp) && cachedExp > Date.now()
        ? cachedExp
        : Date.now() + 20 * 60 * 1000;
      return tdxTokenMemory;
    }

    const body = new URLSearchParams({
      grant_type: "client_credentials",
      client_id: env.TDX_CLIENT_ID,
      client_secret: env.TDX_CLIENT_SECRET,
    });

    const r = await fetch(
      "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/auth/realms/TDXConnect/protocol/openid-connect/token",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
        body,
      }
    );

    if (!r.ok) {
      const t = await r.text();
      throw new Error(`Token fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
    }

    const j = await r.json();
    if (!j.access_token) {
      throw new Error(`Token missing: ${JSON.stringify(j).slice(0, 300)}`);
    }

    const expiresIn = Math.max(300, Number(j.expires_in) || 3600);
    // Refresh about 2 minutes early; cap Cache API TTL conservatively.
    const ttlSec = Math.max(180, Math.min(3300, Math.floor(expiresIn - 120)));
    const expiresAt = Date.now() + ttlSec * 1000;

    tdxTokenMemory = j.access_token;
    tdxTokenExpiresAt = expiresAt;

    await cachePutJSON(tokenCacheKey, {
      access_token: j.access_token,
      expires_at: expiresAt,
    }, ttlSec).catch(() => {});

    return j.access_token;
  })();

  try {
    return await tdxTokenRefreshPromise;
  } finally {
    tdxTokenRefreshPromise = null;
  }
}

function dirLabel(dir) {
  switch ((dir || "").trim().toUpperCase()) {
    case "N": return "北上";
    case "S": return "南下";
    case "E": return "東向";
    case "W": return "西向";
    case "NE": return "東北向";
    case "NW": return "西北向";
    case "SE": return "東南向";
    case "SW": return "西南向";
    default: return "未知";
  }
}

async function getFreewaySectionMetaCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/freeway/section_meta_v2";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const token = await getTokenCached(env);
  const r = await fetch(
    "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/api/basic/v2/Road/Traffic/Section/Freeway?$format=JSON",
    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
  );

  if (!r.ok) {
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(`SectionMeta fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
  }

  const raw = await r.json();
  const arr = normalizeTdxArray(raw);
  if (!arr) throw new Error(`SectionMeta unexpected shape: ${JSON.stringify(raw).slice(0, 800)}`);

  const map = {};
  for (const s of arr) {
    const dir = (s.RoadDirection || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    map[s.SectionID] = {
      road: s.RoadName || "",
      direction: dir,
      directionLabel: dirLabel(dir),
      start: s.RoadSection?.Start || s.Start || "",
      end: s.RoadSection?.End || s.End || "",
    };
  }

  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, map, 604800);
  return map;
}

async function getFreewayLiveCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/freeway/live";
  const lastGoodKey = "https://cache.internal/tdx/freeway/live_last_good_v1";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached) return cached;

  try {
    const token = await getTokenCached(env);
    const r = await fetch(
      "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/api/basic/v2/Road/Traffic/Live/Freeway?$format=JSON",
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
    );

    if (!r.ok) {
      const t = await r.text();
      throw new Error(`FreewayLive fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
    }

    const data = await r.json();
    await Promise.all([
      cachePutJSON(keyUrl, data, 30),
      cachePutJSON(lastGoodKey, data, 900),
    ]);
    return data;
  } catch (err) {
    const stale = await cacheGetJSON(lastGoodKey).catch(() => null);
    if (stale) {
      console.warn("freeway live refresh failed; using last-good TDX snapshot", err?.message || err);
      return withStaleMarker(stale, err);
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

async function getFreewayGeometryCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/freeway/geometry_v1";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached?.length) return cached;

  if (!env.FREEWAY_GEOM_URL) throw new Error("FREEWAY_GEOM_URL missing (set it to your public JSON URL)");

  const r = await fetch(env.FREEWAY_GEOM_URL, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } });
  if (!r.ok) {
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(`Geometry fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
  }

  const raw = await r.json();
  const out = normalizeGeometryArray(raw);
  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, out, 604800);
  return out;
}

    };

  return map;
}

async function getHighwayLiveCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/highway/live";
  const lastGoodKey = "https://cache.internal/tdx/highway/live_last_good_v1";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached) return cached;

  try {
    const token = await getTokenCached(env);
    const r = await fetch(
      "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/api/basic/v2/Road/Traffic/Live/Highway?$format=JSON",
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
    );

    if (!r.ok) {
      const t = await r.text();
      throw new Error(`Highway Live fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
    }

    const data = await r.json();
    await Promise.all([
      cachePutJSON(keyUrl, data, 30),
      cachePutJSON(lastGoodKey, data, 900),
    ]);
    return data;
  } catch (err) {
    const stale = await cacheGetJSON(lastGoodKey).catch(() => null);
    if (stale) {
      console.warn("highway live refresh failed; using last-good TDX snapshot", err?.message || err);
      return withStaleMarker(stale, err);
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

async function getHighwayStandardCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/highway/standard_v2";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached && typeof cached === "object") return cached;

  if (!env.HIGHWAY_STD_URL) {
    throw new Error("HIGHWAY_STD_URL missing (set it to your public JSON URL for provincial highway standard)");
  }

  const r = await fetch(env.HIGHWAY_STD_URL, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } });
  if (!r.ok) {
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(`Highway standard fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
  }

  const raw = await r.json();
  const arr = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : Array.isArray(raw?.CongestionLevels) ? raw.CongestionLevels : Array.isArray(raw?.congestionLevels) ? raw.congestionLevels : null;
  if (!arr || !arr.length) throw new Error("Highway standard unexpected shape");

  const stdMap = {};
  for (const it of arr) {
    const idRaw = it.CongestionLevelID ?? it.congestionLevelID ?? it.id;
    const id = String(idRaw || "").trim();
    const levelsArr = Array.isArray(it.Levels) ? it.Levels : null;
    if (!id || !levelsArr || !levelsArr.length) continue;

    stdMap[id] = levelsArr.map((x) => ({
      Level: Number(x.Level),
      LowValue: x.LowValue === undefined ? null : Number(x.LowValue),
      TopValue: x.TopValue === undefined ? null : Number(x.TopValue),
      LevelName: x.LevelName || "",
    }));
  }

  if (!Object.keys(stdMap).length) throw new Error("Highway standard parsed empty");

  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, stdMap, 604800);
  return stdMap;
}

async function getHighwayGeometryCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/highway/geometry_v2";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached?.length) return cached;

  if (!env.HIGHWAY_GEOM_URL) throw new Error("HIGHWAY_GEOM_URL missing (set it to your public JSON URL)");

  const r = await fetch(env.HIGHWAY_GEOM_URL, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } });
  if (!r.ok) {
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(`Highway geometry fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
  }

  const raw = await r.json();
  const out = normalizeGeometryArray(raw);
  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, out, 604800);
  return out;
}

async function getTaipeiSectionMetaCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/taipei/section_meta_v1";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const token = await getTokenCached(env);
  const r = await fetch(
    "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/api/basic/v2/Road/Traffic/Section/City/Taipei?$format=JSON",
    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
  );

  if (!r.ok) {
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(`Taipei SectionMeta fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
  }

  const raw = await r.json();
  const arr = normalizeTdxArray(raw);
  if (!arr) throw new Error(`Taipei SectionMeta unexpected shape: ${JSON.stringify(raw).slice(0, 800)}`);

  const map = {};
  for (const s of arr) {
    const dir = (s.RoadDirection || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    map[s.SectionID] = {
      road: s.RoadName || "",
      direction: dir,
      directionLabel: dirLabel(dir),
      start: s.RoadSection?.Start || s.Start || s.SectionName || "",
      end: s.RoadSection?.End || s.End || "",
    };
  }

  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, map, 604800);
  return map;
}

  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/taipei/live";
  const lastGoodKey = "https://cache.internal/tdx/taipei/live_last_good_v1";

  if (cached) return cached;

    const token = await getTokenCached(env);

      "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/api/basic/v2/Road/Traffic/Live/City/Taipei?$format=JSON",
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
    );

    if (!r.ok) {
      const t = await r.text();
      throw new Error(`Taipei Live fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
    }

    const data = await r.json();
    await Promise.all([
      cachePutJSON(keyUrl, data, 30),
      cachePutJSON(lastGoodKey, data, 900),
    ]);
    return data;
  } catch (err) {
    const stale = await cacheGetJSON(lastGoodKey).catch(() => null);
    if (stale) {
      console.warn("taipei live refresh failed; using last-good TDX snapshot", err?.message || err);
      return withStaleMarker(stale, err);
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

async function getTaipeiGeometryCached(env) {
  const keyUrl = "https://cache.internal/tdx/taipei/geometry_v1";
  const cached = await cacheGetJSON(keyUrl);
  if (cached?.length) return cached;

  const token = await getTokenCached(env);
  const r = await fetch(
    "https://tdx.transportdata.tw/api/basic/v2/Road/Traffic/SectionShape/City/Taipei?$format=JSON",
    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
  );

  if (!r.ok) {
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(`Taipei geometry fetch failed: ${r.status} ${t.slice(0, 300)}`);
  }

  const raw = await r.json();
  const out = normalizeGeometryArray(raw);
  await cachePutJSON(keyUrl, out, 604800);
  return out;
}

function normalizeTdxArray(raw) {
  return Array.isArray(raw) ? raw :
    Array.isArray(raw?.Sections) ? raw.Sections :
    Array.isArray(raw?.SectionInfos) ? raw.SectionInfos :
    Array.isArray(raw?.data) ? raw.data :
    Array.isArray(raw?.value) ? raw.value :
    null;
}

function normalizeGeometryArray(raw) {
  const arr = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : Array.isArray(raw?.geometries) ? raw.geometries : Array.isArray(raw?.SectionShapes) ? raw.SectionShapes : null;
  if (!arr || !arr.length) throw new Error("Geometry payload empty");

  const out = [];
  for (const it of arr) {
    const sid = it.SectionID || it.sectionId || it.sectionID || it.id;
    const wkt = it.Geometry || it.geometry || it.wkt;
    if (!sid || !wkt) continue;
    out.push({ SectionID: String(sid), Geometry: String(wkt) });
  }
  return out;
}

function normalizeTaipeiLevel(v) {
  const n = Number(v);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return -99;
  return n;
}

function getTaipeiCongestionColor(level) {
  const lv = Number(level);
  if (lv === 1 || lv === 2) return "#16a34a";
  if (lv === 3 || lv === 4) return "#f59e0b";
  if (lv === 5 || lv === 6) return "#dc2626";
  return "#9ca3af";
}

function resolveHighwayLevel(input, stdMap) {
  const lv = Number(input?.congestionLevel);
  if (lv === -1 || lv === -99) return lv;
  if ([1, 2, 3].includes(lv)) return lv;

  const speed = Number(input?.speedKph);
  if (!Number.isFinite(speed)) return 0;

  const cid = String(input?.congestionLevelID || "").trim();
  const levels = (cid && stdMap?.[cid]) ? stdMap[cid] : (stdMap?.["A"] ? stdMap["A"] : null);
  if (!levels || !levels.length) return 0;

  for (const L of levels) {
    const level = Number(L.Level);
    const lo = (L.LowValue === null ? -Infinity : Number(L.LowValue));
    const hi = (L.TopValue === null ? Infinity : Number(L.TopValue));

    if (!Number.isFinite(lo) && lo !== -Infinity) continue;
    if (!Number.isFinite(hi) && hi !== Infinity) continue;

    if (speed >= lo && speed <= hi) {
      return (level === 1 || level === 2 || level === 3) ? level : 0;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}
